================================================================
EmittedTopic :: video_stream
================================================================
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct MessageHeader {
    pub stamp: std::time::SystemTime,
    pub frame_id: u32,
}
#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
pub fn build_message(
    header: MessageHeader,
    encoding: String,
    width: u32,
    frame: Vec<u8>,
) -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {
    let payload = {
        let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
        {
            let mut root = capnp_msg
                .init_root::<
                    crate::capnp::video_stream_message_capnp::video_stream_message::Builder,
                >();
            let mut builder_0 = root.reborrow().init_header();
            let timestamp_1 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time((header).stamp);
            let mut timestamp_builder_2 = builder_0.reborrow().init_stamp();
            timestamp_builder_2.set_sec(timestamp_1.sec);
            timestamp_builder_2.set_nsec(timestamp_1.nsec);
            builder_0.set_frame_id((header).frame_id);
            root.set_encoding(encoding.as_str());
            root.set_width(width);
            root.set_frame(frame.as_ref());
        }
        let mut buffer = Vec::new();
        capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
            .map_err(|source| {
                let context = String::from("video_stream");
                crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
            })?;
        peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
    };
    Ok(payload)
}
/// Declares the topic publisher: takes the central messenger lock once
/// and returns a lock-free `peppylib::TopicPublisher`. Declare once,
/// then call `publisher.publish(build_message(...)?)` per message.
pub async fn declare_publisher(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
) -> crate::Result<peppylib::TopicPublisher> {
    let qos = peppylib::config::QoSProfile::SensorData;
    let retention = peppylib::config::TopicRetention::LiveOnly;
    let as_topic = "video_stream";
    let as_instance_id = node_runner.processor().bound_instance_id();
    let with_core_node = node_runner.processor().bound_core_node();
    let as_target = peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node(
        node_runner.processor().node_name(),
        node_runner.processor().node_tag(),
    )?;
    let publisher = peppylib::TopicMessenger::declare_publisher(
            node_runner.messenger(),
            with_core_node,
            as_instance_id,
            as_target,
            None,
            as_topic,
            qos,
            retention,
        )
        .await?;
    Ok(publisher)
}

pub fn build_message() -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {

/// Declares the topic publisher: takes the central messenger lock once
/// and returns a lock-free `peppylib::TopicPublisher`. Declare once,

    let as_topic = "heartbeat";
    let as_instance_id = node_runner.processor().bound_instance_id();
    let with_core_node = node_runner.processor().bound_core_node();
    let as_target = peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node(
        node_runner.processor().node_name(),
        node_runner.processor().node_tag(),
    )?;
    let publisher = peppylib::TopicMessenger::declare_publisher(
            node_runner.messenger(),
            with_core_node,
            as_instance_id,
            as_target,
            None,
            as_topic,
            qos,
            retention,
        )
        .await?;
    Ok(publisher)
}


================================================================
EmittedTopic :: camera_state
================================================================
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct MessageHeader {
    pub stamp: std::time::SystemTime,
    pub frame_id: u32,
}
#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
pub fn build_message(
    header: MessageHeader,
    encoding: String,
    width: u32,
    frame: Vec<u8>,
) -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {
    let payload = {
        let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
        {
            let mut root = capnp_msg
                .init_root::<
                    crate::capnp::camera_state_message_capnp::camera_state_message::Builder,
                >();
            let mut builder_0 = root.reborrow().init_header();
            let timestamp_1 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time((header).stamp);
            let mut timestamp_builder_2 = builder_0.reborrow().init_stamp();
            timestamp_builder_2.set_sec(timestamp_1.sec);
            timestamp_builder_2.set_nsec(timestamp_1.nsec);
            builder_0.set_frame_id((header).frame_id);
            root.set_encoding(encoding.as_str());
            root.set_width(width);
            root.set_frame(frame.as_ref());
        }
        let mut buffer = Vec::new();
        capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
            .map_err(|source| {
                let context = String::from("camera_state");
                crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
            })?;
        peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
    };
    Ok(payload)
}
/// Declares the topic publisher: takes the central messenger lock once
/// and returns a lock-free `peppylib::TopicPublisher`. Declare once,
/// then call `publisher.publish(build_message(...)?)` per message.
///
///The topic retains its newest 3 messages: a subscription that starts later also receives them.
pub async fn declare_publisher(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
) -> crate::Result<peppylib::TopicPublisher> {
    let qos = peppylib::config::QoSProfile::SensorData;
    let retention = {
        const RETENTION: peppylib::config::TopicRetention = match peppylib::config::TopicRetention::latest(
            3,
        ) {
            Ok(retention) => retention,
            Err(_) => panic!("the topic's manifest states a retention depth in range"),
        };
        RETENTION
    };
    let as_topic = "camera_state";
    let as_instance_id = node_runner.processor().bound_instance_id();
    let with_core_node = node_runner.processor().bound_core_node();
    let as_target = peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node(
        node_runner.processor().node_name(),
        node_runner.processor().node_tag(),
    )?;
    let publisher = peppylib::TopicMessenger::declare_publisher(
            node_runner.messenger(),
            with_core_node,
            as_instance_id,
            as_target,
            None,
            as_topic,
            qos,
            retention,
        )
        .await?;
    Ok(publisher)
}


================================================================
ExposedService :: enable_camera
================================================================
const SERVICE_NAME: &str = "enable_camera";
/// A failure kind of this member: a kind of peppy, or a kind of its
/// contract that it lists.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum FailureKind {
    /// The attempt gave no result
    AttemptFailed,
    /// The bound of the provider passed
    Timeout,
    /// A precondition failed
    PreconditionFailed,
    /// A robot, limb or host is gone
    ResourceUnavailable,
    /// The provider refused the goal, and no other kind says why
    Rejected,
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl FailureKind {
    /// Every kind of this member: the kinds of peppy, then the
    /// kinds of its contract that it lists.
    pub const ALL: [Self; 5] = [
        Self::AttemptFailed,
        Self::Timeout,
        Self::PreconditionFailed,
        Self::ResourceUnavailable,
        Self::Rejected,
    ];
    /// The name of the kind on the wire.
    pub const fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::AttemptFailed => "attempt_failed",
            Self::Timeout => "timeout",
            Self::PreconditionFailed => "precondition_failed",
            Self::ResourceUnavailable => "resource_unavailable",
            Self::Rejected => "rejected",
        }
    }
    /// The kind named `name`, or `None` when this member does not
    /// declare it.
    pub fn parse(name: &str) -> Option<Self> {
        Self::ALL.into_iter().find(|kind| kind.as_str() == name)
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for FailureKind {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.write_str(self.as_str())
    }
}
/// Why this member refused or failed a goal, or gave an error
/// reply: a kind that it declares, and a message for a person.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Failure {
    pub kind: FailureKind,
    pub message: String,
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl Failure {
    pub fn new(kind: FailureKind, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            kind,
            message: message.into(),
        }
    }
    /// The wire form of the failure.
    pub(crate) fn to_wire(&self) -> peppylib::messaging::Failure {
        let kind = peppylib::messaging::FailureKindName::parse(self.kind.as_str())
            .expect("each kind of a member is a valid failure kind name");
        peppylib::messaging::Failure::new(kind, self.message.clone())
    }
    /// Parses a failure from the wire. A kind that this member does
    /// not declare is a deserialization error, whose text keeps the
    /// message of the provider.
    pub(crate) fn from_wire(
        failure: &peppylib::messaging::Failure,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let kind = FailureKind::parse(failure.kind().as_str())
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!(
                        "the failure kind `{}` is not a kind of `{}` (message: {:?})",
                        failure.kind(), SERVICE_NAME, failure.message()
                    ),
                )
            })?;
        Ok(Self::new(kind, failure.message()))
    }
    /// The failure of the error reply that `error` holds: `None`
    /// when `error` is not an error reply of a provider, or when
    /// its kind is not a kind of this member.
    pub fn of(error: &crate::Error) -> Option<Self> {
        let crate::Error::ServiceFailed { failure, .. } = error else {
            return None;
        };
        Self::from_wire(failure).ok()
    }
    /// `error` itself, unless it is an error reply with a kind this
    /// member does not declare: such a reply breaks the contract of
    /// the member, and gives a deserialization error instead.
    pub(crate) fn reject_undeclared_kind(error: crate::Error) -> crate::Error {
        let crate::Error::ServiceFailed { failure, .. } = &error else {
            return error;
        };
        match Self::from_wire(failure) {
            Ok(_) => error,
            Err(undeclared) => undeclared,
        }
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for Failure {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{}: {}", self.kind, self.message)
    }
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Response {
    pub enabled: bool,
    pub error_msg: Option<String>,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Request {
    pub instance_id: String,
    pub core_node: String,
    pub data: RequestData,
}
impl Response {
    pub fn new(enabled: bool, error_msg: Option<String>) -> Self {
        Self { enabled, error_msg }
    }
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct RequestData {
    pub enable: bool,
}
/// Answers the next request of this service with `handler`. The
/// response of the handler is a success; its `Failure` gives the
/// caller an error reply of a kind that this member declares. A
/// request that does not decode gets an error reply of the kind
/// `rejected`, and a response that does not serialize one of
/// the kind `attempt_failed`.
pub async fn handle_next_request<F>(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
    handler: F,
) -> crate::Result<()>
where
    F: Fn(Request) -> std::result::Result<Response, Failure>,
{
    let mut service = peppylib::ServiceMessenger::listen(
            node_runner.messenger(),
            node_runner.processor().bound_core_node(),
            node_runner.processor().bound_instance_id(),
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node(
                node_runner.processor().node_name(),
                node_runner.processor().node_tag(),
            )?,
            SERVICE_NAME,
        )
        .await?;
    service
        .handle_next_request(move |request_context| {
            async move {
                {
                    let message = request_context.message();
                    let payload = message.payload_bytes();
                    let core_node = message.core_node().to_string();
                    let instance_id = message.instance_id().to_string();
                    handle_request_payload(
                        payload.as_ref(),
                        &handler,
                        core_node,
                        instance_id,
                    )
                }
                    .map_err(|failure| failure.to_wire())
            }
        })
        .await?;
    Ok(())
}
fn serialize_response(response: Response) -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {
    Ok({
        let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
        {
            let mut root = capnp_msg
                .init_root::<
                    crate::capnp::enable_camera_response_message_capnp::enable_camera_response_message::Builder,
                >();
            root.set_enabled(response.enabled);
            if let Some(value_0) = (response.error_msg).as_ref() {
                root.set_error_msg(value_0.as_str());
            }
        }
        let mut buffer = Vec::new();
        capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
            .map_err(|source| {
                let context = format!("handle_request_payload {}", SERVICE_NAME);
                crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
            })?;
        peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
    })
}
fn deserialize_request(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<RequestData> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]
    let context = String::from(SERVICE_NAME);
    let mut payload = payload;
    let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader
        .get_root::<
            crate::capnp::enable_camera_message_capnp::enable_camera_message::Reader,
        >()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let enable_0 = root.reborrow().get_enable();
    Ok(RequestData { enable: enable_0 })
}
fn handle_request_payload<F>(
    payload: &[u8],
    handler: &F,
    core_node: String,
    instance_id: String,
) -> std::result::Result<peppylib::Payload, Failure>
where
    F: Fn(Request) -> std::result::Result<Response, Failure>,
{
    let request_data = deserialize_request(payload)
        .map_err(|error| {
            Failure::new(
                FailureKind::Rejected,
                format!("request does not decode: {error}"),
            )
        })?;
    let request = Request {
        instance_id,
        core_node,
        data: request_data,
    };
    let response = handler(request)?;
    serialize_response(response)
        .map_err(|error| {
            Failure::new(
                FailureKind::AttemptFailed,
                format!("response does not serialize: {error}"),
            )
        })
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Request {
    pub instance_id: String,
    pub core_node: String,
    pub data: RequestData,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct RequestData {
    pub mode: u8,
}
/// Answers the next request of this service with `handler`. The
/// response of the handler is a success; its `Failure` gives the
/// caller an error reply of a kind that this member declares. A
/// request that does not decode gets an error reply of the kind
/// `rejected`, and a response that does not serialize one of
/// the kind `attempt_failed`.
pub async fn handle_next_request<F>(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
    handler: F,
) -> crate::Result<()>
where
    F: Fn(Request) -> std::result::Result<(), Failure>,
{
    let mut service = peppylib::ServiceMessenger::listen(
            node_runner.messenger(),
            node_runner.processor().bound_core_node(),
            node_runner.processor().bound_instance_id(),
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node(
                node_runner.processor().node_name(),
                node_runner.processor().node_tag(),
            )?,
            SERVICE_NAME,
        )
        .await?;
    service
        .handle_next_request(move |request_context| {
            async move {
                {
                    let message = request_context.message();
                    let payload = message.payload_bytes();
                    let core_node = message.core_node().to_string();
                    let instance_id = message.instance_id().to_string();
                    handle_request_payload(
                        payload.as_ref(),
                        &handler,
                        core_node,
                        instance_id,
                    )
                }
                    .map_err(|failure| failure.to_wire())
            }
        })
        .await?;
    Ok(())
}

            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader
        .get_root::<crate::capnp::set_mode_message_capnp::set_mode_message::Reader>()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let mode_0 = root.reborrow().get_mode();
    Ok(RequestData { mode: mode_0 })
}
fn handle_request_payload<F>(
    payload: &[u8],
    handler: &F,
    core_node: String,
    instance_id: String,
) -> std::result::Result<peppylib::Payload, Failure>
where
    F: Fn(Request) -> std::result::Result<(), Failure>,
{
    let request_data = deserialize_request(payload)
        .map_err(|error| {
            Failure::new(
                FailureKind::Rejected,
                format!("request does not decode: {error}"),
            )
        })?;
    let request = Request {
        instance_id,
        core_node,
        data: request_data,
    };
    handler(request)?;
    Ok(peppylib::Payload::new())
}


================================================================
ExposedService :: get_system_status
================================================================
const SERVICE_NAME: &str = "get_system_status";
/// A failure kind of this member: a kind of peppy, or a kind of its
/// contract that it lists.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum FailureKind {
    /// The attempt gave no result
    AttemptFailed,
    /// The bound of the provider passed
    Timeout,
    /// A precondition failed
    PreconditionFailed,
    /// A robot, limb or host is gone
    ResourceUnavailable,
    /// The provider refused the goal, and no other kind says why
    Rejected,
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl FailureKind {
    /// Every kind of this member: the kinds of peppy, then the
    /// kinds of its contract that it lists.
    pub const ALL: [Self; 5] = [
        Self::AttemptFailed,
        Self::Timeout,
        Self::PreconditionFailed,
        Self::ResourceUnavailable,
        Self::Rejected,
    ];
    /// The name of the kind on the wire.
    pub const fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::AttemptFailed => "attempt_failed",
            Self::Timeout => "timeout",
            Self::PreconditionFailed => "precondition_failed",
            Self::ResourceUnavailable => "resource_unavailable",
            Self::Rejected => "rejected",
        }
    }
    /// The kind named `name`, or `None` when this member does not
    /// declare it.
    pub fn parse(name: &str) -> Option<Self> {
        Self::ALL.into_iter().find(|kind| kind.as_str() == name)
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for FailureKind {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.write_str(self.as_str())
    }
}
/// Why this member refused or failed a goal, or gave an error
/// reply: a kind that it declares, and a message for a person.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Failure {
    pub kind: FailureKind,
    pub message: String,
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl Failure {
    pub fn new(kind: FailureKind, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            kind,
            message: message.into(),
        }
    }
    /// The wire form of the failure.
    pub(crate) fn to_wire(&self) -> peppylib::messaging::Failure {
        let kind = peppylib::messaging::FailureKindName::parse(self.kind.as_str())
            .expect("each kind of a member is a valid failure kind name");
        peppylib::messaging::Failure::new(kind, self.message.clone())
    }
    /// Parses a failure from the wire. A kind that this member does
    /// not declare is a deserialization error, whose text keeps the
    /// message of the provider.
    pub(crate) fn from_wire(
        failure: &peppylib::messaging::Failure,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let kind = FailureKind::parse(failure.kind().as_str())
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!(
                        "the failure kind `{}` is not a kind of `{}` (message: {:?})",
                        failure.kind(), SERVICE_NAME, failure.message()
                    ),
                )
            })?;
        Ok(Self::new(kind, failure.message()))
    }
    /// The failure of the error reply that `error` holds: `None`
    /// when `error` is not an error reply of a provider, or when
    /// its kind is not a kind of this member.
    pub fn of(error: &crate::Error) -> Option<Self> {
        let crate::Error::ServiceFailed { failure, .. } = error else {
            return None;
        };
        Self::from_wire(failure).ok()
    }
    /// `error` itself, unless it is an error reply with a kind this
    /// member does not declare: such a reply breaks the contract of
    /// the member, and gives a deserialization error instead.
    pub(crate) fn reject_undeclared_kind(error: crate::Error) -> crate::Error {
        let crate::Error::ServiceFailed { failure, .. } = &error else {
            return error;
        };
        match Self::from_wire(failure) {
            Ok(_) => error,
            Err(undeclared) => undeclared,
        }
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for Failure {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{}: {}", self.kind, self.message)
    }
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Response {
    pub healthy: bool,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Request {
    pub instance_id: String,
    pub core_node: String,
}
impl Response {
    pub fn new(healthy: bool) -> Self {
        Self { healthy }
    }
}
/// Answers the next request of this service with `handler`. The
/// response of the handler is a success; its `Failure` gives the
/// caller an error reply of a kind that this member declares. A
/// request that does not decode gets an error reply of the kind
/// `rejected`, and a response that does not serialize one of
/// the kind `attempt_failed`.
pub async fn handle_next_request<F>(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
    handler: F,
) -> crate::Result<()>
where
    F: Fn(Request) -> std::result::Result<Response, Failure>,
{
    let mut service = peppylib::ServiceMessenger::listen(
            node_runner.messenger(),
            node_runner.processor().bound_core_node(),
            node_runner.processor().bound_instance_id(),
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node(
                node_runner.processor().node_name(),
                node_runner.processor().node_tag(),
            )?,
            SERVICE_NAME,
        )
        .await?;
    service
        .handle_next_request(move |request_context| {
            async move {
                {
                    let message = request_context.message();
                    let core_node = message.core_node().to_string();
                    let instance_id = message.instance_id().to_string();
                    handle_request_payload(&handler, core_node, instance_id)
                }
                    .map_err(|failure| failure.to_wire())
            }
        })
        .await?;
    Ok(())
}
fn serialize_response(response: Response) -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {
    Ok({
        let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
        {
            let mut root = capnp_msg
                .init_root::<
                    crate::capnp::get_system_status_response_message_capnp::get_system_status_response_message::Builder,
                >();
            root.set_healthy(response.healthy);
        }
        let mut buffer = Vec::new();
        capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
            .map_err(|source| {
                let context = format!("handle_request_payload {}", SERVICE_NAME);
                crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
            })?;
        peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
    })
}
fn handle_request_payload<F>(
    handler: &F,
    core_node: String,
    instance_id: String,
) -> std::result::Result<peppylib::Payload, Failure>
where
    F: Fn(Request) -> std::result::Result<Response, Failure>,
{
    let request = Request { instance_id, core_node };
    let response = handler(request)?;
    serialize_response(response)
        .map_err(|error| {
            Failure::new(
                FailureKind::AttemptFailed,
                format!("response does not serialize: {error}"),
            )
        })
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Response {
    pub enabled: bool,
    pub error_msg: Option<String>,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Request {
    pub instance_id: String,
    pub core_node: String,
    pub data: RequestData,
}
impl Response {
    pub fn new(enabled: bool, error_msg: Option<String>) -> Self {
        Self { enabled, error_msg }
    }
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct RequestData {
    pub enable: bool,
}
/// Answers the next request of this service with `handler`. The
/// response of the handler is a success; its `Failure` gives the
/// caller an error reply of a kind that this member declares. A
/// request that does not decode gets an error reply of the kind
/// `rejected`, and a response that does not serialize one of
/// the kind `attempt_failed`.
pub async fn handle_next_request<F>(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
    handler: F,
) -> crate::Result<()>
where
    F: Fn(Request) -> std::result::Result<Response, Failure>,
{
    let mut service = peppylib::ServiceMessenger::listen(
            node_runner.messenger(),
            node_runner.processor().bound_core_node(),
            node_runner.processor().bound_instance_id(),
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::contract("camera_feed", "v1")?,
            SERVICE_NAME,
        )
        .await?;
    service
        .handle_next_request(move |request_context| {
            async move {
                {
                    let message = request_context.message();
                    let payload = message.payload_bytes();
                    let core_node = message.core_node().to_string();
                    let instance_id = message.instance_id().to_string();
                    handle_request_payload(
                        payload.as_ref(),
                        &handler,
                        core_node,
                        instance_id,
                    )
                }
                    .map_err(|failure| failure.to_wire())
            }
        })
        .await?;
    Ok(())
}
fn serialize_response(response: Response) -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {
    Ok({
        let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
        {
            let mut root = capnp_msg
                .init_root::<
                    crate::capnp::camera_feed_v1_enable_camera_response_message_capnp::enable_camera_response_message::Builder,
                >();
            root.set_enabled(response.enabled);
            if let Some(value_0) = (response.error_msg).as_ref() {
                root.set_error_msg(value_0.as_str());
            }
        }
        let mut buffer = Vec::new();
        capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
            .map_err(|source| {
                let context = format!("handle_request_payload {}", SERVICE_NAME);
                crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
            })?;
        peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
    })
}
fn deserialize_request(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<RequestData> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]
    let context = String::from(SERVICE_NAME);
    let mut payload = payload;
    let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader
        .get_root::<
            crate::capnp::camera_feed_v1_enable_camera_message_capnp::enable_camera_message::Reader,
        >()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let enable_0 = root.reborrow().get_enable();
    Ok(RequestData { enable: enable_0 })
}
fn handle_request_payload<F>(
    payload: &[u8],
    handler: &F,
    core_node: String,
    instance_id: String,
) -> std::result::Result<peppylib::Payload, Failure>
where
    F: Fn(Request) -> std::result::Result<Response, Failure>,
{
    let request_data = deserialize_request(payload)
        .map_err(|error| {
            Failure::new(
                FailureKind::Rejected,
                format!("request does not decode: {error}"),
            )
        })?;
    let request = Request {
        instance_id,
        core_node,
        data: request_data,
    };
    let response = handler(request)?;
    serialize_response(response)
        .map_err(|error| {
            Failure::new(
                FailureKind::AttemptFailed,
                format!("response does not serialize: {error}"),
            )
        })
}

            kind,
            message: message.into(),
        }
    }
    /// The wire form of the failure.
    pub(crate) fn to_wire(&self) -> peppylib::messaging::Failure {
        let kind = peppylib::messaging::FailureKindName::parse(self.kind.as_str())
            .expect("each kind of a member is a valid failure kind name");
        peppylib::messaging::Failure::new(kind, self.message.clone())
    }
    /// Parses a failure from the wire. A kind that this member does
    /// not declare is a deserialization error, whose text keeps the
    /// message of the provider.
    pub(crate) fn from_wire(
        failure: &peppylib::messaging::Failure,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let kind = FailureKind::parse(failure.kind().as_str())
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!(
                        "the failure kind `{}` is not a kind of `{}` (message: {:?})",
                        failure.kind(), ACTION_NAME, failure.message()
                    ),
                )
            })?;
        Ok(Self::new(kind, failure.message()))
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for Failure {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{}: {}", self.kind, self.message)
    }
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum GoalDecision {
    Accept(GoalResponse),
    Refuse(Failure),
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl GoalDecision {
    /// Accepts the goal and replies with the declared response.
    pub fn accept(response: GoalResponse) -> Self {
        Self::Accept(response)
    }
    /// Refuses the goal with a failure kind that this member
    /// declares and a message for a person. A refusal carries no
    /// goal response.
    pub fn refuse(kind: FailureKind, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self::Refuse(Failure::new(kind, message))
    }
}
pub struct ActionHandle {
    inner: peppylib::messaging::ConcurrentAction,
}
pub struct GoalContext {
    inner: peppylib::messaging::GoalContext,
    request: GoalRequest,
}
impl ActionHandle {
    pub async fn expose(node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let inner = peppylib::messaging::ConcurrentAction::expose(
                node_runner.messenger(),
                node_runner.processor().bound_core_node(),
                node_runner.processor().bound_instance_id(),
                peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node(
                    node_runner.processor().node_name(),
                    node_runner.processor().node_tag(),
                )?,
                ACTION_NAME,
                true,
            )
            .await?;
        Ok(Self { inner })
    }
    /// Sets the sender grace of the action: how long it keeps the goals
    /// of a sender that is gone before they receive their cancel signal,
    /// with the cause `CancelCause::SenderGone`. Zero by default. It
    /// applies to each sender found gone after the call.
    pub fn set_sender_grace(&self, grace: std::time::Duration) {
        self.inner.set_sender_grace(grace);
    }
    pub async fn handle_goal_next_request<F>(
        &mut self,
        decider: F,
    ) -> crate::Result<Option<GoalContext>>
    where
        F: Fn(&GoalRequest) -> crate::Result<GoalDecision>,
    {
        loop {
            let Some(pending) = self.inner.recv_next_goal().await? else {
                return Ok(None);
            };
            let request_data = match deserialize_goal_request(pending.request_bytes()) {
                Ok(request_data) => request_data,
                Err(error) => {
                    let refusal = Failure::new(
                        FailureKind::Rejected,
                        format!("goal request does not decode: {error}"),
                    );
                    pending.refuse(refusal.to_wire()).await?;
                    continue;
                }
            };
            let request = GoalRequest {
                instance_id: pending.instance_id().to_string(),
                core_node: pending.core_node().to_string(),
                data: request_data,
            };
            let decision = match decider(&request) {
                Ok(decision) => decision,
                Err(error) => {
                    let refusal = Failure::new(
                        FailureKind::AttemptFailed,
                        format!("the goal decider failed: {error}"),
                    );
                    pending.refuse(refusal.to_wire()).await?;
                    continue;
                }
            };
            match decision {
                GoalDecision::Accept(response) => {
                    let response_payload = {
                        let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
                        {
                            let mut root = capnp_msg
                                .init_root::<
                                    crate::capnp::move_arm_goal_response_message_capnp::move_arm_goal_response_message::Builder,
                                >();
                            root.set_accepted(response.accepted);
                        }
                        let mut buffer = Vec::new();
                        capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
                            .map_err(|source| {
                                let context = format!(
                                    "{} {}", "handle_goal_next_request", ACTION_NAME
                                );
                                crate::Error::Serialization(
                                    format!("{}: {}", context, source),
                                )
                            })?;
                        peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
                    };
                    let inner = pending.accept(response_payload).await?;
                    return Ok(Some(GoalContext { inner, request }));
                }
                GoalDecision::Refuse(failure) => {
                    pending.refuse(failure.to_wire()).await?;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
impl GoalContext {
    /// The decoded goal request (caller identity and, if any, data).
    pub fn request(&self) -> &GoalRequest {
        &self.request
    }
    /// The client-generated correlation id for this goal.
    pub fn goal_id(&self) -> &str {
        self.inner.goal_id()
    }
    /// The process that sent this goal. The action cancels the goal
    /// when this process is gone and its sender grace passed.
    pub fn sender(&self) -> &peppylib::messaging::ClientPresence {
        self.inner.sender()
    }
    /// Resolves when this goal receives its cancel signal: a cancel
    /// request of its caller, or the end of its sender (see
    /// `cancel_cause`). React by calling `complete_cancelled`.
    pub async fn cancel_signal(&self) {
        self.inner.cancel_signal().await
    }
    /// Whether this goal received its cancel signal.
    pub fn is_cancelled(&self) -> bool {
        self.inner.is_cancelled()
    }
    /// Why this goal received its cancel signal; `None` before it did.
    /// `CancelCause::reason` gives a reason for `complete_cancelled`.
    pub fn cancel_cause(&self) -> Option<peppylib::messaging::CancelCause> {
        self.inner.cancel_cause()
    }
    /// Ends the goal with the status failed: a failure kind that this
    /// member declares, a message for a person, and no result. The
    /// first of `complete`, `fail` and `complete_cancelled` ends the
    /// goal; later calls do nothing.
    pub async fn fail(
        &self,
        kind: FailureKind,
        message: impl Into<String>,
    ) -> crate::Result<()> {
        self.inner.fail(Failure::new(kind, message).to_wire()).await?;
        Ok(())
    }
    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub async fn publish_feedback(&self, new_position: [i32; 3]) -> crate::Result<()> {
        let payload = {
            let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
            {
                let mut root = capnp_msg
                    .init_root::<
                        crate::capnp::emit_move_arm_feedback_message_capnp::move_arm_feedback_message::Builder,
                    >();
                let mut list_0 = root.reborrow().init_new_position(3);
                for (idx_1, value_2) in (new_position).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_0.set(idx_1 as u32, *value_2);
                }
            }
            let mut buffer = Vec::new();
            capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
                .map_err(|source| {
                    let context = format!(
                        "{} {}", "publish_feedback move_arm", ACTION_NAME
                    );
                    crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
                })?;
            peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
        };
        let payload = peppylib::messaging::NonEmptyPayload::try_new(payload)
            .map_err(|_| peppylib::PeppyError::Io(
                std::io::Error::other(
                    "publish_feedback produced an empty payload (codec serialized \
                     to zero bytes); empty is reserved for end-of-stream",
                ),
            ))?;
        self.inner.publish_feedback(payload).await?;
        Ok(())
    }
    /// Ends the goal with the status completed and its result:
    /// the goal is a success. The first of `complete`, `fail`
    /// and `complete_cancelled` ends the goal; later calls do
    /// nothing.
    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub async fn complete(
        &self,
        success: bool,
        error_msg: Option<String>,
    ) -> crate::Result<()> {
        let payload = {
            let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
            {
                let mut root = capnp_msg
                    .init_root::<
                        crate::capnp::move_arm_result_response_message_capnp::move_arm_result_message::Builder,
                    >();
                root.set_success(success);
                if let Some(value_0) = (error_msg).as_ref() {
                    root.set_error_msg(value_0.as_str());
                }
            }
            let mut buffer = Vec::new();
            capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
                .map_err(|source| {
                    let context = format!("{} {}", "move_arm_result", ACTION_NAME);
                    crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
                })?;
            peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
        };
        self.inner.complete(payload).await?;
        Ok(())
    }
    /// Ends the goal with the status cancelled, for `reason`, with its result.
    /// A worker calls it after the cancel signal
    /// (`CancelCause::reason` gives a reason), and a member
    /// that ends the goals of other clients calls it with a
    /// reason of its own.
    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub async fn complete_cancelled(
        &self,
        reason: impl Into<String>,
        success: bool,
        error_msg: Option<String>,
    ) -> crate::Result<()> {
        let payload = {
            let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
            {
                let mut root = capnp_msg
                    .init_root::<
                        crate::capnp::move_arm_result_response_message_capnp::move_arm_result_message::Builder,
                    >();
                root.set_success(success);
                if let Some(value_0) = (error_msg).as_ref() {
                    root.set_error_msg(value_0.as_str());
                }
            }
            let mut buffer = Vec::new();
            capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
                .map_err(|source| {
                    let context = format!("{} {}", "move_arm_result", ACTION_NAME);
                    crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
                })?;
            peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
        };
        self.inner.complete_cancelled(reason, payload).await?;
        Ok(())
    }
}
fn deserialize_goal_request(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<GoalRequestData> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]
    let context = format!("{} {}", stringify!(deserialize_goal_request), ACTION_NAME);
    let mut payload = payload;
    let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader
        .get_root::<
            crate::capnp::move_arm_goal_message_capnp::move_arm_goal_message::Reader,
        >()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let arm_id_0 = root.reborrow().get_arm_id();
    let list_1 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_desired_position()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = format!(
                "{} {}", stringify!(deserialize_goal_request), ACTION_NAME
            );
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "desired_position", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    if list_1.len() as usize != 3 {
        let actual = list_1.len() as usize;
        return Err(
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!(
                    "invalid fixed list length for field '{}': expected {}, got {}",
                    "desired_position", 3, actual
                ),
            ),
        );
    }
    let mut desired_position_2: [i32; 3] = [i32::default(); 3];
    for (idx, element) in list_1.iter().enumerate() {
        desired_position_2[idx] = element;
    }
    Ok(GoalRequestData {
        arm_id: arm_id_0,
        desired_position: desired_position_2,
    })
}

/// A failure kind of this member: a kind of peppy, or a kind of its
/// contract that it lists.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum FailureKind {
    /// The attempt gave no result
    AttemptFailed,
    /// The bound of the provider passed
    Timeout,
    /// A precondition failed
    PreconditionFailed,
    /// A robot, limb or host is gone
    ResourceUnavailable,
    /// The provider refused the goal, and no other kind says why
    Rejected,
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl FailureKind {
    /// Every kind of this member: the kinds of peppy, then the
    /// kinds of its contract that it lists.
    pub const ALL: [Self; 5] = [
        Self::AttemptFailed,
        Self::Timeout,
        Self::PreconditionFailed,
        Self::ResourceUnavailable,
        Self::Rejected,
    ];
    /// The name of the kind on the wire.
    pub const fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::AttemptFailed => "attempt_failed",
            Self::Timeout => "timeout",
            Self::PreconditionFailed => "precondition_failed",
            Self::ResourceUnavailable => "resource_unavailable",
            Self::Rejected => "rejected",
        }
    }
    /// The kind named `name`, or `None` when this member does not
    /// declare it.
    pub fn parse(name: &str) -> Option<Self> {
        Self::ALL.into_iter().find(|kind| kind.as_str() == name)
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for FailureKind {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.write_str(self.as_str())
    }
}
/// Why this member refused or failed a goal, or gave an error
/// reply: a kind that it declares, and a message for a person.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Failure {
    pub kind: FailureKind,
    pub message: String,
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl Failure {
    pub fn new(kind: FailureKind, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            kind,
            message: message.into(),
        }
    }
    /// The wire form of the failure.
    pub(crate) fn to_wire(&self) -> peppylib::messaging::Failure {
        let kind = peppylib::messaging::FailureKindName::parse(self.kind.as_str())
            .expect("each kind of a member is a valid failure kind name");
        peppylib::messaging::Failure::new(kind, self.message.clone())
    }
    /// Parses a failure from the wire. A kind that this member does
    /// not declare is a deserialization error, whose text keeps the
    /// message of the provider.
    pub(crate) fn from_wire(
        failure: &peppylib::messaging::Failure,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let kind = FailureKind::parse(failure.kind().as_str())
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!(
                        "the failure kind `{}` is not a kind of `{}` (message: {:?})",
                        failure.kind(), ACTION_NAME, failure.message()
                    ),
                )
            })?;
        Ok(Self::new(kind, failure.message()))
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for Failure {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{}: {}", self.kind, self.message)
    }
}

impl ActionHandle {
    pub async fn expose(node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let inner = peppylib::messaging::ConcurrentAction::expose(
                node_runner.messenger(),
                node_runner.processor().bound_core_node(),
                node_runner.processor().bound_instance_id(),
                peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node(
                    node_runner.processor().node_name(),
                    node_runner.processor().node_tag(),
                )?,
                ACTION_NAME,
                false,
            )
            .await?;
        Ok(Self { inner })
    }
    /// Sets the sender grace of the action: how long it keeps the goals
    /// of a sender that is gone before they receive their cancel signal,
    /// with the cause `CancelCause::SenderGone`. Zero by default. It
    /// applies to each sender found gone after the call.
    pub fn set_sender_grace(&self, grace: std::time::Duration) {
        self.inner.set_sender_grace(grace);
    }
    pub async fn handle_goal_next_request<F>(
        &mut self,
        decider: F,
    ) -> crate::Result<Option<GoalContext>>
    where
        F: Fn(&GoalRequest) -> crate::Result<GoalDecision>,
    {
        loop {
            let Some(pending) = self.inner.recv_next_goal().await? else {
                return Ok(None);
            };
            let request = GoalRequest {
                instance_id: pending.instance_id().to_string(),
                core_node: pending.core_node().to_string(),

                Ok(decision) => decision,
                Err(error) => {

                        FailureKind::AttemptFailed,
                        format!("the goal decider failed: {error}"),
                    );
                    pending.refuse(refusal.to_wire()).await?;
                    continue;
                }
            };
            match decision {
                GoalDecision::Accept => {
                    let inner = pending.accept(peppylib::Payload::new()).await?;
                    return Ok(Some(GoalContext { inner, request }));
                }
                GoalDecision::Refuse(failure) => {
                    pending.refuse(failure.to_wire()).await?;
                }
            }
        }
    }

impl GoalDecision {
    /// Accepts the goal.
    pub fn accept() -> Self {
        Self::Accept
    }
    /// Refuses the goal with a failure kind that this member
    /// declares and a message for a person. A refusal carries no
    /// goal response.
    pub fn refuse(kind: FailureKind, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self::Refuse(Failure::new(kind, message))
    }
}
pub struct ActionHandle {
    inner: peppylib::messaging::ConcurrentAction,
}
pub struct GoalContext {
    inner: peppylib::messaging::GoalContext,
    request: GoalRequest,
}
impl ActionHandle {
    pub async fn expose(node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let inner = peppylib::messaging::ConcurrentAction::expose(
                node_runner.messenger(),
                node_runner.processor().bound_core_node(),
                node_runner.processor().bound_instance_id(),
                peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node(
                    node_runner.processor().node_name(),
                    node_runner.processor().node_tag(),
                )?,
                ACTION_NAME,
                true,
            )
            .await?;
        Ok(Self { inner })
    }
    /// Sets the sender grace of the action: how long it keeps the goals
    /// of a sender that is gone before they receive their cancel signal,
    /// with the cause `CancelCause::SenderGone`. Zero by default. It
    /// applies to each sender found gone after the call.
    pub fn set_sender_grace(&self, grace: std::time::Duration) {
        self.inner.set_sender_grace(grace);
    }
    pub async fn handle_goal_next_request<F>(
        &mut self,
        decider: F,
    ) -> crate::Result<Option<GoalContext>>
    where
        F: Fn(&GoalRequest) -> crate::Result<GoalDecision>,
    {
        loop {
            let Some(pending) = self.inner.recv_next_goal().await? else {
                return Ok(None);
            };
            let request = GoalRequest {
                instance_id: pending.instance_id().to_string(),
                core_node: pending.core_node().to_string(),
            };
            let decision = match decider(&request) {
                Ok(decision) => decision,
                Err(error) => {
                    let refusal = Failure::new(
                        FailureKind::AttemptFailed,
                        format!("the goal decider failed: {error}"),
                    );
                    pending.refuse(refusal.to_wire()).await?;
                    continue;
                }
            };
            match decision {
                GoalDecision::Accept => {
                    let inner = pending.accept(peppylib::Payload::new()).await?;
                    return Ok(Some(GoalContext { inner, request }));
                }
                GoalDecision::Refuse(failure) => {
                    pending.refuse(failure.to_wire()).await?;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
impl GoalContext {
    /// The decoded goal request (caller identity and, if any, data).
    pub fn request(&self) -> &GoalRequest {
        &self.request
    }
    /// The client-generated correlation id for this goal.
    pub fn goal_id(&self) -> &str {
        self.inner.goal_id()
    }
    /// The process that sent this goal. The action cancels the goal
    /// when this process is gone and its sender grace passed.
    pub fn sender(&self) -> &peppylib::messaging::ClientPresence {
        self.inner.sender()
    }
    /// Resolves when this goal receives its cancel signal: a cancel
    /// request of its caller, or the end of its sender (see
    /// `cancel_cause`). React by calling `complete_cancelled`.
    pub async fn cancel_signal(&self) {
        self.inner.cancel_signal().await
    }
    /// Whether this goal received its cancel signal.
    pub fn is_cancelled(&self) -> bool {
        self.inner.is_cancelled()
    }
    /// Why this goal received its cancel signal; `None` before it did.
    /// `CancelCause::reason` gives a reason for `complete_cancelled`.
    pub fn cancel_cause(&self) -> Option<peppylib::messaging::CancelCause> {
        self.inner.cancel_cause()
    }
    /// Ends the goal with the status failed: a failure kind that this
    /// member declares, a message for a person, and no result. The
    /// first of `complete`, `fail` and `complete_cancelled` ends the
    /// goal; later calls do nothing.
    pub async fn fail(
        &self,
        kind: FailureKind,
        message: impl Into<String>,
    ) -> crate::Result<()> {
        self.inner.fail(Failure::new(kind, message).to_wire()).await?;
        Ok(())
    }
    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub async fn publish_feedback(&self, bearing: f32) -> crate::Result<()> {
        let payload = {
            let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
            {
                let mut root = capnp_msg
                    .init_root::<
                        crate::capnp::emit_track_target_feedback_message_capnp::track_target_feedback_message::Builder,
                    >();
                root.set_bearing(bearing);
            }
            let mut buffer = Vec::new();
            capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
                .map_err(|source| {
                    let context = format!(
                        "{} {}", "publish_feedback track_target", ACTION_NAME
                    );
                    crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
                })?;
            peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
        };
        let payload = peppylib::messaging::NonEmptyPayload::try_new(payload)
            .map_err(|_| peppylib::PeppyError::Io(
                std::io::Error::other(
                    "publish_feedback produced an empty payload (codec serialized \
                     to zero bytes); empty is reserved for end-of-stream",
                ),
            ))?;
        self.inner.publish_feedback(payload).await?;
        Ok(())
    }
    /// Ends the goal with the status completed and its result:
    /// the goal is a success. The first of `complete`, `fail`
    /// and `complete_cancelled` ends the goal; later calls do
    /// nothing.
    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub async fn complete(&self) -> crate::Result<()> {
        let payload = peppylib::Payload::new();
        self.inner.complete(payload).await?;
        Ok(())
    }
    /// Ends the goal with the status cancelled, for `reason`, with its result.
    /// A worker calls it after the cancel signal
    /// (`CancelCause::reason` gives a reason), and a member
    /// that ends the goals of other clients calls it with a
    /// reason of its own.
    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub async fn complete_cancelled(
        &self,
        reason: impl Into<String>,
    ) -> crate::Result<()> {
        let payload = peppylib::Payload::new();
        self.inner.complete_cancelled(reason, payload).await?;
        Ok(())
    }
}

const ACTION_NAME: &str = "nudge";
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct GoalRequest {
    pub instance_id: String,
    pub core_node: String,
}
/// A failure kind of this member: a kind of peppy, or a kind of its
/// contract that it lists.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum FailureKind {
    /// The attempt gave no result
    AttemptFailed,
    /// The bound of the provider passed
    Timeout,
    /// A precondition failed
    PreconditionFailed,
    /// A robot, limb or host is gone
    ResourceUnavailable,
    /// The provider refused the goal, and no other kind says why
    Rejected,
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl FailureKind {
    /// Every kind of this member: the kinds of peppy, then the
    /// kinds of its contract that it lists.
    pub const ALL: [Self; 5] = [
        Self::AttemptFailed,
        Self::Timeout,
        Self::PreconditionFailed,
        Self::ResourceUnavailable,
        Self::Rejected,
    ];
    /// The name of the kind on the wire.
    pub const fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::AttemptFailed => "attempt_failed",
            Self::Timeout => "timeout",
            Self::PreconditionFailed => "precondition_failed",
            Self::ResourceUnavailable => "resource_unavailable",
            Self::Rejected => "rejected",
        }
    }
    /// The kind named `name`, or `None` when this member does not
    /// declare it.
    pub fn parse(name: &str) -> Option<Self> {
        Self::ALL.into_iter().find(|kind| kind.as_str() == name)
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for FailureKind {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.write_str(self.as_str())
    }
}
/// Why this member refused or failed a goal, or gave an error
/// reply: a kind that it declares, and a message for a person.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Failure {
    pub kind: FailureKind,
    pub message: String,
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl Failure {
    pub fn new(kind: FailureKind, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            kind,
            message: message.into(),
        }
    }
    /// The wire form of the failure.
    pub(crate) fn to_wire(&self) -> peppylib::messaging::Failure {
        let kind = peppylib::messaging::FailureKindName::parse(self.kind.as_str())
            .expect("each kind of a member is a valid failure kind name");
        peppylib::messaging::Failure::new(kind, self.message.clone())
    }
    /// Parses a failure from the wire. A kind that this member does
    /// not declare is a deserialization error, whose text keeps the
    /// message of the provider.
    pub(crate) fn from_wire(
        failure: &peppylib::messaging::Failure,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let kind = FailureKind::parse(failure.kind().as_str())
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!(
                        "the failure kind `{}` is not a kind of `{}` (message: {:?})",
                        failure.kind(), ACTION_NAME, failure.message()
                    ),
                )
            })?;
        Ok(Self::new(kind, failure.message()))
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for Failure {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{}: {}", self.kind, self.message)
    }
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum GoalDecision {
    Accept,
    Refuse(Failure),
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl GoalDecision {
    /// Accepts the goal.
    pub fn accept() -> Self {
        Self::Accept
    }
    /// Refuses the goal with a failure kind that this member
    /// declares and a message for a person. A refusal carries no
    /// goal response.
    pub fn refuse(kind: FailureKind, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self::Refuse(Failure::new(kind, message))
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct GoalResponse {
    pub accepted: bool,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct GoalRequest {
    pub instance_id: String,
    pub core_node: String,
    pub data: GoalRequestData,
}
impl GoalResponse {
    pub fn new(accepted: bool) -> Self {
        Self { accepted }
    }
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct GoalRequestData {
    pub arm_id: u16,
    pub desired_position: [i32; 3],
}
/// A failure kind of this member: a kind of peppy, or a kind of its
/// contract that it lists.

    /// A precondition failed

    /// kinds of its contract that it lists.

            message: message.into(),
        }
    }

    pub(crate) fn to_wire(&self) -> peppylib::messaging::Failure {
        let kind = peppylib::messaging::FailureKindName::parse(self.kind.as_str())
            .expect("each kind of a member is a valid failure kind name");

#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum GoalDecision {
    Accept(GoalResponse),
    Refuse(Failure),
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl GoalDecision {
    /// Accepts the goal and replies with the declared response.
    pub fn accept(response: GoalResponse) -> Self {
        Self::Accept(response)
    }
    /// Refuses the goal with a failure kind that this member
    /// declares and a message for a person. A refusal carries no
    /// goal response.
    pub fn refuse(kind: FailureKind, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self::Refuse(Failure::new(kind, message))
    }

pub struct ActionHandle {
    inner: peppylib::messaging::ConcurrentAction,
}
pub struct GoalContext {
    inner: peppylib::messaging::GoalContext,
    request: GoalRequest,
}
impl ActionHandle {
    pub async fn expose(node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let inner = peppylib::messaging::ConcurrentAction::expose(
                node_runner.messenger(),
                node_runner.processor().bound_core_node(),
                node_runner.processor().bound_instance_id(),
                peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::contract("camera_feed", "v1")?,
                ACTION_NAME,
                true,
            )
            .await?;
        Ok(Self { inner })
    }
    /// Sets the sender grace of the action: how long it keeps the goals
    /// of a sender that is gone before they receive their cancel signal,
    /// with the cause `CancelCause::SenderGone`. Zero by default. It
    /// applies to each sender found gone after the call.
    pub fn set_sender_grace(&self, grace: std::time::Duration) {
        self.inner.set_sender_grace(grace);
    }
    pub async fn handle_goal_next_request<F>(
        &mut self,
        decider: F,
    ) -> crate::Result<Option<GoalContext>>
    where
        F: Fn(&GoalRequest) -> crate::Result<GoalDecision>,
    {
        loop {
            let Some(pending) = self.inner.recv_next_goal().await? else {
                return Ok(None);
            };
            let request_data = match deserialize_goal_request(pending.request_bytes()) {
                Ok(request_data) => request_data,
                Err(error) => {
                    let refusal = Failure::new(
                        FailureKind::Rejected,
                        format!("goal request does not decode: {error}"),
                    );
                    pending.refuse(refusal.to_wire()).await?;
                    continue;
                }
            };
            let request = GoalRequest {
                instance_id: pending.instance_id().to_string(),
                core_node: pending.core_node().to_string(),
                data: request_data,
            };
            let decision = match decider(&request) {
                Ok(decision) => decision,
                Err(error) => {
                    let refusal = Failure::new(
                        FailureKind::AttemptFailed,
                        format!("the goal decider failed: {error}"),
                    );
                    pending.refuse(refusal.to_wire()).await?;
                    continue;
                }
            };
            match decision {
                GoalDecision::Accept(response) => {
                    let response_payload = {
                        let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
                        {
                            let mut root = capnp_msg
                                .init_root::<
                                    crate::capnp::camera_feed_v1_move_arm_goal_response_message_capnp::move_arm_goal_response_message::Builder,
                                >();
                            root.set_accepted(response.accepted);
                        }
                        let mut buffer = Vec::new();
                        capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
                            .map_err(|source| {
                                let context = format!(
                                    "{} {}", "handle_goal_next_request", ACTION_NAME
                                );
                                crate::Error::Serialization(
                                    format!("{}: {}", context, source),
                                )
                            })?;
                        peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
                    };
                    let inner = pending.accept(response_payload).await?;
                    return Ok(Some(GoalContext { inner, request }));
                }
                GoalDecision::Refuse(failure) => {
                    pending.refuse(failure.to_wire()).await?;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
impl GoalContext {
    /// The decoded goal request (caller identity and, if any, data).
    pub fn request(&self) -> &GoalRequest {
        &self.request
    }
    /// The client-generated correlation id for this goal.
    pub fn goal_id(&self) -> &str {
        self.inner.goal_id()
    }
    /// The process that sent this goal. The action cancels the goal
    /// when this process is gone and its sender grace passed.
    pub fn sender(&self) -> &peppylib::messaging::ClientPresence {
        self.inner.sender()
    }
    /// Resolves when this goal receives its cancel signal: a cancel
    /// request of its caller, or the end of its sender (see
    /// `cancel_cause`). React by calling `complete_cancelled`.
    pub async fn cancel_signal(&self) {

    }
    /// Whether this goal received its cancel signal.
    pub fn is_cancelled(&self) -> bool {

    }
    /// Why this goal received its cancel signal; `None` before it did.
    /// `CancelCause::reason` gives a reason for `complete_cancelled`.
    pub fn cancel_cause(&self) -> Option<peppylib::messaging::CancelCause> {
        self.inner.cancel_cause()
    }
    /// Ends the goal with the status failed: a failure kind that this
    /// member declares, a message for a person, and no result. The
    /// first of `complete`, `fail` and `complete_cancelled` ends the
    /// goal; later calls do nothing.
    pub async fn fail(
        &self,
        kind: FailureKind,

    ) -> crate::Result<()> {

    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub async fn publish_feedback(&self, new_position: [i32; 3]) -> crate::Result<()> {
        let payload = {
            let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
            {
                let mut root = capnp_msg
                    .init_root::<
                        crate::capnp::camera_feed_v1_emit_move_arm_feedback_message_capnp::move_arm_feedback_message::Builder,
                    >();
                let mut list_0 = root.reborrow().init_new_position(3);
                for (idx_1, value_2) in (new_position).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_0.set(idx_1 as u32, *value_2);
                }
            }
            let mut buffer = Vec::new();
            capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
                .map_err(|source| {
                    let context = format!(
                        "{} {}", "publish_feedback move_arm", ACTION_NAME
                    );
                    crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
                })?;
            peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
        };
        let payload = peppylib::messaging::NonEmptyPayload::try_new(payload)
            .map_err(|_| peppylib::PeppyError::Io(
                std::io::Error::other(
                    "publish_feedback produced an empty payload (codec serialized \
                     to zero bytes); empty is reserved for end-of-stream",
                ),
            ))?;
        self.inner.publish_feedback(payload).await?;
        Ok(())
    }
    /// Ends the goal with the status completed and its result:
    /// the goal is a success. The first of `complete`, `fail`
    /// and `complete_cancelled` ends the goal; later calls do
    /// nothing.
    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub async fn complete(
        &self,
        success: bool,
        error_msg: Option<String>,
    ) -> crate::Result<()> {
        let payload = {
            let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
            {
                let mut root = capnp_msg
                    .init_root::<
                        crate::capnp::camera_feed_v1_move_arm_result_response_message_capnp::move_arm_result_message::Builder,
                    >();
                root.set_success(success);
                if let Some(value_0) = (error_msg).as_ref() {
                    root.set_error_msg(value_0.as_str());
                }
            }
            let mut buffer = Vec::new();
            capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
                .map_err(|source| {
                    let context = format!("{} {}", "move_arm_result", ACTION_NAME);
                    crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
                })?;
            peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
        };
        self.inner.complete(payload).await?;
        Ok(())
    }
    /// Ends the goal with the status cancelled, for `reason`, with its result.
    /// A worker calls it after the cancel signal
    /// (`CancelCause::reason` gives a reason), and a member
    /// that ends the goals of other clients calls it with a
    /// reason of its own.
    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub async fn complete_cancelled(
        &self,
        reason: impl Into<String>,
        success: bool,
        error_msg: Option<String>,
    ) -> crate::Result<()> {
        let payload = {
            let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
            {
                let mut root = capnp_msg
                    .init_root::<
                        crate::capnp::camera_feed_v1_move_arm_result_response_message_capnp::move_arm_result_message::Builder,
                    >();
                root.set_success(success);
                if let Some(value_0) = (error_msg).as_ref() {
                    root.set_error_msg(value_0.as_str());
                }
            }
            let mut buffer = Vec::new();
            capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
                .map_err(|source| {
                    let context = format!("{} {}", "move_arm_result", ACTION_NAME);
                    crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
                })?;
            peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
        };
        self.inner.complete_cancelled(reason, payload).await?;
        Ok(())
    }
}
fn deserialize_goal_request(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<GoalRequestData> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]
    let context = format!("{} {}", stringify!(deserialize_goal_request), ACTION_NAME);
    let mut payload = payload;
    let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader
        .get_root::<
            crate::capnp::camera_feed_v1_move_arm_goal_message_capnp::move_arm_goal_message::Reader,
        >()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let arm_id_0 = root.reborrow().get_arm_id();
    let list_1 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_desired_position()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = format!(
                "{} {}", stringify!(deserialize_goal_request), ACTION_NAME
            );
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "desired_position", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    if list_1.len() as usize != 3 {
        let actual = list_1.len() as usize;
        return Err(
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!(
                    "invalid fixed list length for field '{}': expected {}, got {}",
                    "desired_position", 3, actual
                ),
            ),
        );
    }
    let mut desired_position_2: [i32; 3] = [i32::default(); 3];
    for (idx, element) in list_1.iter().enumerate() {
        desired_position_2[idx] = element;
    }
    Ok(GoalRequestData {
        arm_id: arm_id_0,
        desired_position: desired_position_2,
    })
}
