#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct MessageHeader {
    pub stamp: std::time::SystemTime,
    pub frame_id: u32,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Message {
    pub header: MessageHeader,
    pub width: u32,
}
/// The producer bound to this module's slot.
/// The binding is fixed when the node starts (no live discovery; a
/// producer disconnecting never rebinds it) and shared by every
/// generated module referencing this slot. This slot declares
/// cardinality `one`: launch validation resolved exactly one producer,
/// so the accessor is singular and infallible.
pub fn bound_producer(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
) -> &peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef {
    node_runner.processor().sole_bound_producer("camera_one")
}
/// A held subscription covering every producer bound to this slot: one
/// producer-pinned wire subscription per member of the slot's bound
/// set, merged client-side. Message order is preserved independently
/// per producer (no total ordering across producers), and ready
/// producers are merged fairly. To follow a single producer, filter on
/// the yielded producer identity.
pub struct Subscription {
    inner: peppylib::runtime::BoundSetSubscription,
}
impl Subscription {
    /// Awaits the next message from any producer bound to this slot.
    ///
    /// Returns `Ok(Some((producer, message)))` for each message (the
    /// producer that published it always rides along), `Ok(None)` once the
    /// node is shutting down and no queued message remains, and `Err(..)`
    /// if a received payload fails to deserialize; a decode error does not
    /// tear down the subscription or shrink the bound set. On an empty
    /// set (a `zero_or_more` slot bound to nothing, or a vacant
    /// `zero_or_one` slot) this pends until shutdown, then returns
    /// `Ok(None)`.
    #[allow(clippy::should_implement_trait)]
    pub async fn next(
        &mut self,
    ) -> crate::Result<Option<(peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef, Message)>> {
        let Some((producer, message)) = self.inner.on_next_message().await else {
            return Ok(None);
        };
        let payload = message.payload_bytes();
        let message = deseralize_payload(payload.as_ref())?;
        Ok(Some((producer, message)))
    }
}
/// Subscribes to this topic across the slot's complete bound producer
/// set and returns a held `Subscription` yielding `(producer, message)`.
///
/// The shape is identical for every cardinality; only the size of the
/// bound set changes. Call this once, then loop on `Subscription::next`;
/// each underlying subscription's buffer retains messages published
/// between calls, so nothing is lost to a re-subscribe gap.
pub async fn subscribe(node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner) -> crate::Result<Subscription> {
    let topic_name = "video_stream";
    let node_name = "uvc_camera";
    let qos = peppylib::config::QoSProfile::Standard;
    let retention = peppylib::config::TopicRetention::LiveOnly;
    let inner = peppylib::runtime::subscribe_bound_set(
            node_runner,
            "camera_one",
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("uvc_camera", "v1")?,
            topic_name,
            qos,
            retention,
        )
        .await
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::TopicSubscribe {
            topic_name: topic_name.to_string(),
            node_name: node_name.to_string(),
            source_msg: source.to_string(),
        })?;
    Ok(Subscription { inner })
}
fn deseralize_payload(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<Message> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]
    let context = String::from("Message");
    let mut payload = payload;
    let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader
        .get_root::<
            crate::capnp::on_next_camera_one_video_stream_message_capnp::camera_one_video_stream_message::Reader,
        >()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let reader_0 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_header()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "header", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let timestamp_2 = reader_0
        .reborrow()
        .get_stamp()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "stamp", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let capnp_timestamp_3 = peppylib::encoding::CapnpTimestamp {
        sec: timestamp_2.get_sec(),
        nsec: timestamp_2.get_nsec(),
    };
    let stamp_1 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time_from_capnp(capnp_timestamp_3);
    let frame_id_4 = reader_0.reborrow().get_frame_id();
    let header_5 = MessageHeader {
        stamp: stamp_1,
        frame_id: frame_id_4,
    };
    let width_6 = root.reborrow().get_width();
    Ok(Message {
        header: header_5,
        width: width_6,
    })
}

const NODE_NAME: &str = "uvc_camera";
const SERVICE_NAME: &str = "enable_camera";
const LINK_ID: &str = "camera_one";
/// The producer bound to this module's slot.
/// The binding is fixed when the node starts (no live discovery; a
/// producer disconnecting never rebinds it) and shared by every
/// generated module referencing this slot. This slot declares
/// cardinality `one`: launch validation resolved exactly one producer,
/// so the accessor is singular and infallible.
pub fn bound_producer(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
) -> &peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef {
    node_runner.processor().sole_bound_producer("camera_one")
}
/// A failure kind of this member: a kind of peppy, or a kind of its
/// contract that it lists.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum FailureKind {
    /// The attempt gave no result
    AttemptFailed,
    /// The bound of the provider passed
    Timeout,
    /// A precondition failed
    PreconditionFailed,
    /// A robot, limb or host is gone
    ResourceUnavailable,
    /// The provider refused the goal, and no other kind says why
    Rejected,
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl FailureKind {
    /// Every kind of this member: the kinds of peppy, then the
    /// kinds of its contract that it lists.
    pub const ALL: [Self; 5] = [
        Self::AttemptFailed,
        Self::Timeout,
        Self::PreconditionFailed,
        Self::ResourceUnavailable,
        Self::Rejected,
    ];
    /// The name of the kind on the wire.
    pub const fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::AttemptFailed => "attempt_failed",
            Self::Timeout => "timeout",
            Self::PreconditionFailed => "precondition_failed",
            Self::ResourceUnavailable => "resource_unavailable",
            Self::Rejected => "rejected",
        }
    }
    /// The kind named `name`, or `None` when this member does not
    /// declare it.
    pub fn parse(name: &str) -> Option<Self> {
        Self::ALL.into_iter().find(|kind| kind.as_str() == name)
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for FailureKind {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.write_str(self.as_str())
    }
}
/// Why this member refused or failed a goal, or gave an error
/// reply: a kind that it declares, and a message for a person.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Failure {
    pub kind: FailureKind,
    pub message: String,
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl Failure {
    pub fn new(kind: FailureKind, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            kind,
            message: message.into(),
        }
    }
    /// The wire form of the failure.
    pub(crate) fn to_wire(&self) -> peppylib::messaging::Failure {
        let kind = peppylib::messaging::FailureKindName::parse(self.kind.as_str())
            .expect("each kind of a member is a valid failure kind name");
        peppylib::messaging::Failure::new(kind, self.message.clone())
    }
    /// Parses a failure from the wire. A kind that this member does
    /// not declare is a deserialization error, whose text keeps the
    /// message of the provider.
    pub(crate) fn from_wire(
        failure: &peppylib::messaging::Failure,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let kind = FailureKind::parse(failure.kind().as_str())
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!(
                        "the failure kind `{}` is not a kind of `{}` (message: {:?})",
                        failure.kind(), SERVICE_NAME, failure.message()
                    ),
                )
            })?;
        Ok(Self::new(kind, failure.message()))
    }
    /// The failure of the error reply that `error` holds: `None`
    /// when `error` is not an error reply of a provider, or when
    /// its kind is not a kind of this member.
    pub fn of(error: &crate::Error) -> Option<Self> {
        let crate::Error::ServiceFailed { failure, .. } = error else {
            return None;
        };
        Self::from_wire(failure).ok()
    }
    /// `error` itself, unless it is an error reply with a kind this
    /// member does not declare: such a reply breaks the contract of
    /// the member, and gives a deserialization error instead.
    pub(crate) fn reject_undeclared_kind(error: crate::Error) -> crate::Error {
        let crate::Error::ServiceFailed { failure, .. } = &error else {
            return error;
        };
        match Self::from_wire(failure) {
            Ok(_) => error,
            Err(undeclared) => undeclared,
        }
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for Failure {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{}: {}", self.kind, self.message)
    }
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct ResponseData {
    pub enabled: bool,
    pub error_msg: Option<String>,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Request {
    pub enable: bool,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Response {
    pub instance_id: String,
    pub core_node: String,
    pub data: ResponseData,
}
impl ResponseData {
    pub fn new(enabled: bool, error_msg: Option<String>) -> Self {
        Self { enabled, error_msg }
    }
}
impl Request {
    pub fn new(enable: bool) -> Self {
        Self { enable }
    }
}
/// Polls this service on `target`, a member of this slot's bound set
/// (a `ProducerRef` yielded by the slot's own subscription is a
/// member by construction); a target outside the set fails before
/// anything reaches the wire.
///
/// An error reply of the provider is `Error::ServiceFailed`:
/// `Failure::of` reads its failure, with a kind that this member
/// declares.
///
/// This slot declares cardinality `one`: `target` is its sole bound
/// producer, returned by `bound_producer()`; the explicit parameter
/// keeps call sites uniform across cardinalities.
pub async fn poll(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
    target: &peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef,
    timeout: std::time::Duration,
    request: Request,
) -> crate::Result<Response> {
    node_runner.processor().ensure_target_bound(LINK_ID, target)?;
    let request_payload = {
        let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
        {
            let enable = request.enable;
            let mut root = capnp_msg
                .init_root::<
                    crate::capnp::poll_uvc_camera_enable_camera_message_capnp::enable_camera_message::Builder,
                >();
            root.set_enable(enable);
        }
        let mut buffer = Vec::new();
        capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
            .map_err(|source| {
                let context = format!("poll {} {}", NODE_NAME, SERVICE_NAME);
                crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
            })?;
        peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
    };
    let response_message = peppylib::ServiceMessenger::poll(
            node_runner.messenger(),
            node_runner.processor().bound_core_node(),
            node_runner.processor().bound_instance_id(),
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("uvc_camera", "v1")?,
            SERVICE_NAME,
            peppylib::messaging::ServiceTarget::Producer(target),
            request_payload,
            timeout,
        )
        .await
        .map_err(Failure::reject_undeclared_kind)?;
    let payload = response_message.payload_bytes();
    let response_data = deserialize_response(payload.as_ref())?;
    Ok(Response {
        instance_id: response_message.instance_id().to_string(),
        core_node: response_message.core_node().to_string(),
        data: response_data,
    })
}
fn deserialize_response(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<ResponseData> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]
    let context = format!("{} {} response", NODE_NAME, SERVICE_NAME);
    let mut payload = payload;
    let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader
        .get_root::<
            crate::capnp::poll_uvc_camera_enable_camera_response_message_capnp::enable_camera_response_message::Reader,
        >()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let enabled_0 = root.reborrow().get_enabled();
    let error_msg_opt_1 = if root.reborrow().has_error_msg() {
        let text_3 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_error_msg()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = context.clone();
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "error_msg", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let error_msg_2 = text_3
            .to_str()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = context.clone();
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "error_msg", context, source),
                )
            })?
            .to_owned();
        Some(error_msg_2)
    } else {
        None
    };
    Ok(ResponseData {
        enabled: enabled_0,
        error_msg: error_msg_opt_1,
    })
}

            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "stamp", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let capnp_timestamp_3 = peppylib::encoding::CapnpTimestamp {
        sec: timestamp_2.get_sec(),
        nsec: timestamp_2.get_nsec(),
    };
    let stamp_1 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time_from_capnp(capnp_timestamp_3);
    let frame_id_4 = reader_0.reborrow().get_frame_id();
    let header_5 = MessageHeader {
        stamp: stamp_1,
        frame_id: frame_id_4,
    };
    let width_6 = root.reborrow().get_width();
    Ok(Message {
        header: header_5,
        width: width_6,
    })
}

const NODE_NAME: &str = "uvc_camera";
const SERVICE_NAME: &str = "enable_camera";
const LINK_ID: &str = "camera_zero_or_one";
/// The producer bound to this module's slot, if any.
/// The binding is fixed when the node starts (no live discovery; a
/// producer disconnecting never rebinds it) and shared by every
/// generated module referencing this slot.
/// This slot declares cardinality `zero_or_one`: launch validation
/// resolved at most one producer, and nothing is bound wherever the
/// deployment wrote the slot vacant.
pub fn bound_producer(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
) -> Option<&peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef> {
    node_runner.processor().optional_bound_producer("camera_zero_or_one")
}
/// A failure kind of this member: a kind of peppy, or a kind of its
/// contract that it lists.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum FailureKind {
    /// The attempt gave no result
    AttemptFailed,
    /// The bound of the provider passed
    Timeout,
    /// A precondition failed
    PreconditionFailed,
    /// A robot, limb or host is gone
    ResourceUnavailable,
    /// The provider refused the goal, and no other kind says why
    Rejected,
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl FailureKind {
    /// Every kind of this member: the kinds of peppy, then the
    /// kinds of its contract that it lists.
    pub const ALL: [Self; 5] = [
        Self::AttemptFailed,
        Self::Timeout,
        Self::PreconditionFailed,
        Self::ResourceUnavailable,
        Self::Rejected,
    ];
    /// The name of the kind on the wire.
    pub const fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::AttemptFailed => "attempt_failed",
            Self::Timeout => "timeout",
            Self::PreconditionFailed => "precondition_failed",
            Self::ResourceUnavailable => "resource_unavailable",
            Self::Rejected => "rejected",
        }
    }
    /// The kind named `name`, or `None` when this member does not
    /// declare it.
    pub fn parse(name: &str) -> Option<Self> {
        Self::ALL.into_iter().find(|kind| kind.as_str() == name)
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for FailureKind {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.write_str(self.as_str())
    }
}
/// Why this member refused or failed a goal, or gave an error
/// reply: a kind that it declares, and a message for a person.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Failure {
    pub kind: FailureKind,
    pub message: String,
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl Failure {
    pub fn new(kind: FailureKind, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            kind,
            message: message.into(),
        }
    }
    /// The wire form of the failure.
    pub(crate) fn to_wire(&self) -> peppylib::messaging::Failure {
        let kind = peppylib::messaging::FailureKindName::parse(self.kind.as_str())
            .expect("each kind of a member is a valid failure kind name");
        peppylib::messaging::Failure::new(kind, self.message.clone())
    }
    /// Parses a failure from the wire. A kind that this member does
    /// not declare is a deserialization error, whose text keeps the
    /// message of the provider.
    pub(crate) fn from_wire(
        failure: &peppylib::messaging::Failure,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let kind = FailureKind::parse(failure.kind().as_str())
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!(
                        "the failure kind `{}` is not a kind of `{}` (message: {:?})",
                        failure.kind(), SERVICE_NAME, failure.message()
                    ),
                )
            })?;
        Ok(Self::new(kind, failure.message()))
    }
    /// The failure of the error reply that `error` holds: `None`
    /// when `error` is not an error reply of a provider, or when
    /// its kind is not a kind of this member.
    pub fn of(error: &crate::Error) -> Option<Self> {
        let crate::Error::ServiceFailed { failure, .. } = error else {
            return None;
        };
        Self::from_wire(failure).ok()
    }
    /// `error` itself, unless it is an error reply with a kind this
    /// member does not declare: such a reply breaks the contract of
    /// the member, and gives a deserialization error instead.
    pub(crate) fn reject_undeclared_kind(error: crate::Error) -> crate::Error {
        let crate::Error::ServiceFailed { failure, .. } = &error else {
            return error;
        };
        match Self::from_wire(failure) {
            Ok(_) => error,
            Err(undeclared) => undeclared,
        }
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for Failure {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{}: {}", self.kind, self.message)
    }
}

    pub enabled: bool,
    pub error_msg: Option<String>,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Request {
    pub enable: bool,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]

    pub instance_id: String,
    pub core_node: String,
    pub data: ResponseData,
}

    pub fn new(enabled: bool, error_msg: Option<String>) -> Self {
        Self { enabled, error_msg }
    }
}
impl Request {
    pub fn new(enable: bool) -> Self {
        Self { enable }
    }
}
/// Polls this service on `target`, a member of this slot's bound set
/// (a `ProducerRef` yielded by the slot's own subscription is a
/// member by construction); a target outside the set fails before
/// anything reaches the wire.
///
/// An error reply of the provider is `Error::ServiceFailed`:
/// `Failure::of` reads its failure, with a kind that this member
/// declares.
///
/// This slot declares cardinality `zero_or_one`: `target` is the
/// producer `bound_producer()` returns while the slot is bound. A
/// vacant slot has no target at all, so the caller branches on that
/// accessor before it has a call to make.
pub async fn poll(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
    target: &peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef,
    timeout: std::time::Duration,
    request: Request,
) -> crate::Result<Response> {
    node_runner.processor().ensure_target_bound(LINK_ID, target)?;
    let request_payload = {
        let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
        {
            let enable = request.enable;
            let mut root = capnp_msg
                .init_root::<
                    crate::capnp::poll_uvc_camera_enable_camera_message_capnp::enable_camera_message::Builder,
                >();
            root.set_enable(enable);
        }
        let mut buffer = Vec::new();
        capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
            .map_err(|source| {
                let context = format!("poll {} {}", NODE_NAME, SERVICE_NAME);
                crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
            })?;
        peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
    };
    let response_message = peppylib::ServiceMessenger::poll(
            node_runner.messenger(),
            node_runner.processor().bound_core_node(),
            node_runner.processor().bound_instance_id(),
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("uvc_camera", "v1")?,
            SERVICE_NAME,
            peppylib::messaging::ServiceTarget::Producer(target),
            request_payload,
            timeout,
        )
        .await
        .map_err(Failure::reject_undeclared_kind)?;
    let payload = response_message.payload_bytes();
    let response_data = deserialize_response(payload.as_ref())?;
    Ok(Response {
        instance_id: response_message.instance_id().to_string(),
        core_node: response_message.core_node().to_string(),
        data: response_data,
    })
}
fn deserialize_response(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<ResponseData> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]
    let context = format!("{} {} response", NODE_NAME, SERVICE_NAME);
    let mut payload = payload;
    let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader
        .get_root::<
            crate::capnp::poll_uvc_camera_enable_camera_response_message_capnp::enable_camera_response_message::Reader,
        >()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let enabled_0 = root.reborrow().get_enabled();
    let error_msg_opt_1 = if root.reborrow().has_error_msg() {
        let text_3 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_error_msg()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = context.clone();
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "error_msg", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let error_msg_2 = text_3
            .to_str()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = context.clone();
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "error_msg", context, source),
                )
            })?
            .to_owned();
        Some(error_msg_2)
    } else {
        None
    };
    Ok(ResponseData {
        enabled: enabled_0,
        error_msg: error_msg_opt_1,
    })
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct MessageHeader {
    pub stamp: std::time::SystemTime,
    pub frame_id: u32,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Message {
    pub header: MessageHeader,
    pub width: u32,
}
/// The producer set currently bound to this module's slot, in plan order.
/// A join can grow the set and a removal shrink it, so call the accessor at the
/// point of use; a producer disconnecting never changes it. Every generated module
/// referencing this slot reads the same set.
/// This slot declares cardinality `one_or_more`: the set is never empty, so
/// `first()` is infallible.
pub fn bound_producers(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
) -> peppylib::messaging::NonEmptyProducers {
    node_runner.processor().non_empty_bound_producers("camera_one_or_more")
}
/// The members of the set currently bound to this module's slot, in plan order.
/// Each member is a producer with the copy its instance belongs to, `None` for
/// one the launcher deploys outside any copy. The same set `bound_producers()`
/// answers by producer alone, read under the same rules; a node that groups
/// producers by copy reads this one.
/// This slot declares cardinality `one_or_more`: the set is never empty, so
/// `first()` is infallible.
pub fn bound_members(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
) -> peppylib::messaging::NonEmptyMembers {
    node_runner.processor().non_empty_bound_members("camera_one_or_more")
}
/// A held subscription covering every producer bound to this slot: one
/// producer-pinned wire subscription per member of the slot's bound
/// set, merged client-side. Message order is preserved independently
/// per producer (no total ordering across producers), and ready
/// producers are merged fairly. To follow a single producer, filter on
/// the yielded producer identity.
/// The subscription follows the set as a join grows it or a removal shrinks it.
pub struct Subscription {
    inner: peppylib::runtime::BoundSetSubscription,
}
impl Subscription {
    /// Awaits the next message from any producer bound to this slot.
    ///
    /// Returns `Ok(Some((producer, message)))` for each message (the
    /// producer that published it always rides along), `Ok(None)` once the
    /// node is shutting down and no queued message remains, and `Err(..)`
    /// if a received payload fails to deserialize; a decode error does not
    /// tear down the subscription or shrink the bound set. On an empty
    /// set (a `zero_or_more` slot bound to nothing, or a vacant
    /// `zero_or_one` slot) this pends until shutdown, then returns
    /// `Ok(None)`.
    #[allow(clippy::should_implement_trait)]
    pub async fn next(
        &mut self,
    ) -> crate::Result<Option<(peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef, Message)>> {
        let Some((producer, message)) = self.inner.on_next_message().await else {
            return Ok(None);
        };
        let payload = message.payload_bytes();
        let message = deseralize_payload(payload.as_ref())?;
        Ok(Some((producer, message)))
    }
}
/// Subscribes to this topic across the slot's complete bound producer
/// set and returns a held `Subscription` yielding `(producer, message)`.
///
/// The shape is identical for every cardinality; only the size of the
/// bound set changes. Call this once, then loop on `Subscription::next`;
/// each underlying subscription's buffer retains messages published
/// between calls, so nothing is lost to a re-subscribe gap.
pub async fn subscribe(node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner) -> crate::Result<Subscription> {
    let topic_name = "video_stream";
    let node_name = "uvc_camera";
    let qos = peppylib::config::QoSProfile::Standard;
    let retention = peppylib::config::TopicRetention::LiveOnly;
    let inner = peppylib::runtime::subscribe_bound_set(
            node_runner,
            "camera_one_or_more",
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("uvc_camera", "v1")?,
            topic_name,
            qos,
            retention,
        )
        .await
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::TopicSubscribe {
            topic_name: topic_name.to_string(),
            node_name: node_name.to_string(),
            source_msg: source.to_string(),
        })?;
    Ok(Subscription { inner })
}
fn deseralize_payload(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<Message> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]
    let context = String::from("Message");
    let mut payload = payload;
    let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader
        .get_root::<
            crate::capnp::on_next_camera_one_or_more_video_stream_message_capnp::camera_one_or_more_video_stream_message::Reader,
        >()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let reader_0 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_header()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "header", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let timestamp_2 = reader_0
        .reborrow()
        .get_stamp()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "stamp", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let capnp_timestamp_3 = peppylib::encoding::CapnpTimestamp {
        sec: timestamp_2.get_sec(),
        nsec: timestamp_2.get_nsec(),
    };
    let stamp_1 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time_from_capnp(capnp_timestamp_3);
    let frame_id_4 = reader_0.reborrow().get_frame_id();
    let header_5 = MessageHeader {
        stamp: stamp_1,
        frame_id: frame_id_4,
    };
    let width_6 = root.reborrow().get_width();
    Ok(Message {
        header: header_5,
        width: width_6,
    })
}

/// Polls this service on `target`, a member of this slot's bound set

///
/// An error reply of the provider is `Error::ServiceFailed`:

/// declares.
///
/// This slot declares cardinality `one_or_more`: `target` is a
/// caller-selected member of the never-empty `bound_producers()`
/// set, and addressing several members is a plain loop at the call
/// site.
pub async fn poll(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
    target: &peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef,
    timeout: std::time::Duration,
    request: Request,
) -> crate::Result<Response> {

                    crate::capnp::poll_uvc_camera_enable_camera_message_capnp::enable_camera_message::Builder,
                >();
            root.set_enable(enable);
        }

        capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
            .map_err(|source| {
                let context = format!("poll {} {}", NODE_NAME, SERVICE_NAME);
                crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
            })?;
        peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
    };
    let response_message = peppylib::ServiceMessenger::poll(
            node_runner.messenger(),
            node_runner.processor().bound_core_node(),
            node_runner.processor().bound_instance_id(),
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("uvc_camera", "v1")?,
            SERVICE_NAME,
            peppylib::messaging::ServiceTarget::Producer(target),
            request_payload,
            timeout,
        )
        .await
        .map_err(Failure::reject_undeclared_kind)?;

    Ok(Response {
        instance_id: response_message.instance_id().to_string(),
        core_node: response_message.core_node().to_string(),
        data: response_data,
    })
}
fn deserialize_response(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<ResponseData> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]
    let context = format!("{} {} response", NODE_NAME, SERVICE_NAME);
    let mut payload = payload;
    let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader

            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;

            .reborrow()

/// The producer set currently bound to this module's slot, in plan order.
/// A join can grow the set and a removal shrink it, so call the accessor at the

/// referencing this slot reads the same set.

/// A failure kind of this member: a kind of peppy, or a kind of its
/// contract that it lists.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum FailureKind {
    /// The attempt gave no result
    AttemptFailed,
    /// The bound of the provider passed
    Timeout,
    /// A precondition failed
    PreconditionFailed,
    /// A robot, limb or host is gone
    ResourceUnavailable,
    /// The provider refused the goal, and no other kind says why
    Rejected,
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl FailureKind {
    /// Every kind of this member: the kinds of peppy, then the
    /// kinds of its contract that it lists.
    pub const ALL: [Self; 5] = [
        Self::AttemptFailed,
        Self::Timeout,
        Self::PreconditionFailed,
        Self::ResourceUnavailable,
        Self::Rejected,
    ];
    /// The name of the kind on the wire.
    pub const fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::AttemptFailed => "attempt_failed",
            Self::Timeout => "timeout",
            Self::PreconditionFailed => "precondition_failed",
            Self::ResourceUnavailable => "resource_unavailable",
            Self::Rejected => "rejected",
        }
    }
    /// The kind named `name`, or `None` when this member does not
    /// declare it.
    pub fn parse(name: &str) -> Option<Self> {
        Self::ALL.into_iter().find(|kind| kind.as_str() == name)
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for FailureKind {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.write_str(self.as_str())
    }
}
/// Why this member refused or failed a goal, or gave an error
/// reply: a kind that it declares, and a message for a person.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Failure {
    pub kind: FailureKind,
    pub message: String,
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl Failure {
    pub fn new(kind: FailureKind, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            kind,
            message: message.into(),
        }
    }
    /// The wire form of the failure.
    pub(crate) fn to_wire(&self) -> peppylib::messaging::Failure {
        let kind = peppylib::messaging::FailureKindName::parse(self.kind.as_str())
            .expect("each kind of a member is a valid failure kind name");
        peppylib::messaging::Failure::new(kind, self.message.clone())
    }
    /// Parses a failure from the wire. A kind that this member does
    /// not declare is a deserialization error, whose text keeps the
    /// message of the provider.
    pub(crate) fn from_wire(
        failure: &peppylib::messaging::Failure,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let kind = FailureKind::parse(failure.kind().as_str())
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!(
                        "the failure kind `{}` is not a kind of `{}` (message: {:?})",
                        failure.kind(), TARGET_ACTION_NAME, failure.message()
                    ),
                )
            })?;
        Ok(Self::new(kind, failure.message()))
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for Failure {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{}: {}", self.kind, self.message)
    }
}

    pub desired_position: [i32; 3],

    pub core_node: String,
    pub state: CancelState,

}

pub struct ActionHandle {
    messenger: peppylib::MessengerHandle,
    inner: peppylib::messaging::ActionGoalHandle,
    /// Whether the producer accepted the goal: `refusal` is
    /// `None`.
    pub accepted: bool,
    /// The failure the producer refused the goal with, of a kind
    /// that this member declares; `None` for an accepted goal.
    pub refusal: Option<Failure>,
    /// The declared goal response of an accepted goal; `None`
    /// for a refused goal.
    pub data: Option<GoalResponseData>,
}
impl ActionHandle {
    /// Fires this goal at `target`, a member of this slot's bound set (a
    /// `ProducerRef` yielded by the slot's own subscription is a member
    /// by construction); a target outside the set fails before anything
    /// reaches the wire. Each returned handle stays pinned to its
    /// target: feedback, result retrieval, and cancellation all address
    /// that producer directly.
    ///
    /// The handle keeps every feedback message until it is read, up to
    /// `feedback_bound` unread messages (`DEFAULT_FEEDBACK_BOUND` holds
    /// 1024). A caller that reads the feedback and falls behind by more
    /// cancels the goal, and its next read gives
    /// `Error::ActionFeedbackOverflow`. A caller that never reads the
    /// feedback keeps its goal.
    ///
    /// This slot declares cardinality `one_or_more`: `target` is a
    /// caller-selected member of the never-empty `bound_producers()`
    /// set, and addressing several members is a plain loop at the call
    /// site.
    pub async fn fire_goal(
        node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
        target: &peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef,
        timeout: std::time::Duration,
        request: GoalRequest,
        feedback_bound: peppylib::messaging::FeedbackBound,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        node_runner.processor().ensure_target_bound(LINK_ID, target)?;
        let goal_payload = {
            let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
            {
                let mut root = capnp_msg
                    .init_root::<
                        crate::capnp::uvc_camera_move_arm_fire_goal_message_capnp::uvc_camera_move_arm_action_goal_message_message::Builder,
                    >();
                root.set_arm_id(request.arm_id);
                let mut list_0 = root.reborrow().init_desired_position(3);
                for (idx_1, value_2) in (request.desired_position).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_0.set(idx_1 as u32, *value_2);
                }
            }
            let mut buffer = Vec::new();
            capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
                .map_err(|source| {
                    let context = format!(
                        "fire_goal {} {}", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME
                    );
                    crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
                })?;
            peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
        };
        let action_handle = peppylib::ActionMessenger::send_goal(
                node_runner.messenger(),
                node_runner.processor().bound_core_node(),
                node_runner.processor().bound_instance_id(),
                peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("uvc_camera", "v1")?,
                TARGET_ACTION_NAME,
                Some(target),
                goal_payload,
                feedback_bound,
                timeout,
            )
            .await?;
        let reply = action_handle.goal_reply();
        let refusal = reply.refusal().map(Failure::from_wire).transpose()?;
        let data = match &reply.answer {
            peppylib::messaging::GoalAnswer::Accepted(response) => {
                Some(deserialize_goal_response(response.as_ref())?)
            }
            peppylib::messaging::GoalAnswer::Refused(_) => None,
        };
        Ok(Self {
            messenger: node_runner.messenger().clone(),
            accepted: refusal.is_none(),
            inner: action_handle,
            refusal,
            data,
        })
    }
    pub async fn cancel_goal(
        &self,
        timeout: std::time::Duration,
    ) -> crate::Result<CancelResponse> {
        let response = peppylib::ActionMessenger::cancel_goal(
                &self.messenger,
                &self.inner,
                timeout,
            )
            .await?;
        let state = match peppylib::messaging::decode_cancel_ack(
            response.payload_bytes().as_ref(),
        )? {
            peppylib::messaging::CancelState::Signalled => CancelState::Signalled,
            peppylib::messaging::CancelState::AlreadyTerminal => {
                CancelState::AlreadyTerminal
            }
            peppylib::messaging::CancelState::Unknown => CancelState::Unknown,
        };
        Ok(CancelResponse {
            instance_id: response.instance_id().to_string(),
            core_node: response.core_node().to_string(),
            state,
        })
    }
    /// Receives the next feedback message for this goal. The handle
    /// keeps every message until it is read, up to the feedback bound
    /// of `fire_goal`.
    ///
    /// Terminal errors that end a feedback drain loop:
    /// `Error::ActionFeedbackChannelClosed` when the producer closed
    /// the stream cleanly (end-of-stream sentinel),
    /// `Error::ActionFeedbackProducerGone` when the producer instance
    /// disappeared without closing it (process killed, crashed), in
    /// which case `get_result` resolves to `ResultOutcome::Abandoned`,
    /// and `Error::ActionFeedbackOverflow` when this caller fell behind
    /// by more unread messages than its bound, in which case the
    /// handle cancelled the goal.
    pub async fn on_next_feedback_message(&mut self) -> crate::Result<FeedbackMessage> {
        let feedback = self.inner.on_next_feedback().await?;
        let payload = feedback.payload_bytes();
        deserialize_feedback_payload(payload.as_ref())
    }
    pub async fn get_result(
        &self,
        timeout: std::time::Duration,
    ) -> crate::Result<ResultResponse> {
        let reply = peppylib::ActionMessenger::request_result(
                &self.messenger,
                &self.inner,
                timeout,
            )
            .await?;
        let outcome = match reply.outcome {
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Completed(result) => {
                ResultOutcome::Completed(deserialize_result_response(result.as_ref())?)
            }
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Cancelled { reason, result } => {
                ResultOutcome::Cancelled {
                    reason,
                    data: deserialize_result_response(result.as_ref())?,
                }
            }
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Failed(failure) => {
                ResultOutcome::Failed(Failure::from_wire(&failure)?)
            }
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Abandoned => ResultOutcome::Abandoned,
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Expired => ResultOutcome::Expired,
        };
        Ok(ResultResponse {
            instance_id: reply.instance_id,
            core_node: reply.core_node,
            outcome,
        })
    }
}
fn deserialize_goal_response(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<GoalResponseData> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]
    let context = format!("{} {} GoalResponse", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME);
    let mut payload = payload;
    let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader
        .get_root::<
            crate::capnp::uvc_camera_move_arm_fire_goal_response_message_capnp::goal_response_message_message::Reader,
        >()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let accepted_0 = root.reborrow().get_accepted();
    Ok(GoalResponseData {
        accepted: accepted_0,
    })
}

#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum CancelState {
    Signalled,
    AlreadyTerminal,
    Unknown,

fn deserialize_feedback_payload(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<FeedbackMessage> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]

    let mut payload = payload;

            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader
        .get_root::<
            crate::capnp::uvc_camera_move_arm_feedback_message_capnp::uvc_camera_move_arm_action_feedback_message::Reader,
        >()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let list_0 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_new_position()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = format!(
                "{} {} FeedbackMessage", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME
            );
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "new_position", context, source),
            )
        })?;

        return Err(
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!(
                    "invalid fixed list length for field '{}': expected {}, got {}",

        new_position_1[idx] = element;
    }
    Ok(FeedbackMessage {
        new_position: new_position_1,
    })
}

            .reborrow()

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum ResultOutcome {
    /// The goal is a success.
    Completed(ResultResponseData),
    /// The goal ended cancelled, for `reason`.
    Cancelled { reason: String, data: ResultResponseData },
    /// The goal failed: a failure kind that the action declares,
    /// a message, and no result.
    Failed(Failure),
    /// The goal ended without a result: its worker dropped it, or
    /// the provider is gone.
    Abandoned,
    /// The goal ended, and the provider no longer keeps its result.
    Expired,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct ResultResponse {
    pub instance_id: String,
    pub core_node: String,
    pub outcome: ResultOutcome,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct MessageHeader {
    pub stamp: std::time::SystemTime,
    pub frame_id: u32,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Message {
    pub header: MessageHeader,
    pub width: u32,
}
/// The producer set currently bound to this module's slot, in plan order.
/// A join can grow the set and a removal shrink it, so call the accessor at the
/// point of use; a producer disconnecting never changes it. Every generated module
/// referencing this slot reads the same set.
/// This slot declares cardinality `zero_or_more`: the set may be empty, so
/// callers handle the empty case.
pub fn bound_producers(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
) -> Vec<peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef> {
    node_runner.processor().bound_producers("camera_zero_or_more")
}
/// The members of the set currently bound to this module's slot, in plan order.
/// Each member is a producer with the copy its instance belongs to, `None` for
/// one the launcher deploys outside any copy. The same set `bound_producers()`
/// answers by producer alone, read under the same rules; a node that groups
/// producers by copy reads this one.
/// This slot declares cardinality `zero_or_more`: the set may be empty, so
/// callers handle the empty case.

/// A held subscription covering every producer bound to this slot: one
/// producer-pinned wire subscription per member of the slot's bound
/// set, merged client-side. Message order is preserved independently
/// per producer (no total ordering across producers), and ready
/// producers are merged fairly. To follow a single producer, filter on
/// the yielded producer identity.
/// The subscription follows the set as a join grows it or a removal shrinks it.
pub struct Subscription {
    inner: peppylib::runtime::BoundSetSubscription,
}
impl Subscription {
    /// Awaits the next message from any producer bound to this slot.
    ///
    /// Returns `Ok(Some((producer, message)))` for each message (the
    /// producer that published it always rides along), `Ok(None)` once the
    /// node is shutting down and no queued message remains, and `Err(..)`
    /// if a received payload fails to deserialize; a decode error does not
    /// tear down the subscription or shrink the bound set. On an empty
    /// set (a `zero_or_more` slot bound to nothing, or a vacant
    /// `zero_or_one` slot) this pends until shutdown, then returns
    /// `Ok(None)`.
    #[allow(clippy::should_implement_trait)]
    pub async fn next(
        &mut self,
    ) -> crate::Result<Option<(peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef, Message)>> {
        let Some((producer, message)) = self.inner.on_next_message().await else {
            return Ok(None);
        };
        let payload = message.payload_bytes();
        let message = deseralize_payload(payload.as_ref())?;
        Ok(Some((producer, message)))
    }
}
/// Subscribes to this topic across the slot's complete bound producer
/// set and returns a held `Subscription` yielding `(producer, message)`.
///
/// The shape is identical for every cardinality; only the size of the
/// bound set changes. Call this once, then loop on `Subscription::next`;
/// each underlying subscription's buffer retains messages published
/// between calls, so nothing is lost to a re-subscribe gap.
pub async fn subscribe(node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner) -> crate::Result<Subscription> {
    let topic_name = "video_stream";
    let node_name = "uvc_camera";
    let qos = peppylib::config::QoSProfile::Standard;
    let retention = peppylib::config::TopicRetention::LiveOnly;
    let inner = peppylib::runtime::subscribe_bound_set(
            node_runner,
            "camera_zero_or_more",
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("uvc_camera", "v1")?,
            topic_name,
            qos,
            retention,
        )
        .await
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::TopicSubscribe {
            topic_name: topic_name.to_string(),
            node_name: node_name.to_string(),
            source_msg: source.to_string(),
        })?;
    Ok(Subscription { inner })
}
fn deseralize_payload(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<Message> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]
    let context = String::from("Message");
    let mut payload = payload;
    let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader
        .get_root::<
            crate::capnp::on_next_camera_zero_or_more_video_stream_message_capnp::camera_zero_or_more_video_stream_message::Reader,
        >()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let reader_0 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_header()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "header", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let timestamp_2 = reader_0
        .reborrow()
        .get_stamp()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "stamp", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let capnp_timestamp_3 = peppylib::encoding::CapnpTimestamp {
        sec: timestamp_2.get_sec(),
        nsec: timestamp_2.get_nsec(),
    };
    let stamp_1 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time_from_capnp(capnp_timestamp_3);
    let frame_id_4 = reader_0.reborrow().get_frame_id();
    let header_5 = MessageHeader {
        stamp: stamp_1,
        frame_id: frame_id_4,
    };
    let width_6 = root.reborrow().get_width();
    Ok(Message {
        header: header_5,
        width: width_6,
    })
}

const NODE_NAME: &str = "uvc_camera";
const SERVICE_NAME: &str = "enable_camera";
const LINK_ID: &str = "camera_zero_or_more";
/// The producer set currently bound to this module's slot, in plan order.
/// A join can grow the set and a removal shrink it, so call the accessor at the
/// point of use; a producer disconnecting never changes it. Every generated module
/// referencing this slot reads the same set.
/// This slot declares cardinality `zero_or_more`: the set may be empty, so
/// callers handle the empty case.
pub fn bound_producers(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
) -> Vec<peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef> {
    node_runner.processor().bound_producers("camera_zero_or_more")
}
/// The members of the set currently bound to this module's slot, in plan order.
/// Each member is a producer with the copy its instance belongs to, `None` for
/// one the launcher deploys outside any copy. The same set `bound_producers()`
/// answers by producer alone, read under the same rules; a node that groups
/// producers by copy reads this one.
/// This slot declares cardinality `zero_or_more`: the set may be empty, so
/// callers handle the empty case.
pub fn bound_members(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
) -> Vec<peppylib::messaging::BoundMember> {
    node_runner.processor().bound_members("camera_zero_or_more")
}
/// A failure kind of this member: a kind of peppy, or a kind of its
/// contract that it lists.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum FailureKind {
    /// The attempt gave no result
    AttemptFailed,
    /// The bound of the provider passed
    Timeout,
    /// A precondition failed
    PreconditionFailed,
    /// A robot, limb or host is gone
    ResourceUnavailable,
    /// The provider refused the goal, and no other kind says why
    Rejected,
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl FailureKind {
    /// Every kind of this member: the kinds of peppy, then the
    /// kinds of its contract that it lists.
    pub const ALL: [Self; 5] = [
        Self::AttemptFailed,
        Self::Timeout,
        Self::PreconditionFailed,
        Self::ResourceUnavailable,
        Self::Rejected,
    ];
    /// The name of the kind on the wire.
    pub const fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::AttemptFailed => "attempt_failed",
            Self::Timeout => "timeout",
            Self::PreconditionFailed => "precondition_failed",
            Self::ResourceUnavailable => "resource_unavailable",
            Self::Rejected => "rejected",
        }
    }
    /// The kind named `name`, or `None` when this member does not
    /// declare it.
    pub fn parse(name: &str) -> Option<Self> {
        Self::ALL.into_iter().find(|kind| kind.as_str() == name)
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for FailureKind {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.write_str(self.as_str())
    }
}
/// Why this member refused or failed a goal, or gave an error
/// reply: a kind that it declares, and a message for a person.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Failure {
    pub kind: FailureKind,
    pub message: String,
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl Failure {
    pub fn new(kind: FailureKind, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            kind,
            message: message.into(),
        }
    }
    /// The wire form of the failure.
    pub(crate) fn to_wire(&self) -> peppylib::messaging::Failure {
        let kind = peppylib::messaging::FailureKindName::parse(self.kind.as_str())
            .expect("each kind of a member is a valid failure kind name");
        peppylib::messaging::Failure::new(kind, self.message.clone())
    }
    /// Parses a failure from the wire. A kind that this member does
    /// not declare is a deserialization error, whose text keeps the
    /// message of the provider.
    pub(crate) fn from_wire(
        failure: &peppylib::messaging::Failure,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let kind = FailureKind::parse(failure.kind().as_str())
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!(
                        "the failure kind `{}` is not a kind of `{}` (message: {:?})",
                        failure.kind(), SERVICE_NAME, failure.message()
                    ),
                )
            })?;
        Ok(Self::new(kind, failure.message()))
    }
    /// The failure of the error reply that `error` holds: `None`
    /// when `error` is not an error reply of a provider, or when
    /// its kind is not a kind of this member.
    pub fn of(error: &crate::Error) -> Option<Self> {
        let crate::Error::ServiceFailed { failure, .. } = error else {
            return None;
        };
        Self::from_wire(failure).ok()
    }
    /// `error` itself, unless it is an error reply with a kind this
    /// member does not declare: such a reply breaks the contract of
    /// the member, and gives a deserialization error instead.
    pub(crate) fn reject_undeclared_kind(error: crate::Error) -> crate::Error {
        let crate::Error::ServiceFailed { failure, .. } = &error else {
            return error;
        };
        match Self::from_wire(failure) {
            Ok(_) => error,
            Err(undeclared) => undeclared,
        }
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for Failure {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{}: {}", self.kind, self.message)
    }
}

    }

/// Polls this service on `target`, a member of this slot's bound set
/// (a `ProducerRef` yielded by the slot's own subscription is a
/// member by construction); a target outside the set fails before
/// anything reaches the wire.
///
/// An error reply of the provider is `Error::ServiceFailed`:
/// `Failure::of` reads its failure, with a kind that this member
/// declares.
///
/// This slot declares cardinality `zero_or_more`: `target` is a
/// caller-selected member of the possibly empty `bound_producers()`
/// set, and addressing several members is a plain loop at the call
/// site.
pub async fn poll(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
    target: &peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef,
    timeout: std::time::Duration,
    request: Request,
) -> crate::Result<Response> {
    node_runner.processor().ensure_target_bound(LINK_ID, target)?;
    let request_payload = {
        let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
        {
            let enable = request.enable;
            let mut root = capnp_msg
                .init_root::<
                    crate::capnp::poll_uvc_camera_enable_camera_message_capnp::enable_camera_message::Builder,
                >();
            root.set_enable(enable);
        }
        let mut buffer = Vec::new();
        capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
            .map_err(|source| {
                let context = format!("poll {} {}", NODE_NAME, SERVICE_NAME);
                crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
            })?;
        peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
    };
    let response_message = peppylib::ServiceMessenger::poll(
            node_runner.messenger(),
            node_runner.processor().bound_core_node(),
            node_runner.processor().bound_instance_id(),
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("uvc_camera", "v1")?,
            SERVICE_NAME,
            peppylib::messaging::ServiceTarget::Producer(target),
            request_payload,
            timeout,
        )
        .await
        .map_err(Failure::reject_undeclared_kind)?;
    let payload = response_message.payload_bytes();
    let response_data = deserialize_response(payload.as_ref())?;
    Ok(Response {
        instance_id: response_message.instance_id().to_string(),
        core_node: response_message.core_node().to_string(),
        data: response_data,
    })
}
fn deserialize_response(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<ResponseData> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]
    let context = format!("{} {} response", NODE_NAME, SERVICE_NAME);
    let mut payload = payload;
    let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader
        .get_root::<
            crate::capnp::poll_uvc_camera_enable_camera_response_message_capnp::enable_camera_response_message::Reader,
        >()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let enabled_0 = root.reborrow().get_enabled();
    let error_msg_opt_1 = if root.reborrow().has_error_msg() {
        let text_3 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_error_msg()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = context.clone();
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "error_msg", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let error_msg_2 = text_3
            .to_str()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = context.clone();
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "error_msg", context, source),
                )
            })?
            .to_owned();
        Some(error_msg_2)
    } else {
        None
    };
    Ok(ResponseData {
        enabled: enabled_0,
        error_msg: error_msg_opt_1,
    })
}

    pub stamp: std::time::SystemTime,
    pub frame_id: u32,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Message {
    pub header: MessageHeader,
    pub width: u32,

/// The producer bound to this module's slot.
/// The binding is fixed when the node starts (no live discovery; a
/// producer disconnecting never rebinds it) and shared by every
/// generated module referencing this slot. This slot declares
/// cardinality `one`: launch validation resolved exactly one producer,
/// so the accessor is singular and infallible.
pub fn bound_producer(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
) -> &peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef {
    node_runner.processor().sole_bound_producer("left_arm")
}
/// A held subscription covering every producer bound to this slot: one
/// producer-pinned wire subscription per member of the slot's bound
/// set, merged client-side. Message order is preserved independently
/// per producer (no total ordering across producers), and ready
/// producers are merged fairly. To follow a single producer, filter on
/// the yielded producer identity.
pub struct Subscription {
    inner: peppylib::runtime::BoundSetSubscription,
}
impl Subscription {
    /// Awaits the next message from any producer bound to this slot.
    ///
    /// Returns `Ok(Some((producer, message)))` for each message (the
    /// producer that published it always rides along), `Ok(None)` once the
    /// node is shutting down and no queued message remains, and `Err(..)`
    /// if a received payload fails to deserialize; a decode error does not
    /// tear down the subscription or shrink the bound set. On an empty
    /// set (a `zero_or_more` slot bound to nothing, or a vacant
    /// `zero_or_one` slot) this pends until shutdown, then returns
    /// `Ok(None)`.
    #[allow(clippy::should_implement_trait)]
    pub async fn next(
        &mut self,
    ) -> crate::Result<Option<(peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef, Message)>> {
        let Some((producer, message)) = self.inner.on_next_message().await else {
            return Ok(None);
        };
        let payload = message.payload_bytes();
        let message = deseralize_payload(payload.as_ref())?;
        Ok(Some((producer, message)))
    }
}
/// Subscribes to this topic across the slot's complete bound producer
/// set and returns a held `Subscription` yielding `(producer, message)`.
///
/// The shape is identical for every cardinality; only the size of the
/// bound set changes. Call this once, then loop on `Subscription::next`;
/// each underlying subscription's buffer retains messages published
/// between calls, so nothing is lost to a re-subscribe gap.
///
///The topic retains its newest 3 messages: a subscription that starts later also receives them.
pub async fn subscribe(node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner) -> crate::Result<Subscription> {
    let topic_name = "video_stream";
    let node_name = "arm_control";
    let qos = peppylib::config::QoSProfile::Standard;
    let retention = {
        const RETENTION: peppylib::config::TopicRetention = match peppylib::config::TopicRetention::latest(
            3,
        ) {
            Ok(retention) => retention,
            Err(_) => panic!("the topic's manifest states a retention depth in range"),
        };
        RETENTION
    };
    let inner = peppylib::runtime::subscribe_bound_set(
            node_runner,
            "left_arm",
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::contract("arm_control", "v1")?,
            topic_name,
            qos,
            retention,
        )
        .await
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::TopicSubscribe {
            topic_name: topic_name.to_string(),
            node_name: node_name.to_string(),
            source_msg: source.to_string(),
        })?;
    Ok(Subscription { inner })
}
fn deseralize_payload(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<Message> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]
    let context = String::from("Message");
    let mut payload = payload;
    let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader
        .get_root::<
            crate::capnp::on_next_left_arm_video_stream_message_capnp::left_arm_video_stream_message::Reader,
        >()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let reader_0 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_header()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "header", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let timestamp_2 = reader_0
        .reborrow()
        .get_stamp()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "stamp", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let capnp_timestamp_3 = peppylib::encoding::CapnpTimestamp {
        sec: timestamp_2.get_sec(),
        nsec: timestamp_2.get_nsec(),
    };
    let stamp_1 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time_from_capnp(capnp_timestamp_3);
    let frame_id_4 = reader_0.reborrow().get_frame_id();
    let header_5 = MessageHeader {
        stamp: stamp_1,
        frame_id: frame_id_4,
    };
    let width_6 = root.reborrow().get_width();
    Ok(Message {
        header: header_5,
        width: width_6,
    })
}

const TARGET_NODE_NAME: &str = "arm_control";
const TARGET_ACTION_NAME: &str = "move_arm";
const LINK_ID: &str = "left_arm";
/// The producer bound to this module's slot.
/// The binding is fixed when the node starts (no live discovery; a
/// producer disconnecting never rebinds it) and shared by every
/// generated module referencing this slot. This slot declares
/// cardinality `one`: launch validation resolved exactly one producer,
/// so the accessor is singular and infallible.
pub fn bound_producer(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,

    node_runner.processor().sole_bound_producer("left_arm")

/// A failure kind of this member: a kind of peppy, or a kind of its
/// contract that it lists.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum FailureKind {
    /// The attempt gave no result
    AttemptFailed,
    /// The bound of the provider passed
    Timeout,
    /// A precondition failed
    PreconditionFailed,
    /// A robot, limb or host is gone
    ResourceUnavailable,
    /// The provider refused the goal, and no other kind says why
    Rejected,
    /// No plan to the target
    Unreachable,
    /// A leader streams a limb of the goal
    LeaderStreaming,
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl FailureKind {
    /// Every kind of this member: the kinds of peppy, then the
    /// kinds of its contract that it lists.
    pub const ALL: [Self; 7] = [
        Self::AttemptFailed,
        Self::Timeout,
        Self::PreconditionFailed,
        Self::ResourceUnavailable,
        Self::Rejected,
        Self::Unreachable,
        Self::LeaderStreaming,
    ];
    /// The name of the kind on the wire.
    pub const fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::AttemptFailed => "attempt_failed",
            Self::Timeout => "timeout",
            Self::PreconditionFailed => "precondition_failed",
            Self::ResourceUnavailable => "resource_unavailable",
            Self::Rejected => "rejected",
            Self::Unreachable => "unreachable",
            Self::LeaderStreaming => "leader_streaming",
        }
    }
    /// The kind named `name`, or `None` when this member does not
    /// declare it.
    pub fn parse(name: &str) -> Option<Self> {
        Self::ALL.into_iter().find(|kind| kind.as_str() == name)
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for FailureKind {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.write_str(self.as_str())
    }
}
/// Why this member refused or failed a goal, or gave an error
/// reply: a kind that it declares, and a message for a person.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Failure {
    pub kind: FailureKind,
    pub message: String,
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl Failure {
    pub fn new(kind: FailureKind, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            kind,
            message: message.into(),
        }
    }
    /// The wire form of the failure.
    pub(crate) fn to_wire(&self) -> peppylib::messaging::Failure {
        let kind = peppylib::messaging::FailureKindName::parse(self.kind.as_str())
            .expect("each kind of a member is a valid failure kind name");
        peppylib::messaging::Failure::new(kind, self.message.clone())
    }
    /// Parses a failure from the wire. A kind that this member does
    /// not declare is a deserialization error, whose text keeps the
    /// message of the provider.
    pub(crate) fn from_wire(
        failure: &peppylib::messaging::Failure,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let kind = FailureKind::parse(failure.kind().as_str())
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!(
                        "the failure kind `{}` is not a kind of `{}` (message: {:?})",
                        failure.kind(), TARGET_ACTION_NAME, failure.message()
                    ),
                )
            })?;
        Ok(Self::new(kind, failure.message()))
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for Failure {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{}: {}", self.kind, self.message)
    }
}
