pub struct ActionHandle {
    messenger: peppylib::MessengerHandle,
    inner: peppylib::messaging::ActionGoalHandle,
    /// Whether the producer accepted the goal: `refusal` is
    /// `None`.
    pub accepted: bool,
    /// The failure the producer refused the goal with, of a kind
    /// that this member declares; `None` for an accepted goal.
    pub refusal: Option<Failure>,
    /// The declared goal response of an accepted goal; `None`
    /// for a refused goal.
    pub data: Option<GoalResponseData>,
}
impl ActionHandle {
    /// Fires this goal at `target`, a member of this slot's bound set (a
    /// `ProducerRef` yielded by the slot's own subscription is a member
    /// by construction); a target outside the set fails before anything
    /// reaches the wire. Each returned handle stays pinned to its
    /// target: feedback, result retrieval, and cancellation all address
    /// that producer directly.
    ///
    /// The handle keeps every feedback message until it is read, up to
    /// `feedback_bound` unread messages (`DEFAULT_FEEDBACK_BOUND` holds
    /// 1024). A caller that reads the feedback and falls behind by more
    /// cancels the goal, and its next read gives
    /// `Error::ActionFeedbackOverflow`. A caller that never reads the
    /// feedback keeps its goal.
    ///
    /// This slot declares cardinality `one`: `target` is its sole bound
    /// producer, returned by `bound_producer()`; the explicit parameter
    /// keeps call sites uniform across cardinalities.
    pub async fn fire_goal(
        node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
        target: &peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef,
        timeout: std::time::Duration,
        request: GoalRequest,
        feedback_bound: peppylib::messaging::FeedbackBound,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        node_runner.processor().ensure_target_bound(LINK_ID, target)?;
        let goal_payload = {
            let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
            {
                let mut root = capnp_msg
                    .init_root::<
                        crate::capnp::arm_control_move_arm_fire_goal_message_capnp::arm_control_move_arm_action_goal_message_message::Builder,
                    >();
                root.set_arm_id(request.arm_id);
                let mut list_0 = root.reborrow().init_desired_position(3);
                for (idx_1, value_2) in (request.desired_position).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_0.set(idx_1 as u32, *value_2);
                }
            }
            let mut buffer = Vec::new();
            capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
                .map_err(|source| {
                    let context = format!(
                        "fire_goal {} {}", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME
                    );
                    crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
                })?;
            peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
        };
        let action_handle = peppylib::ActionMessenger::send_goal(
                node_runner.messenger(),
                node_runner.processor().bound_core_node(),
                node_runner.processor().bound_instance_id(),
                peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::contract("arm_control", "v1")?,
                TARGET_ACTION_NAME,
                Some(target),
                goal_payload,
                feedback_bound,
                timeout,
            )
            .await?;
        let reply = action_handle.goal_reply();
        let refusal = reply.refusal().map(Failure::from_wire).transpose()?;
        let data = match &reply.answer {
            peppylib::messaging::GoalAnswer::Accepted(response) => {
                Some(deserialize_goal_response(response.as_ref())?)
            }
            peppylib::messaging::GoalAnswer::Refused(_) => None,
        };
        Ok(Self {
            messenger: node_runner.messenger().clone(),
            accepted: refusal.is_none(),
            inner: action_handle,
            refusal,
            data,
        })
    }
    pub async fn cancel_goal(
        &self,
        timeout: std::time::Duration,
    ) -> crate::Result<CancelResponse> {
        let response = peppylib::ActionMessenger::cancel_goal(
                &self.messenger,
                &self.inner,
                timeout,
            )
            .await?;
        let state = match peppylib::messaging::decode_cancel_ack(
            response.payload_bytes().as_ref(),
        )? {
            peppylib::messaging::CancelState::Signalled => CancelState::Signalled,
            peppylib::messaging::CancelState::AlreadyTerminal => {
                CancelState::AlreadyTerminal
            }
            peppylib::messaging::CancelState::Unknown => CancelState::Unknown,
        };
        Ok(CancelResponse {
            instance_id: response.instance_id().to_string(),
            core_node: response.core_node().to_string(),
            state,
        })
    }
    /// Receives the next feedback message for this goal. The handle
    /// keeps every message until it is read, up to the feedback bound
    /// of `fire_goal`.
    ///
    /// Terminal errors that end a feedback drain loop:
    /// `Error::ActionFeedbackChannelClosed` when the producer closed
    /// the stream cleanly (end-of-stream sentinel),
    /// `Error::ActionFeedbackProducerGone` when the producer instance
    /// disappeared without closing it (process killed, crashed), in
    /// which case `get_result` resolves to `ResultOutcome::Abandoned`,
    /// and `Error::ActionFeedbackOverflow` when this caller fell behind
    /// by more unread messages than its bound, in which case the
    /// handle cancelled the goal.
    pub async fn on_next_feedback_message(&mut self) -> crate::Result<FeedbackMessage> {
        let feedback = self.inner.on_next_feedback().await?;
        let payload = feedback.payload_bytes();
        deserialize_feedback_payload(payload.as_ref())
    }
    pub async fn get_result(
        &self,
        timeout: std::time::Duration,
    ) -> crate::Result<ResultResponse> {
        let reply = peppylib::ActionMessenger::request_result(
                &self.messenger,
                &self.inner,
                timeout,
            )
            .await?;
        let outcome = match reply.outcome {
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Completed(result) => {
                ResultOutcome::Completed(deserialize_result_response(result.as_ref())?)
            }
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Cancelled { reason, result } => {
                ResultOutcome::Cancelled {
                    reason,
                    data: deserialize_result_response(result.as_ref())?,
                }
            }
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Failed(failure) => {
                ResultOutcome::Failed(Failure::from_wire(&failure)?)
            }
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Abandoned => ResultOutcome::Abandoned,
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Expired => ResultOutcome::Expired,
        };
        Ok(ResultResponse {
            instance_id: reply.instance_id,
            core_node: reply.core_node,
            outcome,
        })
    }
}
fn deserialize_goal_response(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<GoalResponseData> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]
    let context = format!("{} {} GoalResponse", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME);
    let mut payload = payload;
    let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader
        .get_root::<
            crate::capnp::arm_control_move_arm_fire_goal_response_message_capnp::goal_response_message_message::Reader,
        >()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let accepted_0 = root.reborrow().get_accepted();
    Ok(GoalResponseData {
        accepted: accepted_0,
    })
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum CancelState {
    Signalled,
    AlreadyTerminal,
    Unknown,
}
fn deserialize_feedback_payload(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<FeedbackMessage> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]
    let context = format!("{} {} FeedbackMessage", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME);
    let mut payload = payload;
    let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader
        .get_root::<
            crate::capnp::arm_control_move_arm_feedback_message_capnp::arm_control_move_arm_action_feedback_message::Reader,
        >()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let list_0 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_new_position()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = format!(
                "{} {} FeedbackMessage", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME
            );
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "new_position", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    if list_0.len() as usize != 3 {
        let actual = list_0.len() as usize;
        return Err(
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!(
                    "invalid fixed list length for field '{}': expected {}, got {}",
                    "new_position", 3, actual
                ),
            ),
        );
    }
    let mut new_position_1: [i32; 3] = [i32::default(); 3];
    for (idx, element) in list_0.iter().enumerate() {
        new_position_1[idx] = element;
    }
    Ok(FeedbackMessage {
        new_position: new_position_1,
    })
}
fn deserialize_result_response(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<ResultResponseData> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]
    let context = format!(
        "{} {} {}", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME, "ResultResponse"
    );
    let mut payload = payload;
    let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader
        .get_root::<
            crate::capnp::arm_control_move_arm_get_result_response_message_capnp::result_response_message_message::Reader,
        >()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let success_0 = root.reborrow().get_success();
    let error_msg_opt_1 = if root.reborrow().has_error_msg() {
        let text_3 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_error_msg()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = format!(
                    "{} {} {}", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME, "ResultResponse"
                );
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "error_msg", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let error_msg_2 = text_3
            .to_str()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = format!(
                    "{} {} {}", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME, "ResultResponse"
                );
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "error_msg", context, source),
                )
            })?
            .to_owned();
        Some(error_msg_2)
    } else {
        None
    };
    Ok(ResultResponseData {
        success: success_0,
        error_msg: error_msg_opt_1,
    })
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum ResultOutcome {
    /// The goal is a success.
    Completed(ResultResponseData),
    /// The goal ended cancelled, for `reason`.
    Cancelled { reason: String, data: ResultResponseData },
    /// The goal failed: a failure kind that the action declares,
    /// a message, and no result.
    Failed(Failure),
    /// The goal ended without a result: its worker dropped it, or
    /// the provider is gone.
    Abandoned,
    /// The goal ended, and the provider no longer keeps its result.
    Expired,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct ResultResponse {
    pub instance_id: String,
    pub core_node: String,
    pub outcome: ResultOutcome,
}

pub const TOPIC_NAME: &str = "joint_states";
/// This node's own pairing-slot link_id.
pub const LINK_ID: &str = "controller_one";
pub const PAIRING_NAME: &str = "arm_link";
pub const PAIRING_TAG: &str = "v1";
/// The peer currently paired on this slot, or `None` while unpaired.
pub fn paired(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
) -> crate::Result<Option<peppylib::messaging::PeerInfo>> {
    Ok(node_runner.peer(LINK_ID)?.paired())
}

/// identity. Returns immediately when already paired.
pub async fn wait_paired(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
) -> crate::Result<peppylib::messaging::PeerInfo> {

}
#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
pub fn build_message(
    positions: [f64; 3],
    timestamp: std::time::SystemTime,
) -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {
    let payload = {
        let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
        {
            let mut root = capnp_msg
                .init_root::<
                    crate::capnp::peer_controller_one_joint_states_message_capnp::peer_controller_one_joint_states_message::Builder,
                >();
            let mut list_0 = root.reborrow().init_positions(3);
            for (idx_1, value_2) in (positions).iter().enumerate() {
                list_0.set(idx_1 as u32, *value_2);
            }
            let timestamp_3 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time(timestamp);
            let mut timestamp_builder_4 = root.reborrow().init_timestamp();
            timestamp_builder_4.set_sec(timestamp_3.sec);
            timestamp_builder_4.set_nsec(timestamp_3.nsec);
        }
        let mut buffer = Vec::new();
        capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
            .map_err(|source| {
                let context = String::from("peer_controller_one_joint_states");
                crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
            })?;
        peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
    };
    Ok(payload)
}
/// Declares the publisher for this pairing topic. `publish`
/// reaches the paired peer; while unpaired it is a legal no-op,
/// since a pairing is a live stream and nothing is waiting.
pub async fn declare_publisher(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
) -> crate::Result<peppylib::TopicPublisher> {
    let qos = peppylib::config::QoSProfile::SensorData;
    peppylib::runtime::declare_sole_peer_publisher(
            node_runner,
            LINK_ID,
            PAIRING_NAME,
            PAIRING_TAG,
            TOPIC_NAME,
            qos,
        )
        .await
}

/// Every pair this module's slot currently holds, in establishment order.
/// The set is live: a pair joins it when its peer's instance starts and
/// leaves it when either side stops. This slot declares cardinality
/// `one_or_more`: the plan pairs at least one peer to it, and the set
/// still empties once every peer has stopped.
/// Each member carries the copy its peer belongs to, so pairs from one
/// `stack join` group by `copy`; `publish_to` names a member's `info`.
pub fn peers(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
) -> crate::Result<Vec<peppylib::messaging::PeerMember>> {
    Ok(node_runner.peer_set(LINK_ID)?.members())
}

PeerConsumedTopic :: sensor_bus/joint_states

pub const TOPIC_NAME: &str = "joint_states";
/// This node's own pairing-slot link_id.
pub const LINK_ID: &str = "sensor_bus";
pub const PAIRING_NAME: &str = "arm_link";
pub const PAIRING_TAG: &str = "v1";
/// The peer currently paired on this slot, or `None` while unpaired.
pub fn paired(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
) -> crate::Result<Option<peppylib::messaging::PeerInfo>> {
    Ok(node_runner.peer(LINK_ID)?.paired())
}
/// Waits until a peer is paired on this slot and returns its
/// identity. Returns immediately when already paired.
pub async fn wait_paired(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
) -> crate::Result<peppylib::messaging::PeerInfo> {
    node_runner.peer(LINK_ID)?.wait_paired().await
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Message {
    pub positions: [f64; 3],
    pub timestamp: std::time::SystemTime,
}
/// A held subscription to this pairing topic. Yields nothing while
/// the slot is unpaired; while paired, only the paired peer's
/// messages surface (triple wire pin + delivery-time identity
/// check). A pairing is a live stream, not a mailbox: messages
/// published before pairing are never delivered.
pub struct Subscription {
    inner: peppylib::runtime::PeerSubscription,
}
impl Subscription {
    /// Awaits the next message from the currently paired peer.
    ///
    /// Returns `Ok(Some((peer, message)))` for each message,
    /// `Ok(None)` once the runtime shuts down, and `Err(..)` if a
    /// received payload fails to deserialize. `peer` is the paired
    /// peer's identity, its wire address plus the link_id of its
    /// complementary slot: the same `PeerInfo` that `paired()`
    /// returns.
    #[allow(clippy::should_implement_trait)]
    pub async fn next(
        &mut self,
    ) -> crate::Result<Option<(peppylib::messaging::PeerInfo, Message)>> {
        let Some((peer, message)) = self.inner.on_next_message().await else {
            return Ok(None);
        };
        let payload = message.payload_bytes();
        let message = deseralize_payload(payload.as_ref())?;
        Ok(Some((peer, message)))
    }
}
/// Subscribes to this pairing topic and returns a held
/// `Subscription` that follows the slot's live pin. Legal while
/// unpaired: the subscription stays silent until a peer pairs.
pub async fn subscribe(node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner) -> crate::Result<Subscription> {
    let qos = peppylib::config::QoSProfile::SensorData;
    let inner = peppylib::runtime::subscribe_peer(
            node_runner,
            LINK_ID,
            PAIRING_NAME,
            PAIRING_TAG,
            TOPIC_NAME,
            qos,
        )
        .await?;
    Ok(Subscription { inner })
}
fn deseralize_payload(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<Message> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]
    let context = String::from("Message");
    let mut payload = payload;
    let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader
        .get_root::<
            crate::capnp::peer_sensor_bus_joint_states_message_capnp::peer_sensor_bus_joint_states_message::Reader,
        >()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let list_0 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_positions()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "positions", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    if list_0.len() as usize != 3 {
        let actual = list_0.len() as usize;
        return Err(
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!(
                    "invalid fixed list length for field '{}': expected {}, got {}",
                    "positions", 3, actual
                ),
            ),
        );
    }
    let mut positions_1: [f64; 3] = [f64::default(); 3];
    for (idx, element) in list_0.iter().enumerate() {
        positions_1[idx] = element;
    }
    let timestamp_3 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_timestamp()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "timestamp", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let capnp_timestamp_4 = peppylib::encoding::CapnpTimestamp {
        sec: timestamp_3.get_sec(),
        nsec: timestamp_3.get_nsec(),
    };
    let timestamp_2 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time_from_capnp(capnp_timestamp_4);
    Ok(Message {
        positions: positions_1,
        timestamp: timestamp_2,
    })
}


================================================================
ObservedTopic :: watchers_one/joint_states
================================================================
pub const TOPIC_NAME: &str = "joint_states";
/// This node's own observer-slot link_id.
pub const LINK_ID: &str = "watchers_one";
pub const PAIRING_NAME: &str = "arm_link";
pub const PAIRING_TAG: &str = "v1";
/// The pairing this module's observer slot observes.
/// The daemon keeps the member's incarnation and liveness current, and a
/// source that is down stays observed. This slot declares cardinality `one`:
/// the plan binds exactly one pairing to it, so the accessor is singular and
/// has no absent case to answer.
/// Purely local configuration state; there is no health-derived helper,
/// because a third node's health is not knowable here.
pub fn source(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
) -> crate::Result<peppylib::messaging::ObservedSource> {
    Ok(node_runner.observation_slot(LINK_ID)?.sole_source())
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Message {
    pub positions: [f64; 3],
    pub timestamp: std::time::SystemTime,
}
/// A held subscription to an observed pairing topic, fanned in
/// across every member of the slot's observed set. Yields nothing
/// while the set is empty or no member is emitting; only observed
/// members' messages surface (triple wire pin +
/// generation-tagged delivery check). A pairing is a live stream,
/// not a mailbox: messages published before observation are never
/// delivered.
pub struct Subscription {
    inner: peppylib::runtime::ObservedTopicSubscription,
}
impl Subscription {
    /// Awaits the next message from any currently observed source.
    ///
    /// Returns `Ok(Some((source, message)))` for each message,
    /// `Ok(None)` once the runtime shuts down, and `Err(..)` if a
    /// received payload fails to deserialize. `source` is the
    /// observed pairing that published it, the producer's wire
    /// address plus the producer-side link_id: the same
    /// `ObservedSource` the slot's accessors enumerate, so a
    /// multi-member slot tells its sources apart even when several
    /// members share one instance.
    #[allow(clippy::should_implement_trait)]
    pub async fn next(
        &mut self,
    ) -> crate::Result<Option<(peppylib::messaging::ObservedSource, Message)>> {
        let Some((source, message)) = self.inner.on_next_message().await else {
            return Ok(None);
        };
        let payload = message.payload_bytes();
        let message = deseralize_payload(payload.as_ref())?;
        Ok(Some((source, message)))
    }
}
/// Subscribes to this observed pairing topic and returns a held
/// `Subscription` pinned to the observer slot's source. Legal before
/// the source is resolved or live: the subscription stays silent until
/// the source emits.
pub async fn subscribe(node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner) -> crate::Result<Subscription> {
    let qos = peppylib::config::QoSProfile::SensorData;
    let inner = peppylib::runtime::subscribe_observed(
            node_runner,
            LINK_ID,
            PAIRING_NAME,
            PAIRING_TAG,
            TOPIC_NAME,
            qos,
        )
        .await?;
    Ok(Subscription { inner })
}
fn deseralize_payload(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<Message> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]
    let context = String::from("Message");
    let mut payload = payload;
    let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader
        .get_root::<
            crate::capnp::peer_watchers_one_joint_states_message_capnp::peer_watchers_one_joint_states_message::Reader,
        >()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let list_0 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_positions()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "positions", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    if list_0.len() as usize != 3 {
        let actual = list_0.len() as usize;
        return Err(
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!(
                    "invalid fixed list length for field '{}': expected {}, got {}",
                    "positions", 3, actual
                ),
            ),
        );
    }
    let mut positions_1: [f64; 3] = [f64::default(); 3];
    for (idx, element) in list_0.iter().enumerate() {
        positions_1[idx] = element;
    }
    let timestamp_3 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_timestamp()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "timestamp", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let capnp_timestamp_4 = peppylib::encoding::CapnpTimestamp {
        sec: timestamp_3.get_sec(),
        nsec: timestamp_3.get_nsec(),
    };
    let timestamp_2 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time_from_capnp(capnp_timestamp_4);
    Ok(Message {
        positions: positions_1,
        timestamp: timestamp_2,
    })
}

/// Every pairing this module's observer slot observes, in plan order.
/// The daemon keeps each member's incarnation and liveness current, a copy
/// joining or leaving adds or removes members, and a member whose source is
/// down stays in the set, at its position. This slot declares
/// cardinality `one_or_more`: every plan binds at least one pairing to it,

/// because a third node's health is not knowable here.

Mock :: deps/camera_one

//!Mock producer for the `camera_one` dependency slot: plays the dependency over the real wire under the identity the harness seeds into the slot's bound set. One messaging session per mock, so [`Mock::stop`] is a whole-producer loss.
/// This node's consumer-slot link_id.
pub const LINK_ID: &str = "camera_one";
/// Core-node segment of the mock's wire identity.
pub const MOCK_CORE_NODE: &str = "mock-core";
/// Default instance segment of the mock's wire identity.
pub const MOCK_INSTANCE_ID: &str = "mock-camera_one";
/// The default mock's wire identity, as the harness seeds it.
pub fn producer_ref() -> peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef {
    producer_ref_for(MOCK_INSTANCE_ID)
}
/// [`producer_ref`] under an explicit instance id (multi-instance slots).
pub fn producer_ref_for(instance_id: &str) -> peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef {
    peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef::new(MOCK_CORE_NODE, instance_id)
}
/// One mocked producer instance for this slot. Fields are the typed
/// per-interface surfaces; construct with [`Mock::start`].
pub struct Mock {
    _session: peppylib::MessengerHandle,
    pub video_stream: video_stream::Publisher,
    pub enable_camera: enable_camera::Service,
}
impl Mock {
    /// Connects a dedicated session and declares every interface of
    /// this dependency now (publishers, queryables, action engines),
    /// under the default [`MOCK_INSTANCE_ID`].
    pub async fn start(
        router: &peppylib::testing::EphemeralRouter,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        Self::start_as(router, MOCK_INSTANCE_ID).await
    }
    /// [`Mock::start`] under an explicit instance id, for slots bound
    /// to several mock instances.
    pub async fn start_as(
        router: &peppylib::testing::EphemeralRouter,
        instance_id: &str,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let session = router.connect().await?;
        let video_stream = video_stream::Publisher::declare(&session, instance_id)
            .await?;
        let enable_camera = enable_camera::Service::listen(&session, instance_id).await?;
        Ok(Self {
            _session: session,
            video_stream,
            enable_camera,
        })
    }
    /// Simulates this producer dying mid-flight, deterministically:
    /// live action goals are disarmed (their eventual drop emits no
    /// clean close), then every declaration and the session drop,
    /// releasing the liveliness tokens consumers latch on.
    pub fn stop(self) {}
}
///Typed mock publisher for the consumed topic `video_stream`: publishes over the real wire as this mock's identity; the first publish waits for the node's subscription to match (no sleeps), and no subscriber within the readiness timeout is a loud error.
pub mod video_stream {
    pub use crate::consumed_topics::camera_one::video_stream::Message;
    #[allow(unused_imports)]
    use crate::consumed_topics::camera_one::video_stream::*;
    pub struct Publisher {
        core: peppylib::testing::TestTopicPublisher,
    }
    impl Publisher {
        pub(super) async fn declare(
            session: &peppylib::MessengerHandle,
            instance_id: &str,
        ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
            let core = peppylib::testing::TestTopicPublisher::declare(
                    session,
                    super::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                    instance_id,
                    peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("uvc_camera", "v1")?,
                    None,
                    "video_stream",
                    peppylib::config::QoSProfile::Standard,
                    peppylib::config::TopicRetention::LiveOnly,
                )
                .await?;
            Ok(Self { core })
        }
        /// Publishes one typed message; lazily waits for the node's
        /// subscription before the first delivery.
        pub async fn publish(&self, message: &Message) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.core.publish(serialize_message(message)?).await
        }
        /// Waits until the node's subscription for this topic is
        /// visible; returns whether it matched within `timeout`.
        pub async fn wait_for_subscriber(
            &self,
            timeout: std::time::Duration,
        ) -> crate::Result<bool> {
            self.core.wait_for_subscriber(timeout).await
        }
    }
    fn serialize_message(value: &Message) -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {
        Ok({
            let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
            {
                let mut root = capnp_msg
                    .init_root::<
                        crate::capnp::on_next_camera_one_video_stream_message_capnp::camera_one_video_stream_message::Builder,
                    >();
                let mut builder_0 = root.reborrow().init_header();
                let timestamp_1 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time((value.header).stamp);
                let mut timestamp_builder_2 = builder_0.reborrow().init_stamp();
                timestamp_builder_2.set_sec(timestamp_1.sec);
                timestamp_builder_2.set_nsec(timestamp_1.nsec);
                builder_0.set_frame_id((value.header).frame_id);
                root.set_width(value.width);
            }
            let mut buffer = Vec::new();
            capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
                .map_err(|source| {
                    let context = String::from("mock publish video_stream");
                    crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
                })?;
            peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
        })
    }
}
///Typed mock server for the consumed service `enable_camera`: a background pump captures every request; scripted responses serve automatically, unscripted requests park for `next_request`.
pub mod enable_camera {
    pub use crate::consumed_services::camera_one::enable_camera::Request;
    pub use crate::consumed_services::camera_one::enable_camera::ResponseData;
    pub use crate::consumed_services::camera_one::enable_camera::{Failure, FailureKind};
    #[allow(unused_imports)]
    use crate::consumed_services::camera_one::enable_camera::*;
    pub struct Service {
        core: peppylib::testing::MockServiceCore,
    }
    /// Answers exactly one parked request; consumed by use.
    pub struct Responder {
        inner: peppylib::messaging::ServiceResponder,
    }
    impl Service {
        pub(super) async fn listen(
            session: &peppylib::MessengerHandle,
            instance_id: &str,
        ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
            let core = peppylib::testing::MockServiceCore::listen(
                    session,
                    super::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                    instance_id,
                    peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("uvc_camera", "v1")?,
                    "enable_camera",
                )
                .await?;
            Ok(Self { core })
        }
        /// The next unscripted request, decoded, with the responder
        /// the test must use to answer it. Errors after `timeout`.
        pub async fn next_request(
            &self,
            timeout: std::time::Duration,
        ) -> crate::Result<(Request, Responder)> {
            let (context, responder) = self.core.next_request(timeout).await?;
            let request = deserialize_request(
                context.message().payload_bytes().as_ref(),
            )?;
            Ok((request, Responder { inner: responder }))
        }
        ///Enqueues one response to be served automatically to the next inbound request (FIFO across repeated calls); only unscripted requests park for `next_request`.
        pub fn enqueue_response(&self, response: ResponseData) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.core.enqueue_response(serialize_response(&response)?);
            Ok(())
        }
        /// Every request received so far (scripted and manual alike),
        /// decoded, in arrival order.
        pub fn captured(&self) -> crate::Result<Vec<Request>> {
            self.core
                .captured()
                .iter()
                .map(|captured| {
                    deserialize_request(captured.message.payload_bytes().as_ref())
                })
                .collect()
        }
    }
    impl Responder {
        /// Sends the typed response for the parked request.
        pub async fn respond(self, response: ResponseData) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.inner.respond(serialize_response(&response)?).await
        }
        /// Answers the parked request with an error reply: a failure
        /// kind that this member declares and a message.
        pub async fn respond_failure(
            self,
            kind: FailureKind,
            message: impl Into<String>,
        ) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.inner.respond_failure(Failure::new(kind, message).to_wire()).await
        }
    }
    fn deserialize_request(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<Request> {
        #[allow(clippy::all)]
        let context = String::from("mock enable_camera request");
        let mut payload = payload;
        let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
                &mut payload,
                capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
            )
            .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("{}: {}", context, source),
            ))?;
        let root = message_reader
            .get_root::<
                crate::capnp::poll_uvc_camera_enable_camera_message_capnp::enable_camera_message::Reader,
            >()
            .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("{}: {}", context, source),
            ))?;
        let enable_0 = root.reborrow().get_enable();
        Ok(Request { enable: enable_0 })
    }
    fn serialize_response(value: &ResponseData) -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {
        Ok({
            let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
            {
                let mut root = capnp_msg
                    .init_root::<
                        crate::capnp::poll_uvc_camera_enable_camera_response_message_capnp::enable_camera_response_message::Builder,
                    >();
                root.set_enabled(value.enabled);
                if let Some(value_0) = (value.error_msg).as_ref() {
                    root.set_error_msg(value_0.as_str());
                }
            }
            let mut buffer = Vec::new();
            capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
                .map_err(|source| {
                    let context = String::from("mock enable_camera response");
                    crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
                })?;
            peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
        })
    }
}

//!Mock producer for the `camera_zero_or_one` dependency slot: plays the dependency over the real wire under the identity the harness seeds into the slot's bound set. One messaging session per mock, so [`Mock::stop`] is a whole-producer loss.
/// This node's consumer-slot link_id.
pub const LINK_ID: &str = "camera_zero_or_one";
/// Core-node segment of the mock's wire identity.
pub const MOCK_CORE_NODE: &str = "mock-core";
/// Default instance segment of the mock's wire identity.
pub const MOCK_INSTANCE_ID: &str = "mock-camera_zero_or_one";
/// The default mock's wire identity, as the harness seeds it.
pub fn producer_ref() -> peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef {
    producer_ref_for(MOCK_INSTANCE_ID)
}
/// [`producer_ref`] under an explicit instance id (multi-instance slots).
pub fn producer_ref_for(instance_id: &str) -> peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef {
    peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef::new(MOCK_CORE_NODE, instance_id)
}
/// One mocked producer instance for this slot. Fields are the typed
/// per-interface surfaces; construct with [`Mock::start`].
pub struct Mock {
    _session: peppylib::MessengerHandle,
    pub video_stream: video_stream::Publisher,
    pub enable_camera: enable_camera::Service,
}
impl Mock {
    /// Connects a dedicated session and declares every interface of
    /// this dependency now (publishers, queryables, action engines),
    /// under the default [`MOCK_INSTANCE_ID`].
    pub async fn start(
        router: &peppylib::testing::EphemeralRouter,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        Self::start_as(router, MOCK_INSTANCE_ID).await
    }
    /// [`Mock::start`] under an explicit instance id, for slots bound
    /// to several mock instances.
    pub async fn start_as(
        router: &peppylib::testing::EphemeralRouter,
        instance_id: &str,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let session = router.connect().await?;
        let video_stream = video_stream::Publisher::declare(&session, instance_id)
            .await?;
        let enable_camera = enable_camera::Service::listen(&session, instance_id).await?;
        Ok(Self {
            _session: session,
            video_stream,
            enable_camera,
        })
    }
    /// Simulates this producer dying mid-flight, deterministically:
    /// live action goals are disarmed (their eventual drop emits no
    /// clean close), then every declaration and the session drop,
    /// releasing the liveliness tokens consumers latch on.
    pub fn stop(self) {}
}
///Typed mock publisher for the consumed topic `video_stream`: publishes over the real wire as this mock's identity; the first publish waits for the node's subscription to match (no sleeps), and no subscriber within the readiness timeout is a loud error.
pub mod video_stream {
    pub use crate::consumed_topics::camera_zero_or_one::video_stream::Message;
    #[allow(unused_imports)]
    use crate::consumed_topics::camera_zero_or_one::video_stream::*;
    pub struct Publisher {
        core: peppylib::testing::TestTopicPublisher,
    }
    impl Publisher {
        pub(super) async fn declare(
            session: &peppylib::MessengerHandle,
            instance_id: &str,
        ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
            let core = peppylib::testing::TestTopicPublisher::declare(
                    session,
                    super::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                    instance_id,
                    peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("uvc_camera", "v1")?,
                    None,
                    "video_stream",
                    peppylib::config::QoSProfile::Standard,
                    peppylib::config::TopicRetention::LiveOnly,
                )
                .await?;
            Ok(Self { core })
        }
        /// Publishes one typed message; lazily waits for the node's
        /// subscription before the first delivery.
        pub async fn publish(&self, message: &Message) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.core.publish(serialize_message(message)?).await
        }
        /// Waits until the node's subscription for this topic is
        /// visible; returns whether it matched within `timeout`.
        pub async fn wait_for_subscriber(
            &self,
            timeout: std::time::Duration,
        ) -> crate::Result<bool> {
            self.core.wait_for_subscriber(timeout).await
        }
    }
    fn serialize_message(value: &Message) -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {
        Ok({
            let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
            {
                let mut root = capnp_msg
                    .init_root::<
                        crate::capnp::on_next_camera_zero_or_one_video_stream_message_capnp::camera_zero_or_one_video_stream_message::Builder,
                    >();
                let mut builder_0 = root.reborrow().init_header();
                let timestamp_1 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time((value.header).stamp);
                let mut timestamp_builder_2 = builder_0.reborrow().init_stamp();
                timestamp_builder_2.set_sec(timestamp_1.sec);
                timestamp_builder_2.set_nsec(timestamp_1.nsec);
                builder_0.set_frame_id((value.header).frame_id);
                root.set_width(value.width);
            }
            let mut buffer = Vec::new();
            capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
                .map_err(|source| {
                    let context = String::from("mock publish video_stream");
                    crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
                })?;
            peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
        })
    }
}

    pub use crate::consumed_actions::camera_one_or_more::move_arm::ResultResponseData;
    #[allow(unused_imports)]
    use crate::consumed_actions::camera_one_or_more::move_arm::*;
    pub use crate::consumed_actions::camera_one_or_more::move_arm::{
        Failure, FailureKind,
    };
    pub struct Action {
        core: peppylib::testing::MockActionServerCore,
    }
    impl Action {
        pub(super) async fn expose(
            session: &peppylib::MessengerHandle,
            instance_id: &str,
        ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
            let core = peppylib::testing::MockActionServerCore::expose(
                    session,
                    super::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                    instance_id,
                    peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("uvc_camera", "v1")?,
                    "move_arm",
                    true,
                )
                .await?;
            Ok(Self { core })
        }
        /// Parks until the node sends a goal, bounded by `timeout`;
        /// the returned goal awaits the test's admission decision.
        pub async fn next_goal(
            &mut self,
            timeout: std::time::Duration,
        ) -> crate::Result<PendingGoal> {
            let pending = self.core.next_goal(timeout).await?;
            let request = deserialize_goal_request(pending.request_bytes())?;
            Ok(PendingGoal { pending, request })
        }
        pub(super) fn stop(self) {
            self.core.stop();
        }
    }
    /// A goal received by the mock, awaiting accept/refuse.
    pub struct PendingGoal {
        pending: peppylib::testing::MockPendingGoal,
        /// The decoded goal request.
        pub request: GoalRequest,
    }
    impl PendingGoal {
        /// The client-generated correlation id for this goal.
        pub fn goal_id(&self) -> &str {
            self.pending.goal_id()
        }
        /// Accepts the goal; the returned handle drives feedback and
        /// completion.
        pub async fn accept(
            self,
            response: GoalResponseData,
        ) -> crate::Result<ActiveGoal> {
            let context = self
                .pending
                .accept(serialize_goal_response(&response)?)
                .await?;
            Ok(ActiveGoal { context })
        }
        /// Refuses the goal with a failure kind that this member
        /// declares and a message. A refusal carries no goal
        /// response.
        pub async fn refuse(
            self,
            kind: FailureKind,
            message: impl Into<String>,
        ) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.pending.refuse(Failure::new(kind, message).to_wire()).await
        }
    }
    /// An accepted goal: drives feedback and terminal completion.
    pub struct ActiveGoal {
        context: std::sync::Arc<peppylib::messaging::GoalContext>,
    }
    impl ActiveGoal {
        /// Publishes one typed feedback message for this goal.
        pub async fn publish_feedback(
            &self,
            feedback: &FeedbackMessage,
        ) -> crate::Result<()> {
            let payload = serialize_feedback(feedback)?;
            let payload = peppylib::messaging::NonEmptyPayload::try_new(payload)
                .map_err(|_| peppylib::PeppyError::Io(
                    std::io::Error::other(
                        "feedback serialized to zero bytes; empty is reserved for \
                         end-of-stream",
                    ),
                ))?;
            self.context.publish_feedback(payload).await?;
            Ok(())
        }
        /// Resolves when this goal receives its cancel signal: a
        /// cancel request of the node, or the end of the node.
        pub async fn cancel_signal(&self) {
            self.context.cancel_signal().await
        }
        /// Whether this goal received its cancel signal.
        pub fn is_cancelled(&self) -> bool {
            self.context.is_cancelled()
        }
        /// Why this goal received its cancel signal; `None` before
        /// it did. `CancelCause::reason` gives a reason for
        /// `complete_cancelled`.
        pub fn cancel_cause(&self) -> Option<peppylib::messaging::CancelCause> {
            self.context.cancel_cause()
        }
        /// Ends the goal with the status completed: a success.
        pub async fn complete(&self, result: &ResultResponseData) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.context.complete(serialize_result(result)?).await?;
            Ok(())
        }
        /// Ends the goal with the status failed: a failure kind
        /// that this member declares, a message, and no result.
        pub async fn fail(
            &self,
            kind: FailureKind,
            message: impl Into<String>,
        ) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.context.fail(Failure::new(kind, message).to_wire()).await?;
            Ok(())
        }
        /// Ends the goal with the status cancelled, for `reason`.
        pub async fn complete_cancelled(
            &self,
            reason: impl Into<String>,
            result: &ResultResponseData,
        ) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.context.complete_cancelled(reason, serialize_result(result)?).await?;
            Ok(())
        }
    }
    fn deserialize_goal_request(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<GoalRequest> {
        #[allow(clippy::all)]
        let context = String::from("mock move_arm goal request");
        let mut payload = payload;
        let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
                &mut payload,
                capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
            )
            .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("{}: {}", context, source),
            ))?;
        let root = message_reader
            .get_root::<
                crate::capnp::uvc_camera_move_arm_fire_goal_message_capnp::uvc_camera_move_arm_action_goal_message_message::Reader,
            >()
            .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("{}: {}", context, source),
            ))?;
        let arm_id_0 = root.reborrow().get_arm_id();
        let list_1 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_desired_position()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("mock move_arm goal request");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "desired_position", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        if list_1.len() as usize != 3 {
            let actual = list_1.len() as usize;
            return Err(
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!(
                        "invalid fixed list length for field '{}': expected {}, got {}",
                        "desired_position", 3, actual
                    ),
                ),
            );
        }
        let mut desired_position_2: [i32; 3] = [i32::default(); 3];
        for (idx, element) in list_1.iter().enumerate() {
            desired_position_2[idx] = element;
        }
        Ok(GoalRequest {
            arm_id: arm_id_0,
            desired_position: desired_position_2,
        })
    }
    fn serialize_goal_response(
        value: &GoalResponseData,
    ) -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {
        Ok({
            let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
            {
                let mut root = capnp_msg
                    .init_root::<
                        crate::capnp::uvc_camera_move_arm_fire_goal_response_message_capnp::goal_response_message_message::Builder,
                    >();
                root.set_accepted(value.accepted);
            }
            let mut buffer = Vec::new();
            capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
                .map_err(|source| {
                    let context = String::from("mock move_arm goal response");
                    crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
                })?;
            peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
        })
    }
    fn serialize_feedback(value: &FeedbackMessage) -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {
        Ok({
            let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
            {
                let mut root = capnp_msg
                    .init_root::<
                        crate::capnp::uvc_camera_move_arm_feedback_message_capnp::uvc_camera_move_arm_action_feedback_message::Builder,
                    >();
                let mut list_0 = root.reborrow().init_new_position(3);
                for (idx_1, value_2) in (value.new_position).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_0.set(idx_1 as u32, *value_2);
                }
            }
            let mut buffer = Vec::new();
            capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
                .map_err(|source| {
                    let context = String::from("mock move_arm feedback");
                    crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
                })?;
            peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
        })
    }
    fn serialize_result(value: &ResultResponseData) -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {
        Ok({
            let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
            {
                let mut root = capnp_msg
                    .init_root::<
                        crate::capnp::uvc_camera_move_arm_get_result_response_message_capnp::result_response_message_message::Builder,
                    >();
                root.set_success(value.success);
                if let Some(value_0) = (value.error_msg).as_ref() {
                    root.set_error_msg(value_0.as_str());
                }
            }
            let mut buffer = Vec::new();
            capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
                .map_err(|source| {
                    let context = String::from("mock move_arm result");
                    crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
                })?;
            peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
        })
    }
}

//!Mock producer for the `camera_zero_or_more` dependency slot: plays the dependency over the real wire under the identity the harness seeds into the slot's bound set. One messaging session per mock, so [`Mock::stop`] is a whole-producer loss.
/// This node's consumer-slot link_id.
pub const LINK_ID: &str = "camera_zero_or_more";
/// Core-node segment of the mock's wire identity.
pub const MOCK_CORE_NODE: &str = "mock-core";
/// Default instance segment of the mock's wire identity.
pub const MOCK_INSTANCE_ID: &str = "mock-camera_zero_or_more";
/// The default mock's wire identity, as the harness seeds it.
pub fn producer_ref() -> peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef {
    producer_ref_for(MOCK_INSTANCE_ID)
}
/// [`producer_ref`] under an explicit instance id (multi-instance slots).
pub fn producer_ref_for(instance_id: &str) -> peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef {
    peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef::new(MOCK_CORE_NODE, instance_id)
}
/// One mocked producer instance for this slot. Fields are the typed
/// per-interface surfaces; construct with [`Mock::start`].
pub struct Mock {
    _session: peppylib::MessengerHandle,
    pub video_stream: video_stream::Publisher,
    pub enable_camera: enable_camera::Service,
}
impl Mock {
    /// Connects a dedicated session and declares every interface of
    /// this dependency now (publishers, queryables, action engines),
    /// under the default [`MOCK_INSTANCE_ID`].
    pub async fn start(
        router: &peppylib::testing::EphemeralRouter,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        Self::start_as(router, MOCK_INSTANCE_ID).await
    }
    /// [`Mock::start`] under an explicit instance id, for slots bound
    /// to several mock instances.
    pub async fn start_as(
        router: &peppylib::testing::EphemeralRouter,
        instance_id: &str,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let session = router.connect().await?;
        let video_stream = video_stream::Publisher::declare(&session, instance_id)
            .await?;
        let enable_camera = enable_camera::Service::listen(&session, instance_id).await?;
        Ok(Self {
            _session: session,
            video_stream,
            enable_camera,
        })
    }
    /// Simulates this producer dying mid-flight, deterministically:
    /// live action goals are disarmed (their eventual drop emits no
    /// clean close), then every declaration and the session drop,
    /// releasing the liveliness tokens consumers latch on.
    pub fn stop(self) {}
}
///Typed mock publisher for the consumed topic `video_stream`: publishes over the real wire as this mock's identity; the first publish waits for the node's subscription to match (no sleeps), and no subscriber within the readiness timeout is a loud error.
pub mod video_stream {
    pub use crate::consumed_topics::camera_zero_or_more::video_stream::Message;
    #[allow(unused_imports)]
    use crate::consumed_topics::camera_zero_or_more::video_stream::*;
    pub struct Publisher {
        core: peppylib::testing::TestTopicPublisher,
    }
    impl Publisher {
        pub(super) async fn declare(
            session: &peppylib::MessengerHandle,
            instance_id: &str,
        ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
            let core = peppylib::testing::TestTopicPublisher::declare(
                    session,
                    super::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                    instance_id,
                    peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("uvc_camera", "v1")?,
                    None,
                    "video_stream",
                    peppylib::config::QoSProfile::Standard,
                    peppylib::config::TopicRetention::LiveOnly,
                )
                .await?;
            Ok(Self { core })
        }
        /// Publishes one typed message; lazily waits for the node's
        /// subscription before the first delivery.
        pub async fn publish(&self, message: &Message) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.core.publish(serialize_message(message)?).await
        }
        /// Waits until the node's subscription for this topic is
        /// visible; returns whether it matched within `timeout`.
        pub async fn wait_for_subscriber(
            &self,
            timeout: std::time::Duration,
        ) -> crate::Result<bool> {
            self.core.wait_for_subscriber(timeout).await
        }
    }
    fn serialize_message(value: &Message) -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {
        Ok({
            let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
            {
                let mut root = capnp_msg
                    .init_root::<
                        crate::capnp::on_next_camera_zero_or_more_video_stream_message_capnp::camera_zero_or_more_video_stream_message::Builder,
                    >();
                let mut builder_0 = root.reborrow().init_header();
                let timestamp_1 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time((value.header).stamp);
                let mut timestamp_builder_2 = builder_0.reborrow().init_stamp();
                timestamp_builder_2.set_sec(timestamp_1.sec);
                timestamp_builder_2.set_nsec(timestamp_1.nsec);
                builder_0.set_frame_id((value.header).frame_id);
                root.set_width(value.width);
            }
            let mut buffer = Vec::new();
            capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
                .map_err(|source| {
                    let context = String::from("mock publish video_stream");
                    crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
                })?;
            peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
        })
    }
}
///Typed mock server for the consumed service `enable_camera`: a background pump captures every request; scripted responses serve automatically, unscripted requests park for `next_request`.
pub mod enable_camera {
    pub use crate::consumed_services::camera_zero_or_more::enable_camera::Request;
    pub use crate::consumed_services::camera_zero_or_more::enable_camera::ResponseData;
    pub use crate::consumed_services::camera_zero_or_more::enable_camera::{
        Failure, FailureKind,
    };
    #[allow(unused_imports)]
    use crate::consumed_services::camera_zero_or_more::enable_camera::*;
    pub struct Service {
        core: peppylib::testing::MockServiceCore,
    }
    /// Answers exactly one parked request; consumed by use.
    pub struct Responder {
        inner: peppylib::messaging::ServiceResponder,
    }
    impl Service {
        pub(super) async fn listen(
            session: &peppylib::MessengerHandle,
            instance_id: &str,
        ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
            let core = peppylib::testing::MockServiceCore::listen(
                    session,
                    super::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                    instance_id,
                    peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("uvc_camera", "v1")?,
                    "enable_camera",
                )
                .await?;
            Ok(Self { core })
        }
        /// The next unscripted request, decoded, with the responder
        /// the test must use to answer it. Errors after `timeout`.
        pub async fn next_request(
            &self,
            timeout: std::time::Duration,
        ) -> crate::Result<(Request, Responder)> {
            let (context, responder) = self.core.next_request(timeout).await?;
            let request = deserialize_request(
                context.message().payload_bytes().as_ref(),
            )?;
            Ok((request, Responder { inner: responder }))
        }
        ///Enqueues one response to be served automatically to the next inbound request (FIFO across repeated calls); only unscripted requests park for `next_request`.
        pub fn enqueue_response(&self, response: ResponseData) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.core.enqueue_response(serialize_response(&response)?);
            Ok(())
        }
        /// Every request received so far (scripted and manual alike),
        /// decoded, in arrival order.
        pub fn captured(&self) -> crate::Result<Vec<Request>> {
            self.core
                .captured()
                .iter()
                .map(|captured| {
                    deserialize_request(captured.message.payload_bytes().as_ref())
                })
                .collect()
        }
    }
    impl Responder {
        /// Sends the typed response for the parked request.
        pub async fn respond(self, response: ResponseData) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.inner.respond(serialize_response(&response)?).await
        }
        /// Answers the parked request with an error reply: a failure
        /// kind that this member declares and a message.
        pub async fn respond_failure(
            self,
            kind: FailureKind,
            message: impl Into<String>,
        ) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.inner.respond_failure(Failure::new(kind, message).to_wire()).await
        }
    }
    fn deserialize_request(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<Request> {
        #[allow(clippy::all)]
        let context = String::from("mock enable_camera request");
        let mut payload = payload;
        let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
                &mut payload,
                capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
            )
            .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("{}: {}", context, source),
            ))?;
        let root = message_reader
            .get_root::<
                crate::capnp::poll_uvc_camera_enable_camera_message_capnp::enable_camera_message::Reader,
            >()
            .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("{}: {}", context, source),
            ))?;
        let enable_0 = root.reborrow().get_enable();
        Ok(Request { enable: enable_0 })
    }
    fn serialize_response(value: &ResponseData) -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {
        Ok({
            let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
            {
                let mut root = capnp_msg
                    .init_root::<
                        crate::capnp::poll_uvc_camera_enable_camera_response_message_capnp::enable_camera_response_message::Builder,
                    >();
                root.set_enabled(value.enabled);
                if let Some(value_0) = (value.error_msg).as_ref() {
                    root.set_error_msg(value_0.as_str());
                }
            }
            let mut buffer = Vec::new();
            capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
                .map_err(|source| {
                    let context = String::from("mock enable_camera response");
                    crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
                })?;
            peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
        })
    }
}
