///Typed mock action server for the consumed action `move_arm`, on the real `ConcurrentAction` engine: identical goal lifecycle to a production producer, plus deterministic producer-loss via the owning mock's `stop()`.
pub mod move_arm {
    pub use crate::consumed_actions::left_arm::move_arm::GoalRequest;
    pub use crate::consumed_actions::left_arm::move_arm::GoalResponseData;
    pub use crate::consumed_actions::left_arm::move_arm::FeedbackMessage;
    pub use crate::consumed_actions::left_arm::move_arm::ResultResponseData;
    #[allow(unused_imports)]
    use crate::consumed_actions::left_arm::move_arm::*;
    pub use crate::consumed_actions::left_arm::move_arm::{Failure, FailureKind};
    pub struct Action {
        core: peppylib::testing::MockActionServerCore,
    }
    impl Action {
        pub(super) async fn expose(
            session: &peppylib::MessengerHandle,
            instance_id: &str,
        ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
            let core = peppylib::testing::MockActionServerCore::expose(
                    session,
                    super::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                    instance_id,
                    peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::contract("arm_control", "v1")?,
                    "move_arm",
                    true,
                )
                .await?;
            Ok(Self { core })
        }
        /// Parks until the node sends a goal, bounded by `timeout`;
        /// the returned goal awaits the test's admission decision.
        pub async fn next_goal(
            &mut self,
            timeout: std::time::Duration,
        ) -> crate::Result<PendingGoal> {
            let pending = self.core.next_goal(timeout).await?;
            let request = deserialize_goal_request(pending.request_bytes())?;
            Ok(PendingGoal { pending, request })
        }
        pub(super) fn stop(self) {
            self.core.stop();
        }
    }
    /// A goal received by the mock, awaiting accept/refuse.
    pub struct PendingGoal {
        pending: peppylib::testing::MockPendingGoal,
        /// The decoded goal request.
        pub request: GoalRequest,
    }
    impl PendingGoal {
        /// The client-generated correlation id for this goal.
        pub fn goal_id(&self) -> &str {
            self.pending.goal_id()
        }
        /// Accepts the goal; the returned handle drives feedback and
        /// completion.
        pub async fn accept(
            self,
            response: GoalResponseData,
        ) -> crate::Result<ActiveGoal> {
            let context = self
                .pending
                .accept(serialize_goal_response(&response)?)
                .await?;
            Ok(ActiveGoal { context })
        }
        /// Refuses the goal with a failure kind that this member
        /// declares and a message. A refusal carries no goal
        /// response.
        pub async fn refuse(
            self,
            kind: FailureKind,
            message: impl Into<String>,
        ) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.pending.refuse(Failure::new(kind, message).to_wire()).await
        }
    }
    /// An accepted goal: drives feedback and terminal completion.
    pub struct ActiveGoal {
        context: std::sync::Arc<peppylib::messaging::GoalContext>,
    }
    impl ActiveGoal {
        /// Publishes one typed feedback message for this goal.
        pub async fn publish_feedback(
            &self,
            feedback: &FeedbackMessage,
        ) -> crate::Result<()> {
            let payload = serialize_feedback(feedback)?;
            let payload = peppylib::messaging::NonEmptyPayload::try_new(payload)
                .map_err(|_| peppylib::PeppyError::Io(
                    std::io::Error::other(
                        "feedback serialized to zero bytes; empty is reserved for \
                         end-of-stream",
                    ),
                ))?;
            self.context.publish_feedback(payload).await?;
            Ok(())
        }
        /// Resolves when this goal receives its cancel signal: a
        /// cancel request of the node, or the end of the node.
        pub async fn cancel_signal(&self) {
            self.context.cancel_signal().await
        }
        /// Whether this goal received its cancel signal.
        pub fn is_cancelled(&self) -> bool {
            self.context.is_cancelled()
        }
        /// Why this goal received its cancel signal; `None` before
        /// it did. `CancelCause::reason` gives a reason for
        /// `complete_cancelled`.
        pub fn cancel_cause(&self) -> Option<peppylib::messaging::CancelCause> {
            self.context.cancel_cause()
        }
        /// Ends the goal with the status completed: a success.
        pub async fn complete(&self, result: &ResultResponseData) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.context.complete(serialize_result(result)?).await?;
            Ok(())
        }
        /// Ends the goal with the status failed: a failure kind
        /// that this member declares, a message, and no result.
        pub async fn fail(
            &self,
            kind: FailureKind,
            message: impl Into<String>,
        ) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.context.fail(Failure::new(kind, message).to_wire()).await?;
            Ok(())
        }
        /// Ends the goal with the status cancelled, for `reason`.
        pub async fn complete_cancelled(
            &self,
            reason: impl Into<String>,
            result: &ResultResponseData,
        ) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.context.complete_cancelled(reason, serialize_result(result)?).await?;
            Ok(())
        }
    }
    fn deserialize_goal_request(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<GoalRequest> {
        #[allow(clippy::all)]
        let context = String::from("mock move_arm goal request");
        let mut payload = payload;
        let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
                &mut payload,
                capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
            )
            .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("{}: {}", context, source),
            ))?;
        let root = message_reader
            .get_root::<
                crate::capnp::arm_control_move_arm_fire_goal_message_capnp::arm_control_move_arm_action_goal_message_message::Reader,
            >()
            .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("{}: {}", context, source),
            ))?;
        let arm_id_0 = root.reborrow().get_arm_id();
        let list_1 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_desired_position()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("mock move_arm goal request");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "desired_position", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        if list_1.len() as usize != 3 {
            let actual = list_1.len() as usize;
            return Err(
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!(
                        "invalid fixed list length for field '{}': expected {}, got {}",
                        "desired_position", 3, actual
                    ),
                ),
            );
        }
        let mut desired_position_2: [i32; 3] = [i32::default(); 3];
        for (idx, element) in list_1.iter().enumerate() {
            desired_position_2[idx] = element;
        }
        Ok(GoalRequest {
            arm_id: arm_id_0,
            desired_position: desired_position_2,
        })
    }
    fn serialize_goal_response(
        value: &GoalResponseData,
    ) -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {
        Ok({
            let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
            {
                let mut root = capnp_msg
                    .init_root::<
                        crate::capnp::arm_control_move_arm_fire_goal_response_message_capnp::goal_response_message_message::Builder,
                    >();
                root.set_accepted(value.accepted);
            }
            let mut buffer = Vec::new();
            capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
                .map_err(|source| {
                    let context = String::from("mock move_arm goal response");
                    crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
                })?;
            peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
        })
    }
    fn serialize_feedback(value: &FeedbackMessage) -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {
        Ok({
            let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
            {
                let mut root = capnp_msg
                    .init_root::<
                        crate::capnp::arm_control_move_arm_feedback_message_capnp::arm_control_move_arm_action_feedback_message::Builder,
                    >();
                let mut list_0 = root.reborrow().init_new_position(3);
                for (idx_1, value_2) in (value.new_position).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_0.set(idx_1 as u32, *value_2);
                }
            }
            let mut buffer = Vec::new();
            capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
                .map_err(|source| {
                    let context = String::from("mock move_arm feedback");
                    crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
                })?;
            peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
        })
    }
    fn serialize_result(value: &ResultResponseData) -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {
        Ok({
            let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
            {
                let mut root = capnp_msg
                    .init_root::<
                        crate::capnp::arm_control_move_arm_get_result_response_message_capnp::result_response_message_message::Builder,
                    >();
                root.set_success(value.success);
                if let Some(value_0) = (value.error_msg).as_ref() {
                    root.set_error_msg(value_0.as_str());
                }
            }
            let mut buffer = Vec::new();
            capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
                .map_err(|source| {
                    let context = String::from("mock move_arm result");
                    crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
                })?;
            peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
        })
    }
}


================================================================
Mock :: pairings/controller_one
================================================================
//!Mock peer for the `controller_one` pairing slot: publishes the topics the node consumes to the node's slot and holds pinned subscriptions to the topics the node emits, under the pin identity the harness seeds (`peer_info()`); a multi slot starts several instances with distinct instance ids.
/// This node's own pairing-slot link_id.
pub const LINK_ID: &str = "controller_one";
pub const PAIRING_NAME: &str = "arm_link";
pub const PAIRING_TAG: &str = "v1";
/// The mock peer's own (complementary) slot id: what the node's
/// `paired()` reports as `peer_link_id` under the harness.
pub const PEER_LINK_ID: &str = "mock_peer";
/// Core-node segment of the mock's wire identity.
pub const MOCK_CORE_NODE: &str = "mock-core";
/// Default instance segment of the mock's wire identity.
pub const MOCK_INSTANCE_ID: &str = "mock-controller_one";
/// The default mock peer's wire identity.
pub fn producer_ref() -> peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef {
    peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef::new(MOCK_CORE_NODE, MOCK_INSTANCE_ID)
}
/// The full pin identity the harness seeds for this slot: what the
/// node's `paired()` / `wait_paired()` resolve to.
pub fn peer_info() -> peppylib::messaging::PeerInfo {
    peer_info_for(MOCK_INSTANCE_ID)
}
/// [`peer_info`] under an explicit instance id (multi slots): the
/// `info` of the member the node's `peers()` lists for it.
pub fn peer_info_for(instance_id: &str) -> peppylib::messaging::PeerInfo {
    peppylib::messaging::PeerInfo {
        producer: peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef::new(MOCK_CORE_NODE, instance_id),
        peer_link_id: PEER_LINK_ID.to_string(),
    }
}
/// One mock peer instance for this slot; construct with
/// [`Mock::start`] / [`Mock::start_as`].
pub struct Mock {
    _session: peppylib::MessengerHandle,
    pub joint_states: joint_states::Subscription,
}
impl Mock {
    /// Connects a dedicated session, declares the peer publishers and
    /// opens the pinned subscriptions to the node's emissions, under
    /// the default [`MOCK_INSTANCE_ID`]. `node_instance_id` is the
    /// node-under-test's instance id (the harness passes its own).
    pub async fn start(
        router: &peppylib::testing::EphemeralRouter,
        node_instance_id: &str,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        Self::start_as(router, MOCK_INSTANCE_ID, node_instance_id).await
    }
    /// [`Mock::start`] under an explicit instance id, for pairing
    /// several mock peers into one multi slot.
    pub async fn start_as(
        router: &peppylib::testing::EphemeralRouter,
        instance_id: &str,
        node_instance_id: &str,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let session = router.connect().await?;
        let node = peppylib::messaging::PeerInfo {
            producer: peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef::new(
                peppylib::testing::STANDALONE_CORE_NODE,
                node_instance_id,
            ),
            peer_link_id: LINK_ID.to_string(),
        };
        let joint_states = joint_states::Subscription::open(&session, instance_id, &node)
            .await?;
        Ok(Self {
            _session: session,
            joint_states,
        })
    }
    /// Simulates the peer disappearing: every declaration and the
    /// session drop.
    pub fn stop(self) {}
}
///Typed subscription to `joint_states` (the node emits this direction): pinned to the node's identity and slot id exactly as a real paired peer would be.
pub mod joint_states {
    /// A held subscription to the node's emissions on this slot. It
    /// keeps the newest messages the test has not read, up to the
    /// queue size of the topic's QoS tier: when the queue is full, a
    /// new message drops the oldest unread one. A test can leave it
    /// unread, and its unread messages never hold up the other
    /// messages to the mock's session.
    pub struct Subscription {
        inner: peppylib::messaging::Subscription,
    }
    impl Subscription {
        pub(super) async fn open(
            session: &peppylib::MessengerHandle,
            instance_id: &str,
            node: &peppylib::messaging::PeerInfo,
        ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
            let inner = peppylib::testing::subscribe_peer_pinned(
                    session,
                    super::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                    instance_id,
                    super::PEER_LINK_ID,
                    peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::pairing("arm_link", "v1")?,
                    &node.producer,
                    super::LINK_ID,
                    "joint_states",
                    peppylib::config::QoSProfile::SensorData,
                )
                .await?;
            Ok(Self { inner })
        }
        /// Awaits the node's next message on this topic; `Ok(None)`
        /// once the mock's session closes.
        #[allow(clippy::should_implement_trait)]
        pub async fn next(&mut self) -> crate::Result<Option<Message>> {
            let Some(message) = self.inner.on_next_message().await else {
                return Ok(None);
            };
            let message = deserialize_message(message.payload_bytes().as_ref())?;
            Ok(Some(message))
        }
    }
    #[derive(Debug, Clone)]
    #[allow(dead_code)]
    pub struct Message {
        pub positions: [f64; 3],
        pub timestamp: std::time::SystemTime,
    }
    fn deserialize_message(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<Message> {
        #[allow(clippy::all)]
        let context = String::from("mock peer receive joint_states");
        let mut payload = payload;
        let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
                &mut payload,
                capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
            )
            .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("{}: {}", context, source),
            ))?;
        let root = message_reader
            .get_root::<
                crate::capnp::peer_controller_one_joint_states_message_capnp::peer_controller_one_joint_states_message::Reader,
            >()
            .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("{}: {}", context, source),
            ))?;
        let list_0 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_positions()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("mock peer receive joint_states");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "positions", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        if list_0.len() as usize != 3 {
            let actual = list_0.len() as usize;
            return Err(
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!(
                        "invalid fixed list length for field '{}': expected {}, got {}",
                        "positions", 3, actual
                    ),
                ),
            );
        }
        let mut positions_1: [f64; 3] = [f64::default(); 3];
        for (idx, element) in list_0.iter().enumerate() {
            positions_1[idx] = element;
        }
        let timestamp_3 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_timestamp()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("mock peer receive joint_states");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "timestamp", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let capnp_timestamp_4 = peppylib::encoding::CapnpTimestamp {
            sec: timestamp_3.get_sec(),
            nsec: timestamp_3.get_nsec(),
        };
        let timestamp_2 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time_from_capnp(capnp_timestamp_4);
        Ok(Message {
            positions: positions_1,
            timestamp: timestamp_2,
        })
    }
}

Mock :: pairings/controller_one_or_more

//!Mock peer for the `controller_one_or_more` pairing slot: publishes the topics the node consumes to the node's slot and holds pinned subscriptions to the topics the node emits, under the pin identity the harness seeds (`peer_info()`); a multi slot starts several instances with distinct instance ids.
/// This node's own pairing-slot link_id.

/// `paired()` reports as `peer_link_id` under the harness.

/// The full pin identity the harness seeds for this slot: what the
/// node's `paired()` / `wait_paired()` resolve to.
pub fn peer_info() -> peppylib::messaging::PeerInfo {
    peer_info_for(MOCK_INSTANCE_ID)
}
/// [`peer_info`] under an explicit instance id (multi slots): the
/// `info` of the member the node's `peers()` lists for it.
pub fn peer_info_for(instance_id: &str) -> peppylib::messaging::PeerInfo {
    peppylib::messaging::PeerInfo {
        producer: peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef::new(MOCK_CORE_NODE, instance_id),
        peer_link_id: PEER_LINK_ID.to_string(),
    }
}
/// One mock peer instance for this slot; construct with
/// [`Mock::start`] / [`Mock::start_as`].
pub struct Mock {
    _session: peppylib::MessengerHandle,
    pub joint_states: joint_states::Subscription,
}
impl Mock {
    /// Connects a dedicated session, declares the peer publishers and
    /// opens the pinned subscriptions to the node's emissions, under
    /// the default [`MOCK_INSTANCE_ID`]. `node_instance_id` is the
    /// node-under-test's instance id (the harness passes its own).
    pub async fn start(
        router: &peppylib::testing::EphemeralRouter,
        node_instance_id: &str,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        Self::start_as(router, MOCK_INSTANCE_ID, node_instance_id).await
    }
    /// [`Mock::start`] under an explicit instance id, for pairing
    /// several mock peers into one multi slot.
    pub async fn start_as(
        router: &peppylib::testing::EphemeralRouter,
        instance_id: &str,
        node_instance_id: &str,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let session = router.connect().await?;
        let node = peppylib::messaging::PeerInfo {
            producer: peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef::new(
                peppylib::testing::STANDALONE_CORE_NODE,
                node_instance_id,
            ),
            peer_link_id: LINK_ID.to_string(),
        };
        let joint_states = joint_states::Subscription::open(&session, instance_id, &node)
            .await?;
        Ok(Self {
            _session: session,
            joint_states,
        })
    }
    /// Simulates the peer disappearing: every declaration and the
    /// session drop.
    pub fn stop(self) {}
}
///Typed subscription to `joint_states` (the node emits this direction): pinned to the node's identity and slot id exactly as a real paired peer would be.
pub mod joint_states {
    /// A held subscription to the node's emissions on this slot. It
    /// keeps the newest messages the test has not read, up to the
    /// queue size of the topic's QoS tier: when the queue is full, a
    /// new message drops the oldest unread one. A test can leave it
    /// unread, and its unread messages never hold up the other
    /// messages to the mock's session.
    pub struct Subscription {
        inner: peppylib::messaging::Subscription,
    }
    impl Subscription {
        pub(super) async fn open(
            session: &peppylib::MessengerHandle,
            instance_id: &str,
            node: &peppylib::messaging::PeerInfo,
        ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
            let inner = peppylib::testing::subscribe_peer_pinned(
                    session,
                    super::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                    instance_id,
                    super::PEER_LINK_ID,
                    peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::pairing("arm_link", "v1")?,
                    &node.producer,
                    super::LINK_ID,
                    "joint_states",
                    peppylib::config::QoSProfile::SensorData,
                )
                .await?;
            Ok(Self { inner })
        }
        /// Awaits the node's next message on this topic; `Ok(None)`
        /// once the mock's session closes.
        #[allow(clippy::should_implement_trait)]
        pub async fn next(&mut self) -> crate::Result<Option<Message>> {
            let Some(message) = self.inner.on_next_message().await else {
                return Ok(None);
            };
            let message = deserialize_message(message.payload_bytes().as_ref())?;
            Ok(Some(message))
        }
    }
    #[derive(Debug, Clone)]
    #[allow(dead_code)]
    pub struct Message {
        pub positions: [f64; 3],
        pub timestamp: std::time::SystemTime,
    }
    fn deserialize_message(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<Message> {
        #[allow(clippy::all)]
        let context = String::from("mock peer receive joint_states");
        let mut payload = payload;
        let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
                &mut payload,
                capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
            )
            .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("{}: {}", context, source),
            ))?;
        let root = message_reader
            .get_root::<
                crate::capnp::peer_controller_one_or_more_joint_states_message_capnp::peer_controller_one_or_more_joint_states_message::Reader,
            >()
            .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("{}: {}", context, source),
            ))?;
        let list_0 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_positions()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("mock peer receive joint_states");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "positions", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        if list_0.len() as usize != 3 {
            let actual = list_0.len() as usize;
            return Err(
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!(
                        "invalid fixed list length for field '{}': expected {}, got {}",
                        "positions", 3, actual
                    ),
                ),
            );
        }
        let mut positions_1: [f64; 3] = [f64::default(); 3];
        for (idx, element) in list_0.iter().enumerate() {
            positions_1[idx] = element;
        }
        let timestamp_3 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_timestamp()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("mock peer receive joint_states");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "timestamp", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let capnp_timestamp_4 = peppylib::encoding::CapnpTimestamp {
            sec: timestamp_3.get_sec(),
            nsec: timestamp_3.get_nsec(),
        };
        let timestamp_2 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time_from_capnp(capnp_timestamp_4);
        Ok(Message {
            positions: positions_1,
            timestamp: timestamp_2,
        })
    }
}

    /// opens the pinned subscriptions to the node's emissions, under
    /// the default [`MOCK_INSTANCE_ID`]. `node_instance_id` is the
    /// node-under-test's instance id (the harness passes its own).
    pub async fn start(
        router: &peppylib::testing::EphemeralRouter,
        node_instance_id: &str,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        Self::start_as(router, MOCK_INSTANCE_ID, node_instance_id).await
    }
    /// [`Mock::start`] under an explicit instance id, for pairing
    /// several mock peers into one multi slot.
    pub async fn start_as(
        router: &peppylib::testing::EphemeralRouter,
        instance_id: &str,
        node_instance_id: &str,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {

        let node = peppylib::messaging::PeerInfo {
            producer: peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef::new(
                peppylib::testing::STANDALONE_CORE_NODE,
                node_instance_id,
            ),

        };

            .await?;

    /// session drop.

///Typed peer publisher for `joint_states` (the node consumes this direction): publishes from the mock peer's slot to the node's slot, so the node's pinned subscription receives it.
pub mod joint_states {
    pub use crate::paired_topics::sensor_bus::joint_states::Message;
    #[allow(unused_imports)]
    use crate::paired_topics::sensor_bus::joint_states::*;
    pub struct Publisher {
        core: peppylib::testing::TestTopicPublisher,
    }
    impl Publisher {
        pub(super) async fn declare(
            session: &peppylib::MessengerHandle,
            instance_id: &str,
            node: &peppylib::messaging::PeerInfo,
        ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
            let core = peppylib::testing::TestTopicPublisher::declare_to_peer(
                    session,
                    super::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                    instance_id,
                    peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::pairing("arm_link", "v1")?,
                    super::PEER_LINK_ID,
                    "joint_states",
                    peppylib::config::QoSProfile::SensorData,
                    node.clone(),
                )
                .await?;
            Ok(Self { core })
        }
        /// Publishes one typed message; lazily waits for the node's
        /// pinned subscription before the first delivery.
        pub async fn publish(&self, message: &Message) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.core.publish(serialize_message(message)?).await
        }
        /// Waits until the node's pinned subscription is visible;
        /// returns whether it matched within `timeout`.
        pub async fn wait_for_subscriber(
            &self,
            timeout: std::time::Duration,
        ) -> crate::Result<bool> {
            self.core.wait_for_subscriber(timeout).await
        }
    }
    fn serialize_message(value: &Message) -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {
        Ok({
            let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
            {
                let mut root = capnp_msg
                    .init_root::<
                        crate::capnp::peer_sensor_bus_joint_states_message_capnp::peer_sensor_bus_joint_states_message::Builder,
                    >();
                let mut list_0 = root.reborrow().init_positions(3);
                for (idx_1, value_2) in (value.positions).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_0.set(idx_1 as u32, *value_2);
                }
                let timestamp_3 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time(value.timestamp);
                let mut timestamp_builder_4 = root.reborrow().init_timestamp();
                timestamp_builder_4.set_sec(timestamp_3.sec);
                timestamp_builder_4.set_nsec(timestamp_3.nsec);
            }
            let mut buffer = Vec::new();
            capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
                .map_err(|source| {
                    let context = String::from("mock peer publish joint_states");
                    crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
                })?;
            peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
        })
    }
}

Mock :: observed/watchers_one

//!Mock observed source for the `watchers_one` observer slot: a publish-only pairing member under the source identity the harness seeds (`source()`); multi-member slots start several instances with distinct instance ids.
/// This node's own observer-slot link_id.
pub const LINK_ID: &str = "watchers_one";
pub const PAIRING_NAME: &str = "arm_link";
pub const PAIRING_TAG: &str = "v1";
/// The producer-side link_id mock sources publish under.
pub const SOURCE_LINK_ID: &str = "mock_source";
/// The slot of the peer each mock source publishes to: a pairing
/// emission names its peer, and the node observes every pair of the
/// source's slot.
pub const PEER_LINK_ID: &str = "mock_peer";
/// Core-node segment of the mock's wire identity.
pub const MOCK_CORE_NODE: &str = "mock-core";
/// Default instance segment of the mock's wire identity.
pub const MOCK_INSTANCE_ID: &str = "mock-watchers_one";

pub fn source() -> peppylib::messaging::ObservedSource {
    source_for(MOCK_INSTANCE_ID)
}
/// The peer a mock source under `instance_id` publishes to.
pub fn peer_of(instance_id: &str) -> peppylib::messaging::PeerInfo {
    peppylib::messaging::PeerInfo {
        producer: peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef::new(
            MOCK_CORE_NODE,
            format!("{instance_id}-peer"),
        ),
        peer_link_id: PEER_LINK_ID.to_string(),
    }
}
/// [`source`] under an explicit instance id (multi-member slots).
pub fn source_for(instance_id: &str) -> peppylib::messaging::ObservedSource {
    peppylib::messaging::ObservedSource {
        producer: peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef::new(MOCK_CORE_NODE, instance_id),
        source_link_id: SOURCE_LINK_ID.to_string(),
        peer: None,
    }
}
/// One mock source instance for this slot; construct with
/// [`Mock::start`] / [`Mock::start_as`].
pub struct Mock {
    _session: peppylib::MessengerHandle,
    pub joint_states: joint_states::Publisher,
}
impl Mock {
    /// Connects a dedicated session and declares this source's
    /// publishers under the default [`MOCK_INSTANCE_ID`].
    pub async fn start(
        router: &peppylib::testing::EphemeralRouter,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        Self::start_as(router, MOCK_INSTANCE_ID).await
    }
    /// [`Mock::start`] under an explicit instance id, for observing
    /// several mock sources at once.
    pub async fn start_as(
        router: &peppylib::testing::EphemeralRouter,
        instance_id: &str,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let session = router.connect().await?;
        let joint_states = joint_states::Publisher::declare(&session, instance_id)
            .await?;
        Ok(Self {
            _session: session,
            joint_states,
        })
    }
    /// Simulates this source disappearing: every declaration and the
    /// session drop. (Standalone observation liveness never changes,
    /// so the node keeps observing; messages simply stop.)
    pub fn stop(self) {}
}
///Typed source publisher for the observed topic `joint_states`: publishes from the mock source's slot to its own peer, so the node's generation-checked observation subscription receives it.
pub mod joint_states {
    pub use crate::paired_topics::watchers_one::joint_states::Message;
    #[allow(unused_imports)]
    use crate::paired_topics::watchers_one::joint_states::*;
    pub struct Publisher {
        core: peppylib::testing::TestTopicPublisher,
    }
    impl Publisher {
        pub(super) async fn declare(
            session: &peppylib::MessengerHandle,
            instance_id: &str,
        ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
            let core = peppylib::testing::TestTopicPublisher::declare_to_peer(
                    session,
                    super::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                    instance_id,
                    peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::pairing("arm_link", "v1")?,
                    super::SOURCE_LINK_ID,
                    "joint_states",
                    peppylib::config::QoSProfile::SensorData,
                    super::peer_of(instance_id),
                )
                .await?;
            Ok(Self { core })
        }
        /// Publishes one typed message; lazily waits for the node's
        /// observation subscription before the first delivery.
        pub async fn publish(&self, message: &Message) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.core.publish(serialize_message(message)?).await
        }
        /// Waits until the node's observation subscription is
        /// visible; returns whether it matched within `timeout`.
        pub async fn wait_for_subscriber(
            &self,
            timeout: std::time::Duration,
        ) -> crate::Result<bool> {
            self.core.wait_for_subscriber(timeout).await
        }
    }
    fn serialize_message(value: &Message) -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {
        Ok({
            let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
            {
                let mut root = capnp_msg
                    .init_root::<
                        crate::capnp::peer_watchers_one_joint_states_message_capnp::peer_watchers_one_joint_states_message::Builder,
                    >();
                let mut list_0 = root.reborrow().init_positions(3);
                for (idx_1, value_2) in (value.positions).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_0.set(idx_1 as u32, *value_2);
                }
                let timestamp_3 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time(value.timestamp);
                let mut timestamp_builder_4 = root.reborrow().init_timestamp();
                timestamp_builder_4.set_sec(timestamp_3.sec);
                timestamp_builder_4.set_nsec(timestamp_3.nsec);
            }
            let mut buffer = Vec::new();
            capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
                .map_err(|source| {
                    let context = String::from("mock observed publish joint_states");
                    crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
                })?;
            peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
        })
    }
}

Mock :: observed/watchers_zero_or_one
================================================================
//!Mock observed source for the `watchers_zero_or_one` observer slot: a publish-only pairing member under the source identity the harness seeds (`source()`); multi-member slots start several instances with distinct instance ids.
/// This node's own observer-slot link_id.
pub const LINK_ID: &str = "watchers_zero_or_one";
pub const PAIRING_NAME: &str = "arm_link";
pub const PAIRING_TAG: &str = "v1";
/// The producer-side link_id mock sources publish under.
pub const SOURCE_LINK_ID: &str = "mock_source";
/// The slot of the peer each mock source publishes to: a pairing
/// emission names its peer, and the node observes every pair of the
/// source's slot.
pub const PEER_LINK_ID: &str = "mock_peer";
/// Core-node segment of the mock's wire identity.
pub const MOCK_CORE_NODE: &str = "mock-core";
/// Default instance segment of the mock's wire identity.
pub const MOCK_INSTANCE_ID: &str = "mock-watchers_zero_or_one";
/// The default mock source, as the harness seeds it.
pub fn source() -> peppylib::messaging::ObservedSource {
    source_for(MOCK_INSTANCE_ID)
}
/// The peer a mock source under `instance_id` publishes to.
pub fn peer_of(instance_id: &str) -> peppylib::messaging::PeerInfo {
    peppylib::messaging::PeerInfo {
        producer: peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef::new(
            MOCK_CORE_NODE,
            format!("{instance_id}-peer"),
        ),
        peer_link_id: PEER_LINK_ID.to_string(),
    }
}
/// [`source`] under an explicit instance id (multi-member slots).
pub fn source_for(instance_id: &str) -> peppylib::messaging::ObservedSource {
    peppylib::messaging::ObservedSource {
        producer: peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef::new(MOCK_CORE_NODE, instance_id),
        source_link_id: SOURCE_LINK_ID.to_string(),
        peer: None,
    }
}
/// One mock source instance for this slot; construct with
/// [`Mock::start`] / [`Mock::start_as`].
pub struct Mock {
    _session: peppylib::MessengerHandle,
    pub joint_states: joint_states::Publisher,
}
impl Mock {
    /// Connects a dedicated session and declares this source's
    /// publishers under the default [`MOCK_INSTANCE_ID`].
    pub async fn start(
        router: &peppylib::testing::EphemeralRouter,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        Self::start_as(router, MOCK_INSTANCE_ID).await
    }
    /// [`Mock::start`] under an explicit instance id, for observing
    /// several mock sources at once.
    pub async fn start_as(
        router: &peppylib::testing::EphemeralRouter,
        instance_id: &str,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let session = router.connect().await?;
        let joint_states = joint_states::Publisher::declare(&session, instance_id)
            .await?;
        Ok(Self {
            _session: session,
            joint_states,
        })
    }
    /// Simulates this source disappearing: every declaration and the
    /// session drop. (Standalone observation liveness never changes,
    /// so the node keeps observing; messages simply stop.)
    pub fn stop(self) {}
}
///Typed source publisher for the observed topic `joint_states`: publishes from the mock source's slot to its own peer, so the node's generation-checked observation subscription receives it.
pub mod joint_states {
    pub use crate::paired_topics::watchers_zero_or_one::joint_states::Message;
    #[allow(unused_imports)]
    use crate::paired_topics::watchers_zero_or_one::joint_states::*;
    pub struct Publisher {
        core: peppylib::testing::TestTopicPublisher,
    }
    impl Publisher {
        pub(super) async fn declare(
            session: &peppylib::MessengerHandle,
            instance_id: &str,
        ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
            let core = peppylib::testing::TestTopicPublisher::declare_to_peer(
                    session,
                    super::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                    instance_id,
                    peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::pairing("arm_link", "v1")?,
                    super::SOURCE_LINK_ID,
                    "joint_states",
                    peppylib::config::QoSProfile::SensorData,
                    super::peer_of(instance_id),
                )
                .await?;
            Ok(Self { core })
        }
        /// Publishes one typed message; lazily waits for the node's
        /// observation subscription before the first delivery.
        pub async fn publish(&self, message: &Message) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.core.publish(serialize_message(message)?).await
        }
        /// Waits until the node's observation subscription is
        /// visible; returns whether it matched within `timeout`.
        pub async fn wait_for_subscriber(
            &self,
            timeout: std::time::Duration,
        ) -> crate::Result<bool> {
            self.core.wait_for_subscriber(timeout).await
        }
    }
    fn serialize_message(value: &Message) -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {
        Ok({
            let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
            {
                let mut root = capnp_msg
                    .init_root::<
                        crate::capnp::peer_watchers_zero_or_one_joint_states_message_capnp::peer_watchers_zero_or_one_joint_states_message::Builder,
                    >();
                let mut list_0 = root.reborrow().init_positions(3);
                for (idx_1, value_2) in (value.positions).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_0.set(idx_1 as u32, *value_2);
                }
                let timestamp_3 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time(value.timestamp);
                let mut timestamp_builder_4 = root.reborrow().init_timestamp();
                timestamp_builder_4.set_sec(timestamp_3.sec);
                timestamp_builder_4.set_nsec(timestamp_3.nsec);
            }
            let mut buffer = Vec::new();
            capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
                .map_err(|source| {
                    let context = String::from("mock observed publish joint_states");
                    crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
                })?;
            peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
        })
    }
}

================================================================
Fixture :: emitted_topics/video_stream
================================================================
//!Typed observation of the node's emitted topic `video_stream`: the harness subscribes before the node boots (pinned to the node's identity) and barriers on the subscription, so the node's very first publish is captured.
/// A held subscription to the node's emissions on this topic. It
/// keeps the newest messages the test has not read, up to the queue
/// size of the topic's QoS tier, or its retention depth when that is
/// larger: when the queue is full, a new message drops the oldest
/// unread one. A test can leave it unread, and its unread messages
/// never hold up the other messages to the harness's session.
pub struct Subscription {
    inner: peppylib::messaging::Subscription,
}
pub(crate) async fn subscribe(
    session: &peppylib::MessengerHandle,
    node_instance_id: &str,
) -> crate::Result<Subscription> {
    let producer = peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef::new(
        peppylib::testing::STANDALONE_CORE_NODE,
        node_instance_id,
    );
    let inner = peppylib::TopicMessenger::subscribe(
            session,
            "fixture-core",
            "fixture-observer",
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("relay_node", "v1")?,
            "video_stream",
            &producer,
            peppylib::config::QoSProfile::SensorData,
            peppylib::config::TopicRetention::LiveOnly,
            peppylib::messaging::TopicBuffer::KeepLatest,
        )
        .await?;
    Ok(Subscription { inner })
}
impl Subscription {
    /// Awaits the node's next message on this topic; `Ok(None)` once
    /// the fixture session closes.
    #[allow(clippy::should_implement_trait)]
    pub async fn next(&mut self) -> crate::Result<Option<Message>> {
        let Some(message) = self.inner.on_next_message().await else {
            return Ok(None);
        };
        let message = deserialize_message(message.payload_bytes().as_ref())?;
        Ok(Some(message))
    }
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct MessageHeader {
    pub stamp: std::time::SystemTime,
    pub frame_id: u32,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Message {
    pub header: MessageHeader,
    pub encoding: String,
    pub width: u32,
    pub frame: Vec<u8>,
}
fn deserialize_message(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<Message> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]
    let context = String::from("fixtures observe video_stream");
    let mut payload = payload;
    let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader
        .get_root::<
            crate::capnp::video_stream_message_capnp::video_stream_message::Reader,
        >()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let reader_0 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_header()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures observe video_stream");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "header", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let timestamp_2 = reader_0
        .reborrow()
        .get_stamp()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures observe video_stream");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "stamp", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let capnp_timestamp_3 = peppylib::encoding::CapnpTimestamp {
        sec: timestamp_2.get_sec(),
        nsec: timestamp_2.get_nsec(),
    };
    let stamp_1 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time_from_capnp(capnp_timestamp_3);
    let frame_id_4 = reader_0.reborrow().get_frame_id();
    let header_5 = MessageHeader {
        stamp: stamp_1,
        frame_id: frame_id_4,
    };
    let text_7 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_encoding()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures observe video_stream");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "encoding", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let encoding_6 = text_7
        .to_str()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures observe video_stream");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "encoding", context, source),
            )
        })?
        .to_owned();
    let width_8 = root.reborrow().get_width();
    let data_9 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_frame()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures observe video_stream");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "frame", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let frame_10 = data_9.to_vec();
    Ok(Message {
        header: header_5,
        encoding: encoding_6,
        width: width_8,
        frame: frame_10,
    })
}

Fixture :: emitted_topics/feed/video_stream
================================================================
//!Typed observation of the node's emitted topic `video_stream`: the harness subscribes before the node boots (pinned to the node's identity) and barriers on the subscription, so the node's very first publish is captured.

            peppylib::config::QoSProfile::SensorData,
            peppylib::config::TopicRetention::LiveOnly,
            peppylib::messaging::TopicBuffer::KeepLatest,
        )
        .await?;
    Ok(Subscription { inner })
}
impl Subscription {
    /// Awaits the node's next message on this topic; `Ok(None)` once
    /// the fixture session closes.
    #[allow(clippy::should_implement_trait)]
    pub async fn next(&mut self) -> crate::Result<Option<Message>> {
        let Some(message) = self.inner.on_next_message().await else {
            return Ok(None);
        };
        let message = deserialize_message(message.payload_bytes().as_ref())?;
        Ok(Some(message))
    }
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct MessageHeader {
    pub stamp: std::time::SystemTime,
    pub frame_id: u32,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Message {
    pub header: MessageHeader,
    pub encoding: String,
    pub width: u32,
    pub frame: Vec<u8>,
}
fn deserialize_message(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<Message> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]
    let context = String::from("fixtures observe video_stream");
    let mut payload = payload;
    let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader
        .get_root::<
            crate::capnp::camera_feed_v1_video_stream_message_capnp::video_stream_message::Reader,
        >()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let reader_0 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_header()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures observe video_stream");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "header", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let timestamp_2 = reader_0
        .reborrow()
        .get_stamp()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures observe video_stream");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "stamp", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let capnp_timestamp_3 = peppylib::encoding::CapnpTimestamp {
        sec: timestamp_2.get_sec(),
        nsec: timestamp_2.get_nsec(),
    };
    let stamp_1 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time_from_capnp(capnp_timestamp_3);
    let frame_id_4 = reader_0.reborrow().get_frame_id();
    let header_5 = MessageHeader {
        stamp: stamp_1,
        frame_id: frame_id_4,
    };
    let text_7 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_encoding()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures observe video_stream");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "encoding", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let encoding_6 = text_7
        .to_str()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures observe video_stream");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "encoding", context, source),
            )
        })?
        .to_owned();
    let width_8 = root.reborrow().get_width();
    let data_9 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_frame()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures observe video_stream");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "frame", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let frame_10 = data_9.to_vec();
    Ok(Message {
        header: header_5,
        encoding: encoding_6,
        width: width_8,
        frame: frame_10,
    })
}


================================================================
Fixture :: emitted_topics/heartbeat
================================================================
//!Typed observation of the node's emitted topic `heartbeat`: the harness subscribes before the node boots (pinned to the node's identity) and barriers on the subscription, so the node's very first publish is captured.
/// A held subscription to the node's emissions on this topic. It
/// keeps the newest messages the test has not read, up to the queue
/// size of the topic's QoS tier, or its retention depth when that is
/// larger: when the queue is full, a new message drops the oldest
/// unread one. A test can leave it unread, and its unread messages
/// never hold up the other messages to the harness's session.
pub struct Subscription {
    inner: peppylib::messaging::Subscription,
}
pub(crate) async fn subscribe(
    session: &peppylib::MessengerHandle,
    node_instance_id: &str,
) -> crate::Result<Subscription> {
    let producer = peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef::new(
        peppylib::testing::STANDALONE_CORE_NODE,
        node_instance_id,
    );
    let inner = peppylib::TopicMessenger::subscribe(
            session,
            "fixture-core",
            "fixture-observer",
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("relay_node", "v1")?,
            "heartbeat",
            &producer,
            peppylib::config::QoSProfile::Reliable,
            peppylib::config::TopicRetention::LiveOnly,
            peppylib::messaging::TopicBuffer::KeepLatest,
        )
        .await?;
    Ok(Subscription { inner })
}
impl Subscription {
    /// Awaits the node's next message on this topic; `Ok(None)` once
    /// the fixture session closes.
    #[allow(clippy::should_implement_trait)]
    pub async fn next(&mut self) -> crate::Result<Option<Message>> {
        let Some(message) = self.inner.on_next_message().await else {
            return Ok(None);
        };
        let message = deserialize_message(message.payload_bytes().as_ref())?;
        Ok(Some(message))
    }
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Message {}
fn deserialize_message(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<Message> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]
    let context = String::from("fixtures observe heartbeat");
    let mut payload = payload;
    let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    message_reader
        .get_root::<crate::capnp::heartbeat_message_capnp::heartbeat_message::Reader>()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    Ok(Message {})
}

Fixture :: emitted_topics/camera_state

//!Typed observation of the node's emitted topic `camera_state`: the harness subscribes before the node boots (pinned to the node's identity) and barriers on the subscription, so the node's very first publish is captured.
/// A held subscription to the node's emissions on this topic. It
/// keeps the newest messages the test has not read, up to the queue
/// size of the topic's QoS tier, or its retention depth when that is
/// larger: when the queue is full, a new message drops the oldest
/// unread one. A test can leave it unread, and its unread messages
/// never hold up the other messages to the harness's session.
pub struct Subscription {
    inner: peppylib::messaging::Subscription,
}
pub(crate) async fn subscribe(
    session: &peppylib::MessengerHandle,
    node_instance_id: &str,
) -> crate::Result<Subscription> {
    let producer = peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef::new(
        peppylib::testing::STANDALONE_CORE_NODE,
        node_instance_id,
    );
    let inner = peppylib::TopicMessenger::subscribe(
            session,
            "fixture-core",
            "fixture-observer",
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("relay_node", "v1")?,
            "camera_state",
            &producer,
            peppylib::config::QoSProfile::SensorData,
            {
                const RETENTION: peppylib::config::TopicRetention = match peppylib::config::TopicRetention::latest(
                    3,
                ) {
                    Ok(retention) => retention,
                    Err(_) => {
                        panic!("the topic's manifest states a retention depth in range")
                    }
                };
                RETENTION
            },
            peppylib::messaging::TopicBuffer::KeepLatest,
        )
        .await?;
    Ok(Subscription { inner })
}

    /// Awaits the node's next message on this topic; `Ok(None)` once

Fixture :: exposed_services/enable_camera
================================================================
//!Identity-explicit caller for the node's exposed service `enable_camera`: polls from the fixture session, pinned to the node under test, gating on reachability first (the fixture session is a fresh caller).
pub use crate::exposed_services::enable_camera::RequestData;
pub use crate::exposed_services::enable_camera::Response;
pub use crate::exposed_services::enable_camera::{Failure, FailureKind};
#[allow(unused_imports)]
use crate::exposed_services::enable_camera::*;
/// Polls the node's service once and decodes the reply. `timeout`
/// bounds the reachability gate and the poll individually. An error
/// reply of the node is `Error::ServiceFailed`: `Failure::of` reads
/// its failure.
pub async fn poll(
    harness: &crate::fixtures::harness::Harness,
    request: &RequestData,
    timeout: std::time::Duration,
) -> crate::Result<Response> {
    let producer = harness.node_producer_ref();
    peppylib::testing::wait_service_reachable(
            harness.session(),
            "fixture-core",
            "fixture-observer",
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("relay_node", "v1")?,
            "enable_camera",
            &producer,
            timeout,
        )
        .await?;
    let response_message = peppylib::ServiceMessenger::poll(
            harness.session(),
            "fixture-core",
            "fixture-observer",
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("relay_node", "v1")?,
            "enable_camera",
            peppylib::messaging::ServiceTarget::Producer(&producer),
            serialize_request(request)?,
            timeout,
        )
        .await
        .map_err(Failure::reject_undeclared_kind)?;
    deserialize_response(response_message.payload_bytes().as_ref())
}
fn serialize_request(value: &RequestData) -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {
    Ok({
        let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
        {
            let mut root = capnp_msg
                .init_root::<
                    crate::capnp::enable_camera_message_capnp::enable_camera_message::Builder,
                >();
            root.set_enable(value.enable);
        }
        let mut buffer = Vec::new();
        capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
            .map_err(|source| {
                let context = String::from("fixtures poll enable_camera");
                crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
            })?;
        peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
    })
}
fn deserialize_response(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<Response> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]
    let context = String::from("fixtures poll enable_camera response");
    let mut payload = payload;
    let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader
        .get_root::<
            crate::capnp::enable_camera_response_message_capnp::enable_camera_response_message::Reader,
        >()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let enabled_0 = root.reborrow().get_enabled();
    let error_msg_opt_1 = if root.reborrow().has_error_msg() {
        let text_3 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_error_msg()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("fixtures poll enable_camera response");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "error_msg", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let error_msg_2 = text_3
            .to_str()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("fixtures poll enable_camera response");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "error_msg", context, source),
                )
            })?
            .to_owned();
        Some(error_msg_2)
    } else {
        None
    };
    Ok(Response {
        enabled: enabled_0,
        error_msg: error_msg_opt_1,
    })
}

//!Identity-explicit caller for the node's exposed action `move_arm`: drives the full goal lifecycle from the fixture session, pinned to the node under test, gating on reachability first.
pub use crate::exposed_actions::move_arm::GoalRequestData;
pub use crate::exposed_actions::move_arm::GoalResponse;
pub use crate::exposed_actions::move_arm::{Failure, FailureKind};
#[allow(unused_imports)]
use crate::exposed_actions::move_arm::*;
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Feedback {
    pub new_position: [i32; 3],
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct ResultData {
    pub success: bool,
    pub error_msg: Option<String>,
}
/// A goal in flight against the node's action.
pub struct GoalHandle {
    messenger: peppylib::MessengerHandle,
    inner: peppylib::messaging::ActionGoalHandle,
    /// Whether the node accepted the goal: `refusal` is `None`.
    pub accepted: bool,
    /// The failure the node refused the goal with; `None` for an
    /// accepted goal.
    pub refusal: Option<Failure>,
    /// The declared goal response of an accepted goal;
    /// `None` for a refused goal.
    pub data: Option<GoalResponse>,
}
/// Sends a goal to the node and decodes the admission reply. The
/// handle keeps up to `feedback_bound` unread feedback messages.
pub async fn send_goal(
    harness: &crate::fixtures::harness::Harness,
    request: &GoalRequestData,
    feedback_bound: peppylib::messaging::FeedbackBound,
    timeout: std::time::Duration,
) -> crate::Result<GoalHandle> {
    let producer = harness.node_producer_ref();
    peppylib::testing::wait_action_reachable(
            harness.session(),
            "fixture-core",
            "fixture-observer",
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("relay_node", "v1")?,
            "move_arm",
            &producer,
            timeout,
        )
        .await?;
    let inner = peppylib::ActionMessenger::send_goal(
            harness.session(),
            "fixture-core",
            "fixture-observer",
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("relay_node", "v1")?,
            "move_arm",
            Some(&producer),
            serialize_goal_request(request)?,
            feedback_bound,
            timeout,
        )
        .await?;
    let reply = inner.goal_reply();
    let refusal = reply.refusal().map(Failure::from_wire).transpose()?;
    let data = match &reply.answer {
        peppylib::messaging::GoalAnswer::Accepted(response) => {
            Some(deserialize_goal_response(response.as_ref())?)
        }
        peppylib::messaging::GoalAnswer::Refused(_) => None,
    };
    Ok(GoalHandle {
        messenger: harness.session().clone(),
        accepted: refusal.is_none(),
        inner,
        refusal,
        data,
    })
}
/// How a finished goal ended.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum ResultOutcome {
    /// The goal is a success.
    Completed(ResultData),
    /// The goal ended cancelled, for `reason`.
    Cancelled { reason: String, data: ResultData },
    /// The goal failed: a failure kind that the action declares,
    /// a message, and no result.
    Failed(Failure),
    /// The goal ended without a result: its worker dropped it, or
    /// the provider is gone.
    Abandoned,
    /// The goal ended, and the provider no longer keeps its result.
    Expired,
}
/// The decoded result reply.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct ResultResponse {
    pub instance_id: String,
    pub core_node: String,
    pub outcome: ResultOutcome,
}
/// Cancel acknowledgement states, mirroring the runtime's.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum CancelState {
    Signalled,
    AlreadyTerminal,
    Unknown,
}
impl GoalHandle {
    /// Receives the next decoded feedback message for this goal.
    ///
    /// Terminal errors that end a drain loop:
    /// `Error::ActionFeedbackChannelClosed` on a clean close,
    /// `Error::ActionFeedbackProducerGone` when the node instance
    /// disappeared without closing the stream, and
    /// `Error::ActionFeedbackOverflow` when this caller fell
    /// behind by more unread messages than its bound.
    pub async fn on_next_feedback(&mut self) -> crate::Result<Feedback> {
        let feedback = self.inner.on_next_feedback().await?;
        let payload = feedback.payload_bytes();
        deserialize_feedback(payload.as_ref())
    }
    /// Requests cancellation of this goal.
    pub async fn cancel_goal(
        &self,
        timeout: std::time::Duration,
    ) -> crate::Result<CancelState> {
        let response = peppylib::ActionMessenger::cancel_goal(
                &self.messenger,
                &self.inner,
                timeout,
            )
            .await?;
        let state = match peppylib::messaging::decode_cancel_ack(
            response.payload_bytes().as_ref(),
        )? {
            peppylib::messaging::CancelState::Signalled => CancelState::Signalled,
            peppylib::messaging::CancelState::AlreadyTerminal => {
                CancelState::AlreadyTerminal
            }
            peppylib::messaging::CancelState::Unknown => CancelState::Unknown,
        };
        Ok(state)
    }
    /// Retrieves the goal's terminal result.
    pub async fn get_result(
        &self,
        timeout: std::time::Duration,
    ) -> crate::Result<ResultResponse> {
        let reply = peppylib::ActionMessenger::request_result(
                &self.messenger,
                &self.inner,
                timeout,
            )
            .await?;
        let outcome = match reply.outcome {
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Completed(result) => {
                ResultOutcome::Completed(deserialize_result(result.as_ref())?)
            }
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Cancelled { reason, result } => {
                ResultOutcome::Cancelled {
                    reason,
                    data: deserialize_result(result.as_ref())?,
                }
            }
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Failed(failure) => {
                ResultOutcome::Failed(Failure::from_wire(&failure)?)
            }
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Abandoned => ResultOutcome::Abandoned,
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Expired => ResultOutcome::Expired,
        };
        Ok(ResultResponse {
            instance_id: reply.instance_id,
            core_node: reply.core_node,
            outcome,
        })
    }
}
fn serialize_goal_request(value: &GoalRequestData) -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {
    Ok({
        let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
        {
            let mut root = capnp_msg
                .init_root::<
                    crate::capnp::move_arm_goal_message_capnp::move_arm_goal_message::Builder,
                >();
            root.set_arm_id(value.arm_id);
            let mut list_0 = root.reborrow().init_desired_position(3);
            for (idx_1, value_2) in (value.desired_position).iter().enumerate() {
                list_0.set(idx_1 as u32, *value_2);
            }
        }
        let mut buffer = Vec::new();
        capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
            .map_err(|source| {
                let context = String::from("fixtures send_goal move_arm");
                crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
            })?;
        peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
    })
}

        ))?;

    })

pub use crate::exposed_actions::nudge::{Failure, FailureKind};
#[allow(unused_imports)]
use crate::exposed_actions::nudge::*;
/// A goal in flight against the node's action.
pub struct GoalHandle {
    messenger: peppylib::MessengerHandle,
    inner: peppylib::messaging::ActionGoalHandle,
    /// Whether the node accepted the goal: `refusal` is `None`.
    pub accepted: bool,
    /// The failure the node refused the goal with; `None` for an
    /// accepted goal.
    pub refusal: Option<Failure>,
}
/// Sends a goal to the node and decodes the admission reply. The
/// handle keeps up to `feedback_bound` unread feedback messages.
pub async fn send_goal(
    harness: &crate::fixtures::harness::Harness,
    feedback_bound: peppylib::messaging::FeedbackBound,
    timeout: std::time::Duration,
) -> crate::Result<GoalHandle> {
    let producer = harness.node_producer_ref();
    peppylib::testing::wait_action_reachable(
            harness.session(),
            "fixture-core",
            "fixture-observer",
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("relay_node", "v1")?,
            "nudge",
            &producer,
            timeout,
        )
        .await?;
    let inner = peppylib::ActionMessenger::send_goal(
            harness.session(),
            "fixture-core",
            "fixture-observer",
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("relay_node", "v1")?,
            "nudge",
            Some(&producer),
            peppylib::Payload::new(),
            feedback_bound,
            timeout,
        )
        .await?;
    let reply = inner.goal_reply();
    let refusal = reply.refusal().map(Failure::from_wire).transpose()?;
    Ok(GoalHandle {
        messenger: harness.session().clone(),
        accepted: refusal.is_none(),
        inner,
        refusal,
    })
}
/// How a finished goal ended.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum ResultOutcome {
    /// The goal is a success.
    Completed,
    /// The goal ended cancelled, for `reason`.
    Cancelled { reason: String },
    /// The goal failed: a failure kind that the action declares,
    /// a message, and no result.
    Failed(Failure),
    /// The goal ended without a result: its worker dropped it, or
    /// the provider is gone.
    Abandoned,
    /// The goal ended, and the provider no longer keeps its result.
    Expired,
}
/// The decoded result reply.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct ResultResponse {
    pub instance_id: String,
    pub core_node: String,
    pub outcome: ResultOutcome,
}
/// Cancel acknowledgement states, mirroring the runtime's.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum CancelState {
    Signalled,
    AlreadyTerminal,
    Unknown,
}
impl GoalHandle {
    /// Requests cancellation of this goal.
    pub async fn cancel_goal(
        &self,
        timeout: std::time::Duration,
    ) -> crate::Result<CancelState> {
        let response = peppylib::ActionMessenger::cancel_goal(
                &self.messenger,
                &self.inner,
                timeout,
            )
            .await?;
        let state = match peppylib::messaging::decode_cancel_ack(
            response.payload_bytes().as_ref(),
        )? {
            peppylib::messaging::CancelState::Signalled => CancelState::Signalled,
            peppylib::messaging::CancelState::AlreadyTerminal => {
                CancelState::AlreadyTerminal
            }
            peppylib::messaging::CancelState::Unknown => CancelState::Unknown,
        };
        Ok(state)
    }
    /// Retrieves the goal's terminal result.
    pub async fn get_result(
        &self,
        timeout: std::time::Duration,
    ) -> crate::Result<ResultResponse> {
        let reply = peppylib::ActionMessenger::request_result(
                &self.messenger,
                &self.inner,
                timeout,
            )
            .await?;
        let outcome = match reply.outcome {
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Completed(_) => ResultOutcome::Completed,
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Cancelled { reason, .. } => {
                ResultOutcome::Cancelled { reason }
            }
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Failed(failure) => {
                ResultOutcome::Failed(Failure::from_wire(&failure)?)
            }
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Abandoned => ResultOutcome::Abandoned,
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Expired => ResultOutcome::Expired,
        };
        Ok(ResultResponse {
            instance_id: reply.instance_id,
            core_node: reply.core_node,
            outcome,
        })
    }
}
