//!Identity-explicit caller for the node's exposed action `move_arm`: drives the full goal lifecycle from the fixture session, pinned to the node under test, gating on reachability first.
pub use crate::exposed_actions::feed::move_arm::GoalRequestData;
pub use crate::exposed_actions::feed::move_arm::GoalResponse;
pub use crate::exposed_actions::feed::move_arm::{Failure, FailureKind};
#[allow(unused_imports)]
use crate::exposed_actions::feed::move_arm::*;
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Feedback {
    pub new_position: [i32; 3],
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct ResultData {
    pub success: bool,
    pub error_msg: Option<String>,
}
/// A goal in flight against the node's action.
pub struct GoalHandle {
    messenger: peppylib::MessengerHandle,
    inner: peppylib::messaging::ActionGoalHandle,
    /// Whether the node accepted the goal: `refusal` is `None`.
    pub accepted: bool,
    /// The failure the node refused the goal with; `None` for an
    /// accepted goal.
    pub refusal: Option<Failure>,
    /// The declared goal response of an accepted goal;
    /// `None` for a refused goal.
    pub data: Option<GoalResponse>,
}
/// Sends a goal to the node and decodes the admission reply. The
/// handle keeps up to `feedback_bound` unread feedback messages.
pub async fn send_goal(
    harness: &crate::fixtures::harness::Harness,
    request: &GoalRequestData,
    feedback_bound: peppylib::messaging::FeedbackBound,
    timeout: std::time::Duration,
) -> crate::Result<GoalHandle> {
    let producer = harness.node_producer_ref();
    peppylib::testing::wait_action_reachable(
            harness.session(),
            "fixture-core",
            "fixture-observer",
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::contract("camera_feed", "v1")?,
            "move_arm",
            &producer,
            timeout,
        )
        .await?;
    let inner = peppylib::ActionMessenger::send_goal(
            harness.session(),
            "fixture-core",
            "fixture-observer",
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::contract("camera_feed", "v1")?,
            "move_arm",
            Some(&producer),
            serialize_goal_request(request)?,
            feedback_bound,
            timeout,
        )
        .await?;
    let reply = inner.goal_reply();
    let refusal = reply.refusal().map(Failure::from_wire).transpose()?;
    let data = match &reply.answer {
        peppylib::messaging::GoalAnswer::Accepted(response) => {
            Some(deserialize_goal_response(response.as_ref())?)
        }
        peppylib::messaging::GoalAnswer::Refused(_) => None,
    };
    Ok(GoalHandle {
        messenger: harness.session().clone(),
        accepted: refusal.is_none(),
        inner,
        refusal,
        data,
    })
}
/// How a finished goal ended.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum ResultOutcome {
    /// The goal is a success.
    Completed(ResultData),
    /// The goal ended cancelled, for `reason`.
    Cancelled { reason: String, data: ResultData },
    /// The goal failed: a failure kind that the action declares,
    /// a message, and no result.
    Failed(Failure),
    /// The goal ended without a result: its worker dropped it, or
    /// the provider is gone.
    Abandoned,
    /// The goal ended, and the provider no longer keeps its result.
    Expired,
}
/// The decoded result reply.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct ResultResponse {
    pub instance_id: String,
    pub core_node: String,
    pub outcome: ResultOutcome,
}
/// Cancel acknowledgement states, mirroring the runtime's.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum CancelState {
    Signalled,
    AlreadyTerminal,
    Unknown,
}
impl GoalHandle {
    /// Receives the next decoded feedback message for this goal.
    ///
    /// Terminal errors that end a drain loop:
    /// `Error::ActionFeedbackChannelClosed` on a clean close,
    /// `Error::ActionFeedbackProducerGone` when the node instance
    /// disappeared without closing the stream, and
    /// `Error::ActionFeedbackOverflow` when this caller fell
    /// behind by more unread messages than its bound.
    pub async fn on_next_feedback(&mut self) -> crate::Result<Feedback> {
        let feedback = self.inner.on_next_feedback().await?;
        let payload = feedback.payload_bytes();
        deserialize_feedback(payload.as_ref())
    }
    /// Requests cancellation of this goal.
    pub async fn cancel_goal(
        &self,
        timeout: std::time::Duration,
    ) -> crate::Result<CancelState> {
        let response = peppylib::ActionMessenger::cancel_goal(
                &self.messenger,
                &self.inner,
                timeout,
            )
            .await?;
        let state = match peppylib::messaging::decode_cancel_ack(
            response.payload_bytes().as_ref(),
        )? {
            peppylib::messaging::CancelState::Signalled => CancelState::Signalled,
            peppylib::messaging::CancelState::AlreadyTerminal => {
                CancelState::AlreadyTerminal
            }
            peppylib::messaging::CancelState::Unknown => CancelState::Unknown,
        };
        Ok(state)
    }
    /// Retrieves the goal's terminal result.
    pub async fn get_result(
        &self,
        timeout: std::time::Duration,
    ) -> crate::Result<ResultResponse> {
        let reply = peppylib::ActionMessenger::request_result(
                &self.messenger,
                &self.inner,
                timeout,
            )
            .await?;
        let outcome = match reply.outcome {
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Completed(result) => {
                ResultOutcome::Completed(deserialize_result(result.as_ref())?)
            }
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Cancelled { reason, result } => {
                ResultOutcome::Cancelled {
                    reason,
                    data: deserialize_result(result.as_ref())?,
                }
            }
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Failed(failure) => {
                ResultOutcome::Failed(Failure::from_wire(&failure)?)
            }
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Abandoned => ResultOutcome::Abandoned,
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Expired => ResultOutcome::Expired,
        };
        Ok(ResultResponse {
            instance_id: reply.instance_id,
            core_node: reply.core_node,
            outcome,
        })
    }
}
fn serialize_goal_request(value: &GoalRequestData) -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {
    Ok({
        let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
        {
            let mut root = capnp_msg
                .init_root::<
                    crate::capnp::camera_feed_v1_move_arm_goal_message_capnp::move_arm_goal_message::Builder,
                >();
            root.set_arm_id(value.arm_id);
            let mut list_0 = root.reborrow().init_desired_position(3);
            for (idx_1, value_2) in (value.desired_position).iter().enumerate() {
                list_0.set(idx_1 as u32, *value_2);
            }
        }
        let mut buffer = Vec::new();
        capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
            .map_err(|source| {
                let context = String::from("fixtures send_goal move_arm");
                crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
            })?;
        peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
    })
}

//!Generated test harness: ephemeral router + started mocks + seeded `StandaloneConfig` + the node running in-process behind the readiness barriers. Construct with [`Harness::start`] (or [`Harness::start_with`] for parameter/slot overrides); tear down with [`Harness::shutdown`].
/// Wire identity of the fixture caller/observer session.
pub const FIXTURE_CORE_NODE: &str = "fixture-core";
pub const FIXTURE_INSTANCE_ID: &str = "fixture-observer";
/// The node's manifest identity, baked at sync time.
pub const NODE_NAME: &str = "relay_node";
pub const NODE_TAG: &str = "v1";
/// The node's `peppy.json5`, resolved relative to the generated
/// crate's manifest (always `<node>/.peppy/libs/peppygen`).
pub const PEPPY_CONFIG_PATH: &str = concat!(
    env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"), "/../../../peppy.json5"
);
/// One mock peer of a multi pairing slot, as [`Config`] names it:
/// its instance id and the copy it belongs to, `None` outside a copy.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct PeerMemberSpec {
    pub instance_id: String,
    pub copy: Option<String>,
}
/// Optional overrides for [`Harness::start_with`].
pub struct Config {
    /// Typed parameters for the node; `None` uses the schema
    /// defaults (an error at boot if a required parameter has none).
    pub parameters: Option<crate::Parameters>,
    /// Explicit node instance id; `None` generates a unique one.
    pub instance_id: Option<String>,
    /// Which clock the node boots on, the test-side spelling of a
    /// deployment's `framework.clock`.
    ///
    /// `Wall` (the default) reads OS wall time, which the harness
    /// clock ticks like a daemon. `Consumer` binds the node to the
    /// harness's simulated domain, where `peppygen::clock::now_ns`
    /// reads no time until the test advances it with
    /// [`Harness::clock`]'s `tick`. `Publisher` makes the node the
    /// instance supplying that domain: it publishes its own instants
    /// and the test reads them back with
    /// `peppylib::clock::subscribe(harness.node_runner(),
    /// peppylib::messaging::TopicBuffer::KeepLatest)`, which keeps
    /// the newest ticks and never holds up the node's session when
    /// the test reads late.
    pub clock: peppylib::testing::HarnessClock,
    ///Leave the `camera_zero_or_one` dependency slot vacant (its cardinality admits an empty binding).
    pub camera_zero_or_one_vacant: bool,
    ///How many mock producer instances to start and bind for the `camera_one_or_more` dependency slot.
    pub camera_one_or_more_instances: usize,
    ///Explicit instance ids for the `camera_one_or_more` mock producers, overriding `camera_one_or_more_instances` when non-empty. For nodes that classify producers by instance name.
    pub camera_one_or_more_instance_ids: Vec<String>,
    ///How many mock producer instances to start and bind for the `camera_zero_or_more` dependency slot.
    pub camera_zero_or_more_instances: usize,
    ///Explicit instance ids for the `camera_zero_or_more` mock producers, overriding `camera_zero_or_more_instances` when non-empty. For nodes that classify producers by instance name.
    pub camera_zero_or_more_instance_ids: Vec<String>,
    ///Boot with the `zero_or_one` pairing slot `controller_zero_or_one` unpaired: the peer pin is not seeded, so the node's `paired()` stays `None`, its publishes on this slot are no-ops, and the still-started mock's subscriptions stay silent.
    pub controller_zero_or_one_vacant: bool,
    ///How many mock peers to pair into the `controller_one_or_more` pairing slot, each under its own instance id and outside any copy.
    pub controller_one_or_more_instances: usize,
    ///Explicit mock peers for the `controller_one_or_more` pairing slot, each with the copy it belongs to, overriding `controller_one_or_more_instances` when non-empty. For nodes that group pairs by copy or classify peers by instance name.
    pub controller_one_or_more_members: Vec<PeerMemberSpec>,
    ///How many mock peers to pair into the `controller_zero_or_more` pairing slot, each under its own instance id and outside any copy.
    pub controller_zero_or_more_instances: usize,
    ///Explicit mock peers for the `controller_zero_or_more` pairing slot, each with the copy it belongs to, overriding `controller_zero_or_more_instances` when non-empty. For nodes that group pairs by copy or classify peers by instance name.
    pub controller_zero_or_more_members: Vec<PeerMemberSpec>,
    ///Leave the `watchers_zero_or_one` observer slot empty (its cardinality admits an empty set).
    pub watchers_zero_or_one_vacant: bool,
    ///How many mock sources to start for the `watchers_one_or_more` observer slot.
    pub watchers_one_or_more_instances: usize,
    ///Explicit instance ids for the `watchers_one_or_more` mock sources, overriding `watchers_one_or_more_instances` when non-empty. For nodes that classify sources by instance name.
    pub watchers_one_or_more_instance_ids: Vec<String>,
    ///How many mock sources to start for the `watchers_zero_or_more` observer slot.
    pub watchers_zero_or_more_instances: usize,
    ///Explicit instance ids for the `watchers_zero_or_more` mock sources, overriding `watchers_zero_or_more_instances` when non-empty. For nodes that classify sources by instance name.
    pub watchers_zero_or_more_instance_ids: Vec<String>,
}
#[allow(clippy::derivable_impls)]
impl Default for Config {

/// producer-loss) without giving up the harness.

    pub pairings: PairingMocks,
    pub observed: ObservedMocks,

/// Typed subscriptions to the node's emitted topics, opened before
/// the node booted (so its very first publish is captured). Each
/// keeps the newest messages the test has not read and never holds
/// up the harness's session.
pub struct Emitted {
    pub video_stream: crate::fixtures::emitted_topics::video_stream::Subscription,
    pub feed_video_stream: crate::fixtures::emitted_topics::feed::video_stream::Subscription,
    pub heartbeat: crate::fixtures::emitted_topics::heartbeat::Subscription,
    pub camera_state: crate::fixtures::emitted_topics::camera_state::Subscription,
}
pub struct Harness {
    core: peppylib::testing::HarnessCore,
    /// Observation subscriptions to the node's own emissions.
    pub emitted: Emitted,
    /// The daemon-clock stand-in serving `peppylib`'s
    /// `clock::synchronize` and the `clock` topic, in the shape
    /// `Config::clock` asked for. The service answers wall time in
    /// every mode (skewable via `set_offset_ns`); `tick` drives the
    /// simulated domain and is only accepted under
    /// `HarnessClock::Consumer`.
    pub clock: peppylib::testing::MockClock,
    session: peppylib::MessengerHandle,
    router: Option<peppylib::testing::EphemeralRouter>,
    instance_id: String,
}
impl Harness {
    /// Boots the full fixture under default configuration. `setup`
    /// is the node's real entry point (the exact closure shape
    /// `NodeBuilder::run` takes), spawned only after every readiness
    /// barrier passed.
    pub async fn start<F, Fut>(setup: F) -> crate::Result<(Self, Mocks)>
    where
        F: FnOnce(crate::Parameters, std::sync::Arc<crate::NodeRunner>) -> Fut,
        Fut: std::future::Future<Output = crate::Result<()>> + Send + 'static,
    {
        Self::start_with(Config::default(), setup).await
    }
    /// [`Harness::start`] with parameter / slot overrides.
    #[allow(clippy::vec_init_then_push)]
    pub async fn start_with<F, Fut>(
        config: Config,
        setup: F,
    ) -> crate::Result<(Self, Mocks)>
    where
        F: FnOnce(crate::Parameters, std::sync::Arc<crate::NodeRunner>) -> Fut,
        Fut: std::future::Future<Output = crate::Result<()>> + Send + 'static,
    {
        let router = peppylib::testing::EphemeralRouter::start().await?;
        let instance_id = config
            .instance_id
            .clone()
            .unwrap_or_else(peppylib::testing::unique_test_instance_id);
        let camera_one = crate::mock::deps::camera_one::Mock::start(&router).await?;
        let camera_zero_or_one = if config.camera_zero_or_one_vacant {
            None
        } else {
            Some(crate::mock::deps::camera_zero_or_one::Mock::start(&router).await?)
        };
        let camera_one_or_more_member_ids: Vec<String> = if config
            .camera_one_or_more_instance_ids
            .is_empty()
        {
            (0..config.camera_one_or_more_instances)
                .map(|index| {
                    format!(
                        "{}-{}", crate
                        ::mock::deps::camera_one_or_more::MOCK_INSTANCE_ID, index
                    )
                })
                .collect()
        } else {
            config.camera_one_or_more_instance_ids.clone()
        };
        let mut camera_one_or_more = Vec::new();
        for member_instance_id in &camera_one_or_more_member_ids {
            camera_one_or_more
                .push(
                    crate::mock::deps::camera_one_or_more::Mock::start_as(
                            &router,
                            member_instance_id,
                        )
                        .await?,
                );
        }
        let camera_zero_or_more_member_ids: Vec<String> = if config
            .camera_zero_or_more_instance_ids
            .is_empty()
        {
            (0..config.camera_zero_or_more_instances)
                .map(|index| {
                    format!(
                        "{}-{}", crate
                        ::mock::deps::camera_zero_or_more::MOCK_INSTANCE_ID, index
                    )
                })
                .collect()
        } else {
            config.camera_zero_or_more_instance_ids.clone()
        };
        let mut camera_zero_or_more = Vec::new();
        for member_instance_id in &camera_zero_or_more_member_ids {
            camera_zero_or_more
                .push(
                    crate::mock::deps::camera_zero_or_more::Mock::start_as(
                            &router,
                            member_instance_id,
                        )
                        .await?,
                );
        }
        let left_arm = crate::mock::deps::left_arm::Mock::start(&router).await?;
        let controller_one = crate::mock::pairings::controller_one::Mock::start(
                &router,
                &instance_id,
            )
            .await?;
        let controller_zero_or_one = crate::mock::pairings::controller_zero_or_one::Mock::start(
                &router,
                &instance_id,
            )
            .await?;
        let controller_one_or_more_member_specs: Vec<PeerMemberSpec> = if config
            .controller_one_or_more_members
            .is_empty()
        {
            (0..config.controller_one_or_more_instances)
                .map(|index| PeerMemberSpec {
                    instance_id: format!(
                        "{}-{}", crate
                        ::mock::pairings::controller_one_or_more::MOCK_INSTANCE_ID, index
                    ),
                    copy: None,
                })
                .collect()
        } else {
            config.controller_one_or_more_members.clone()
        };
        let mut controller_one_or_more = Vec::new();
        for member in &controller_one_or_more_member_specs {
            controller_one_or_more
                .push(
                    crate::mock::pairings::controller_one_or_more::Mock::start_as(
                            &router,
                            &member.instance_id,
                            &instance_id,
                        )
                        .await?,
                );
        }
        let controller_zero_or_more_member_specs: Vec<PeerMemberSpec> = if config
            .controller_zero_or_more_members
            .is_empty()
        {
            (0..config.controller_zero_or_more_instances)
                .map(|index| PeerMemberSpec {
                    instance_id: format!(
                        "{}-{}", crate
                        ::mock::pairings::controller_zero_or_more::MOCK_INSTANCE_ID,
                        index
                    ),
                    copy: None,
                })
                .collect()
        } else {
            config.controller_zero_or_more_members.clone()
        };
        let mut controller_zero_or_more = Vec::new();
        for member in &controller_zero_or_more_member_specs {
            controller_zero_or_more
                .push(
                    crate::mock::pairings::controller_zero_or_more::Mock::start_as(
                            &router,
                            &member.instance_id,
                            &instance_id,
                        )
                        .await?,
                );
        }
        let sensor_bus = crate::mock::pairings::sensor_bus::Mock::start(
                &router,
                &instance_id,
            )
            .await?;
        let watchers_one = crate::mock::observed::watchers_one::Mock::start(&router)
            .await?;
        let watchers_zero_or_one = if config.watchers_zero_or_one_vacant {
            None
        } else {
            Some(
                crate::mock::observed::watchers_zero_or_one::Mock::start(&router).await?,
            )
        };
        let watchers_one_or_more_member_ids: Vec<String> = if config
            .watchers_one_or_more_instance_ids
            .is_empty()
        {
            (0..config.watchers_one_or_more_instances)
                .map(|index| {
                    format!(
                        "{}-{}", crate
                        ::mock::observed::watchers_one_or_more::MOCK_INSTANCE_ID, index
                    )
                })
                .collect()
        } else {
            config.watchers_one_or_more_instance_ids.clone()
        };
        let mut watchers_one_or_more = Vec::new();
        for member_instance_id in &watchers_one_or_more_member_ids {
            watchers_one_or_more
                .push(
                    crate::mock::observed::watchers_one_or_more::Mock::start_as(
                            &router,
                            member_instance_id,
                        )
                        .await?,
                );
        }
        let watchers_zero_or_more_member_ids: Vec<String> = if config
            .watchers_zero_or_more_instance_ids
            .is_empty()
        {
            (0..config.watchers_zero_or_more_instances)
                .map(|index| {
                    format!(
                        "{}-{}", crate
                        ::mock::observed::watchers_zero_or_more::MOCK_INSTANCE_ID, index
                    )
                })
                .collect()
        } else {
            config.watchers_zero_or_more_instance_ids.clone()
        };
        let mut watchers_zero_or_more = Vec::new();
        for member_instance_id in &watchers_zero_or_more_member_ids {
            watchers_zero_or_more
                .push(
                    crate::mock::observed::watchers_zero_or_more::Mock::start_as(
                            &router,
                            member_instance_id,
                        )
                        .await?,
                );
        }
        let session = router.connect().await?;
        let video_stream = crate::fixtures::emitted_topics::video_stream::subscribe(
                &session,
                &instance_id,
            )
            .await?;
        let feed_video_stream = crate::fixtures::emitted_topics::feed::video_stream::subscribe(
                &session,
                &instance_id,
            )
            .await?;
        let heartbeat = crate::fixtures::emitted_topics::heartbeat::subscribe(
                &session,
                &instance_id,
            )
            .await?;
        let camera_state = crate::fixtures::emitted_topics::camera_state::subscribe(
                &session,
                &instance_id,
            )
            .await?;
        let clock = peppylib::testing::MockClock::start(
                &session,
                peppylib::testing::STANDALONE_CORE_NODE,
                peppylib::testing::MOCK_CLOCK_INSTANCE_ID,
                config.clock,
            )
            .await?;
        let mut standalone = peppylib::runtime::StandaloneConfig::new()
            .with_messaging(router.host(), router.port())
            .with_instance_id(instance_id.clone())
            .with_clock(clock.binding()?);
        if let Some(parameters) = &config.parameters {
            standalone = standalone.with_parameters(parameters);
        }
        standalone = standalone
            .with_bound_producer(
                "camera_one",
                crate::mock::deps::camera_one::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                crate::mock::deps::camera_one::MOCK_INSTANCE_ID,
            );
        if config.camera_zero_or_one_vacant {
            standalone = standalone.with_vacant_producer_slot("camera_zero_or_one");
        } else {
            standalone = standalone
                .with_bound_producer(
                    "camera_zero_or_one",
                    crate::mock::deps::camera_zero_or_one::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                    crate::mock::deps::camera_zero_or_one::MOCK_INSTANCE_ID,
                );
        }
        for member_instance_id in &camera_one_or_more_member_ids {
            standalone = standalone
                .with_bound_producer(
                    "camera_one_or_more",
                    crate::mock::deps::camera_one_or_more::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                    member_instance_id.clone(),
                );
        }
        for member_instance_id in &camera_zero_or_more_member_ids {
            standalone = standalone
                .with_bound_producer(
                    "camera_zero_or_more",
                    crate::mock::deps::camera_zero_or_more::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                    member_instance_id.clone(),
                );
        }
        standalone = standalone
            .with_bound_producer(
                "left_arm",
                crate::mock::deps::left_arm::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                crate::mock::deps::left_arm::MOCK_INSTANCE_ID,
            );
        standalone = standalone
            .with_peer_pin(
                "controller_one",
                crate::mock::pairings::controller_one::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                crate::mock::pairings::controller_one::MOCK_INSTANCE_ID,
                crate::mock::pairings::controller_one::PEER_LINK_ID,
            );
        if !config.controller_zero_or_one_vacant {
            standalone = standalone
                .with_peer_pin(
                    "controller_zero_or_one",
                    crate::mock::pairings::controller_zero_or_one::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                    crate::mock::pairings::controller_zero_or_one::MOCK_INSTANCE_ID,
                    crate::mock::pairings::controller_zero_or_one::PEER_LINK_ID,
                );
        }
        for member in &controller_one_or_more_member_specs {
            standalone = match &member.copy {
                Some(copy) => {
                    standalone
                        .with_peer_pin_in_copy(
                            "controller_one_or_more",
                            crate::mock::pairings::controller_one_or_more::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                            member.instance_id.clone(),
                            crate::mock::pairings::controller_one_or_more::PEER_LINK_ID,
                            copy.clone(),
                        )
                }
                None => {
                    standalone
                        .with_peer_pin(
                            "controller_one_or_more",
                            crate::mock::pairings::controller_one_or_more::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                            member.instance_id.clone(),
                            crate::mock::pairings::controller_one_or_more::PEER_LINK_ID,
                        )
                }
            };
        }
        for member in &controller_zero_or_more_member_specs {
            standalone = match &member.copy {
                Some(copy) => {
                    standalone
                        .with_peer_pin_in_copy(
                            "controller_zero_or_more",
                            crate::mock::pairings::controller_zero_or_more::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                            member.instance_id.clone(),
                            crate::mock::pairings::controller_zero_or_more::PEER_LINK_ID,
                            copy.clone(),
                        )
                }
                None => {
                    standalone
                        .with_peer_pin(
                            "controller_zero_or_more",
                            crate::mock::pairings::controller_zero_or_more::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                            member.instance_id.clone(),
                            crate::mock::pairings::controller_zero_or_more::PEER_LINK_ID,
                        )
                }
            };
        }
        standalone = standalone
            .with_peer_pin(
                "sensor_bus",
                crate::mock::pairings::sensor_bus::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                crate::mock::pairings::sensor_bus::MOCK_INSTANCE_ID,
                crate::mock::pairings::sensor_bus::PEER_LINK_ID,
            );
        standalone = standalone
            .with_observed_source(
                "watchers_one",
                crate::mock::observed::watchers_one::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                crate::mock::observed::watchers_one::MOCK_INSTANCE_ID,
                crate::mock::observed::watchers_one::SOURCE_LINK_ID,
            );
        if !config.watchers_zero_or_one_vacant {
            standalone = standalone
                .with_observed_source(
                    "watchers_zero_or_one",
                    crate::mock::observed::watchers_zero_or_one::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                    crate::mock::observed::watchers_zero_or_one::MOCK_INSTANCE_ID,
                    crate::mock::observed::watchers_zero_or_one::SOURCE_LINK_ID,
                );
        }
        for member_instance_id in &watchers_one_or_more_member_ids {
            standalone = standalone
                .with_observed_source(
                    "watchers_one_or_more",
                    crate::mock::observed::watchers_one_or_more::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                    member_instance_id.clone(),
                    crate::mock::observed::watchers_one_or_more::SOURCE_LINK_ID,
                );
        }
        for member_instance_id in &watchers_zero_or_more_member_ids {
            standalone = standalone
                .with_observed_source(
                    "watchers_zero_or_more",
                    crate::mock::observed::watchers_zero_or_more::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                    member_instance_id.clone(),
                    crate::mock::observed::watchers_zero_or_more::SOURCE_LINK_ID,
                );
        }
        let mut publisher_readiness: Vec<peppylib::testing::PublisherReadiness> = Vec::new();
        publisher_readiness
            .push(peppylib::testing::PublisherReadiness {
                target: peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::pairing("arm_link", "v1")?,
                link_id: Some("controller_one".to_string()),
                peer: Some(crate::mock::pairings::controller_one::peer_info()),
                topic: "joint_states".to_string(),
            });
        publisher_readiness
            .push(peppylib::testing::PublisherReadiness {
                target: peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::pairing("arm_link", "v1")?,
                link_id: Some("controller_zero_or_one".to_string()),
                peer: Some(crate::mock::pairings::controller_zero_or_one::peer_info()),
                topic: "joint_states".to_string(),
            });
        for member in &controller_one_or_more_member_specs {
            publisher_readiness
                .push(peppylib::testing::PublisherReadiness {
                    target: peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::pairing(
                        "arm_link",
                        "v1",
                    )?,
                    link_id: Some("controller_one_or_more".to_string()),
                    peer: Some(
                        crate::mock::pairings::controller_one_or_more::peer_info_for(
                            &member.instance_id,
                        ),
                    ),
                    topic: "joint_states".to_string(),
                });
        }
        for member in &controller_zero_or_more_member_specs {
            publisher_readiness
                .push(peppylib::testing::PublisherReadiness {
                    target: peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::pairing(
                        "arm_link",
                        "v1",
                    )?,
                    link_id: Some("controller_zero_or_more".to_string()),
                    peer: Some(
                        crate::mock::pairings::controller_zero_or_more::peer_info_for(
                            &member.instance_id,
                        ),
                    ),
                    topic: "joint_states".to_string(),
                });
        }
        publisher_readiness
            .push(peppylib::testing::PublisherReadiness {
                target: peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("relay_node", "v1")?,
                link_id: None,
                peer: None,
                topic: "video_stream".to_string(),
            });
        publisher_readiness
            .push(peppylib::testing::PublisherReadiness {
                target: peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::contract(
                    "camera_feed",
                    "v1",
                )?,
                link_id: None,
                peer: None,
                topic: "video_stream".to_string(),
            });
        publisher_readiness
            .push(peppylib::testing::PublisherReadiness {
                target: peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("relay_node", "v1")?,
                link_id: None,
                peer: None,
                topic: "heartbeat".to_string(),
            });
        publisher_readiness
            .push(peppylib::testing::PublisherReadiness {
                target: peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("relay_node", "v1")?,
                link_id: None,
                peer: None,
                topic: "camera_state".to_string(),
            });
        let mut service_readiness: Vec<peppylib::testing::ServiceReadiness> = Vec::new();
        service_readiness.push(clock.readiness()?);
        service_readiness
            .push(peppylib::testing::ServiceReadiness {
                target: peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("uvc_camera", "v1")?,
                name: "enable_camera".to_string(),
                producer: crate::mock::deps::camera_one::producer_ref_for(
                    crate::mock::deps::camera_one::MOCK_INSTANCE_ID,
                ),
                kind: peppylib::testing::ServiceReadinessKind::Service,
            });
        if !config.camera_zero_or_one_vacant {
            service_readiness
                .push(peppylib::testing::ServiceReadiness {
                    target: peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("uvc_camera", "v1")?,
                    name: "enable_camera".to_string(),
                    producer: crate::mock::deps::camera_zero_or_one::producer_ref_for(
                        crate::mock::deps::camera_zero_or_one::MOCK_INSTANCE_ID,
                    ),
                    kind: peppylib::testing::ServiceReadinessKind::Service,
                });
        }
        for member_instance_id in &camera_one_or_more_member_ids {
            let member_instance_id = member_instance_id.as_str();
            service_readiness
                .push(peppylib::testing::ServiceReadiness {
                    target: peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("uvc_camera", "v1")?,
                    name: "enable_camera".to_string(),
                    producer: crate::mock::deps::camera_one_or_more::producer_ref_for(
                        member_instance_id,
                    ),
                    kind: peppylib::testing::ServiceReadinessKind::Service,
                });
            service_readiness
                .push(peppylib::testing::ServiceReadiness {
                    target: peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("uvc_camera", "v1")?,
                    name: "move_arm".to_string(),
                    producer: crate::mock::deps::camera_one_or_more::producer_ref_for(
                        member_instance_id,
                    ),
                    kind: peppylib::testing::ServiceReadinessKind::Action,
                });
        }
        for member_instance_id in &camera_zero_or_more_member_ids {
            let member_instance_id = member_instance_id.as_str();
            service_readiness
                .push(peppylib::testing::ServiceReadiness {
                    target: peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("uvc_camera", "v1")?,
                    name: "enable_camera".to_string(),
                    producer: crate::mock::deps::camera_zero_or_more::producer_ref_for(
                        member_instance_id,
                    ),
                    kind: peppylib::testing::ServiceReadinessKind::Service,
                });
        }
        service_readiness
            .push(peppylib::testing::ServiceReadiness {
                target: peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::contract(
                    "arm_control",
                    "v1",
                )?,
                name: "move_arm".to_string(),
                producer: crate::mock::deps::left_arm::producer_ref_for(
                    crate::mock::deps::left_arm::MOCK_INSTANCE_ID,
                ),
                kind: peppylib::testing::ServiceReadinessKind::Action,
            });
        let core = peppylib::testing::HarnessCore::start::<
            crate::Parameters,
            _,
            _,
        >(PEPPY_CONFIG_PATH, standalone, &publisher_readiness, &service_readiness, setup)
            .await?;
        let harness = Harness {
            core,
            emitted: Emitted {
                video_stream,
                feed_video_stream,
                heartbeat,
                camera_state,
            },
            clock,
            session,
            router: Some(router),
            instance_id,
        };
        let mocks = Mocks {
            deps: DepMocks {
                camera_one,
                camera_zero_or_one,
                camera_one_or_more,
                camera_zero_or_more,
                left_arm,
            },
            pairings: PairingMocks {
                controller_one,
                controller_zero_or_one,
                controller_one_or_more,
                controller_zero_or_more,
                sensor_bus,
            },
            observed: ObservedMocks {
                watchers_one,
                watchers_zero_or_one,
                watchers_one_or_more,
                watchers_zero_or_more,
            },
        };
        Ok((harness, mocks))
    }
    /// The running node, for calling its runtime surface directly.
    pub fn node_runner(&self) -> &std::sync::Arc<crate::NodeRunner> {
        self.core.node_runner()
    }
    /// The fixture caller/observer session (not the node's).
    pub fn session(&self) -> &peppylib::MessengerHandle {
        &self.session
    }
    /// The node-under-test's instance id.
    pub fn instance_id(&self) -> &str {
        &self.instance_id
    }
    /// The node-under-test's wire identity.
    pub fn node_producer_ref(&self) -> peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef {
        peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef::new(
            self.core.bound_core_node(),
            &self.instance_id,
        )
    }
    /// Whether the node's `setup` has returned.
    pub fn setup_finished(&self) -> bool {
        self.core.setup_finished()
    }
    /// Tears the fixture down in lifecycle order: node convergence
    /// (cancel → bounded setup await → shutdown hooks, propagating a
    /// setup error), then the clock stand-in, then the fixture
    /// session, then the router.
    pub async fn shutdown(self) -> crate::Result<()> {
        let Harness { core, emitted, clock, session, router, .. } = self;
        let result = core.shutdown().await;
        drop(clock);
        drop(emitted);
        drop(session);
        if let Some(router) = router {
            router.shutdown().await?;
        }
        result
    }
}

EmittedTopic :: rich_stream
================================================================
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct MessagePoseFrame {
    pub id: u32,
    pub name: String,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct MessagePose {
    pub x: f64,
    pub frame: MessagePoseFrame,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct MessageCalibration {
    pub gain: f32,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct MessagePointsItem {
    pub x: f32,
    pub tag: String,
}
#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
pub fn build_message(
    max_speed: f32,
    type_: String,
    payload: Vec<u8>,
    stamp: std::time::SystemTime,
    note: Option<String>,
    pose: MessagePose,
    calibration: Option<MessageCalibration>,
    digest: [u8; 4],
    raw: Vec<u8>,
    gains: [f32; 3],
    readings: Vec<i16>,
    names: Vec<String>,
    chunks: Vec<Vec<u8>>,
    limits: Option<Vec<f64>>,
    points: Vec<MessagePointsItem>,
) -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {
    let payload = {
        let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
        {
            let mut root = capnp_msg
                .init_root::<
                    crate::capnp::rich_stream_message_capnp::rich_stream_message::Builder,
                >();
            root.set_max_speed(max_speed);
            root.set_type(type_.as_str());
            root.set_payload(payload.as_ref());
            let timestamp_0 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time(stamp);
            let mut timestamp_builder_1 = root.reborrow().init_stamp();
            timestamp_builder_1.set_sec(timestamp_0.sec);
            timestamp_builder_1.set_nsec(timestamp_0.nsec);
            if let Some(value_2) = (note).as_ref() {
                root.set_note(value_2.as_str());
            }
            let mut builder_3 = root.reborrow().init_pose();
            builder_3.set_x((pose).x);
            let mut builder_4 = builder_3.reborrow().init_frame();
            builder_4.set_id(((pose).frame).id);
            builder_4.set_name(((pose).frame).name.as_str());
            if let Some(value_5) = (calibration).as_ref() {
                let mut builder_6 = root.reborrow().init_calibration();
                builder_6.set_gain(*&(value_5).gain);
            }
            root.set_digest(digest.as_ref());
            root.set_raw(raw.as_ref());
            let mut list_7 = root.reborrow().init_gains(3);
            for (idx_8, value_9) in (gains).iter().enumerate() {
                list_7.set(idx_8 as u32, *value_9);
            }
            let mut list_10 = root
                .reborrow()
                .init_readings(
                    u32::try_from((readings).len())
                        .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                );
            for (idx_11, value_12) in (readings).iter().enumerate() {
                list_10.set(idx_11 as u32, *value_12);
            }
            let mut list_13 = root
                .reborrow()
                .init_names(
                    u32::try_from((names).len()).expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                );
            for (idx_14, value_15) in (names).iter().enumerate() {
                list_13.set(idx_14 as u32, value_15.as_str());
            }
            let mut list_16 = root
                .reborrow()
                .init_chunks(
                    u32::try_from((chunks).len()).expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                );
            for (idx_17, value_18) in (chunks).iter().enumerate() {
                list_16.set(idx_17 as u32, value_18.as_ref());
            }
            if let Some(value_19) = (limits).as_ref() {
                let mut list_20 = root
                    .reborrow()
                    .init_limits(
                        u32::try_from((value_19).len())
                            .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                    );
                for (idx_21, value_22) in (value_19).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_20.set(idx_21 as u32, *value_22);
                }
            }
            let mut list_23 = root
                .reborrow()
                .init_points(
                    u32::try_from((points).len()).expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                );
            for (idx_24, element_25) in (points).iter().enumerate() {
                let mut builder_26 = list_23.reborrow().get(idx_24 as u32);
                builder_26.set_x(element_25.x);
                builder_26.set_tag(element_25.tag.as_str());
            }
        }
        let mut buffer = Vec::new();
        capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
            .map_err(|source| {
                let context = String::from("rich_stream");
                crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
            })?;
        peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
    };
    Ok(payload)
}
/// Declares the topic publisher: takes the central messenger lock once
/// and returns a lock-free `peppylib::TopicPublisher`. Declare once,
/// then call `publisher.publish(build_message(...)?)` per message.
pub async fn declare_publisher(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
) -> crate::Result<peppylib::TopicPublisher> {
    let qos = peppylib::config::QoSProfile::SensorData;
    let retention = peppylib::config::TopicRetention::LiveOnly;
    let as_topic = "rich_stream";
    let as_instance_id = node_runner.processor().bound_instance_id();
    let with_core_node = node_runner.processor().bound_core_node();
    let as_target = peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node(
        node_runner.processor().node_name(),
        node_runner.processor().node_tag(),
    )?;
    let publisher = peppylib::TopicMessenger::declare_publisher(
            node_runner.messenger(),
            with_core_node,
            as_instance_id,
            as_target,
            None,
            as_topic,
            qos,
            retention,
        )
        .await?;
    Ok(publisher)
}


================================================================
ExposedService :: rich_service
================================================================
const SERVICE_NAME: &str = "rich_service";
/// A failure kind of this member: a kind of peppy, or a kind of its
/// contract that it lists.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum FailureKind {
    /// The attempt gave no result
    AttemptFailed,
    /// The bound of the provider passed
    Timeout,
    /// A precondition failed
    PreconditionFailed,
    /// A robot, limb or host is gone
    ResourceUnavailable,
    /// The provider refused the goal, and no other kind says why
    Rejected,
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl FailureKind {
    /// Every kind of this member: the kinds of peppy, then the
    /// kinds of its contract that it lists.
    pub const ALL: [Self; 5] = [
        Self::AttemptFailed,
        Self::Timeout,
        Self::PreconditionFailed,
        Self::ResourceUnavailable,
        Self::Rejected,
    ];
    /// The name of the kind on the wire.
    pub const fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::AttemptFailed => "attempt_failed",
            Self::Timeout => "timeout",
            Self::PreconditionFailed => "precondition_failed",
            Self::ResourceUnavailable => "resource_unavailable",
            Self::Rejected => "rejected",
        }
    }
    /// The kind named `name`, or `None` when this member does not
    /// declare it.
    pub fn parse(name: &str) -> Option<Self> {
        Self::ALL.into_iter().find(|kind| kind.as_str() == name)
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for FailureKind {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.write_str(self.as_str())
    }
}
/// Why this member refused or failed a goal, or gave an error
/// reply: a kind that it declares, and a message for a person.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Failure {
    pub kind: FailureKind,
    pub message: String,
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl Failure {
    pub fn new(kind: FailureKind, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            kind,
            message: message.into(),
        }
    }
    /// The wire form of the failure.
    pub(crate) fn to_wire(&self) -> peppylib::messaging::Failure {
        let kind = peppylib::messaging::FailureKindName::parse(self.kind.as_str())
            .expect("each kind of a member is a valid failure kind name");
        peppylib::messaging::Failure::new(kind, self.message.clone())
    }
    /// Parses a failure from the wire. A kind that this member does
    /// not declare is a deserialization error, whose text keeps the
    /// message of the provider.
    pub(crate) fn from_wire(
        failure: &peppylib::messaging::Failure,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let kind = FailureKind::parse(failure.kind().as_str())
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!(
                        "the failure kind `{}` is not a kind of `{}` (message: {:?})",
                        failure.kind(), SERVICE_NAME, failure.message()
                    ),
                )
            })?;
        Ok(Self::new(kind, failure.message()))
    }
    /// The failure of the error reply that `error` holds: `None`
    /// when `error` is not an error reply of a provider, or when
    /// its kind is not a kind of this member.
    pub fn of(error: &crate::Error) -> Option<Self> {
        let crate::Error::ServiceFailed { failure, .. } = error else {
            return None;
        };
        Self::from_wire(failure).ok()
    }
    /// `error` itself, unless it is an error reply with a kind this
    /// member does not declare: such a reply breaks the contract of
    /// the member, and gives a deserialization error instead.
    pub(crate) fn reject_undeclared_kind(error: crate::Error) -> crate::Error {
        let crate::Error::ServiceFailed { failure, .. } = &error else {
            return error;
        };
        match Self::from_wire(failure) {
            Ok(_) => error,
            Err(undeclared) => undeclared,
        }
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for Failure {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{}: {}", self.kind, self.message)
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Request {
    pub instance_id: String,
    pub core_node: String,
    pub data: RequestData,
}
impl Response {
    pub fn new(
        max_speed: f32,
        type_: String,
        payload: Vec<u8>,
        stamp: std::time::SystemTime,
        note: Option<String>,
        pose: ResponsePose,
        calibration: Option<ResponseCalibration>,
        digest: [u8; 4],
        raw: Vec<u8>,
        gains: [f32; 3],
        readings: Vec<i16>,
        names: Vec<String>,
        chunks: Vec<Vec<u8>>,
        limits: Option<Vec<f64>>,
        points: Vec<ResponsePointsItem>,
    ) -> Self {
        Self {
            max_speed,
            type_,
            payload,
            stamp,
            note,
            pose,
            calibration,
            digest,
            raw,
            gains,
            readings,
            names,
            chunks,
            limits,
            points,
        }
    }
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct RequestData {
    pub max_speed: f32,
    pub type_: String,
    pub payload: Vec<u8>,
    pub stamp: std::time::SystemTime,
    pub note: Option<String>,
    pub pose: RichServicePose,
    pub calibration: Option<RichServiceCalibration>,
    pub digest: [u8; 4],
    pub raw: Vec<u8>,
    pub gains: [f32; 3],
    pub readings: Vec<i16>,
    pub names: Vec<String>,
    pub chunks: Vec<Vec<u8>>,
    pub limits: Option<Vec<f64>>,
    pub points: Vec<RichServicePointsItem>,
}
/// Answers the next request of this service with `handler`. The
/// response of the handler is a success; its `Failure` gives the
/// caller an error reply of a kind that this member declares. A
/// request that does not decode gets an error reply of the kind
/// `rejected`, and a response that does not serialize one of
/// the kind `attempt_failed`.
pub async fn handle_next_request<F>(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
    handler: F,
) -> crate::Result<()>
where
    F: Fn(Request) -> std::result::Result<Response, Failure>,
{
    let mut service = peppylib::ServiceMessenger::listen(
            node_runner.messenger(),
            node_runner.processor().bound_core_node(),
            node_runner.processor().bound_instance_id(),
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node(
                node_runner.processor().node_name(),
                node_runner.processor().node_tag(),
            )?,
            SERVICE_NAME,
        )
        .await?;
    service
        .handle_next_request(move |request_context| {
            async move {
                {
                    let message = request_context.message();
                    let payload = message.payload_bytes();
                    let core_node = message.core_node().to_string();
                    let instance_id = message.instance_id().to_string();
                    handle_request_payload(
                        payload.as_ref(),
                        &handler,
                        core_node,
                        instance_id,
                    )
                }
                    .map_err(|failure| failure.to_wire())
            }
        })
        .await?;
    Ok(())
}
fn serialize_response(response: Response) -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {
    Ok({
        let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
        {
            let mut root = capnp_msg
                .init_root::<
                    crate::capnp::rich_service_response_message_capnp::rich_service_response_message::Builder,
                >();
            root.set_max_speed(response.max_speed);
            root.set_type(response.type_.as_str());
            root.set_payload(response.payload.as_ref());
            let timestamp_0 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time(response.stamp);
            let mut timestamp_builder_1 = root.reborrow().init_stamp();
            timestamp_builder_1.set_sec(timestamp_0.sec);
            timestamp_builder_1.set_nsec(timestamp_0.nsec);
            if let Some(value_2) = (response.note).as_ref() {
                root.set_note(value_2.as_str());
            }
            let mut builder_3 = root.reborrow().init_pose();
            builder_3.set_x((response.pose).x);
            let mut builder_4 = builder_3.reborrow().init_frame();
            builder_4.set_id(((response.pose).frame).id);
            builder_4.set_name(((response.pose).frame).name.as_str());
            if let Some(value_5) = (response.calibration).as_ref() {
                let mut builder_6 = root.reborrow().init_calibration();
                builder_6.set_gain(*&(value_5).gain);
            }
            root.set_digest(response.digest.as_ref());
            root.set_raw(response.raw.as_ref());
            let mut list_7 = root.reborrow().init_gains(3);
            for (idx_8, value_9) in (response.gains).iter().enumerate() {
                list_7.set(idx_8 as u32, *value_9);
            }
            let mut list_10 = root
                .reborrow()
                .init_readings(
                    u32::try_from((response.readings).len())
                        .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                );
            for (idx_11, value_12) in (response.readings).iter().enumerate() {
                list_10.set(idx_11 as u32, *value_12);
            }
            let mut list_13 = root
                .reborrow()
                .init_names(
                    u32::try_from((response.names).len())
                        .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                );
            for (idx_14, value_15) in (response.names).iter().enumerate() {
                list_13.set(idx_14 as u32, value_15.as_str());
            }
            let mut list_16 = root
                .reborrow()
                .init_chunks(
                    u32::try_from((response.chunks).len())
                        .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                );
            for (idx_17, value_18) in (response.chunks).iter().enumerate() {
                list_16.set(idx_17 as u32, value_18.as_ref());
            }
            if let Some(value_19) = (response.limits).as_ref() {
                let mut list_20 = root
                    .reborrow()
                    .init_limits(
                        u32::try_from((value_19).len())
                            .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                    );
                for (idx_21, value_22) in (value_19).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_20.set(idx_21 as u32, *value_22);
                }
            }
            let mut list_23 = root
                .reborrow()
                .init_points(
                    u32::try_from((response.points).len())
                        .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                );
            for (idx_24, element_25) in (response.points).iter().enumerate() {
                let mut builder_26 = list_23.reborrow().get(idx_24 as u32);
                builder_26.set_x(element_25.x);
                builder_26.set_tag(element_25.tag.as_str());
            }
        }
        let mut buffer = Vec::new();
        capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
            .map_err(|source| {
                let context = format!("handle_request_payload {}", SERVICE_NAME);
                crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
            })?;
        peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
    })
}
fn deserialize_request(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<RequestData> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]
    let context = String::from(SERVICE_NAME);
    let mut payload = payload;
    let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader
        .get_root::<
            crate::capnp::rich_service_message_capnp::rich_service_message::Reader,
        >()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let max_speed_0 = root.reborrow().get_max_speed();
    let text_2 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_type()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from(SERVICE_NAME);
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "type", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let type_1 = text_2
        .to_str()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from(SERVICE_NAME);
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "type", context, source),
            )
        })?
        .to_owned();
    let data_4 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_payload()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from(SERVICE_NAME);
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "payload", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let payload_3 = data_4.to_vec();
    let timestamp_6 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_stamp()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from(SERVICE_NAME);
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "stamp", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let capnp_timestamp_7 = peppylib::encoding::CapnpTimestamp {
        sec: timestamp_6.get_sec(),
        nsec: timestamp_6.get_nsec(),
    };
    let stamp_5 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time_from_capnp(capnp_timestamp_7);
    let note_opt_8 = if root.reborrow().has_note() {
        let text_10 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_note()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from(SERVICE_NAME);
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "note", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let note_9 = text_10
            .to_str()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from(SERVICE_NAME);
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "note", context, source),
                )
            })?
            .to_owned();
        Some(note_9)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let reader_11 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_pose()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from(SERVICE_NAME);
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "pose", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let x_12 = reader_11.reborrow().get_x();
    let reader_13 = reader_11
        .reborrow()
        .get_frame()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from(SERVICE_NAME);
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "frame", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let id_14 = reader_13.reborrow().get_id();
    let text_16 = reader_13
        .reborrow()
        .get_name()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from(SERVICE_NAME);
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "name", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let name_15 = text_16
        .to_str()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from(SERVICE_NAME);
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "name", context, source),
            )
        })?
        .to_owned();
    let frame_17 = RichServicePoseFrame {
        id: id_14,
        name: name_15,
    };
    let pose_18 = RichServicePose {
        x: x_12,
        frame: frame_17,
    };
    let calibration_opt_19 = if root.reborrow().has_calibration() {
        let reader_20 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_calibration()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from(SERVICE_NAME);
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "calibration", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let gain_21 = reader_20.reborrow().get_gain();
        let calibration_22 = RichServiceCalibration {
            gain: gain_21,
        };
        Some(calibration_22)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let data_23 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_digest()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from(SERVICE_NAME);
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "digest", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    if data_23.len() as usize != 4 {
        let actual = data_23.len() as usize;
        return Err(
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!(
                    "invalid fixed bytes length for field '{}': expected {}, got {}",
                    "digest", 4, actual
                ),
            ),
        );
    }
    let mut bytes_25 = [0u8; 4];
    for (idx, byte) in data_23.iter().enumerate() {
        bytes_25[idx] = *byte;
    }
    let digest_24 = bytes_25;
    let data_26 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_raw()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from(SERVICE_NAME);
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "raw", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let raw_27 = data_26.to_vec();
    let list_28 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_gains()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from(SERVICE_NAME);
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "gains", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    if list_28.len() as usize != 3 {
        let actual = list_28.len() as usize;
        return Err(
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!(
                    "invalid fixed list length for field '{}': expected {}, got {}",
                    "gains", 3, actual
                ),
            ),
        );
    }
    let mut gains_29: [f32; 3] = [f32::default(); 3];
    for (idx, element) in list_28.iter().enumerate() {
        gains_29[idx] = element;
    }
    let list_30 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_readings()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from(SERVICE_NAME);
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "readings", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let readings_31 = list_30.iter().collect::<Vec<i16>>();
    let list_32 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_names()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from(SERVICE_NAME);
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "names", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let names_33 = list_32
        .iter()
        .map(|element| {
            (element
                .map_err(|source| source.to_string())
                .and_then(|text| {
                    text.to_str().map(str::to_owned).map_err(|source| source.to_string())
                }))
                .map_err(|source| {
                    #[allow(clippy::all)]
                    let context = String::from(SERVICE_NAME);
                    crate::Error::Deserialization(
                        format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "names", context, source),
                    )
                })
        })
        .collect::<::std::result::Result<Vec<String>, crate::Error>>()?;
    let list_34 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_chunks()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from(SERVICE_NAME);
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "chunks", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let chunks_35 = list_34
        .iter()
        .map(|element| {
            (element.map(|data| data.to_vec()).map_err(|source| source.to_string()))
                .map_err(|source| {
                    #[allow(clippy::all)]
                    let context = String::from(SERVICE_NAME);
                    crate::Error::Deserialization(
                        format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "chunks", context, source),
                    )
                })
        })
        .collect::<::std::result::Result<Vec<Vec<u8>>, crate::Error>>()?;
    let limits_opt_36 = if root.reborrow().has_limits() {
        let list_37 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_limits()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from(SERVICE_NAME);
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "limits", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let limits_38 = list_37.iter().collect::<Vec<f64>>();
        Some(limits_38)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let list_39 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_points()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from(SERVICE_NAME);
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "points", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let mut result_40 = Vec::with_capacity(list_39.len() as usize);
    for element_42 in list_39.iter() {
        let x_43 = element_42.reborrow().get_x();
        let text_45 = element_42
            .reborrow()
            .get_tag()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from(SERVICE_NAME);
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "tag", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let tag_44 = text_45
            .to_str()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from(SERVICE_NAME);
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "tag", context, source),
                )
            })?
            .to_owned();
        result_40
            .push(RichServicePointsItem {
                x: x_43,
                tag: tag_44,
            });
    }
    let points_41 = result_40;
    Ok(RequestData {
        max_speed: max_speed_0,
        type_: type_1,
        payload: payload_3,
        stamp: stamp_5,
        note: note_opt_8,
        pose: pose_18,
        calibration: calibration_opt_19,
        digest: digest_24,
        raw: raw_27,
        gains: gains_29,
        readings: readings_31,
        names: names_33,
        chunks: chunks_35,
        limits: limits_opt_36,
        points: points_41,
    })
}
fn handle_request_payload<F>(
    payload: &[u8],
    handler: &F,
    core_node: String,
    instance_id: String,
) -> std::result::Result<peppylib::Payload, Failure>
where
    F: Fn(Request) -> std::result::Result<Response, Failure>,
{
    let request_data = deserialize_request(payload)
        .map_err(|error| {
            Failure::new(
                FailureKind::Rejected,
                format!("request does not decode: {error}"),
            )
        })?;
    let request = Request {
        instance_id,
        core_node,
        data: request_data,
    };
    let response = handler(request)?;
    serialize_response(response)
        .map_err(|error| {
            Failure::new(
                FailureKind::AttemptFailed,
                format!("response does not serialize: {error}"),
            )
        })
}

ExposedAction :: rich_action
