const ACTION_NAME: &str = "rich_action";
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct GoalRequest {
    pub instance_id: String,
    pub core_node: String,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct RichActionFeedbackPoseFrame {
    pub id: u32,
    pub name: String,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct RichActionFeedbackPose {
    pub x: f64,
    pub frame: RichActionFeedbackPoseFrame,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct RichActionFeedbackCalibration {
    pub gain: f32,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct RichActionFeedbackPointsItem {
    pub x: f32,
    pub tag: String,
}
/// A failure kind of this member: a kind of peppy, or a kind of its
/// contract that it lists.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum FailureKind {
    /// The attempt gave no result
    AttemptFailed,
    /// The bound of the provider passed
    Timeout,
    /// A precondition failed
    PreconditionFailed,
    /// A robot, limb or host is gone
    ResourceUnavailable,
    /// The provider refused the goal, and no other kind says why
    Rejected,
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl FailureKind {
    /// Every kind of this member: the kinds of peppy, then the
    /// kinds of its contract that it lists.
    pub const ALL: [Self; 5] = [
        Self::AttemptFailed,
        Self::Timeout,
        Self::PreconditionFailed,
        Self::ResourceUnavailable,
        Self::Rejected,
    ];
    /// The name of the kind on the wire.
    pub const fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::AttemptFailed => "attempt_failed",
            Self::Timeout => "timeout",
            Self::PreconditionFailed => "precondition_failed",
            Self::ResourceUnavailable => "resource_unavailable",
            Self::Rejected => "rejected",
        }
    }
    /// The kind named `name`, or `None` when this member does not
    /// declare it.
    pub fn parse(name: &str) -> Option<Self> {
        Self::ALL.into_iter().find(|kind| kind.as_str() == name)
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for FailureKind {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.write_str(self.as_str())
    }
}
/// Why this member refused or failed a goal, or gave an error
/// reply: a kind that it declares, and a message for a person.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Failure {
    pub kind: FailureKind,
    pub message: String,
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl Failure {
    pub fn new(kind: FailureKind, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            kind,
            message: message.into(),
        }
    }
    /// The wire form of the failure.
    pub(crate) fn to_wire(&self) -> peppylib::messaging::Failure {
        let kind = peppylib::messaging::FailureKindName::parse(self.kind.as_str())
            .expect("each kind of a member is a valid failure kind name");
        peppylib::messaging::Failure::new(kind, self.message.clone())
    }
    /// Parses a failure from the wire. A kind that this member does
    /// not declare is a deserialization error, whose text keeps the
    /// message of the provider.
    pub(crate) fn from_wire(
        failure: &peppylib::messaging::Failure,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let kind = FailureKind::parse(failure.kind().as_str())
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!(
                        "the failure kind `{}` is not a kind of `{}` (message: {:?})",
                        failure.kind(), ACTION_NAME, failure.message()
                    ),
                )
            })?;
        Ok(Self::new(kind, failure.message()))
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for Failure {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{}: {}", self.kind, self.message)
    }
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum GoalDecision {
    Accept,
    Refuse(Failure),
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl GoalDecision {
    /// Accepts the goal.
    pub fn accept() -> Self {
        Self::Accept
    }
    /// Refuses the goal with a failure kind that this member
    /// declares and a message for a person. A refusal carries no
    /// goal response.
    pub fn refuse(kind: FailureKind, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self::Refuse(Failure::new(kind, message))
    }
}
pub struct ActionHandle {
    inner: peppylib::messaging::ConcurrentAction,
}
pub struct GoalContext {
    inner: peppylib::messaging::GoalContext,
    request: GoalRequest,
}
impl ActionHandle {
    pub async fn expose(node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let inner = peppylib::messaging::ConcurrentAction::expose(
                node_runner.messenger(),
                node_runner.processor().bound_core_node(),
                node_runner.processor().bound_instance_id(),
                peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node(
                    node_runner.processor().node_name(),
                    node_runner.processor().node_tag(),
                )?,
                ACTION_NAME,
                true,
            )
            .await?;
        Ok(Self { inner })
    }
    /// Sets the sender grace of the action: how long it keeps the goals
    /// of a sender that is gone before they receive their cancel signal,
    /// with the cause `CancelCause::SenderGone`. Zero by default. It
    /// applies to each sender found gone after the call.
    pub fn set_sender_grace(&self, grace: std::time::Duration) {
        self.inner.set_sender_grace(grace);
    }
    pub async fn handle_goal_next_request<F>(
        &mut self,
        decider: F,
    ) -> crate::Result<Option<GoalContext>>
    where
        F: Fn(&GoalRequest) -> crate::Result<GoalDecision>,
    {
        loop {
            let Some(pending) = self.inner.recv_next_goal().await? else {
                return Ok(None);
            };
            let request = GoalRequest {
                instance_id: pending.instance_id().to_string(),
                core_node: pending.core_node().to_string(),
            };
            let decision = match decider(&request) {
                Ok(decision) => decision,
                Err(error) => {
                    let refusal = Failure::new(
                        FailureKind::AttemptFailed,
                        format!("the goal decider failed: {error}"),
                    );
                    pending.refuse(refusal.to_wire()).await?;
                    continue;
                }
            };
            match decision {
                GoalDecision::Accept => {
                    let inner = pending.accept(peppylib::Payload::new()).await?;
                    return Ok(Some(GoalContext { inner, request }));
                }
                GoalDecision::Refuse(failure) => {
                    pending.refuse(failure.to_wire()).await?;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
impl GoalContext {
    /// The decoded goal request (caller identity and, if any, data).
    pub fn request(&self) -> &GoalRequest {
        &self.request
    }
    /// The client-generated correlation id for this goal.
    pub fn goal_id(&self) -> &str {
        self.inner.goal_id()
    }
    /// The process that sent this goal. The action cancels the goal
    /// when this process is gone and its sender grace passed.
    pub fn sender(&self) -> &peppylib::messaging::ClientPresence {
        self.inner.sender()
    }
    /// Resolves when this goal receives its cancel signal: a cancel
    /// request of its caller, or the end of its sender (see
    /// `cancel_cause`). React by calling `complete_cancelled`.
    pub async fn cancel_signal(&self) {
        self.inner.cancel_signal().await
    }
    /// Whether this goal received its cancel signal.
    pub fn is_cancelled(&self) -> bool {
        self.inner.is_cancelled()
    }
    /// Why this goal received its cancel signal; `None` before it did.
    /// `CancelCause::reason` gives a reason for `complete_cancelled`.
    pub fn cancel_cause(&self) -> Option<peppylib::messaging::CancelCause> {
        self.inner.cancel_cause()
    }
    /// Ends the goal with the status failed: a failure kind that this
    /// member declares, a message for a person, and no result. The
    /// first of `complete`, `fail` and `complete_cancelled` ends the
    /// goal; later calls do nothing.
    pub async fn fail(
        &self,
        kind: FailureKind,
        message: impl Into<String>,
    ) -> crate::Result<()> {
        self.inner.fail(Failure::new(kind, message).to_wire()).await?;
        Ok(())
    }
    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub async fn publish_feedback(
        &self,
        max_speed: f32,
        type_: String,
        payload: Vec<u8>,
        stamp: std::time::SystemTime,
        note: Option<String>,
        pose: RichActionFeedbackPose,
        calibration: Option<RichActionFeedbackCalibration>,
        digest: [u8; 4],
        raw: Vec<u8>,
        gains: [f32; 3],
        readings: Vec<i16>,
        names: Vec<String>,
        chunks: Vec<Vec<u8>>,
        limits: Option<Vec<f64>>,
        points: Vec<RichActionFeedbackPointsItem>,
    ) -> crate::Result<()> {
        let payload = {
            let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
            {
                let mut root = capnp_msg
                    .init_root::<
                        crate::capnp::emit_rich_action_feedback_message_capnp::rich_action_feedback_message::Builder,
                    >();
                root.set_max_speed(max_speed);
                root.set_type(type_.as_str());
                root.set_payload(payload.as_ref());
                let timestamp_0 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time(stamp);
                let mut timestamp_builder_1 = root.reborrow().init_stamp();
                timestamp_builder_1.set_sec(timestamp_0.sec);
                timestamp_builder_1.set_nsec(timestamp_0.nsec);
                if let Some(value_2) = (note).as_ref() {
                    root.set_note(value_2.as_str());
                }
                let mut builder_3 = root.reborrow().init_pose();
                builder_3.set_x((pose).x);
                let mut builder_4 = builder_3.reborrow().init_frame();
                builder_4.set_id(((pose).frame).id);
                builder_4.set_name(((pose).frame).name.as_str());
                if let Some(value_5) = (calibration).as_ref() {
                    let mut builder_6 = root.reborrow().init_calibration();
                    builder_6.set_gain(*&(value_5).gain);
                }
                root.set_digest(digest.as_ref());
                root.set_raw(raw.as_ref());
                let mut list_7 = root.reborrow().init_gains(3);
                for (idx_8, value_9) in (gains).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_7.set(idx_8 as u32, *value_9);
                }
                let mut list_10 = root
                    .reborrow()
                    .init_readings(
                        u32::try_from((readings).len())
                            .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                    );
                for (idx_11, value_12) in (readings).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_10.set(idx_11 as u32, *value_12);
                }
                let mut list_13 = root
                    .reborrow()
                    .init_names(
                        u32::try_from((names).len())
                            .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                    );
                for (idx_14, value_15) in (names).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_13.set(idx_14 as u32, value_15.as_str());
                }
                let mut list_16 = root
                    .reborrow()
                    .init_chunks(
                        u32::try_from((chunks).len())
                            .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                    );
                for (idx_17, value_18) in (chunks).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_16.set(idx_17 as u32, value_18.as_ref());
                }
                if let Some(value_19) = (limits).as_ref() {
                    let mut list_20 = root
                        .reborrow()
                        .init_limits(
                            u32::try_from((value_19).len())
                                .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                        );
                    for (idx_21, value_22) in (value_19).iter().enumerate() {
                        list_20.set(idx_21 as u32, *value_22);
                    }
                }
                let mut list_23 = root
                    .reborrow()
                    .init_points(
                        u32::try_from((points).len())
                            .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                    );
                for (idx_24, element_25) in (points).iter().enumerate() {
                    let mut builder_26 = list_23.reborrow().get(idx_24 as u32);
                    builder_26.set_x(element_25.x);
                    builder_26.set_tag(element_25.tag.as_str());
                }
            }
            let mut buffer = Vec::new();
            capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
                .map_err(|source| {
                    let context = format!(
                        "{} {}", "publish_feedback rich_action", ACTION_NAME
                    );
                    crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
                })?;
            peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
        };
        let payload = peppylib::messaging::NonEmptyPayload::try_new(payload)
            .map_err(|_| peppylib::PeppyError::Io(
                std::io::Error::other(
                    "publish_feedback produced an empty payload (codec serialized \
                     to zero bytes); empty is reserved for end-of-stream",
                ),
            ))?;
        self.inner.publish_feedback(payload).await?;
        Ok(())
    }
    /// Ends the goal with the status completed and its result:
    /// the goal is a success. The first of `complete`, `fail`
    /// and `complete_cancelled` ends the goal; later calls do
    /// nothing.
    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub async fn complete(&self) -> crate::Result<()> {
        let payload = peppylib::Payload::new();
        self.inner.complete(payload).await?;
        Ok(())
    }
    /// Ends the goal with the status cancelled, for `reason`, with its result.
    /// A worker calls it after the cancel signal
    /// (`CancelCause::reason` gives a reason), and a member
    /// that ends the goals of other clients calls it with a
    /// reason of its own.
    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub async fn complete_cancelled(
        &self,
        reason: impl Into<String>,
    ) -> crate::Result<()> {
        let payload = peppylib::Payload::new();
        self.inner.complete_cancelled(reason, payload).await?;
        Ok(())
    }
}


================================================================
ConsumedTopic :: rich_link/rich_stream
================================================================
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct MessagePoseFrame {
    pub id: u32,
    pub name: String,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct MessagePose {
    pub x: f64,
    pub frame: MessagePoseFrame,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct MessageCalibration {
    pub gain: f32,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct MessagePointsItem {
    pub x: f32,
    pub tag: String,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Message {
    pub max_speed: f32,
    pub type_: String,
    pub payload: Vec<u8>,
    pub stamp: std::time::SystemTime,
    pub note: Option<String>,
    pub pose: MessagePose,
    pub calibration: Option<MessageCalibration>,
    pub digest: [u8; 4],
    pub raw: Vec<u8>,
    pub gains: [f32; 3],
    pub readings: Vec<i16>,
    pub names: Vec<String>,
    pub chunks: Vec<Vec<u8>>,
    pub limits: Option<Vec<f64>>,
    pub points: Vec<MessagePointsItem>,
}
/// The producer bound to this module's slot.
/// The binding is fixed when the node starts (no live discovery; a
/// producer disconnecting never rebinds it) and shared by every
/// generated module referencing this slot. This slot declares
/// cardinality `one`: launch validation resolved exactly one producer,
/// so the accessor is singular and infallible.
pub fn bound_producer(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
) -> &peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef {
    node_runner.processor().sole_bound_producer("rich_link")
}
/// A held subscription covering every producer bound to this slot: one
/// producer-pinned wire subscription per member of the slot's bound
/// set, merged client-side. Message order is preserved independently
/// per producer (no total ordering across producers), and ready
/// producers are merged fairly. To follow a single producer, filter on
/// the yielded producer identity.
pub struct Subscription {
    inner: peppylib::runtime::BoundSetSubscription,
}
impl Subscription {
    /// Awaits the next message from any producer bound to this slot.
    ///
    /// Returns `Ok(Some((producer, message)))` for each message (the
    /// producer that published it always rides along), `Ok(None)` once the
    /// node is shutting down and no queued message remains, and `Err(..)`
    /// if a received payload fails to deserialize; a decode error does not
    /// tear down the subscription or shrink the bound set. On an empty
    /// set (a `zero_or_more` slot bound to nothing, or a vacant
    /// `zero_or_one` slot) this pends until shutdown, then returns
    /// `Ok(None)`.
    #[allow(clippy::should_implement_trait)]
    pub async fn next(
        &mut self,
    ) -> crate::Result<Option<(peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef, Message)>> {
        let Some((producer, message)) = self.inner.on_next_message().await else {
            return Ok(None);
        };
        let payload = message.payload_bytes();
        let message = deseralize_payload(payload.as_ref())?;
        Ok(Some((producer, message)))
    }
}
/// Subscribes to this topic across the slot's complete bound producer
/// set and returns a held `Subscription` yielding `(producer, message)`.
///
/// The shape is identical for every cardinality; only the size of the
/// bound set changes. Call this once, then loop on `Subscription::next`;
/// each underlying subscription's buffer retains messages published
/// between calls, so nothing is lost to a re-subscribe gap.
pub async fn subscribe(node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner) -> crate::Result<Subscription> {
    let topic_name = "rich_stream";
    let node_name = "rich_producer";
    let qos = peppylib::config::QoSProfile::Standard;
    let retention = peppylib::config::TopicRetention::LiveOnly;
    let inner = peppylib::runtime::subscribe_bound_set(
            node_runner,
            "rich_link",
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("rich_producer", "v1")?,
            topic_name,
            qos,
            retention,
        )
        .await
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::TopicSubscribe {
            topic_name: topic_name.to_string(),
            node_name: node_name.to_string(),
            source_msg: source.to_string(),
        })?;
    Ok(Subscription { inner })
}
fn deseralize_payload(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<Message> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]
    let context = String::from("Message");
    let mut payload = payload;
    let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader
        .get_root::<
            crate::capnp::on_next_rich_link_rich_stream_message_capnp::rich_link_rich_stream_message::Reader,
        >()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let max_speed_0 = root.reborrow().get_max_speed();
    let text_2 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_type()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "type", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let type_1 = text_2
        .to_str()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "type", context, source),
            )
        })?
        .to_owned();
    let data_4 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_payload()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "payload", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let payload_3 = data_4.to_vec();
    let timestamp_6 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_stamp()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "stamp", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let capnp_timestamp_7 = peppylib::encoding::CapnpTimestamp {
        sec: timestamp_6.get_sec(),
        nsec: timestamp_6.get_nsec(),
    };
    let stamp_5 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time_from_capnp(capnp_timestamp_7);
    let note_opt_8 = if root.reborrow().has_note() {
        let text_10 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_note()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("Message");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "note", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let note_9 = text_10
            .to_str()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("Message");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "note", context, source),
                )
            })?
            .to_owned();
        Some(note_9)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let reader_11 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_pose()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "pose", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let x_12 = reader_11.reborrow().get_x();
    let reader_13 = reader_11
        .reborrow()
        .get_frame()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "frame", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let id_14 = reader_13.reborrow().get_id();
    let text_16 = reader_13
        .reborrow()
        .get_name()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "name", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let name_15 = text_16
        .to_str()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "name", context, source),
            )
        })?
        .to_owned();
    let frame_17 = MessagePoseFrame {
        id: id_14,
        name: name_15,
    };
    let pose_18 = MessagePose {
        x: x_12,
        frame: frame_17,
    };
    let calibration_opt_19 = if root.reborrow().has_calibration() {
        let reader_20 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_calibration()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("Message");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "calibration", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let gain_21 = reader_20.reborrow().get_gain();
        let calibration_22 = MessageCalibration {
            gain: gain_21,
        };
        Some(calibration_22)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let data_23 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_digest()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "digest", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    if data_23.len() as usize != 4 {
        let actual = data_23.len() as usize;
        return Err(
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!(
                    "invalid fixed bytes length for field '{}': expected {}, got {}",
                    "digest", 4, actual
                ),
            ),
        );
    }
    let mut bytes_25 = [0u8; 4];
    for (idx, byte) in data_23.iter().enumerate() {
        bytes_25[idx] = *byte;
    }
    let digest_24 = bytes_25;
    let data_26 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_raw()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "raw", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let raw_27 = data_26.to_vec();
    let list_28 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_gains()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "gains", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    if list_28.len() as usize != 3 {
        let actual = list_28.len() as usize;
        return Err(
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!(
                    "invalid fixed list length for field '{}': expected {}, got {}",
                    "gains", 3, actual
                ),
            ),
        );
    }
    let mut gains_29: [f32; 3] = [f32::default(); 3];
    for (idx, element) in list_28.iter().enumerate() {
        gains_29[idx] = element;
    }
    let list_30 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_readings()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "readings", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let readings_31 = list_30.iter().collect::<Vec<i16>>();
    let list_32 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_names()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "names", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let names_33 = list_32
        .iter()
        .map(|element| {
            (element
                .map_err(|source| source.to_string())
                .and_then(|text| {
                    text.to_str().map(str::to_owned).map_err(|source| source.to_string())
                }))
                .map_err(|source| {
                    #[allow(clippy::all)]
                    let context = String::from("Message");
                    crate::Error::Deserialization(
                        format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "names", context, source),
                    )
                })
        })
        .collect::<::std::result::Result<Vec<String>, crate::Error>>()?;
    let list_34 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_chunks()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "chunks", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let chunks_35 = list_34
        .iter()
        .map(|element| {
            (element.map(|data| data.to_vec()).map_err(|source| source.to_string()))
                .map_err(|source| {
                    #[allow(clippy::all)]
                    let context = String::from("Message");
                    crate::Error::Deserialization(
                        format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "chunks", context, source),
                    )
                })
        })
        .collect::<::std::result::Result<Vec<Vec<u8>>, crate::Error>>()?;
    let limits_opt_36 = if root.reborrow().has_limits() {
        let list_37 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_limits()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("Message");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "limits", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let limits_38 = list_37.iter().collect::<Vec<f64>>();
        Some(limits_38)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let list_39 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_points()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "points", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let mut result_40 = Vec::with_capacity(list_39.len() as usize);
    for element_42 in list_39.iter() {
        let x_43 = element_42.reborrow().get_x();
        let text_45 = element_42
            .reborrow()
            .get_tag()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("Message");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "tag", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let tag_44 = text_45
            .to_str()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("Message");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "tag", context, source),
                )
            })?
            .to_owned();
        result_40
            .push(MessagePointsItem {
                x: x_43,
                tag: tag_44,
            });
    }
    let points_41 = result_40;
    Ok(Message {
        max_speed: max_speed_0,
        type_: type_1,
        payload: payload_3,
        stamp: stamp_5,
        note: note_opt_8,
        pose: pose_18,
        calibration: calibration_opt_19,
        digest: digest_24,
        raw: raw_27,
        gains: gains_29,
        readings: readings_31,
        names: names_33,
        chunks: chunks_35,
        limits: limits_opt_36,
        points: points_41,
    })
}


================================================================
ConsumedService :: rich_link/rich_service

const NODE_NAME: &str = "rich_producer";
const SERVICE_NAME: &str = "rich_service";
const LINK_ID: &str = "rich_link";
/// The producer bound to this module's slot.
/// The binding is fixed when the node starts (no live discovery; a
/// producer disconnecting never rebinds it) and shared by every
/// generated module referencing this slot. This slot declares
/// cardinality `one`: launch validation resolved exactly one producer,
/// so the accessor is singular and infallible.
pub fn bound_producer(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
) -> &peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef {
    node_runner.processor().sole_bound_producer("rich_link")
}
/// A failure kind of this member: a kind of peppy, or a kind of its
/// contract that it lists.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum FailureKind {
    /// The attempt gave no result
    AttemptFailed,
    /// The bound of the provider passed
    Timeout,
    /// A precondition failed
    PreconditionFailed,
    /// A robot, limb or host is gone
    ResourceUnavailable,
    /// The provider refused the goal, and no other kind says why
    Rejected,
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl FailureKind {
    /// Every kind of this member: the kinds of peppy, then the
    /// kinds of its contract that it lists.
    pub const ALL: [Self; 5] = [
        Self::AttemptFailed,
        Self::Timeout,
        Self::PreconditionFailed,
        Self::ResourceUnavailable,
        Self::Rejected,
    ];
    /// The name of the kind on the wire.
    pub const fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::AttemptFailed => "attempt_failed",
            Self::Timeout => "timeout",
            Self::PreconditionFailed => "precondition_failed",
            Self::ResourceUnavailable => "resource_unavailable",
            Self::Rejected => "rejected",
        }
    }
    /// The kind named `name`, or `None` when this member does not
    /// declare it.
    pub fn parse(name: &str) -> Option<Self> {
        Self::ALL.into_iter().find(|kind| kind.as_str() == name)
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for FailureKind {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.write_str(self.as_str())
    }
}
/// Why this member refused or failed a goal, or gave an error
/// reply: a kind that it declares, and a message for a person.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Failure {
    pub kind: FailureKind,
    pub message: String,
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl Failure {
    pub fn new(kind: FailureKind, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            kind,
            message: message.into(),
        }
    }
    /// The wire form of the failure.
    pub(crate) fn to_wire(&self) -> peppylib::messaging::Failure {
        let kind = peppylib::messaging::FailureKindName::parse(self.kind.as_str())
            .expect("each kind of a member is a valid failure kind name");
        peppylib::messaging::Failure::new(kind, self.message.clone())
    }
    /// Parses a failure from the wire. A kind that this member does
    /// not declare is a deserialization error, whose text keeps the
    /// message of the provider.
    pub(crate) fn from_wire(
        failure: &peppylib::messaging::Failure,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let kind = FailureKind::parse(failure.kind().as_str())
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!(
                        "the failure kind `{}` is not a kind of `{}` (message: {:?})",
                        failure.kind(), SERVICE_NAME, failure.message()
                    ),
                )
            })?;
        Ok(Self::new(kind, failure.message()))
    }
    /// The failure of the error reply that `error` holds: `None`
    /// when `error` is not an error reply of a provider, or when
    /// its kind is not a kind of this member.
    pub fn of(error: &crate::Error) -> Option<Self> {
        let crate::Error::ServiceFailed { failure, .. } = error else {
            return None;
        };
        Self::from_wire(failure).ok()
    }
    /// `error` itself, unless it is an error reply with a kind this
    /// member does not declare: such a reply breaks the contract of
    /// the member, and gives a deserialization error instead.
    pub(crate) fn reject_undeclared_kind(error: crate::Error) -> crate::Error {
        let crate::Error::ServiceFailed { failure, .. } = &error else {
            return error;
        };
        match Self::from_wire(failure) {
            Ok(_) => error,
            Err(undeclared) => undeclared,
        }
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for Failure {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{}: {}", self.kind, self.message)
    }
}

/// Polls this service on `target`, a member of this slot's bound set
/// (a `ProducerRef` yielded by the slot's own subscription is a
/// member by construction); a target outside the set fails before
/// anything reaches the wire.
///
/// An error reply of the provider is `Error::ServiceFailed`:
/// `Failure::of` reads its failure, with a kind that this member
/// declares.
///
/// This slot declares cardinality `one`: `target` is its sole bound
/// producer, returned by `bound_producer()`; the explicit parameter
/// keeps call sites uniform across cardinalities.
pub async fn poll(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
    target: &peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef,
    timeout: std::time::Duration,
    request: Request,
) -> crate::Result<Response> {
    node_runner.processor().ensure_target_bound(LINK_ID, target)?;
    let request_payload = {
        let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
        {
            let max_speed = request.max_speed;
            let type_ = request.type_;
            let payload = request.payload;
            let stamp = request.stamp;
            let note = request.note;
            let pose = request.pose;
            let calibration = request.calibration;
            let digest = request.digest;
            let raw = request.raw;
            let gains = request.gains;
            let readings = request.readings;
            let names = request.names;
            let chunks = request.chunks;
            let limits = request.limits;
            let points = request.points;
            let mut root = capnp_msg
                .init_root::<
                    crate::capnp::poll_rich_producer_rich_service_message_capnp::rich_service_message::Builder,
                >();
            root.set_max_speed(max_speed);
            root.set_type(type_.as_str());
            root.set_payload(payload.as_ref());
            let timestamp_0 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time(stamp);
            let mut timestamp_builder_1 = root.reborrow().init_stamp();
            timestamp_builder_1.set_sec(timestamp_0.sec);
            timestamp_builder_1.set_nsec(timestamp_0.nsec);
            if let Some(value_2) = (note).as_ref() {
                root.set_note(value_2.as_str());
            }
            let mut builder_3 = root.reborrow().init_pose();
            builder_3.set_x((pose).x);
            let mut builder_4 = builder_3.reborrow().init_frame();
            builder_4.set_id(((pose).frame).id);
            builder_4.set_name(((pose).frame).name.as_str());
            if let Some(value_5) = (calibration).as_ref() {
                let mut builder_6 = root.reborrow().init_calibration();
                builder_6.set_gain(*&(value_5).gain);
            }
            root.set_digest(digest.as_ref());
            root.set_raw(raw.as_ref());
            let mut list_7 = root.reborrow().init_gains(3);
            for (idx_8, value_9) in (gains).iter().enumerate() {
                list_7.set(idx_8 as u32, *value_9);
            }
            let mut list_10 = root
                .reborrow()
                .init_readings(
                    u32::try_from((readings).len())
                        .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                );
            for (idx_11, value_12) in (readings).iter().enumerate() {
                list_10.set(idx_11 as u32, *value_12);
            }
            let mut list_13 = root
                .reborrow()
                .init_names(
                    u32::try_from((names).len()).expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                );
            for (idx_14, value_15) in (names).iter().enumerate() {
                list_13.set(idx_14 as u32, value_15.as_str());
            }
            let mut list_16 = root
                .reborrow()
                .init_chunks(
                    u32::try_from((chunks).len()).expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                );
            for (idx_17, value_18) in (chunks).iter().enumerate() {
                list_16.set(idx_17 as u32, value_18.as_ref());
            }
            if let Some(value_19) = (limits).as_ref() {
                let mut list_20 = root
                    .reborrow()
                    .init_limits(
                        u32::try_from((value_19).len())
                            .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                    );
                for (idx_21, value_22) in (value_19).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_20.set(idx_21 as u32, *value_22);
                }
            }
            let mut list_23 = root
                .reborrow()
                .init_points(
                    u32::try_from((points).len()).expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                );
            for (idx_24, element_25) in (points).iter().enumerate() {
                let mut builder_26 = list_23.reborrow().get(idx_24 as u32);
                builder_26.set_x(element_25.x);
                builder_26.set_tag(element_25.tag.as_str());
            }
        }
        let mut buffer = Vec::new();
        capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
            .map_err(|source| {
                let context = format!("poll {} {}", NODE_NAME, SERVICE_NAME);
                crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
            })?;
        peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
    };
    let response_message = peppylib::ServiceMessenger::poll(
            node_runner.messenger(),
            node_runner.processor().bound_core_node(),
            node_runner.processor().bound_instance_id(),
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("rich_producer", "v1")?,
            SERVICE_NAME,
            peppylib::messaging::ServiceTarget::Producer(target),
            request_payload,
            timeout,
        )
        .await
        .map_err(Failure::reject_undeclared_kind)?;
    let payload = response_message.payload_bytes();
    let response_data = deserialize_response(payload.as_ref())?;
    Ok(Response {
        instance_id: response_message.instance_id().to_string(),
        core_node: response_message.core_node().to_string(),
        data: response_data,
    })
}
fn deserialize_response(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<ResponseData> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]
    let context = format!("{} {} response", NODE_NAME, SERVICE_NAME);
    let mut payload = payload;
    let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader
        .get_root::<
            crate::capnp::poll_rich_producer_rich_service_response_message_capnp::rich_service_response_message::Reader,
        >()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let max_speed_0 = root.reborrow().get_max_speed();
    let text_2 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_type()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = context.clone();
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "type", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let type_1 = text_2
        .to_str()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = context.clone();
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "type", context, source),
            )
        })?
        .to_owned();
    let data_4 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_payload()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = context.clone();
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "payload", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let payload_3 = data_4.to_vec();
    let timestamp_6 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_stamp()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = context.clone();
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "stamp", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let capnp_timestamp_7 = peppylib::encoding::CapnpTimestamp {
        sec: timestamp_6.get_sec(),
        nsec: timestamp_6.get_nsec(),
    };
    let stamp_5 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time_from_capnp(capnp_timestamp_7);
    let note_opt_8 = if root.reborrow().has_note() {
        let text_10 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_note()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = context.clone();
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "note", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let note_9 = text_10
            .to_str()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = context.clone();
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "note", context, source),
                )
            })?
            .to_owned();
        Some(note_9)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let reader_11 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_pose()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = context.clone();
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "pose", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let x_12 = reader_11.reborrow().get_x();
    let reader_13 = reader_11
        .reborrow()
        .get_frame()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = context.clone();
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "frame", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let id_14 = reader_13.reborrow().get_id();
    let text_16 = reader_13
        .reborrow()
        .get_name()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = context.clone();
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "name", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let name_15 = text_16
        .to_str()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = context.clone();
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "name", context, source),
            )
        })?
        .to_owned();
    let frame_17 = ResponseDataPoseFrame {
        id: id_14,
        name: name_15,
    };
    let pose_18 = ResponseDataPose {
        x: x_12,
        frame: frame_17,
    };
    let calibration_opt_19 = if root.reborrow().has_calibration() {
        let reader_20 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_calibration()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = context.clone();
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "calibration", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let gain_21 = reader_20.reborrow().get_gain();
        let calibration_22 = ResponseDataCalibration {
            gain: gain_21,
        };
        Some(calibration_22)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let data_23 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_digest()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = context.clone();
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "digest", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    if data_23.len() as usize != 4 {
        let actual = data_23.len() as usize;
        return Err(
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!(
                    "invalid fixed bytes length for field '{}': expected {}, got {}",
                    "digest", 4, actual
                ),
            ),
        );
    }
    let mut bytes_25 = [0u8; 4];
    for (idx, byte) in data_23.iter().enumerate() {
        bytes_25[idx] = *byte;
    }
    let digest_24 = bytes_25;
    let data_26 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_raw()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = context.clone();
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "raw", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let raw_27 = data_26.to_vec();
    let list_28 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_gains()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = context.clone();
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "gains", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    if list_28.len() as usize != 3 {
        let actual = list_28.len() as usize;
        return Err(
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!(
                    "invalid fixed list length for field '{}': expected {}, got {}",
                    "gains", 3, actual
                ),
            ),
        );
    }
    let mut gains_29: [f32; 3] = [f32::default(); 3];
    for (idx, element) in list_28.iter().enumerate() {
        gains_29[idx] = element;
    }
    let list_30 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_readings()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = context.clone();
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "readings", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let readings_31 = list_30.iter().collect::<Vec<i16>>();
    let list_32 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_names()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = context.clone();
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "names", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let names_33 = list_32
        .iter()
        .map(|element| {
            (element
                .map_err(|source| source.to_string())
                .and_then(|text| {
                    text.to_str().map(str::to_owned).map_err(|source| source.to_string())
                }))
                .map_err(|source| {
                    #[allow(clippy::all)]
                    let context = context.clone();
                    crate::Error::Deserialization(
                        format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "names", context, source),
                    )
                })
        })
        .collect::<::std::result::Result<Vec<String>, crate::Error>>()?;
    let list_34 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_chunks()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = context.clone();
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "chunks", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let chunks_35 = list_34
        .iter()
        .map(|element| {
            (element.map(|data| data.to_vec()).map_err(|source| source.to_string()))
                .map_err(|source| {
                    #[allow(clippy::all)]
                    let context = context.clone();
                    crate::Error::Deserialization(
                        format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "chunks", context, source),
                    )
                })
        })
        .collect::<::std::result::Result<Vec<Vec<u8>>, crate::Error>>()?;
    let limits_opt_36 = if root.reborrow().has_limits() {
        let list_37 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_limits()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = context.clone();
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "limits", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let limits_38 = list_37.iter().collect::<Vec<f64>>();
        Some(limits_38)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let list_39 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_points()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = context.clone();
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "points", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let mut result_40 = Vec::with_capacity(list_39.len() as usize);
    for element_42 in list_39.iter() {
        let x_43 = element_42.reborrow().get_x();
        let text_45 = element_42
            .reborrow()
            .get_tag()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = context.clone();
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "tag", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let tag_44 = text_45
            .to_str()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = context.clone();
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "tag", context, source),
                )
            })?
            .to_owned();
        result_40
            .push(ResponseDataPointsItem {
                x: x_43,
                tag: tag_44,
            });
    }
    let points_41 = result_40;
    Ok(ResponseData {
        max_speed: max_speed_0,
        type_: type_1,
        payload: payload_3,
        stamp: stamp_5,
        note: note_opt_8,
        pose: pose_18,
        calibration: calibration_opt_19,
        digest: digest_24,
        raw: raw_27,
        gains: gains_29,
        readings: readings_31,
        names: names_33,
        chunks: chunks_35,
        limits: limits_opt_36,
        points: points_41,
    })
}

ConsumedAction :: rich_link/rich_action
