const TARGET_NODE_NAME: &str = "rich_producer";
const TARGET_ACTION_NAME: &str = "rich_action";
const LINK_ID: &str = "rich_link";
/// The producer bound to this module's slot.
/// The binding is fixed when the node starts (no live discovery; a
/// producer disconnecting never rebinds it) and shared by every
/// generated module referencing this slot. This slot declares
/// cardinality `one`: launch validation resolved exactly one producer,
/// so the accessor is singular and infallible.
pub fn bound_producer(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
) -> &peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef {
    node_runner.processor().sole_bound_producer("rich_link")
}
/// A failure kind of this member: a kind of peppy, or a kind of its
/// contract that it lists.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum FailureKind {
    /// The attempt gave no result
    AttemptFailed,
    /// The bound of the provider passed
    Timeout,
    /// A precondition failed
    PreconditionFailed,
    /// A robot, limb or host is gone
    ResourceUnavailable,
    /// The provider refused the goal, and no other kind says why
    Rejected,
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl FailureKind {
    /// Every kind of this member: the kinds of peppy, then the
    /// kinds of its contract that it lists.
    pub const ALL: [Self; 5] = [
        Self::AttemptFailed,
        Self::Timeout,
        Self::PreconditionFailed,
        Self::ResourceUnavailable,
        Self::Rejected,
    ];
    /// The name of the kind on the wire.
    pub const fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::AttemptFailed => "attempt_failed",
            Self::Timeout => "timeout",
            Self::PreconditionFailed => "precondition_failed",
            Self::ResourceUnavailable => "resource_unavailable",
            Self::Rejected => "rejected",
        }
    }
    /// The kind named `name`, or `None` when this member does not
    /// declare it.
    pub fn parse(name: &str) -> Option<Self> {
        Self::ALL.into_iter().find(|kind| kind.as_str() == name)
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for FailureKind {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.write_str(self.as_str())
    }
}
/// Why this member refused or failed a goal, or gave an error
/// reply: a kind that it declares, and a message for a person.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Failure {
    pub kind: FailureKind,
    pub message: String,
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl Failure {
    pub fn new(kind: FailureKind, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            kind,
            message: message.into(),
        }
    }
    /// The wire form of the failure.
    pub(crate) fn to_wire(&self) -> peppylib::messaging::Failure {
        let kind = peppylib::messaging::FailureKindName::parse(self.kind.as_str())
            .expect("each kind of a member is a valid failure kind name");
        peppylib::messaging::Failure::new(kind, self.message.clone())
    }
    /// Parses a failure from the wire. A kind that this member does
    /// not declare is a deserialization error, whose text keeps the
    /// message of the provider.
    pub(crate) fn from_wire(
        failure: &peppylib::messaging::Failure,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let kind = FailureKind::parse(failure.kind().as_str())
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!(
                        "the failure kind `{}` is not a kind of `{}` (message: {:?})",
                        failure.kind(), TARGET_ACTION_NAME, failure.message()
                    ),
                )
            })?;
        Ok(Self::new(kind, failure.message()))
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for Failure {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{}: {}", self.kind, self.message)
    }
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct CancelResponse {
    pub instance_id: String,
    pub core_node: String,
    pub state: CancelState,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct RichProducerRichActionActionFeedbackMessagePoseFrame {
    pub id: u32,
    pub name: String,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct RichProducerRichActionActionFeedbackMessagePose {
    pub x: f64,
    pub frame: RichProducerRichActionActionFeedbackMessagePoseFrame,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct RichProducerRichActionActionFeedbackMessageCalibration {
    pub gain: f32,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct RichProducerRichActionActionFeedbackMessagePointsItem {
    pub x: f32,
    pub tag: String,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct FeedbackMessage {
    pub max_speed: f32,
    pub type_: String,
    pub payload: Vec<u8>,
    pub stamp: std::time::SystemTime,
    pub note: Option<String>,
    pub pose: RichProducerRichActionActionFeedbackMessagePose,
    pub calibration: Option<RichProducerRichActionActionFeedbackMessageCalibration>,
    pub digest: [u8; 4],
    pub raw: Vec<u8>,
    pub gains: [f32; 3],
    pub readings: Vec<i16>,
    pub names: Vec<String>,
    pub chunks: Vec<Vec<u8>>,
    pub limits: Option<Vec<f64>>,
    pub points: Vec<RichProducerRichActionActionFeedbackMessagePointsItem>,
}
pub struct ActionHandle {
    messenger: peppylib::MessengerHandle,
    inner: peppylib::messaging::ActionGoalHandle,
    /// Whether the producer accepted the goal: `refusal` is
    /// `None`.
    pub accepted: bool,
    /// The failure the producer refused the goal with, of a kind
    /// that this member declares; `None` for an accepted goal.
    pub refusal: Option<Failure>,
}
impl ActionHandle {
    /// Fires this goal at `target`, a member of this slot's bound set (a
    /// `ProducerRef` yielded by the slot's own subscription is a member
    /// by construction); a target outside the set fails before anything
    /// reaches the wire. Each returned handle stays pinned to its
    /// target: feedback, result retrieval, and cancellation all address
    /// that producer directly.
    ///
    /// The handle keeps every feedback message until it is read, up to
    /// `feedback_bound` unread messages (`DEFAULT_FEEDBACK_BOUND` holds
    /// 1024). A caller that reads the feedback and falls behind by more
    /// cancels the goal, and its next read gives
    /// `Error::ActionFeedbackOverflow`. A caller that never reads the
    /// feedback keeps its goal.
    ///
    /// This slot declares cardinality `one`: `target` is its sole bound
    /// producer, returned by `bound_producer()`; the explicit parameter
    /// keeps call sites uniform across cardinalities.
    pub async fn fire_goal(
        node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
        target: &peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef,
        timeout: std::time::Duration,
        feedback_bound: peppylib::messaging::FeedbackBound,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        node_runner.processor().ensure_target_bound(LINK_ID, target)?;
        let goal_payload = peppylib::Payload::new();
        let action_handle = peppylib::ActionMessenger::send_goal(
                node_runner.messenger(),
                node_runner.processor().bound_core_node(),
                node_runner.processor().bound_instance_id(),
                peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("rich_producer", "v1")?,
                TARGET_ACTION_NAME,
                Some(target),
                goal_payload,
                feedback_bound,
                timeout,
            )
            .await?;
        let reply = action_handle.goal_reply();
        let refusal = reply.refusal().map(Failure::from_wire).transpose()?;
        Ok(Self {
            messenger: node_runner.messenger().clone(),
            accepted: refusal.is_none(),
            inner: action_handle,
            refusal,
        })
    }
    pub async fn cancel_goal(
        &self,
        timeout: std::time::Duration,
    ) -> crate::Result<CancelResponse> {
        let response = peppylib::ActionMessenger::cancel_goal(
                &self.messenger,
                &self.inner,
                timeout,
            )
            .await?;
        let state = match peppylib::messaging::decode_cancel_ack(
            response.payload_bytes().as_ref(),
        )? {
            peppylib::messaging::CancelState::Signalled => CancelState::Signalled,
            peppylib::messaging::CancelState::AlreadyTerminal => {
                CancelState::AlreadyTerminal
            }
            peppylib::messaging::CancelState::Unknown => CancelState::Unknown,
        };
        Ok(CancelResponse {
            instance_id: response.instance_id().to_string(),
            core_node: response.core_node().to_string(),
            state,
        })
    }
    /// Receives the next feedback message for this goal. The handle
    /// keeps every message until it is read, up to the feedback bound
    /// of `fire_goal`.
    ///
    /// Terminal errors that end a feedback drain loop:
    /// `Error::ActionFeedbackChannelClosed` when the producer closed
    /// the stream cleanly (end-of-stream sentinel),
    /// `Error::ActionFeedbackProducerGone` when the producer instance
    /// disappeared without closing it (process killed, crashed), in
    /// which case `get_result` resolves to `ResultOutcome::Abandoned`,
    /// and `Error::ActionFeedbackOverflow` when this caller fell behind
    /// by more unread messages than its bound, in which case the
    /// handle cancelled the goal.
    pub async fn on_next_feedback_message(&mut self) -> crate::Result<FeedbackMessage> {
        let feedback = self.inner.on_next_feedback().await?;
        let payload = feedback.payload_bytes();
        deserialize_feedback_payload(payload.as_ref())
    }
    pub async fn get_result(
        &self,
        timeout: std::time::Duration,
    ) -> crate::Result<ResultResponse> {
        let reply = peppylib::ActionMessenger::request_result(
                &self.messenger,
                &self.inner,
                timeout,
            )
            .await?;
        let outcome = match reply.outcome {
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Completed(_) => ResultOutcome::Completed,
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Cancelled { reason, .. } => {
                ResultOutcome::Cancelled { reason }
            }
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Failed(failure) => {
                ResultOutcome::Failed(Failure::from_wire(&failure)?)
            }
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Abandoned => ResultOutcome::Abandoned,
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Expired => ResultOutcome::Expired,
        };
        Ok(ResultResponse {
            instance_id: reply.instance_id,
            core_node: reply.core_node,
            outcome,
        })
    }
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum CancelState {
    Signalled,
    AlreadyTerminal,
    Unknown,
}
fn deserialize_feedback_payload(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<FeedbackMessage> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]
    let context = format!("{} {} FeedbackMessage", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME);
    let mut payload = payload;
    let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader
        .get_root::<
            crate::capnp::rich_producer_rich_action_feedback_message_capnp::rich_producer_rich_action_action_feedback_message::Reader,
        >()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let max_speed_0 = root.reborrow().get_max_speed();
    let text_2 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_type()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = format!(
                "{} {} FeedbackMessage", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME
            );
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "type", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let type_1 = text_2
        .to_str()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = format!(
                "{} {} FeedbackMessage", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME
            );
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "type", context, source),
            )
        })?
        .to_owned();
    let data_4 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_payload()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = format!(
                "{} {} FeedbackMessage", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME
            );
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "payload", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let payload_3 = data_4.to_vec();
    let timestamp_6 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_stamp()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = format!(
                "{} {} FeedbackMessage", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME
            );
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "stamp", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let capnp_timestamp_7 = peppylib::encoding::CapnpTimestamp {
        sec: timestamp_6.get_sec(),
        nsec: timestamp_6.get_nsec(),
    };
    let stamp_5 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time_from_capnp(capnp_timestamp_7);
    let note_opt_8 = if root.reborrow().has_note() {
        let text_10 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_note()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = format!(
                    "{} {} FeedbackMessage", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME
                );
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "note", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let note_9 = text_10
            .to_str()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = format!(
                    "{} {} FeedbackMessage", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME
                );
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "note", context, source),
                )
            })?
            .to_owned();
        Some(note_9)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let reader_11 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_pose()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = format!(
                "{} {} FeedbackMessage", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME
            );
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "pose", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let x_12 = reader_11.reborrow().get_x();
    let reader_13 = reader_11
        .reborrow()
        .get_frame()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = format!(
                "{} {} FeedbackMessage", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME
            );
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "frame", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let id_14 = reader_13.reborrow().get_id();
    let text_16 = reader_13
        .reborrow()
        .get_name()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = format!(
                "{} {} FeedbackMessage", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME
            );
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "name", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let name_15 = text_16
        .to_str()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = format!(
                "{} {} FeedbackMessage", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME
            );
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "name", context, source),
            )
        })?
        .to_owned();
    let frame_17 = RichProducerRichActionActionFeedbackMessagePoseFrame {
        id: id_14,
        name: name_15,
    };
    let pose_18 = RichProducerRichActionActionFeedbackMessagePose {
        x: x_12,
        frame: frame_17,
    };
    let calibration_opt_19 = if root.reborrow().has_calibration() {
        let reader_20 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_calibration()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = format!(
                    "{} {} FeedbackMessage", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME
                );
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "calibration", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let gain_21 = reader_20.reborrow().get_gain();
        let calibration_22 = RichProducerRichActionActionFeedbackMessageCalibration {
            gain: gain_21,
        };
        Some(calibration_22)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let data_23 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_digest()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = format!(
                "{} {} FeedbackMessage", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME
            );
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "digest", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    if data_23.len() as usize != 4 {
        let actual = data_23.len() as usize;
        return Err(
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!(
                    "invalid fixed bytes length for field '{}': expected {}, got {}",
                    "digest", 4, actual
                ),
            ),
        );
    }
    let mut bytes_25 = [0u8; 4];
    for (idx, byte) in data_23.iter().enumerate() {
        bytes_25[idx] = *byte;
    }
    let digest_24 = bytes_25;
    let data_26 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_raw()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = format!(
                "{} {} FeedbackMessage", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME
            );
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "raw", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let raw_27 = data_26.to_vec();
    let list_28 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_gains()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = format!(
                "{} {} FeedbackMessage", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME
            );
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "gains", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    if list_28.len() as usize != 3 {
        let actual = list_28.len() as usize;
        return Err(
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!(
                    "invalid fixed list length for field '{}': expected {}, got {}",
                    "gains", 3, actual
                ),
            ),
        );
    }
    let mut gains_29: [f32; 3] = [f32::default(); 3];
    for (idx, element) in list_28.iter().enumerate() {
        gains_29[idx] = element;
    }
    let list_30 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_readings()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = format!(
                "{} {} FeedbackMessage", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME
            );
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "readings", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let readings_31 = list_30.iter().collect::<Vec<i16>>();
    let list_32 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_names()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = format!(
                "{} {} FeedbackMessage", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME
            );
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "names", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let names_33 = list_32
        .iter()
        .map(|element| {
            (element
                .map_err(|source| source.to_string())
                .and_then(|text| {
                    text.to_str().map(str::to_owned).map_err(|source| source.to_string())
                }))
                .map_err(|source| {
                    #[allow(clippy::all)]
                    let context = format!(
                        "{} {} FeedbackMessage", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME
                    );
                    crate::Error::Deserialization(
                        format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "names", context, source),
                    )
                })
        })
        .collect::<::std::result::Result<Vec<String>, crate::Error>>()?;
    let list_34 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_chunks()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = format!(
                "{} {} FeedbackMessage", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME
            );
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "chunks", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let chunks_35 = list_34
        .iter()
        .map(|element| {
            (element.map(|data| data.to_vec()).map_err(|source| source.to_string()))
                .map_err(|source| {
                    #[allow(clippy::all)]
                    let context = format!(
                        "{} {} FeedbackMessage", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME
                    );
                    crate::Error::Deserialization(
                        format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "chunks", context, source),
                    )
                })
        })
        .collect::<::std::result::Result<Vec<Vec<u8>>, crate::Error>>()?;
    let limits_opt_36 = if root.reborrow().has_limits() {
        let list_37 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_limits()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = format!(
                    "{} {} FeedbackMessage", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME
                );
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "limits", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let limits_38 = list_37.iter().collect::<Vec<f64>>();
        Some(limits_38)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let list_39 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_points()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = format!(
                "{} {} FeedbackMessage", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME
            );
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "points", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let mut result_40 = Vec::with_capacity(list_39.len() as usize);
    for element_42 in list_39.iter() {
        let x_43 = element_42.reborrow().get_x();
        let text_45 = element_42
            .reborrow()
            .get_tag()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = format!(
                    "{} {} FeedbackMessage", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME
                );
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "tag", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let tag_44 = text_45
            .to_str()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = format!(
                    "{} {} FeedbackMessage", TARGET_NODE_NAME, TARGET_ACTION_NAME
                );
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "tag", context, source),
                )
            })?
            .to_owned();
        result_40
            .push(RichProducerRichActionActionFeedbackMessagePointsItem {
                x: x_43,
                tag: tag_44,
            });
    }
    let points_41 = result_40;
    Ok(FeedbackMessage {
        max_speed: max_speed_0,
        type_: type_1,
        payload: payload_3,
        stamp: stamp_5,
        note: note_opt_8,
        pose: pose_18,
        calibration: calibration_opt_19,
        digest: digest_24,
        raw: raw_27,
        gains: gains_29,
        readings: readings_31,
        names: names_33,
        chunks: chunks_35,
        limits: limits_opt_36,
        points: points_41,
    })
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum ResultOutcome {
    /// The goal is a success.
    Completed,
    /// The goal ended cancelled, for `reason`.
    Cancelled { reason: String },
    /// The goal failed: a failure kind that the action declares,
    /// a message, and no result.
    Failed(Failure),
    /// The goal ended without a result: its worker dropped it, or
    /// the provider is gone.
    Abandoned,
    /// The goal ended, and the provider no longer keeps its result.
    Expired,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct ResultResponse {
    pub instance_id: String,
    pub core_node: String,
    pub outcome: ResultOutcome,
}

PeerConsumedTopic :: rich_sensor_bus/rich_states

pub const TOPIC_NAME: &str = "rich_states";
/// This node's own pairing-slot link_id.
pub const LINK_ID: &str = "rich_sensor_bus";
pub const PAIRING_NAME: &str = "arm_link";
pub const PAIRING_TAG: &str = "v1";
/// The peer currently paired on this slot, or `None` while unpaired.
pub fn paired(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
) -> crate::Result<Option<peppylib::messaging::PeerInfo>> {
    Ok(node_runner.peer(LINK_ID)?.paired())
}
/// Waits until a peer is paired on this slot and returns its
/// identity. Returns immediately when already paired.
pub async fn wait_paired(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
) -> crate::Result<peppylib::messaging::PeerInfo> {
    node_runner.peer(LINK_ID)?.wait_paired().await
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct MessagePoseFrame {
    pub id: u32,
    pub name: String,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct MessagePose {
    pub x: f64,
    pub frame: MessagePoseFrame,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct MessageCalibration {
    pub gain: f32,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct MessagePointsItem {
    pub x: f32,
    pub tag: String,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Message {
    pub max_speed: f32,
    pub type_: String,
    pub payload: Vec<u8>,
    pub stamp: std::time::SystemTime,
    pub note: Option<String>,
    pub pose: MessagePose,
    pub calibration: Option<MessageCalibration>,
    pub digest: [u8; 4],
    pub raw: Vec<u8>,
    pub gains: [f32; 3],
    pub readings: Vec<i16>,
    pub names: Vec<String>,
    pub chunks: Vec<Vec<u8>>,
    pub limits: Option<Vec<f64>>,
    pub points: Vec<MessagePointsItem>,
}
/// A held subscription to this pairing topic. Yields nothing while
/// the slot is unpaired; while paired, only the paired peer's
/// messages surface (triple wire pin + delivery-time identity
/// check). A pairing is a live stream, not a mailbox: messages
/// published before pairing are never delivered.
pub struct Subscription {
    inner: peppylib::runtime::PeerSubscription,
}
impl Subscription {
    /// Awaits the next message from the currently paired peer.
    ///
    /// Returns `Ok(Some((peer, message)))` for each message,
    /// `Ok(None)` once the runtime shuts down, and `Err(..)` if a
    /// received payload fails to deserialize. `peer` is the paired
    /// peer's identity, its wire address plus the link_id of its
    /// complementary slot: the same `PeerInfo` that `paired()`
    /// returns.
    #[allow(clippy::should_implement_trait)]
    pub async fn next(
        &mut self,
    ) -> crate::Result<Option<(peppylib::messaging::PeerInfo, Message)>> {
        let Some((peer, message)) = self.inner.on_next_message().await else {
            return Ok(None);
        };
        let payload = message.payload_bytes();
        let message = deseralize_payload(payload.as_ref())?;
        Ok(Some((peer, message)))
    }
}
/// Subscribes to this pairing topic and returns a held
/// `Subscription` that follows the slot's live pin. Legal while
/// unpaired: the subscription stays silent until a peer pairs.
pub async fn subscribe(node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner) -> crate::Result<Subscription> {
    let qos = peppylib::config::QoSProfile::SensorData;
    let inner = peppylib::runtime::subscribe_peer(
            node_runner,
            LINK_ID,
            PAIRING_NAME,
            PAIRING_TAG,
            TOPIC_NAME,
            qos,
        )
        .await?;
    Ok(Subscription { inner })
}
fn deseralize_payload(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<Message> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]
    let context = String::from("Message");
    let mut payload = payload;
    let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader
        .get_root::<
            crate::capnp::peer_rich_sensor_bus_rich_states_message_capnp::peer_rich_sensor_bus_rich_states_message::Reader,
        >()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let max_speed_0 = root.reborrow().get_max_speed();
    let text_2 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_type()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "type", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let type_1 = text_2
        .to_str()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "type", context, source),
            )
        })?
        .to_owned();
    let data_4 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_payload()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "payload", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let payload_3 = data_4.to_vec();
    let timestamp_6 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_stamp()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "stamp", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let capnp_timestamp_7 = peppylib::encoding::CapnpTimestamp {
        sec: timestamp_6.get_sec(),
        nsec: timestamp_6.get_nsec(),
    };
    let stamp_5 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time_from_capnp(capnp_timestamp_7);
    let note_opt_8 = if root.reborrow().has_note() {
        let text_10 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_note()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("Message");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "note", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let note_9 = text_10
            .to_str()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("Message");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "note", context, source),
                )
            })?
            .to_owned();
        Some(note_9)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let reader_11 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_pose()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "pose", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let x_12 = reader_11.reborrow().get_x();
    let reader_13 = reader_11
        .reborrow()
        .get_frame()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "frame", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let id_14 = reader_13.reborrow().get_id();
    let text_16 = reader_13
        .reborrow()
        .get_name()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "name", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let name_15 = text_16
        .to_str()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "name", context, source),
            )
        })?
        .to_owned();
    let frame_17 = MessagePoseFrame {
        id: id_14,
        name: name_15,
    };
    let pose_18 = MessagePose {
        x: x_12,
        frame: frame_17,
    };
    let calibration_opt_19 = if root.reborrow().has_calibration() {
        let reader_20 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_calibration()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("Message");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "calibration", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let gain_21 = reader_20.reborrow().get_gain();
        let calibration_22 = MessageCalibration {
            gain: gain_21,
        };
        Some(calibration_22)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let data_23 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_digest()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "digest", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    if data_23.len() as usize != 4 {
        let actual = data_23.len() as usize;
        return Err(
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!(
                    "invalid fixed bytes length for field '{}': expected {}, got {}",
                    "digest", 4, actual
                ),
            ),
        );
    }
    let mut bytes_25 = [0u8; 4];
    for (idx, byte) in data_23.iter().enumerate() {
        bytes_25[idx] = *byte;
    }
    let digest_24 = bytes_25;
    let data_26 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_raw()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "raw", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let raw_27 = data_26.to_vec();
    let list_28 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_gains()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "gains", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    if list_28.len() as usize != 3 {
        let actual = list_28.len() as usize;
        return Err(
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!(
                    "invalid fixed list length for field '{}': expected {}, got {}",
                    "gains", 3, actual
                ),
            ),
        );
    }
    let mut gains_29: [f32; 3] = [f32::default(); 3];
    for (idx, element) in list_28.iter().enumerate() {
        gains_29[idx] = element;
    }
    let list_30 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_readings()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "readings", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let readings_31 = list_30.iter().collect::<Vec<i16>>();
    let list_32 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_names()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "names", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let names_33 = list_32
        .iter()
        .map(|element| {
            (element
                .map_err(|source| source.to_string())
                .and_then(|text| {
                    text.to_str().map(str::to_owned).map_err(|source| source.to_string())
                }))
                .map_err(|source| {
                    #[allow(clippy::all)]
                    let context = String::from("Message");
                    crate::Error::Deserialization(
                        format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "names", context, source),
                    )
                })
        })
        .collect::<::std::result::Result<Vec<String>, crate::Error>>()?;
    let list_34 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_chunks()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "chunks", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let chunks_35 = list_34
        .iter()
        .map(|element| {
            (element.map(|data| data.to_vec()).map_err(|source| source.to_string()))
                .map_err(|source| {
                    #[allow(clippy::all)]
                    let context = String::from("Message");
                    crate::Error::Deserialization(
                        format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "chunks", context, source),
                    )
                })
        })
        .collect::<::std::result::Result<Vec<Vec<u8>>, crate::Error>>()?;
    let limits_opt_36 = if root.reborrow().has_limits() {
        let list_37 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_limits()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("Message");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "limits", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let limits_38 = list_37.iter().collect::<Vec<f64>>();
        Some(limits_38)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let list_39 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_points()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("Message");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "points", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let mut result_40 = Vec::with_capacity(list_39.len() as usize);
    for element_42 in list_39.iter() {
        let x_43 = element_42.reborrow().get_x();
        let text_45 = element_42
            .reborrow()
            .get_tag()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("Message");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "tag", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let tag_44 = text_45
            .to_str()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("Message");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "tag", context, source),
                )
            })?
            .to_owned();
        result_40
            .push(MessagePointsItem {
                x: x_43,
                tag: tag_44,
            });
    }
    let points_41 = result_40;
    Ok(Message {
        max_speed: max_speed_0,
        type_: type_1,
        payload: payload_3,
        stamp: stamp_5,
        note: note_opt_8,
        pose: pose_18,
        calibration: calibration_opt_19,
        digest: digest_24,
        raw: raw_27,
        gains: gains_29,
        readings: readings_31,
        names: names_33,
        chunks: chunks_35,
        limits: limits_opt_36,
        points: points_41,
    })
}
