//!Mock producer for the `rich_link` dependency slot: plays the dependency over the real wire under the identity the harness seeds into the slot's bound set. One messaging session per mock, so [`Mock::stop`] is a whole-producer loss.
/// This node's consumer-slot link_id.
pub const LINK_ID: &str = "rich_link";
/// Core-node segment of the mock's wire identity.
pub const MOCK_CORE_NODE: &str = "mock-core";
/// Default instance segment of the mock's wire identity.
pub const MOCK_INSTANCE_ID: &str = "mock-rich_link";
/// The default mock's wire identity, as the harness seeds it.
pub fn producer_ref() -> peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef {
    producer_ref_for(MOCK_INSTANCE_ID)
}
/// [`producer_ref`] under an explicit instance id (multi-instance slots).
pub fn producer_ref_for(instance_id: &str) -> peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef {
    peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef::new(MOCK_CORE_NODE, instance_id)
}
/// One mocked producer instance for this slot. Fields are the typed
/// per-interface surfaces; construct with [`Mock::start`].
pub struct Mock {
    _session: peppylib::MessengerHandle,
    pub rich_stream: rich_stream::Publisher,
    pub rich_service: rich_service::Service,
    pub rich_action: rich_action::Action,
}
impl Mock {
    /// Connects a dedicated session and declares every interface of
    /// this dependency now (publishers, queryables, action engines),
    /// under the default [`MOCK_INSTANCE_ID`].
    pub async fn start(
        router: &peppylib::testing::EphemeralRouter,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        Self::start_as(router, MOCK_INSTANCE_ID).await
    }
    /// [`Mock::start`] under an explicit instance id, for slots bound
    /// to several mock instances.
    pub async fn start_as(
        router: &peppylib::testing::EphemeralRouter,
        instance_id: &str,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let session = router.connect().await?;
        let rich_stream = rich_stream::Publisher::declare(&session, instance_id).await?;
        let rich_service = rich_service::Service::listen(&session, instance_id).await?;
        let rich_action = rich_action::Action::expose(&session, instance_id).await?;
        Ok(Self {
            _session: session,
            rich_stream,
            rich_service,
            rich_action,
        })
    }
    /// Simulates this producer dying mid-flight, deterministically:
    /// live action goals are disarmed (their eventual drop emits no
    /// clean close), then every declaration and the session drop,
    /// releasing the liveliness tokens consumers latch on.
    pub fn stop(self) {
        self.rich_action.stop();
    }
}
///Typed mock publisher for the consumed topic `rich_stream`: publishes over the real wire as this mock's identity; the first publish waits for the node's subscription to match (no sleeps), and no subscriber within the readiness timeout is a loud error.
pub mod rich_stream {
    pub use crate::consumed_topics::rich_link::rich_stream::Message;
    #[allow(unused_imports)]
    use crate::consumed_topics::rich_link::rich_stream::*;
    pub struct Publisher {
        core: peppylib::testing::TestTopicPublisher,
    }
    impl Publisher {
        pub(super) async fn declare(
            session: &peppylib::MessengerHandle,
            instance_id: &str,
        ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
            let core = peppylib::testing::TestTopicPublisher::declare(
                    session,
                    super::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                    instance_id,
                    peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("rich_producer", "v1")?,
                    None,
                    "rich_stream",
                    peppylib::config::QoSProfile::Standard,
                    peppylib::config::TopicRetention::LiveOnly,
                )
                .await?;
            Ok(Self { core })
        }
        /// Publishes one typed message; lazily waits for the node's
        /// subscription before the first delivery.
        pub async fn publish(&self, message: &Message) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.core.publish(serialize_message(message)?).await
        }
        /// Waits until the node's subscription for this topic is
        /// visible; returns whether it matched within `timeout`.
        pub async fn wait_for_subscriber(
            &self,
            timeout: std::time::Duration,
        ) -> crate::Result<bool> {
            self.core.wait_for_subscriber(timeout).await
        }
    }
    fn serialize_message(value: &Message) -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {
        Ok({
            let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
            {
                let mut root = capnp_msg
                    .init_root::<
                        crate::capnp::on_next_rich_link_rich_stream_message_capnp::rich_link_rich_stream_message::Builder,
                    >();
                root.set_max_speed(value.max_speed);
                root.set_type(value.type_.as_str());
                root.set_payload(value.payload.as_ref());
                let timestamp_0 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time(value.stamp);
                let mut timestamp_builder_1 = root.reborrow().init_stamp();
                timestamp_builder_1.set_sec(timestamp_0.sec);
                timestamp_builder_1.set_nsec(timestamp_0.nsec);
                if let Some(value_2) = (value.note).as_ref() {
                    root.set_note(value_2.as_str());
                }
                let mut builder_3 = root.reborrow().init_pose();
                builder_3.set_x((value.pose).x);
                let mut builder_4 = builder_3.reborrow().init_frame();
                builder_4.set_id(((value.pose).frame).id);
                builder_4.set_name(((value.pose).frame).name.as_str());
                if let Some(value_5) = (value.calibration).as_ref() {
                    let mut builder_6 = root.reborrow().init_calibration();
                    builder_6.set_gain(*&(value_5).gain);
                }
                root.set_digest(value.digest.as_ref());
                root.set_raw(value.raw.as_ref());
                let mut list_7 = root.reborrow().init_gains(3);
                for (idx_8, value_9) in (value.gains).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_7.set(idx_8 as u32, *value_9);
                }
                let mut list_10 = root
                    .reborrow()
                    .init_readings(
                        u32::try_from((value.readings).len())
                            .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                    );
                for (idx_11, value_12) in (value.readings).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_10.set(idx_11 as u32, *value_12);
                }
                let mut list_13 = root
                    .reborrow()
                    .init_names(
                        u32::try_from((value.names).len())
                            .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                    );
                for (idx_14, value_15) in (value.names).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_13.set(idx_14 as u32, value_15.as_str());
                }
                let mut list_16 = root
                    .reborrow()
                    .init_chunks(
                        u32::try_from((value.chunks).len())
                            .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                    );
                for (idx_17, value_18) in (value.chunks).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_16.set(idx_17 as u32, value_18.as_ref());
                }
                if let Some(value_19) = (value.limits).as_ref() {
                    let mut list_20 = root
                        .reborrow()
                        .init_limits(
                            u32::try_from((value_19).len())
                                .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                        );
                    for (idx_21, value_22) in (value_19).iter().enumerate() {
                        list_20.set(idx_21 as u32, *value_22);
                    }
                }
                let mut list_23 = root
                    .reborrow()
                    .init_points(
                        u32::try_from((value.points).len())
                            .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                    );
                for (idx_24, element_25) in (value.points).iter().enumerate() {
                    let mut builder_26 = list_23.reborrow().get(idx_24 as u32);
                    builder_26.set_x(element_25.x);
                    builder_26.set_tag(element_25.tag.as_str());
                }
            }
            let mut buffer = Vec::new();
            capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
                .map_err(|source| {
                    let context = String::from("mock publish rich_stream");
                    crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
                })?;
            peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
        })
    }
}
///Typed mock server for the consumed service `rich_service`: a background pump captures every request; scripted responses serve automatically, unscripted requests park for `next_request`.
pub mod rich_service {
    pub use crate::consumed_services::rich_link::rich_service::Request;
    pub use crate::consumed_services::rich_link::rich_service::ResponseData;
    pub use crate::consumed_services::rich_link::rich_service::{Failure, FailureKind};
    #[allow(unused_imports)]
    use crate::consumed_services::rich_link::rich_service::*;
    pub struct Service {
        core: peppylib::testing::MockServiceCore,
    }
    /// Answers exactly one parked request; consumed by use.
    pub struct Responder {
        inner: peppylib::messaging::ServiceResponder,
    }
    impl Service {
        pub(super) async fn listen(
            session: &peppylib::MessengerHandle,
            instance_id: &str,
        ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
            let core = peppylib::testing::MockServiceCore::listen(
                    session,
                    super::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                    instance_id,
                    peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("rich_producer", "v1")?,
                    "rich_service",
                )
                .await?;
            Ok(Self { core })
        }
        /// The next unscripted request, decoded, with the responder
        /// the test must use to answer it. Errors after `timeout`.
        pub async fn next_request(
            &self,
            timeout: std::time::Duration,
        ) -> crate::Result<(Request, Responder)> {
            let (context, responder) = self.core.next_request(timeout).await?;
            let request = deserialize_request(
                context.message().payload_bytes().as_ref(),
            )?;
            Ok((request, Responder { inner: responder }))
        }
        ///Enqueues one response to be served automatically to the next inbound request (FIFO across repeated calls); only unscripted requests park for `next_request`.
        pub fn enqueue_response(&self, response: ResponseData) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.core.enqueue_response(serialize_response(&response)?);
            Ok(())
        }
        /// Every request received so far (scripted and manual alike),
        /// decoded, in arrival order.
        pub fn captured(&self) -> crate::Result<Vec<Request>> {
            self.core
                .captured()
                .iter()
                .map(|captured| {
                    deserialize_request(captured.message.payload_bytes().as_ref())
                })
                .collect()
        }
    }
    impl Responder {
        /// Sends the typed response for the parked request.
        pub async fn respond(self, response: ResponseData) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.inner.respond(serialize_response(&response)?).await
        }
        /// Answers the parked request with an error reply: a failure
        /// kind that this member declares and a message.
        pub async fn respond_failure(
            self,
            kind: FailureKind,
            message: impl Into<String>,
        ) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.inner.respond_failure(Failure::new(kind, message).to_wire()).await
        }
    }
    fn deserialize_request(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<Request> {
        #[allow(clippy::all)]
        let context = String::from("mock rich_service request");
        let mut payload = payload;
        let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
                &mut payload,
                capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
            )
            .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("{}: {}", context, source),
            ))?;
        let root = message_reader
            .get_root::<
                crate::capnp::poll_rich_producer_rich_service_message_capnp::rich_service_message::Reader,
            >()
            .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("{}: {}", context, source),
            ))?;
        let max_speed_0 = root.reborrow().get_max_speed();
        let text_2 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_type()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("mock rich_service request");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "type", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let type_1 = text_2
            .to_str()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("mock rich_service request");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "type", context, source),
                )
            })?
            .to_owned();
        let data_4 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_payload()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("mock rich_service request");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "payload", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let payload_3 = data_4.to_vec();
        let timestamp_6 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_stamp()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("mock rich_service request");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "stamp", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let capnp_timestamp_7 = peppylib::encoding::CapnpTimestamp {
            sec: timestamp_6.get_sec(),
            nsec: timestamp_6.get_nsec(),
        };
        let stamp_5 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time_from_capnp(capnp_timestamp_7);
        let note_opt_8 = if root.reborrow().has_note() {
            let text_10 = root
                .reborrow()
                .get_note()
                .map_err(|source| {
                    #[allow(clippy::all)]
                    let context = String::from("mock rich_service request");
                    crate::Error::Deserialization(
                        format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "note", context, source),
                    )
                })?;
            let note_9 = text_10
                .to_str()
                .map_err(|source| {
                    #[allow(clippy::all)]
                    let context = String::from("mock rich_service request");
                    crate::Error::Deserialization(
                        format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "note", context, source),
                    )
                })?
                .to_owned();
            Some(note_9)
        } else {
            None
        };
        let reader_11 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_pose()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("mock rich_service request");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "pose", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let x_12 = reader_11.reborrow().get_x();
        let reader_13 = reader_11
            .reborrow()
            .get_frame()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("mock rich_service request");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "frame", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let id_14 = reader_13.reborrow().get_id();
        let text_16 = reader_13
            .reborrow()
            .get_name()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("mock rich_service request");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "name", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let name_15 = text_16
            .to_str()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("mock rich_service request");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "name", context, source),
                )
            })?
            .to_owned();
        let frame_17 = RichServicePoseFrame {
            id: id_14,
            name: name_15,
        };
        let pose_18 = RichServicePose {
            x: x_12,
            frame: frame_17,
        };
        let calibration_opt_19 = if root.reborrow().has_calibration() {
            let reader_20 = root
                .reborrow()
                .get_calibration()
                .map_err(|source| {
                    #[allow(clippy::all)]
                    let context = String::from("mock rich_service request");
                    crate::Error::Deserialization(
                        format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "calibration", context, source),
                    )
                })?;
            let gain_21 = reader_20.reborrow().get_gain();
            let calibration_22 = RichServiceCalibration {
                gain: gain_21,
            };
            Some(calibration_22)
        } else {
            None
        };
        let data_23 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_digest()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("mock rich_service request");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "digest", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        if data_23.len() as usize != 4 {
            let actual = data_23.len() as usize;
            return Err(
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!(
                        "invalid fixed bytes length for field '{}': expected {}, got {}",
                        "digest", 4, actual
                    ),
                ),
            );
        }
        let mut bytes_25 = [0u8; 4];
        for (idx, byte) in data_23.iter().enumerate() {
            bytes_25[idx] = *byte;
        }
        let digest_24 = bytes_25;
        let data_26 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_raw()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("mock rich_service request");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "raw", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let raw_27 = data_26.to_vec();
        let list_28 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_gains()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("mock rich_service request");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "gains", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        if list_28.len() as usize != 3 {
            let actual = list_28.len() as usize;
            return Err(
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!(
                        "invalid fixed list length for field '{}': expected {}, got {}",
                        "gains", 3, actual
                    ),
                ),
            );
        }
        let mut gains_29: [f32; 3] = [f32::default(); 3];
        for (idx, element) in list_28.iter().enumerate() {
            gains_29[idx] = element;
        }
        let list_30 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_readings()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("mock rich_service request");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "readings", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let readings_31 = list_30.iter().collect::<Vec<i16>>();
        let list_32 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_names()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("mock rich_service request");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "names", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let names_33 = list_32
            .iter()
            .map(|element| {
                (element
                    .map_err(|source| source.to_string())
                    .and_then(|text| {
                        text.to_str()
                            .map(str::to_owned)
                            .map_err(|source| source.to_string())
                    }))
                    .map_err(|source| {
                        #[allow(clippy::all)]
                        let context = String::from("mock rich_service request");
                        crate::Error::Deserialization(
                            format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "names", context, source),
                        )
                    })
            })
            .collect::<::std::result::Result<Vec<String>, crate::Error>>()?;
        let list_34 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_chunks()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("mock rich_service request");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "chunks", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let chunks_35 = list_34
            .iter()
            .map(|element| {
                (element.map(|data| data.to_vec()).map_err(|source| source.to_string()))
                    .map_err(|source| {
                        #[allow(clippy::all)]
                        let context = String::from("mock rich_service request");
                        crate::Error::Deserialization(
                            format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "chunks", context, source),
                        )
                    })
            })
            .collect::<::std::result::Result<Vec<Vec<u8>>, crate::Error>>()?;
        let limits_opt_36 = if root.reborrow().has_limits() {
            let list_37 = root
                .reborrow()
                .get_limits()
                .map_err(|source| {
                    #[allow(clippy::all)]
                    let context = String::from("mock rich_service request");
                    crate::Error::Deserialization(
                        format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "limits", context, source),
                    )
                })?;
            let limits_38 = list_37.iter().collect::<Vec<f64>>();
            Some(limits_38)
        } else {
            None
        };
        let list_39 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_points()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("mock rich_service request");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "points", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let mut result_40 = Vec::with_capacity(list_39.len() as usize);
        for element_42 in list_39.iter() {
            let x_43 = element_42.reborrow().get_x();
            let text_45 = element_42
                .reborrow()
                .get_tag()
                .map_err(|source| {
                    #[allow(clippy::all)]
                    let context = String::from("mock rich_service request");
                    crate::Error::Deserialization(
                        format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "tag", context, source),
                    )
                })?;
            let tag_44 = text_45
                .to_str()
                .map_err(|source| {
                    #[allow(clippy::all)]
                    let context = String::from("mock rich_service request");
                    crate::Error::Deserialization(
                        format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "tag", context, source),
                    )
                })?
                .to_owned();
            result_40
                .push(RichServicePointsItem {
                    x: x_43,
                    tag: tag_44,
                });
        }
        let points_41 = result_40;
        Ok(Request {
            max_speed: max_speed_0,
            type_: type_1,
            payload: payload_3,
            stamp: stamp_5,
            note: note_opt_8,
            pose: pose_18,
            calibration: calibration_opt_19,
            digest: digest_24,
            raw: raw_27,
            gains: gains_29,
            readings: readings_31,
            names: names_33,
            chunks: chunks_35,
            limits: limits_opt_36,
            points: points_41,
        })
    }
    fn serialize_response(value: &ResponseData) -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {
        Ok({
            let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
            {
                let mut root = capnp_msg
                    .init_root::<
                        crate::capnp::poll_rich_producer_rich_service_response_message_capnp::rich_service_response_message::Builder,
                    >();
                root.set_max_speed(value.max_speed);
                root.set_type(value.type_.as_str());
                root.set_payload(value.payload.as_ref());
                let timestamp_0 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time(value.stamp);
                let mut timestamp_builder_1 = root.reborrow().init_stamp();
                timestamp_builder_1.set_sec(timestamp_0.sec);
                timestamp_builder_1.set_nsec(timestamp_0.nsec);
                if let Some(value_2) = (value.note).as_ref() {
                    root.set_note(value_2.as_str());
                }
                let mut builder_3 = root.reborrow().init_pose();
                builder_3.set_x((value.pose).x);
                let mut builder_4 = builder_3.reborrow().init_frame();
                builder_4.set_id(((value.pose).frame).id);
                builder_4.set_name(((value.pose).frame).name.as_str());
                if let Some(value_5) = (value.calibration).as_ref() {
                    let mut builder_6 = root.reborrow().init_calibration();
                    builder_6.set_gain(*&(value_5).gain);
                }
                root.set_digest(value.digest.as_ref());
                root.set_raw(value.raw.as_ref());
                let mut list_7 = root.reborrow().init_gains(3);
                for (idx_8, value_9) in (value.gains).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_7.set(idx_8 as u32, *value_9);
                }
                let mut list_10 = root
                    .reborrow()
                    .init_readings(
                        u32::try_from((value.readings).len())
                            .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                    );
                for (idx_11, value_12) in (value.readings).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_10.set(idx_11 as u32, *value_12);
                }
                let mut list_13 = root
                    .reborrow()
                    .init_names(
                        u32::try_from((value.names).len())
                            .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                    );
                for (idx_14, value_15) in (value.names).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_13.set(idx_14 as u32, value_15.as_str());
                }
                let mut list_16 = root
                    .reborrow()
                    .init_chunks(
                        u32::try_from((value.chunks).len())
                            .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                    );
                for (idx_17, value_18) in (value.chunks).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_16.set(idx_17 as u32, value_18.as_ref());
                }
                if let Some(value_19) = (value.limits).as_ref() {
                    let mut list_20 = root
                        .reborrow()
                        .init_limits(
                            u32::try_from((value_19).len())
                                .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                        );
                    for (idx_21, value_22) in (value_19).iter().enumerate() {
                        list_20.set(idx_21 as u32, *value_22);
                    }
                }
                let mut list_23 = root
                    .reborrow()
                    .init_points(
                        u32::try_from((value.points).len())
                            .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                    );
                for (idx_24, element_25) in (value.points).iter().enumerate() {
                    let mut builder_26 = list_23.reborrow().get(idx_24 as u32);
                    builder_26.set_x(element_25.x);
                    builder_26.set_tag(element_25.tag.as_str());
                }
            }
            let mut buffer = Vec::new();
            capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
                .map_err(|source| {
                    let context = String::from("mock rich_service response");
                    crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
                })?;
            peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
        })
    }
}
///Typed mock action server for the consumed action `rich_action`, on the real `ConcurrentAction` engine: identical goal lifecycle to a production producer, plus deterministic producer-loss via the owning mock's `stop()`.
pub mod rich_action {
    pub use crate::consumed_actions::rich_link::rich_action::FeedbackMessage;
    #[allow(unused_imports)]
    use crate::consumed_actions::rich_link::rich_action::*;
    pub use crate::consumed_actions::rich_link::rich_action::{Failure, FailureKind};
    pub struct Action {
        core: peppylib::testing::MockActionServerCore,
    }
    impl Action {
        pub(super) async fn expose(
            session: &peppylib::MessengerHandle,
            instance_id: &str,
        ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
            let core = peppylib::testing::MockActionServerCore::expose(
                    session,
                    super::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                    instance_id,
                    peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("rich_producer", "v1")?,
                    "rich_action",
                    true,
                )
                .await?;
            Ok(Self { core })
        }
        /// Parks until the node sends a goal, bounded by `timeout`;
        /// the returned goal awaits the test's admission decision.
        pub async fn next_goal(
            &mut self,
            timeout: std::time::Duration,
        ) -> crate::Result<PendingGoal> {
            let pending = self.core.next_goal(timeout).await?;
            Ok(PendingGoal { pending })
        }
        pub(super) fn stop(self) {
            self.core.stop();
        }
    }
    /// A goal received by the mock, awaiting accept/refuse.
    pub struct PendingGoal {
        pending: peppylib::testing::MockPendingGoal,
    }
    impl PendingGoal {
        /// The client-generated correlation id for this goal.
        pub fn goal_id(&self) -> &str {
            self.pending.goal_id()
        }
        /// Accepts the goal; the returned handle drives feedback and
        /// completion.
        pub async fn accept(self) -> crate::Result<ActiveGoal> {
            let context = self.pending.accept(peppylib::Payload::new()).await?;
            Ok(ActiveGoal { context })
        }
        /// Refuses the goal with a failure kind that this member
        /// declares and a message. A refusal carries no goal
        /// response.
        pub async fn refuse(
            self,
            kind: FailureKind,
            message: impl Into<String>,
        ) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.pending.refuse(Failure::new(kind, message).to_wire()).await
        }
    }
    /// An accepted goal: drives feedback and terminal completion.
    pub struct ActiveGoal {
        context: std::sync::Arc<peppylib::messaging::GoalContext>,
    }
    impl ActiveGoal {
        /// Publishes one typed feedback message for this goal.
        pub async fn publish_feedback(
            &self,
            feedback: &FeedbackMessage,
        ) -> crate::Result<()> {
            let payload = serialize_feedback(feedback)?;
            let payload = peppylib::messaging::NonEmptyPayload::try_new(payload)
                .map_err(|_| peppylib::PeppyError::Io(
                    std::io::Error::other(
                        "feedback serialized to zero bytes; empty is reserved for \
                         end-of-stream",
                    ),
                ))?;
            self.context.publish_feedback(payload).await?;
            Ok(())
        }
        /// Resolves when this goal receives its cancel signal: a
        /// cancel request of the node, or the end of the node.
        pub async fn cancel_signal(&self) {
            self.context.cancel_signal().await
        }
        /// Whether this goal received its cancel signal.
        pub fn is_cancelled(&self) -> bool {
            self.context.is_cancelled()
        }
        /// Why this goal received its cancel signal; `None` before
        /// it did. `CancelCause::reason` gives a reason for
        /// `complete_cancelled`.
        pub fn cancel_cause(&self) -> Option<peppylib::messaging::CancelCause> {
            self.context.cancel_cause()
        }
        /// Ends the goal with the status completed: a success.
        pub async fn complete(&self) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.context.complete(peppylib::Payload::new()).await?;
            Ok(())
        }
        /// Ends the goal with the status failed: a failure kind
        /// that this member declares, a message, and no result.
        pub async fn fail(
            &self,
            kind: FailureKind,
            message: impl Into<String>,
        ) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.context.fail(Failure::new(kind, message).to_wire()).await?;
            Ok(())
        }
        /// Ends the goal with the status cancelled, for `reason`.
        pub async fn complete_cancelled(
            &self,
            reason: impl Into<String>,
        ) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.context.complete_cancelled(reason, peppylib::Payload::new()).await?;
            Ok(())
        }
    }
    fn serialize_feedback(value: &FeedbackMessage) -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {
        Ok({
            let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
            {
                let mut root = capnp_msg
                    .init_root::<
                        crate::capnp::rich_producer_rich_action_feedback_message_capnp::rich_producer_rich_action_action_feedback_message::Builder,
                    >();
                root.set_max_speed(value.max_speed);
                root.set_type(value.type_.as_str());
                root.set_payload(value.payload.as_ref());
                let timestamp_0 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time(value.stamp);
                let mut timestamp_builder_1 = root.reborrow().init_stamp();
                timestamp_builder_1.set_sec(timestamp_0.sec);
                timestamp_builder_1.set_nsec(timestamp_0.nsec);
                if let Some(value_2) = (value.note).as_ref() {
                    root.set_note(value_2.as_str());
                }
                let mut builder_3 = root.reborrow().init_pose();
                builder_3.set_x((value.pose).x);
                let mut builder_4 = builder_3.reborrow().init_frame();
                builder_4.set_id(((value.pose).frame).id);
                builder_4.set_name(((value.pose).frame).name.as_str());
                if let Some(value_5) = (value.calibration).as_ref() {
                    let mut builder_6 = root.reborrow().init_calibration();
                    builder_6.set_gain(*&(value_5).gain);
                }
                root.set_digest(value.digest.as_ref());
                root.set_raw(value.raw.as_ref());
                let mut list_7 = root.reborrow().init_gains(3);
                for (idx_8, value_9) in (value.gains).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_7.set(idx_8 as u32, *value_9);
                }
                let mut list_10 = root
                    .reborrow()
                    .init_readings(
                        u32::try_from((value.readings).len())
                            .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                    );
                for (idx_11, value_12) in (value.readings).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_10.set(idx_11 as u32, *value_12);
                }
                let mut list_13 = root
                    .reborrow()
                    .init_names(
                        u32::try_from((value.names).len())
                            .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                    );
                for (idx_14, value_15) in (value.names).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_13.set(idx_14 as u32, value_15.as_str());
                }
                let mut list_16 = root
                    .reborrow()
                    .init_chunks(
                        u32::try_from((value.chunks).len())
                            .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                    );
                for (idx_17, value_18) in (value.chunks).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_16.set(idx_17 as u32, value_18.as_ref());
                }
                if let Some(value_19) = (value.limits).as_ref() {
                    let mut list_20 = root
                        .reborrow()
                        .init_limits(
                            u32::try_from((value_19).len())
                                .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                        );
                    for (idx_21, value_22) in (value_19).iter().enumerate() {
                        list_20.set(idx_21 as u32, *value_22);
                    }
                }
                let mut list_23 = root
                    .reborrow()
                    .init_points(
                        u32::try_from((value.points).len())
                            .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                    );
                for (idx_24, element_25) in (value.points).iter().enumerate() {
                    let mut builder_26 = list_23.reborrow().get(idx_24 as u32);
                    builder_26.set_x(element_25.x);
                    builder_26.set_tag(element_25.tag.as_str());
                }
            }
            let mut buffer = Vec::new();
            capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
                .map_err(|source| {
                    let context = String::from("mock rich_action feedback");
                    crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
                })?;
            peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
        })
    }
}


================================================================
Mock :: pairings/rich_sensor_bus
================================================================
//!Mock peer for the `rich_sensor_bus` pairing slot: publishes the topics the node consumes to the node's slot and holds pinned subscriptions to the topics the node emits, under the pin identity the harness seeds (`peer_info()`); a multi slot starts several instances with distinct instance ids.
/// This node's own pairing-slot link_id.
pub const LINK_ID: &str = "rich_sensor_bus";
pub const PAIRING_NAME: &str = "arm_link";
pub const PAIRING_TAG: &str = "v1";
/// The mock peer's own (complementary) slot id: what the node's
/// `paired()` reports as `peer_link_id` under the harness.
pub const PEER_LINK_ID: &str = "mock_peer";
/// Core-node segment of the mock's wire identity.
pub const MOCK_CORE_NODE: &str = "mock-core";
/// Default instance segment of the mock's wire identity.
pub const MOCK_INSTANCE_ID: &str = "mock-rich_sensor_bus";
/// The default mock peer's wire identity.
pub fn producer_ref() -> peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef {
    peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef::new(MOCK_CORE_NODE, MOCK_INSTANCE_ID)
}
/// The full pin identity the harness seeds for this slot: what the
/// node's `paired()` / `wait_paired()` resolve to.
pub fn peer_info() -> peppylib::messaging::PeerInfo {
    peer_info_for(MOCK_INSTANCE_ID)
}
/// [`peer_info`] under an explicit instance id (multi slots): the
/// `info` of the member the node's `peers()` lists for it.
pub fn peer_info_for(instance_id: &str) -> peppylib::messaging::PeerInfo {
    peppylib::messaging::PeerInfo {
        producer: peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef::new(MOCK_CORE_NODE, instance_id),
        peer_link_id: PEER_LINK_ID.to_string(),
    }
}
/// One mock peer instance for this slot; construct with
/// [`Mock::start`] / [`Mock::start_as`].
pub struct Mock {
    _session: peppylib::MessengerHandle,
    pub rich_states: rich_states::Publisher,
}
impl Mock {
    /// Connects a dedicated session, declares the peer publishers and
    /// opens the pinned subscriptions to the node's emissions, under
    /// the default [`MOCK_INSTANCE_ID`]. `node_instance_id` is the
    /// node-under-test's instance id (the harness passes its own).
    pub async fn start(
        router: &peppylib::testing::EphemeralRouter,
        node_instance_id: &str,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        Self::start_as(router, MOCK_INSTANCE_ID, node_instance_id).await
    }
    /// [`Mock::start`] under an explicit instance id, for pairing
    /// several mock peers into one multi slot.
    pub async fn start_as(
        router: &peppylib::testing::EphemeralRouter,
        instance_id: &str,
        node_instance_id: &str,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let session = router.connect().await?;
        let node = peppylib::messaging::PeerInfo {
            producer: peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef::new(
                peppylib::testing::STANDALONE_CORE_NODE,
                node_instance_id,
            ),
            peer_link_id: LINK_ID.to_string(),
        };
        let rich_states = rich_states::Publisher::declare(&session, instance_id, &node)
            .await?;
        Ok(Self {
            _session: session,
            rich_states,
        })
    }
    /// Simulates the peer disappearing: every declaration and the
    /// session drop.
    pub fn stop(self) {}
}
///Typed peer publisher for `rich_states` (the node consumes this direction): publishes from the mock peer's slot to the node's slot, so the node's pinned subscription receives it.
pub mod rich_states {
    pub use crate::paired_topics::rich_sensor_bus::rich_states::Message;
    #[allow(unused_imports)]
    use crate::paired_topics::rich_sensor_bus::rich_states::*;
    pub struct Publisher {
        core: peppylib::testing::TestTopicPublisher,
    }
    impl Publisher {
        pub(super) async fn declare(
            session: &peppylib::MessengerHandle,
            instance_id: &str,
            node: &peppylib::messaging::PeerInfo,
        ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
            let core = peppylib::testing::TestTopicPublisher::declare_to_peer(
                    session,
                    super::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                    instance_id,
                    peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::pairing("arm_link", "v1")?,
                    super::PEER_LINK_ID,
                    "rich_states",
                    peppylib::config::QoSProfile::SensorData,
                    node.clone(),
                )
                .await?;
            Ok(Self { core })
        }
        /// Publishes one typed message; lazily waits for the node's
        /// pinned subscription before the first delivery.
        pub async fn publish(&self, message: &Message) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.core.publish(serialize_message(message)?).await
        }
        /// Waits until the node's pinned subscription is visible;
        /// returns whether it matched within `timeout`.
        pub async fn wait_for_subscriber(
            &self,
            timeout: std::time::Duration,
        ) -> crate::Result<bool> {
            self.core.wait_for_subscriber(timeout).await
        }
    }
    fn serialize_message(value: &Message) -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {
        Ok({
            let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
            {
                let mut root = capnp_msg
                    .init_root::<
                        crate::capnp::peer_rich_sensor_bus_rich_states_message_capnp::peer_rich_sensor_bus_rich_states_message::Builder,
                    >();
                root.set_max_speed(value.max_speed);
                root.set_type(value.type_.as_str());
                root.set_payload(value.payload.as_ref());
                let timestamp_0 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time(value.stamp);
                let mut timestamp_builder_1 = root.reborrow().init_stamp();
                timestamp_builder_1.set_sec(timestamp_0.sec);
                timestamp_builder_1.set_nsec(timestamp_0.nsec);
                if let Some(value_2) = (value.note).as_ref() {
                    root.set_note(value_2.as_str());
                }
                let mut builder_3 = root.reborrow().init_pose();
                builder_3.set_x((value.pose).x);
                let mut builder_4 = builder_3.reborrow().init_frame();
                builder_4.set_id(((value.pose).frame).id);
                builder_4.set_name(((value.pose).frame).name.as_str());
                if let Some(value_5) = (value.calibration).as_ref() {
                    let mut builder_6 = root.reborrow().init_calibration();
                    builder_6.set_gain(*&(value_5).gain);
                }
                root.set_digest(value.digest.as_ref());
                root.set_raw(value.raw.as_ref());
                let mut list_7 = root.reborrow().init_gains(3);
                for (idx_8, value_9) in (value.gains).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_7.set(idx_8 as u32, *value_9);
                }
                let mut list_10 = root
                    .reborrow()
                    .init_readings(
                        u32::try_from((value.readings).len())
                            .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                    );
                for (idx_11, value_12) in (value.readings).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_10.set(idx_11 as u32, *value_12);
                }
                let mut list_13 = root
                    .reborrow()
                    .init_names(
                        u32::try_from((value.names).len())
                            .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                    );
                for (idx_14, value_15) in (value.names).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_13.set(idx_14 as u32, value_15.as_str());
                }
                let mut list_16 = root
                    .reborrow()
                    .init_chunks(
                        u32::try_from((value.chunks).len())
                            .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                    );
                for (idx_17, value_18) in (value.chunks).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_16.set(idx_17 as u32, value_18.as_ref());
                }
                if let Some(value_19) = (value.limits).as_ref() {
                    let mut list_20 = root
                        .reborrow()
                        .init_limits(
                            u32::try_from((value_19).len())
                                .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                        );
                    for (idx_21, value_22) in (value_19).iter().enumerate() {
                        list_20.set(idx_21 as u32, *value_22);
                    }
                }
                let mut list_23 = root
                    .reborrow()
                    .init_points(
                        u32::try_from((value.points).len())
                            .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                    );
                for (idx_24, element_25) in (value.points).iter().enumerate() {
                    let mut builder_26 = list_23.reborrow().get(idx_24 as u32);
                    builder_26.set_x(element_25.x);
                    builder_26.set_tag(element_25.tag.as_str());
                }
            }
            let mut buffer = Vec::new();
            capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
                .map_err(|source| {
                    let context = String::from("mock peer publish rich_states");
                    crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
                })?;
            peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
        })
    }
}


================================================================
Fixture :: emitted_topics/rich_stream
================================================================
//!Typed observation of the node's emitted topic `rich_stream`: the harness subscribes before the node boots (pinned to the node's identity) and barriers on the subscription, so the node's very first publish is captured.
/// A held subscription to the node's emissions on this topic. It
/// keeps the newest messages the test has not read, up to the queue
/// size of the topic's QoS tier, or its retention depth when that is
/// larger: when the queue is full, a new message drops the oldest
/// unread one. A test can leave it unread, and its unread messages
/// never hold up the other messages to the harness's session.
pub struct Subscription {
    inner: peppylib::messaging::Subscription,
}
pub(crate) async fn subscribe(
    session: &peppylib::MessengerHandle,
    node_instance_id: &str,
) -> crate::Result<Subscription> {
    let producer = peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef::new(
        peppylib::testing::STANDALONE_CORE_NODE,
        node_instance_id,
    );
    let inner = peppylib::TopicMessenger::subscribe(
            session,
            "fixture-core",
            "fixture-observer",
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("rich_node", "v1")?,
            "rich_stream",
            &producer,
            peppylib::config::QoSProfile::SensorData,
            peppylib::config::TopicRetention::LiveOnly,
            peppylib::messaging::TopicBuffer::KeepLatest,
        )
        .await?;
    Ok(Subscription { inner })
}
impl Subscription {
    /// Awaits the node's next message on this topic; `Ok(None)` once
    /// the fixture session closes.
    #[allow(clippy::should_implement_trait)]
    pub async fn next(&mut self) -> crate::Result<Option<Message>> {
        let Some(message) = self.inner.on_next_message().await else {
            return Ok(None);
        };
        let message = deserialize_message(message.payload_bytes().as_ref())?;
        Ok(Some(message))
    }
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct MessagePoseFrame {
    pub id: u32,
    pub name: String,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct MessagePose {
    pub x: f64,
    pub frame: MessagePoseFrame,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct MessageCalibration {
    pub gain: f32,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct MessagePointsItem {
    pub x: f32,
    pub tag: String,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Message {
    pub max_speed: f32,
    pub type_: String,
    pub payload: Vec<u8>,
    pub stamp: std::time::SystemTime,
    pub note: Option<String>,
    pub pose: MessagePose,
    pub calibration: Option<MessageCalibration>,
    pub digest: [u8; 4],
    pub raw: Vec<u8>,
    pub gains: [f32; 3],
    pub readings: Vec<i16>,
    pub names: Vec<String>,
    pub chunks: Vec<Vec<u8>>,
    pub limits: Option<Vec<f64>>,
    pub points: Vec<MessagePointsItem>,
}
fn deserialize_message(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<Message> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]
    let context = String::from("fixtures observe rich_stream");
    let mut payload = payload;
    let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader
        .get_root::<
            crate::capnp::rich_stream_message_capnp::rich_stream_message::Reader,
        >()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let max_speed_0 = root.reborrow().get_max_speed();
    let text_2 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_type()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures observe rich_stream");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "type", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let type_1 = text_2
        .to_str()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures observe rich_stream");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "type", context, source),
            )
        })?
        .to_owned();
    let data_4 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_payload()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures observe rich_stream");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "payload", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let payload_3 = data_4.to_vec();
    let timestamp_6 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_stamp()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures observe rich_stream");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "stamp", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let capnp_timestamp_7 = peppylib::encoding::CapnpTimestamp {
        sec: timestamp_6.get_sec(),
        nsec: timestamp_6.get_nsec(),
    };
    let stamp_5 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time_from_capnp(capnp_timestamp_7);
    let note_opt_8 = if root.reborrow().has_note() {
        let text_10 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_note()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("fixtures observe rich_stream");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "note", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let note_9 = text_10
            .to_str()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("fixtures observe rich_stream");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "note", context, source),
                )
            })?
            .to_owned();
        Some(note_9)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let reader_11 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_pose()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures observe rich_stream");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "pose", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let x_12 = reader_11.reborrow().get_x();
    let reader_13 = reader_11
        .reborrow()
        .get_frame()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures observe rich_stream");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "frame", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let id_14 = reader_13.reborrow().get_id();
    let text_16 = reader_13
        .reborrow()
        .get_name()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures observe rich_stream");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "name", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let name_15 = text_16
        .to_str()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures observe rich_stream");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "name", context, source),
            )
        })?
        .to_owned();
    let frame_17 = MessagePoseFrame {
        id: id_14,
        name: name_15,
    };
    let pose_18 = MessagePose {
        x: x_12,
        frame: frame_17,
    };
    let calibration_opt_19 = if root.reborrow().has_calibration() {
        let reader_20 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_calibration()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("fixtures observe rich_stream");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "calibration", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let gain_21 = reader_20.reborrow().get_gain();
        let calibration_22 = MessageCalibration {
            gain: gain_21,
        };
        Some(calibration_22)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let data_23 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_digest()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures observe rich_stream");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "digest", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    if data_23.len() as usize != 4 {
        let actual = data_23.len() as usize;
        return Err(
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!(
                    "invalid fixed bytes length for field '{}': expected {}, got {}",
                    "digest", 4, actual
                ),
            ),
        );
    }
    let mut bytes_25 = [0u8; 4];
    for (idx, byte) in data_23.iter().enumerate() {
        bytes_25[idx] = *byte;
    }
    let digest_24 = bytes_25;
    let data_26 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_raw()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures observe rich_stream");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "raw", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let raw_27 = data_26.to_vec();
    let list_28 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_gains()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures observe rich_stream");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "gains", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    if list_28.len() as usize != 3 {
        let actual = list_28.len() as usize;
        return Err(
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!(
                    "invalid fixed list length for field '{}': expected {}, got {}",
                    "gains", 3, actual
                ),
            ),
        );
    }
    let mut gains_29: [f32; 3] = [f32::default(); 3];
    for (idx, element) in list_28.iter().enumerate() {
        gains_29[idx] = element;
    }
    let list_30 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_readings()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures observe rich_stream");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "readings", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let readings_31 = list_30.iter().collect::<Vec<i16>>();
    let list_32 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_names()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures observe rich_stream");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "names", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let names_33 = list_32
        .iter()
        .map(|element| {
            (element
                .map_err(|source| source.to_string())
                .and_then(|text| {
                    text.to_str().map(str::to_owned).map_err(|source| source.to_string())
                }))
                .map_err(|source| {
                    #[allow(clippy::all)]
                    let context = String::from("fixtures observe rich_stream");
                    crate::Error::Deserialization(
                        format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "names", context, source),
                    )
                })
        })
        .collect::<::std::result::Result<Vec<String>, crate::Error>>()?;
    let list_34 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_chunks()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures observe rich_stream");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "chunks", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let chunks_35 = list_34
        .iter()
        .map(|element| {
            (element.map(|data| data.to_vec()).map_err(|source| source.to_string()))
                .map_err(|source| {
                    #[allow(clippy::all)]
                    let context = String::from("fixtures observe rich_stream");
                    crate::Error::Deserialization(
                        format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "chunks", context, source),
                    )
                })
        })
        .collect::<::std::result::Result<Vec<Vec<u8>>, crate::Error>>()?;
    let limits_opt_36 = if root.reborrow().has_limits() {
        let list_37 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_limits()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("fixtures observe rich_stream");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "limits", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let limits_38 = list_37.iter().collect::<Vec<f64>>();
        Some(limits_38)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let list_39 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_points()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures observe rich_stream");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "points", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let mut result_40 = Vec::with_capacity(list_39.len() as usize);
    for element_42 in list_39.iter() {
        let x_43 = element_42.reborrow().get_x();
        let text_45 = element_42
            .reborrow()
            .get_tag()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("fixtures observe rich_stream");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "tag", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let tag_44 = text_45
            .to_str()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("fixtures observe rich_stream");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "tag", context, source),
                )
            })?
            .to_owned();
        result_40
            .push(MessagePointsItem {
                x: x_43,
                tag: tag_44,
            });
    }
    let points_41 = result_40;
    Ok(Message {
        max_speed: max_speed_0,
        type_: type_1,
        payload: payload_3,
        stamp: stamp_5,
        note: note_opt_8,
        pose: pose_18,
        calibration: calibration_opt_19,
        digest: digest_24,
        raw: raw_27,
        gains: gains_29,
        readings: readings_31,
        names: names_33,
        chunks: chunks_35,
        limits: limits_opt_36,
        points: points_41,
    })
}
