//!Identity-explicit caller for the node's exposed service `rich_service`: polls from the fixture session, pinned to the node under test, gating on reachability first (the fixture session is a fresh caller).
pub use crate::exposed_services::rich_service::RequestData;
pub use crate::exposed_services::rich_service::Response;
pub use crate::exposed_services::rich_service::{Failure, FailureKind};
#[allow(unused_imports)]
use crate::exposed_services::rich_service::*;
/// Polls the node's service once and decodes the reply. `timeout`
/// bounds the reachability gate and the poll individually. An error
/// reply of the node is `Error::ServiceFailed`: `Failure::of` reads
/// its failure.
pub async fn poll(
    harness: &crate::fixtures::harness::Harness,
    request: &RequestData,
    timeout: std::time::Duration,
) -> crate::Result<Response> {
    let producer = harness.node_producer_ref();
    peppylib::testing::wait_service_reachable(
            harness.session(),
            "fixture-core",
            "fixture-observer",
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("rich_node", "v1")?,
            "rich_service",
            &producer,
            timeout,
        )
        .await?;
    let response_message = peppylib::ServiceMessenger::poll(
            harness.session(),
            "fixture-core",
            "fixture-observer",
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("rich_node", "v1")?,
            "rich_service",
            peppylib::messaging::ServiceTarget::Producer(&producer),
            serialize_request(request)?,
            timeout,
        )
        .await
        .map_err(Failure::reject_undeclared_kind)?;
    deserialize_response(response_message.payload_bytes().as_ref())
}
fn serialize_request(value: &RequestData) -> crate::Result<peppylib::Payload> {
    Ok({
        let mut capnp_msg = capnp::message::Builder::new_default();
        {
            let mut root = capnp_msg
                .init_root::<
                    crate::capnp::rich_service_message_capnp::rich_service_message::Builder,
                >();
            root.set_max_speed(value.max_speed);
            root.set_type(value.type_.as_str());
            root.set_payload(value.payload.as_ref());
            let timestamp_0 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time(value.stamp);
            let mut timestamp_builder_1 = root.reborrow().init_stamp();
            timestamp_builder_1.set_sec(timestamp_0.sec);
            timestamp_builder_1.set_nsec(timestamp_0.nsec);
            if let Some(value_2) = (value.note).as_ref() {
                root.set_note(value_2.as_str());
            }
            let mut builder_3 = root.reborrow().init_pose();
            builder_3.set_x((value.pose).x);
            let mut builder_4 = builder_3.reborrow().init_frame();
            builder_4.set_id(((value.pose).frame).id);
            builder_4.set_name(((value.pose).frame).name.as_str());
            if let Some(value_5) = (value.calibration).as_ref() {
                let mut builder_6 = root.reborrow().init_calibration();
                builder_6.set_gain(*&(value_5).gain);
            }
            root.set_digest(value.digest.as_ref());
            root.set_raw(value.raw.as_ref());
            let mut list_7 = root.reborrow().init_gains(3);
            for (idx_8, value_9) in (value.gains).iter().enumerate() {
                list_7.set(idx_8 as u32, *value_9);
            }
            let mut list_10 = root
                .reborrow()
                .init_readings(
                    u32::try_from((value.readings).len())
                        .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                );
            for (idx_11, value_12) in (value.readings).iter().enumerate() {
                list_10.set(idx_11 as u32, *value_12);
            }
            let mut list_13 = root
                .reborrow()
                .init_names(
                    u32::try_from((value.names).len())
                        .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                );
            for (idx_14, value_15) in (value.names).iter().enumerate() {
                list_13.set(idx_14 as u32, value_15.as_str());
            }
            let mut list_16 = root
                .reborrow()
                .init_chunks(
                    u32::try_from((value.chunks).len())
                        .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                );
            for (idx_17, value_18) in (value.chunks).iter().enumerate() {
                list_16.set(idx_17 as u32, value_18.as_ref());
            }
            if let Some(value_19) = (value.limits).as_ref() {
                let mut list_20 = root
                    .reborrow()
                    .init_limits(
                        u32::try_from((value_19).len())
                            .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                    );
                for (idx_21, value_22) in (value_19).iter().enumerate() {
                    list_20.set(idx_21 as u32, *value_22);
                }
            }
            let mut list_23 = root
                .reborrow()
                .init_points(
                    u32::try_from((value.points).len())
                        .expect("list length exceeds u32::MAX"),
                );
            for (idx_24, element_25) in (value.points).iter().enumerate() {
                let mut builder_26 = list_23.reborrow().get(idx_24 as u32);
                builder_26.set_x(element_25.x);
                builder_26.set_tag(element_25.tag.as_str());
            }
        }
        let mut buffer = Vec::new();
        capnp::serialize::write_message(&mut buffer, &capnp_msg)
            .map_err(|source| {
                let context = String::from("fixtures poll rich_service");
                crate::Error::Serialization(format!("{}: {}", context, source))
            })?;
        peppylib::Payload::from(buffer)
    })
}
fn deserialize_response(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<Response> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]
    let context = String::from("fixtures poll rich_service response");
    let mut payload = payload;
    let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader
        .get_root::<
            crate::capnp::rich_service_response_message_capnp::rich_service_response_message::Reader,
        >()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let max_speed_0 = root.reborrow().get_max_speed();
    let text_2 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_type()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures poll rich_service response");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "type", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let type_1 = text_2
        .to_str()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures poll rich_service response");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "type", context, source),
            )
        })?
        .to_owned();
    let data_4 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_payload()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures poll rich_service response");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "payload", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let payload_3 = data_4.to_vec();
    let timestamp_6 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_stamp()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures poll rich_service response");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "stamp", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let capnp_timestamp_7 = peppylib::encoding::CapnpTimestamp {
        sec: timestamp_6.get_sec(),
        nsec: timestamp_6.get_nsec(),
    };
    let stamp_5 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time_from_capnp(capnp_timestamp_7);
    let note_opt_8 = if root.reborrow().has_note() {
        let text_10 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_note()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("fixtures poll rich_service response");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "note", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let note_9 = text_10
            .to_str()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("fixtures poll rich_service response");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "note", context, source),
                )
            })?
            .to_owned();
        Some(note_9)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let reader_11 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_pose()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures poll rich_service response");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "pose", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let x_12 = reader_11.reborrow().get_x();
    let reader_13 = reader_11
        .reborrow()
        .get_frame()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures poll rich_service response");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "frame", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let id_14 = reader_13.reborrow().get_id();
    let text_16 = reader_13
        .reborrow()
        .get_name()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures poll rich_service response");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "name", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let name_15 = text_16
        .to_str()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures poll rich_service response");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "name", context, source),
            )
        })?
        .to_owned();
    let frame_17 = ResponsePoseFrame {
        id: id_14,
        name: name_15,
    };
    let pose_18 = ResponsePose {
        x: x_12,
        frame: frame_17,
    };
    let calibration_opt_19 = if root.reborrow().has_calibration() {
        let reader_20 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_calibration()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("fixtures poll rich_service response");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "calibration", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let gain_21 = reader_20.reborrow().get_gain();
        let calibration_22 = ResponseCalibration {
            gain: gain_21,
        };
        Some(calibration_22)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let data_23 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_digest()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures poll rich_service response");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "digest", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    if data_23.len() as usize != 4 {
        let actual = data_23.len() as usize;
        return Err(
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!(
                    "invalid fixed bytes length for field '{}': expected {}, got {}",
                    "digest", 4, actual
                ),
            ),
        );
    }
    let mut bytes_25 = [0u8; 4];
    for (idx, byte) in data_23.iter().enumerate() {
        bytes_25[idx] = *byte;
    }
    let digest_24 = bytes_25;
    let data_26 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_raw()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures poll rich_service response");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "raw", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let raw_27 = data_26.to_vec();
    let list_28 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_gains()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures poll rich_service response");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "gains", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    if list_28.len() as usize != 3 {
        let actual = list_28.len() as usize;
        return Err(
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!(
                    "invalid fixed list length for field '{}': expected {}, got {}",
                    "gains", 3, actual
                ),
            ),
        );
    }
    let mut gains_29: [f32; 3] = [f32::default(); 3];
    for (idx, element) in list_28.iter().enumerate() {
        gains_29[idx] = element;
    }
    let list_30 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_readings()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures poll rich_service response");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "readings", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let readings_31 = list_30.iter().collect::<Vec<i16>>();
    let list_32 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_names()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures poll rich_service response");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "names", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let names_33 = list_32
        .iter()
        .map(|element| {
            (element
                .map_err(|source| source.to_string())
                .and_then(|text| {
                    text.to_str().map(str::to_owned).map_err(|source| source.to_string())
                }))
                .map_err(|source| {
                    #[allow(clippy::all)]
                    let context = String::from("fixtures poll rich_service response");
                    crate::Error::Deserialization(
                        format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "names", context, source),
                    )
                })
        })
        .collect::<::std::result::Result<Vec<String>, crate::Error>>()?;
    let list_34 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_chunks()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures poll rich_service response");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "chunks", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let chunks_35 = list_34
        .iter()
        .map(|element| {
            (element.map(|data| data.to_vec()).map_err(|source| source.to_string()))
                .map_err(|source| {
                    #[allow(clippy::all)]
                    let context = String::from("fixtures poll rich_service response");
                    crate::Error::Deserialization(
                        format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "chunks", context, source),
                    )
                })
        })
        .collect::<::std::result::Result<Vec<Vec<u8>>, crate::Error>>()?;
    let limits_opt_36 = if root.reborrow().has_limits() {
        let list_37 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_limits()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("fixtures poll rich_service response");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "limits", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let limits_38 = list_37.iter().collect::<Vec<f64>>();
        Some(limits_38)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let list_39 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_points()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures poll rich_service response");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "points", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let mut result_40 = Vec::with_capacity(list_39.len() as usize);
    for element_42 in list_39.iter() {
        let x_43 = element_42.reborrow().get_x();
        let text_45 = element_42
            .reborrow()
            .get_tag()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("fixtures poll rich_service response");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "tag", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let tag_44 = text_45
            .to_str()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("fixtures poll rich_service response");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "tag", context, source),
                )
            })?
            .to_owned();
        result_40
            .push(ResponsePointsItem {
                x: x_43,
                tag: tag_44,
            });
    }
    let points_41 = result_40;
    Ok(Response {
        max_speed: max_speed_0,
        type_: type_1,
        payload: payload_3,
        stamp: stamp_5,
        note: note_opt_8,
        pose: pose_18,
        calibration: calibration_opt_19,
        digest: digest_24,
        raw: raw_27,
        gains: gains_29,
        readings: readings_31,
        names: names_33,
        chunks: chunks_35,
        limits: limits_opt_36,
        points: points_41,
    })
}


================================================================
Fixture :: exposed_actions/rich_action
================================================================
//!Identity-explicit caller for the node's exposed action `rich_action`: drives the full goal lifecycle from the fixture session, pinned to the node under test, gating on reachability first.
pub use crate::exposed_actions::rich_action::{Failure, FailureKind};
#[allow(unused_imports)]
use crate::exposed_actions::rich_action::*;
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct FeedbackPoseFrame {
    pub id: u32,
    pub name: String,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct FeedbackPose {
    pub x: f64,
    pub frame: FeedbackPoseFrame,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct FeedbackCalibration {
    pub gain: f32,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct FeedbackPointsItem {
    pub x: f32,
    pub tag: String,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Feedback {
    pub max_speed: f32,
    pub type_: String,
    pub payload: Vec<u8>,
    pub stamp: std::time::SystemTime,
    pub note: Option<String>,
    pub pose: FeedbackPose,
    pub calibration: Option<FeedbackCalibration>,
    pub digest: [u8; 4],
    pub raw: Vec<u8>,
    pub gains: [f32; 3],
    pub readings: Vec<i16>,
    pub names: Vec<String>,
    pub chunks: Vec<Vec<u8>>,
    pub limits: Option<Vec<f64>>,
    pub points: Vec<FeedbackPointsItem>,
}
/// A goal in flight against the node's action.
pub struct GoalHandle {
    messenger: peppylib::MessengerHandle,
    inner: peppylib::messaging::ActionGoalHandle,
    /// Whether the node accepted the goal: `refusal` is `None`.
    pub accepted: bool,
    /// The failure the node refused the goal with; `None` for an
    /// accepted goal.
    pub refusal: Option<Failure>,
}
/// Sends a goal to the node and decodes the admission reply. The
/// handle keeps up to `feedback_bound` unread feedback messages.
pub async fn send_goal(
    harness: &crate::fixtures::harness::Harness,
    feedback_bound: peppylib::messaging::FeedbackBound,
    timeout: std::time::Duration,
) -> crate::Result<GoalHandle> {
    let producer = harness.node_producer_ref();
    peppylib::testing::wait_action_reachable(
            harness.session(),
            "fixture-core",
            "fixture-observer",
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("rich_node", "v1")?,
            "rich_action",
            &producer,
            timeout,
        )
        .await?;
    let inner = peppylib::ActionMessenger::send_goal(
            harness.session(),
            "fixture-core",
            "fixture-observer",
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("rich_node", "v1")?,
            "rich_action",
            Some(&producer),
            peppylib::Payload::new(),
            feedback_bound,
            timeout,
        )
        .await?;
    let reply = inner.goal_reply();
    let refusal = reply.refusal().map(Failure::from_wire).transpose()?;
    Ok(GoalHandle {
        messenger: harness.session().clone(),
        accepted: refusal.is_none(),
        inner,
        refusal,
    })
}
/// How a finished goal ended.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum ResultOutcome {
    /// The goal is a success.
    Completed,
    /// The goal ended cancelled, for `reason`.
    Cancelled { reason: String },
    /// The goal failed: a failure kind that the action declares,
    /// a message, and no result.
    Failed(Failure),
    /// The goal ended without a result: its worker dropped it, or
    /// the provider is gone.
    Abandoned,
    /// The goal ended, and the provider no longer keeps its result.
    Expired,
}
/// The decoded result reply.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct ResultResponse {
    pub instance_id: String,
    pub core_node: String,
    pub outcome: ResultOutcome,
}
/// Cancel acknowledgement states, mirroring the runtime's.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum CancelState {
    Signalled,
    AlreadyTerminal,
    Unknown,
}
impl GoalHandle {
    /// Receives the next decoded feedback message for this goal.
    ///
    /// Terminal errors that end a drain loop:
    /// `Error::ActionFeedbackChannelClosed` on a clean close,
    /// `Error::ActionFeedbackProducerGone` when the node instance
    /// disappeared without closing the stream, and
    /// `Error::ActionFeedbackOverflow` when this caller fell
    /// behind by more unread messages than its bound.
    pub async fn on_next_feedback(&mut self) -> crate::Result<Feedback> {
        let feedback = self.inner.on_next_feedback().await?;
        let payload = feedback.payload_bytes();
        deserialize_feedback(payload.as_ref())
    }
    /// Requests cancellation of this goal.
    pub async fn cancel_goal(
        &self,
        timeout: std::time::Duration,
    ) -> crate::Result<CancelState> {
        let response = peppylib::ActionMessenger::cancel_goal(
                &self.messenger,
                &self.inner,
                timeout,
            )
            .await?;
        let state = match peppylib::messaging::decode_cancel_ack(
            response.payload_bytes().as_ref(),
        )? {
            peppylib::messaging::CancelState::Signalled => CancelState::Signalled,
            peppylib::messaging::CancelState::AlreadyTerminal => {
                CancelState::AlreadyTerminal
            }
            peppylib::messaging::CancelState::Unknown => CancelState::Unknown,
        };
        Ok(state)
    }
    /// Retrieves the goal's terminal result.
    pub async fn get_result(
        &self,
        timeout: std::time::Duration,
    ) -> crate::Result<ResultResponse> {
        let reply = peppylib::ActionMessenger::request_result(
                &self.messenger,
                &self.inner,
                timeout,
            )
            .await?;
        let outcome = match reply.outcome {
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Completed(_) => ResultOutcome::Completed,
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Cancelled { reason, .. } => {
                ResultOutcome::Cancelled { reason }
            }
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Failed(failure) => {
                ResultOutcome::Failed(Failure::from_wire(&failure)?)
            }
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Abandoned => ResultOutcome::Abandoned,
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Expired => ResultOutcome::Expired,
        };
        Ok(ResultResponse {
            instance_id: reply.instance_id,
            core_node: reply.core_node,
            outcome,
        })
    }
}
fn deserialize_feedback(payload: &[u8]) -> crate::Result<Feedback> {
    #[allow(clippy::all)]
    let context = String::from("fixtures rich_action feedback");
    let mut payload = payload;
    let message_reader = capnp::serialize::read_message_from_flat_slice(
            &mut payload,
            capnp::message::ReaderOptions::new(),
        )
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let root = message_reader
        .get_root::<
            crate::capnp::emit_rich_action_feedback_message_capnp::rich_action_feedback_message::Reader,
        >()
        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::Deserialization(
            format!("{}: {}", context, source),
        ))?;
    let max_speed_0 = root.reborrow().get_max_speed();
    let text_2 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_type()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures rich_action feedback");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "type", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let type_1 = text_2
        .to_str()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures rich_action feedback");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "type", context, source),
            )
        })?
        .to_owned();
    let data_4 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_payload()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures rich_action feedback");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "payload", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let payload_3 = data_4.to_vec();
    let timestamp_6 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_stamp()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures rich_action feedback");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "stamp", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let capnp_timestamp_7 = peppylib::encoding::CapnpTimestamp {
        sec: timestamp_6.get_sec(),
        nsec: timestamp_6.get_nsec(),
    };
    let stamp_5 = peppylib::encoding::convert_time_from_capnp(capnp_timestamp_7);
    let note_opt_8 = if root.reborrow().has_note() {
        let text_10 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_note()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("fixtures rich_action feedback");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "note", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let note_9 = text_10
            .to_str()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("fixtures rich_action feedback");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "note", context, source),
                )
            })?
            .to_owned();
        Some(note_9)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let reader_11 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_pose()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures rich_action feedback");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "pose", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let x_12 = reader_11.reborrow().get_x();
    let reader_13 = reader_11
        .reborrow()
        .get_frame()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures rich_action feedback");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "frame", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let id_14 = reader_13.reborrow().get_id();
    let text_16 = reader_13
        .reborrow()
        .get_name()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures rich_action feedback");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "name", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let name_15 = text_16
        .to_str()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures rich_action feedback");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "name", context, source),
            )
        })?
        .to_owned();
    let frame_17 = FeedbackPoseFrame {
        id: id_14,
        name: name_15,
    };
    let pose_18 = FeedbackPose {
        x: x_12,
        frame: frame_17,
    };
    let calibration_opt_19 = if root.reborrow().has_calibration() {
        let reader_20 = root
            .reborrow()
            .get_calibration()
            .map_err(|source| {
                #[allow(clippy::all)]
                let context = String::from("fixtures rich_action feedback");
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "calibration", context, source),
                )
            })?;
        let gain_21 = reader_20.reborrow().get_gain();
        let calibration_22 = FeedbackCalibration {
            gain: gain_21,
        };
        Some(calibration_22)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let data_23 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_digest()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures rich_action feedback");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "digest", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    if data_23.len() as usize != 4 {
        let actual = data_23.len() as usize;
        return Err(
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!(
                    "invalid fixed bytes length for field '{}': expected {}, got {}",
                    "digest", 4, actual
                ),
            ),
        );
    }
    let mut bytes_25 = [0u8; 4];
    for (idx, byte) in data_23.iter().enumerate() {
        bytes_25[idx] = *byte;
    }
    let digest_24 = bytes_25;
    let data_26 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_raw()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures rich_action feedback");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "raw", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let raw_27 = data_26.to_vec();
    let list_28 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_gains()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures rich_action feedback");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "gains", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    if list_28.len() as usize != 3 {
        let actual = list_28.len() as usize;
        return Err(
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!(
                    "invalid fixed list length for field '{}': expected {}, got {}",
                    "gains", 3, actual
                ),
            ),
        );
    }
    let mut gains_29: [f32; 3] = [f32::default(); 3];
    for (idx, element) in list_28.iter().enumerate() {
        gains_29[idx] = element;
    }
    let list_30 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_readings()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures rich_action feedback");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "readings", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let readings_31 = list_30.iter().collect::<Vec<i16>>();
    let list_32 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_names()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures rich_action feedback");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "names", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let names_33 = list_32
        .iter()
        .map(|element| {
            (element
                .map_err(|source| source.to_string())
                .and_then(|text| {
                    text.to_str().map(str::to_owned).map_err(|source| source.to_string())
                }))
                .map_err(|source| {
                    #[allow(clippy::all)]
                    let context = String::from("fixtures rich_action feedback");
                    crate::Error::Deserialization(
                        format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "names", context, source),
                    )
                })
        })
        .collect::<::std::result::Result<Vec<String>, crate::Error>>()?;
    let list_34 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_chunks()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures rich_action feedback");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "chunks", context, source),
            )
        })?;
    let chunks_35 = list_34
        .iter()
        .map(|element| {
            (element.map(|data| data.to_vec()).map_err(|source| source.to_string()))
                .map_err(|source| {
                    #[allow(clippy::all)]
                    let context = String::from("fixtures rich_action feedback");
                    crate::Error::Deserialization(
                        format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "chunks", context, source),
                    )
                })
        })
        .collect::<::std::result::Result<Vec<Vec<u8>>, crate::Error>>()?;
    let limits_opt_36 = if root.reborrow().has_limits() {

            .reborrow()

    } else {

    };
    let list_39 = root
        .reborrow()
        .get_points()
        .map_err(|source| {
            #[allow(clippy::all)]
            let context = String::from("fixtures rich_action feedback");
            crate::Error::Deserialization(
                format!("field '{}' in {}: {}", "points", context, source),

        })?;
    let mut result_40 = Vec::with_capacity(list_39.len() as usize);
    for element_42 in list_39.iter() {

            .reborrow()

    let points_41 = result_40;
    Ok(Feedback {
        max_speed: max_speed_0,
        type_: type_1,
        payload: payload_3,
        stamp: stamp_5,
        note: note_opt_8,
        pose: pose_18,
        calibration: calibration_opt_19,

//!Generated test harness: ephemeral router + started mocks + seeded `StandaloneConfig` + the node running in-process behind the readiness barriers. Construct with [`Harness::start`] (or [`Harness::start_with`] for parameter/slot overrides); tear down with [`Harness::shutdown`].
/// Wire identity of the fixture caller/observer session.
pub const FIXTURE_CORE_NODE: &str = "fixture-core";
pub const FIXTURE_INSTANCE_ID: &str = "fixture-observer";
/// The node's manifest identity, baked at sync time.
pub const NODE_NAME: &str = "rich_node";
pub const NODE_TAG: &str = "v1";
/// The node's `peppy.json5`, resolved relative to the generated
/// crate's manifest (always `<node>/.peppy/libs/peppygen`).
pub const PEPPY_CONFIG_PATH: &str = concat!(
    env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"), "/../../../peppy.json5"
);
/// One mock peer of a multi pairing slot, as [`Config`] names it:
/// its instance id and the copy it belongs to, `None` outside a copy.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct PeerMemberSpec {
    pub instance_id: String,
    pub copy: Option<String>,
}
/// Optional overrides for [`Harness::start_with`].
pub struct Config {
    /// Typed parameters for the node; `None` uses the schema
    /// defaults (an error at boot if a required parameter has none).
    pub parameters: Option<crate::Parameters>,
    /// Explicit node instance id; `None` generates a unique one.
    pub instance_id: Option<String>,
    /// Which clock the node boots on, the test-side spelling of a
    /// deployment's `framework.clock`.
    ///
    /// `Wall` (the default) reads OS wall time, which the harness
    /// clock ticks like a daemon. `Consumer` binds the node to the
    /// harness's simulated domain, where `peppygen::clock::now_ns`
    /// reads no time until the test advances it with
    /// [`Harness::clock`]'s `tick`. `Publisher` makes the node the
    /// instance supplying that domain: it publishes its own instants
    /// and the test reads them back with
    /// `peppylib::clock::subscribe(harness.node_runner(),
    /// peppylib::messaging::TopicBuffer::KeepLatest)`, which keeps
    /// the newest ticks and never holds up the node's session when
    /// the test reads late.
    pub clock: peppylib::testing::HarnessClock,
}
#[allow(clippy::derivable_impls)]
impl Default for Config {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            parameters: None,
            instance_id: None,
            clock: peppylib::testing::HarnessClock::Wall,
        }
    }
}
/// Every started mock, one field per link. Returned alongside the
/// harness so a test can consume an individual mock (`stop()` is
/// producer-loss) without giving up the harness.
pub struct Mocks {
    pub deps: DepMocks,
    pub pairings: PairingMocks,
    pub observed: ObservedMocks,
}
pub struct DepMocks {
    pub rich_link: crate::mock::deps::rich_link::Mock,
}
pub struct PairingMocks {
    pub rich_sensor_bus: crate::mock::pairings::rich_sensor_bus::Mock,
}
pub struct ObservedMocks {}
/// Typed subscriptions to the node's emitted topics, opened before
/// the node booted (so its very first publish is captured). Each
/// keeps the newest messages the test has not read and never holds
/// up the harness's session.
pub struct Emitted {
    pub rich_stream: crate::fixtures::emitted_topics::rich_stream::Subscription,
}
pub struct Harness {
    core: peppylib::testing::HarnessCore,
    /// Observation subscriptions to the node's own emissions.
    pub emitted: Emitted,
    /// The daemon-clock stand-in serving `peppylib`'s
    /// `clock::synchronize` and the `clock` topic, in the shape
    /// `Config::clock` asked for. The service answers wall time in
    /// every mode (skewable via `set_offset_ns`); `tick` drives the
    /// simulated domain and is only accepted under
    /// `HarnessClock::Consumer`.
    pub clock: peppylib::testing::MockClock,
    session: peppylib::MessengerHandle,
    router: Option<peppylib::testing::EphemeralRouter>,
    instance_id: String,
}
impl Harness {
    /// Boots the full fixture under default configuration. `setup`
    /// is the node's real entry point (the exact closure shape
    /// `NodeBuilder::run` takes), spawned only after every readiness
    /// barrier passed.
    pub async fn start<F, Fut>(setup: F) -> crate::Result<(Self, Mocks)>
    where
        F: FnOnce(crate::Parameters, std::sync::Arc<crate::NodeRunner>) -> Fut,
        Fut: std::future::Future<Output = crate::Result<()>> + Send + 'static,
    {
        Self::start_with(Config::default(), setup).await
    }
    /// [`Harness::start`] with parameter / slot overrides.
    #[allow(clippy::vec_init_then_push)]
    pub async fn start_with<F, Fut>(
        config: Config,
        setup: F,
    ) -> crate::Result<(Self, Mocks)>
    where
        F: FnOnce(crate::Parameters, std::sync::Arc<crate::NodeRunner>) -> Fut,
        Fut: std::future::Future<Output = crate::Result<()>> + Send + 'static,
    {
        let router = peppylib::testing::EphemeralRouter::start().await?;
        let instance_id = config
            .instance_id
            .clone()
            .unwrap_or_else(peppylib::testing::unique_test_instance_id);
        let rich_link = crate::mock::deps::rich_link::Mock::start(&router).await?;
        let rich_sensor_bus = crate::mock::pairings::rich_sensor_bus::Mock::start(
                &router,
                &instance_id,
            )
            .await?;
        let session = router.connect().await?;
        let rich_stream = crate::fixtures::emitted_topics::rich_stream::subscribe(
                &session,
                &instance_id,
            )
            .await?;
        let clock = peppylib::testing::MockClock::start(
                &session,
                peppylib::testing::STANDALONE_CORE_NODE,
                peppylib::testing::MOCK_CLOCK_INSTANCE_ID,
                config.clock,
            )
            .await?;
        let mut standalone = peppylib::runtime::StandaloneConfig::new()
            .with_messaging(router.host(), router.port())
            .with_instance_id(instance_id.clone())
            .with_clock(clock.binding()?);
        if let Some(parameters) = &config.parameters {
            standalone = standalone.with_parameters(parameters);
        }
        standalone = standalone
            .with_bound_producer(
                "rich_link",
                crate::mock::deps::rich_link::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                crate::mock::deps::rich_link::MOCK_INSTANCE_ID,
            );
        standalone = standalone
            .with_peer_pin(
                "rich_sensor_bus",
                crate::mock::pairings::rich_sensor_bus::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                crate::mock::pairings::rich_sensor_bus::MOCK_INSTANCE_ID,
                crate::mock::pairings::rich_sensor_bus::PEER_LINK_ID,
            );
        let mut publisher_readiness: Vec<peppylib::testing::PublisherReadiness> = Vec::new();
        publisher_readiness
            .push(peppylib::testing::PublisherReadiness {
                target: peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("rich_node", "v1")?,
                link_id: None,
                peer: None,
                topic: "rich_stream".to_string(),
            });
        let mut service_readiness: Vec<peppylib::testing::ServiceReadiness> = Vec::new();
        service_readiness.push(clock.readiness()?);
        service_readiness
            .push(peppylib::testing::ServiceReadiness {
                target: peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("rich_producer", "v1")?,
                name: "rich_service".to_string(),
                producer: crate::mock::deps::rich_link::producer_ref_for(
                    crate::mock::deps::rich_link::MOCK_INSTANCE_ID,
                ),
                kind: peppylib::testing::ServiceReadinessKind::Service,
            });
        service_readiness
            .push(peppylib::testing::ServiceReadiness {
                target: peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("rich_producer", "v1")?,
                name: "rich_action".to_string(),
                producer: crate::mock::deps::rich_link::producer_ref_for(
                    crate::mock::deps::rich_link::MOCK_INSTANCE_ID,
                ),
                kind: peppylib::testing::ServiceReadinessKind::Action,
            });
        let core = peppylib::testing::HarnessCore::start::<
            crate::Parameters,
            _,
            _,
        >(PEPPY_CONFIG_PATH, standalone, &publisher_readiness, &service_readiness, setup)
            .await?;
        let harness = Harness {
            core,
            emitted: Emitted { rich_stream },
            clock,
            session,
            router: Some(router),
            instance_id,
        };
        let mocks = Mocks {
            deps: DepMocks { rich_link },
            pairings: PairingMocks { rich_sensor_bus },
            observed: ObservedMocks {},
        };
        Ok((harness, mocks))
    }
    /// The running node, for calling its runtime surface directly.
    pub fn node_runner(&self) -> &std::sync::Arc<crate::NodeRunner> {
        self.core.node_runner()
    }
    /// The fixture caller/observer session (not the node's).
    pub fn session(&self) -> &peppylib::MessengerHandle {
        &self.session
    }
    /// The node-under-test's instance id.
    pub fn instance_id(&self) -> &str {
        &self.instance_id
    }
    /// The node-under-test's wire identity.
    pub fn node_producer_ref(&self) -> peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef {
        peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef::new(
            self.core.bound_core_node(),
            &self.instance_id,
        )
    }
    /// Whether the node's `setup` has returned.
    pub fn setup_finished(&self) -> bool {
        self.core.setup_finished()
    }
    /// Tears the fixture down in lifecycle order: node convergence
    /// (cancel → bounded setup await → shutdown hooks, propagating a
    /// setup error), then the clock stand-in, then the fixture
    /// session, then the router.
    pub async fn shutdown(self) -> crate::Result<()> {
        let Harness { core, emitted, clock, session, router, .. } = self;
        let result = core.shutdown().await;
        drop(clock);
        drop(emitted);
        drop(session);
        if let Some(router) = router {
            router.shutdown().await?;
        }
        result
    }
}

struct RichActionFeedbackMessage {
  maxSpeed @0 :Float32;
  type @1 :Text;
  payload @2 :Data;
  stamp @3 :Timestamp;
  note @4 :Text;
  pose @5 :Pose;
  calibration @6 :Calibration;
  digest @7 :Data;
  raw @8 :Data;

ConsumedService :: bare_link/ping

const NODE_NAME: &str = "bare_producer";
const SERVICE_NAME: &str = "ping";
const LINK_ID: &str = "bare_link";
/// The producer bound to this module's slot.
/// The binding is fixed when the node starts (no live discovery; a
/// producer disconnecting never rebinds it) and shared by every
/// generated module referencing this slot. This slot declares
/// cardinality `one`: launch validation resolved exactly one producer,
/// so the accessor is singular and infallible.
pub fn bound_producer(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
) -> &peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef {
    node_runner.processor().sole_bound_producer("bare_link")
}
/// A failure kind of this member: a kind of peppy, or a kind of its
/// contract that it lists.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum FailureKind {
    /// The attempt gave no result
    AttemptFailed,
    /// The bound of the provider passed
    Timeout,
    /// A precondition failed
    PreconditionFailed,
    /// A robot, limb or host is gone
    ResourceUnavailable,
    /// The provider refused the goal, and no other kind says why
    Rejected,
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl FailureKind {
    /// Every kind of this member: the kinds of peppy, then the
    /// kinds of its contract that it lists.
    pub const ALL: [Self; 5] = [
        Self::AttemptFailed,
        Self::Timeout,
        Self::PreconditionFailed,
        Self::ResourceUnavailable,
        Self::Rejected,
    ];
    /// The name of the kind on the wire.
    pub const fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::AttemptFailed => "attempt_failed",
            Self::Timeout => "timeout",
            Self::PreconditionFailed => "precondition_failed",
            Self::ResourceUnavailable => "resource_unavailable",
            Self::Rejected => "rejected",
        }
    }
    /// The kind named `name`, or `None` when this member does not
    /// declare it.
    pub fn parse(name: &str) -> Option<Self> {
        Self::ALL.into_iter().find(|kind| kind.as_str() == name)
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for FailureKind {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.write_str(self.as_str())
    }
}
/// Why this member refused or failed a goal, or gave an error
/// reply: a kind that it declares, and a message for a person.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Failure {
    pub kind: FailureKind,
    pub message: String,
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl Failure {
    pub fn new(kind: FailureKind, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            kind,
            message: message.into(),
        }
    }
    /// The wire form of the failure.
    pub(crate) fn to_wire(&self) -> peppylib::messaging::Failure {
        let kind = peppylib::messaging::FailureKindName::parse(self.kind.as_str())
            .expect("each kind of a member is a valid failure kind name");
        peppylib::messaging::Failure::new(kind, self.message.clone())
    }
    /// Parses a failure from the wire. A kind that this member does
    /// not declare is a deserialization error, whose text keeps the
    /// message of the provider.
    pub(crate) fn from_wire(
        failure: &peppylib::messaging::Failure,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let kind = FailureKind::parse(failure.kind().as_str())
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!(
                        "the failure kind `{}` is not a kind of `{}` (message: {:?})",
                        failure.kind(), SERVICE_NAME, failure.message()
                    ),
                )
            })?;
        Ok(Self::new(kind, failure.message()))
    }
    /// The failure of the error reply that `error` holds: `None`
    /// when `error` is not an error reply of a provider, or when
    /// its kind is not a kind of this member.
    pub fn of(error: &crate::Error) -> Option<Self> {
        let crate::Error::ServiceFailed { failure, .. } = error else {
            return None;
        };
        Self::from_wire(failure).ok()
    }
    /// `error` itself, unless it is an error reply with a kind this
    /// member does not declare: such a reply breaks the contract of
    /// the member, and gives a deserialization error instead.
    pub(crate) fn reject_undeclared_kind(error: crate::Error) -> crate::Error {
        let crate::Error::ServiceFailed { failure, .. } = &error else {
            return error;
        };
        match Self::from_wire(failure) {
            Ok(_) => error,
            Err(undeclared) => undeclared,
        }
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for Failure {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{}: {}", self.kind, self.message)
    }
}
/// Polls this service on `target`, a member of this slot's bound set
/// (a `ProducerRef` yielded by the slot's own subscription is a
/// member by construction); a target outside the set fails before
/// anything reaches the wire.
///
/// An error reply of the provider is `Error::ServiceFailed`:
/// `Failure::of` reads its failure, with a kind that this member
/// declares.
///
/// This slot declares cardinality `one`: `target` is its sole bound
/// producer, returned by `bound_producer()`; the explicit parameter
/// keeps call sites uniform across cardinalities.
pub async fn poll(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
    target: &peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef,
    timeout: std::time::Duration,
) -> crate::Result<()> {
    node_runner.processor().ensure_target_bound(LINK_ID, target)?;
    let request_payload = peppylib::Payload::new();
    let _ = peppylib::ServiceMessenger::poll(
            node_runner.messenger(),
            node_runner.processor().bound_core_node(),
            node_runner.processor().bound_instance_id(),
            peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("bare_producer", "v1")?,
            SERVICE_NAME,
            peppylib::messaging::ServiceTarget::Producer(target),
            request_payload,
            timeout,
        )
        .await
        .map_err(Failure::reject_undeclared_kind)?;
    Ok(())
}

ConsumedAction :: bare_link/home_all

const TARGET_ACTION_NAME: &str = "home_all";
const LINK_ID: &str = "bare_link";
/// The producer bound to this module's slot.
/// The binding is fixed when the node starts (no live discovery; a
/// producer disconnecting never rebinds it) and shared by every
/// generated module referencing this slot. This slot declares
/// cardinality `one`: launch validation resolved exactly one producer,
/// so the accessor is singular and infallible.
pub fn bound_producer(
    node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
) -> &peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef {
    node_runner.processor().sole_bound_producer("bare_link")
}
/// A failure kind of this member: a kind of peppy, or a kind of its
/// contract that it lists.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum FailureKind {
    /// The attempt gave no result
    AttemptFailed,
    /// The bound of the provider passed
    Timeout,
    /// A precondition failed
    PreconditionFailed,
    /// A robot, limb or host is gone
    ResourceUnavailable,
    /// The provider refused the goal, and no other kind says why
    Rejected,
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl FailureKind {
    /// Every kind of this member: the kinds of peppy, then the
    /// kinds of its contract that it lists.
    pub const ALL: [Self; 5] = [
        Self::AttemptFailed,
        Self::Timeout,
        Self::PreconditionFailed,
        Self::ResourceUnavailable,
        Self::Rejected,
    ];
    /// The name of the kind on the wire.
    pub const fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::AttemptFailed => "attempt_failed",
            Self::Timeout => "timeout",
            Self::PreconditionFailed => "precondition_failed",
            Self::ResourceUnavailable => "resource_unavailable",
            Self::Rejected => "rejected",
        }
    }
    /// The kind named `name`, or `None` when this member does not
    /// declare it.
    pub fn parse(name: &str) -> Option<Self> {
        Self::ALL.into_iter().find(|kind| kind.as_str() == name)
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for FailureKind {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.write_str(self.as_str())
    }
}
/// Why this member refused or failed a goal, or gave an error
/// reply: a kind that it declares, and a message for a person.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct Failure {
    pub kind: FailureKind,
    pub message: String,
}
#[allow(dead_code)]
impl Failure {
    pub fn new(kind: FailureKind, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            kind,
            message: message.into(),
        }
    }
    /// The wire form of the failure.
    pub(crate) fn to_wire(&self) -> peppylib::messaging::Failure {
        let kind = peppylib::messaging::FailureKindName::parse(self.kind.as_str())
            .expect("each kind of a member is a valid failure kind name");
        peppylib::messaging::Failure::new(kind, self.message.clone())
    }
    /// Parses a failure from the wire. A kind that this member does
    /// not declare is a deserialization error, whose text keeps the
    /// message of the provider.
    pub(crate) fn from_wire(
        failure: &peppylib::messaging::Failure,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let kind = FailureKind::parse(failure.kind().as_str())
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                crate::Error::Deserialization(
                    format!(
                        "the failure kind `{}` is not a kind of `{}` (message: {:?})",
                        failure.kind(), TARGET_ACTION_NAME, failure.message()
                    ),
                )
            })?;
        Ok(Self::new(kind, failure.message()))
    }
}
impl std::fmt::Display for Failure {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{}: {}", self.kind, self.message)
    }
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct CancelResponse {
    pub instance_id: String,
    pub core_node: String,
    pub state: CancelState,
}
pub struct ActionHandle {
    messenger: peppylib::MessengerHandle,
    inner: peppylib::messaging::ActionGoalHandle,
    /// Whether the producer accepted the goal: `refusal` is
    /// `None`.
    pub accepted: bool,
    /// The failure the producer refused the goal with, of a kind
    /// that this member declares; `None` for an accepted goal.
    pub refusal: Option<Failure>,
}
impl ActionHandle {
    /// Fires this goal at `target`, a member of this slot's bound set (a
    /// `ProducerRef` yielded by the slot's own subscription is a member
    /// by construction); a target outside the set fails before anything
    /// reaches the wire. Each returned handle stays pinned to its
    /// target: feedback, result retrieval, and cancellation all address
    /// that producer directly.
    ///
    /// The handle keeps every feedback message until it is read, up to
    /// `feedback_bound` unread messages (`DEFAULT_FEEDBACK_BOUND` holds
    /// 1024). A caller that reads the feedback and falls behind by more
    /// cancels the goal, and its next read gives
    /// `Error::ActionFeedbackOverflow`. A caller that never reads the
    /// feedback keeps its goal.
    ///
    /// This slot declares cardinality `one`: `target` is its sole bound
    /// producer, returned by `bound_producer()`; the explicit parameter
    /// keeps call sites uniform across cardinalities.
    pub async fn fire_goal(
        node_runner: &crate::NodeRunner,
        target: &peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef,
        timeout: std::time::Duration,
        feedback_bound: peppylib::messaging::FeedbackBound,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        node_runner.processor().ensure_target_bound(LINK_ID, target)?;
        let goal_payload = peppylib::Payload::new();
        let action_handle = peppylib::ActionMessenger::send_goal(
                node_runner.messenger(),
                node_runner.processor().bound_core_node(),
                node_runner.processor().bound_instance_id(),
                peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("bare_producer", "v1")?,
                TARGET_ACTION_NAME,
                Some(target),
                goal_payload,
                feedback_bound,
                timeout,
            )
            .await?;
        let reply = action_handle.goal_reply();
        let refusal = reply.refusal().map(Failure::from_wire).transpose()?;
        Ok(Self {
            messenger: node_runner.messenger().clone(),
            accepted: refusal.is_none(),
            inner: action_handle,
            refusal,
        })
    }
    pub async fn cancel_goal(
        &self,
        timeout: std::time::Duration,
    ) -> crate::Result<CancelResponse> {
        let response = peppylib::ActionMessenger::cancel_goal(
                &self.messenger,
                &self.inner,
                timeout,
            )
            .await?;
        let state = match peppylib::messaging::decode_cancel_ack(
            response.payload_bytes().as_ref(),
        )? {
            peppylib::messaging::CancelState::Signalled => CancelState::Signalled,
            peppylib::messaging::CancelState::AlreadyTerminal => {
                CancelState::AlreadyTerminal
            }
            peppylib::messaging::CancelState::Unknown => CancelState::Unknown,
        };
        Ok(CancelResponse {
            instance_id: response.instance_id().to_string(),
            core_node: response.core_node().to_string(),
            state,
        })
    }
    pub async fn get_result(
        &self,
        timeout: std::time::Duration,
    ) -> crate::Result<ResultResponse> {
        let reply = peppylib::ActionMessenger::request_result(
                &self.messenger,
                &self.inner,
                timeout,
            )
            .await?;
        let outcome = match reply.outcome {
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Completed(_) => ResultOutcome::Completed,
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Cancelled { reason, .. } => {
                ResultOutcome::Cancelled { reason }
            }
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Failed(failure) => {
                ResultOutcome::Failed(Failure::from_wire(&failure)?)
            }
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Abandoned => ResultOutcome::Abandoned,
            peppylib::messaging::GoalOutcome::Expired => ResultOutcome::Expired,
        };
        Ok(ResultResponse {
            instance_id: reply.instance_id,
            core_node: reply.core_node,
            outcome,
        })
    }
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum CancelState {
    Signalled,
    AlreadyTerminal,
    Unknown,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub enum ResultOutcome {
    /// The goal is a success.
    Completed,
    /// The goal ended cancelled, for `reason`.
    Cancelled { reason: String },
    /// The goal failed: a failure kind that the action declares,
    /// a message, and no result.
    Failed(Failure),
    /// The goal ended without a result: its worker dropped it, or
    /// the provider is gone.
    Abandoned,
    /// The goal ended, and the provider no longer keeps its result.
    Expired,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct ResultResponse {
    pub instance_id: String,
    pub core_node: String,
    pub outcome: ResultOutcome,
}
