Mock :: deps/bare_link
================================================================
//!Mock producer for the `bare_link` dependency slot: plays the dependency over the real wire under the identity the harness seeds into the slot's bound set. One messaging session per mock, so [`Mock::stop`] is a whole-producer loss.
/// This node's consumer-slot link_id.
pub const LINK_ID: &str = "bare_link";
/// Core-node segment of the mock's wire identity.
pub const MOCK_CORE_NODE: &str = "mock-core";
/// Default instance segment of the mock's wire identity.
pub const MOCK_INSTANCE_ID: &str = "mock-bare_link";
/// The default mock's wire identity, as the harness seeds it.
pub fn producer_ref() -> peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef {
    producer_ref_for(MOCK_INSTANCE_ID)
}
/// [`producer_ref`] under an explicit instance id (multi-instance slots).
pub fn producer_ref_for(instance_id: &str) -> peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef {
    peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef::new(MOCK_CORE_NODE, instance_id)
}
/// One mocked producer instance for this slot. Fields are the typed
/// per-interface surfaces; construct with [`Mock::start`].
pub struct Mock {
    _session: peppylib::MessengerHandle,
    pub ping: ping::Service,
    pub home_all: home_all::Action,
}
impl Mock {
    /// Connects a dedicated session and declares every interface of
    /// this dependency now (publishers, queryables, action engines),
    /// under the default [`MOCK_INSTANCE_ID`].
    pub async fn start(
        router: &peppylib::testing::EphemeralRouter,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        Self::start_as(router, MOCK_INSTANCE_ID).await
    }
    /// [`Mock::start`] under an explicit instance id, for slots bound
    /// to several mock instances.
    pub async fn start_as(
        router: &peppylib::testing::EphemeralRouter,
        instance_id: &str,
    ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
        let session = router.connect().await?;
        let ping = ping::Service::listen(&session, instance_id).await?;
        let home_all = home_all::Action::expose(&session, instance_id).await?;
        Ok(Self {
            _session: session,
            ping,
            home_all,
        })
    }
    /// Simulates this producer dying mid-flight, deterministically:
    /// live action goals are disarmed (their eventual drop emits no
    /// clean close), then every declaration and the session drop,
    /// releasing the liveliness tokens consumers latch on.
    pub fn stop(self) {
        self.home_all.stop();
    }
}
///Typed mock server for the consumed service `ping`: a background pump captures every request; scripted responses serve automatically, unscripted requests park for `next_request`.
pub mod ping {
    pub use crate::consumed_services::bare_link::ping::{Failure, FailureKind};
    #[allow(unused_imports)]
    use crate::consumed_services::bare_link::ping::*;
    pub struct Service {
        core: peppylib::testing::MockServiceCore,
    }
    /// Answers exactly one parked request; consumed by use.
    pub struct Responder {
        inner: peppylib::messaging::ServiceResponder,
    }
    impl Service {
        pub(super) async fn listen(
            session: &peppylib::MessengerHandle,
            instance_id: &str,
        ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
            let core = peppylib::testing::MockServiceCore::listen(
                    session,
                    super::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                    instance_id,
                    peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("bare_producer", "v1")?,
                    "ping",
                )
                .await?;
            Ok(Self { core })
        }
        /// Parks until the node's next unscripted call, returning the
        /// responder the test must use to answer it. Errors after
        /// `timeout`.
        pub async fn next_request(
            &self,
            timeout: std::time::Duration,
        ) -> crate::Result<Responder> {
            let (_context, responder) = self.core.next_request(timeout).await?;
            Ok(Responder { inner: responder })
        }
        ///Enqueues one response to be served automatically to the next inbound request (FIFO across repeated calls); only unscripted requests park for `next_request`.
        pub fn enqueue_response(&self) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.core.enqueue_response(peppylib::Payload::new());
            Ok(())
        }
        /// How many calls the node has made so far.
        pub fn captured_count(&self) -> usize {
            self.core.captured().len()
        }
    }
    impl Responder {
        /// Sends the typed response for the parked request.
        pub async fn respond(self) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.inner.respond(peppylib::Payload::new()).await
        }
        /// Answers the parked request with an error reply: a failure
        /// kind that this member declares and a message.
        pub async fn respond_failure(
            self,
            kind: FailureKind,
            message: impl Into<String>,
        ) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.inner.respond_failure(Failure::new(kind, message).to_wire()).await
        }
    }
}
///Typed mock action server for the consumed action `home_all`, on the real `ConcurrentAction` engine: identical goal lifecycle to a production producer, plus deterministic producer-loss via the owning mock's `stop()`.
pub mod home_all {
    #[allow(unused_imports)]
    use crate::consumed_actions::bare_link::home_all::*;
    pub use crate::consumed_actions::bare_link::home_all::{Failure, FailureKind};
    pub struct Action {
        core: peppylib::testing::MockActionServerCore,
    }
    impl Action {
        pub(super) async fn expose(
            session: &peppylib::MessengerHandle,
            instance_id: &str,
        ) -> crate::Result<Self> {
            let core = peppylib::testing::MockActionServerCore::expose(
                    session,
                    super::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                    instance_id,
                    peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("bare_producer", "v1")?,
                    "home_all",
                    false,
                )
                .await?;
            Ok(Self { core })
        }
        /// Parks until the node sends a goal, bounded by `timeout`;
        /// the returned goal awaits the test's admission decision.
        pub async fn next_goal(
            &mut self,
            timeout: std::time::Duration,
        ) -> crate::Result<PendingGoal> {
            let pending = self.core.next_goal(timeout).await?;
            Ok(PendingGoal { pending })
        }
        pub(super) fn stop(self) {
            self.core.stop();
        }
    }
    /// A goal received by the mock, awaiting accept/refuse.
    pub struct PendingGoal {
        pending: peppylib::testing::MockPendingGoal,
    }
    impl PendingGoal {
        /// The client-generated correlation id for this goal.
        pub fn goal_id(&self) -> &str {
            self.pending.goal_id()
        }
        /// Accepts the goal; the returned handle drives feedback and
        /// completion.
        pub async fn accept(self) -> crate::Result<ActiveGoal> {
            let context = self.pending.accept(peppylib::Payload::new()).await?;
            Ok(ActiveGoal { context })
        }
        /// Refuses the goal with a failure kind that this member
        /// declares and a message. A refusal carries no goal
        /// response.
        pub async fn refuse(
            self,
            kind: FailureKind,
            message: impl Into<String>,
        ) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.pending.refuse(Failure::new(kind, message).to_wire()).await
        }
    }
    /// An accepted goal: drives feedback and terminal completion.
    pub struct ActiveGoal {
        context: std::sync::Arc<peppylib::messaging::GoalContext>,
    }
    impl ActiveGoal {
        /// Resolves when this goal receives its cancel signal: a
        /// cancel request of the node, or the end of the node.
        pub async fn cancel_signal(&self) {
            self.context.cancel_signal().await
        }
        /// Whether this goal received its cancel signal.
        pub fn is_cancelled(&self) -> bool {
            self.context.is_cancelled()
        }
        /// Why this goal received its cancel signal; `None` before
        /// it did. `CancelCause::reason` gives a reason for
        /// `complete_cancelled`.
        pub fn cancel_cause(&self) -> Option<peppylib::messaging::CancelCause> {
            self.context.cancel_cause()
        }
        /// Ends the goal with the status completed: a success.
        pub async fn complete(&self) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.context.complete(peppylib::Payload::new()).await?;
            Ok(())
        }
        /// Ends the goal with the status failed: a failure kind
        /// that this member declares, a message, and no result.
        pub async fn fail(
            &self,
            kind: FailureKind,
            message: impl Into<String>,
        ) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.context.fail(Failure::new(kind, message).to_wire()).await?;
            Ok(())
        }
        /// Ends the goal with the status cancelled, for `reason`.
        pub async fn complete_cancelled(
            &self,
            reason: impl Into<String>,
        ) -> crate::Result<()> {
            self.context.complete_cancelled(reason, peppylib::Payload::new()).await?;
            Ok(())
        }
    }
}


================================================================
Fixture :: harness
================================================================
//!Generated test harness: ephemeral router + started mocks + seeded `StandaloneConfig` + the node running in-process behind the readiness barriers. Construct with [`Harness::start`] (or [`Harness::start_with`] for parameter/slot overrides); tear down with [`Harness::shutdown`].
/// Wire identity of the fixture caller/observer session.
pub const FIXTURE_CORE_NODE: &str = "fixture-core";
pub const FIXTURE_INSTANCE_ID: &str = "fixture-observer";
/// The node's manifest identity, baked at sync time.
pub const NODE_NAME: &str = "relay_node";
pub const NODE_TAG: &str = "v1";
/// The node's `peppy.json5`, resolved relative to the generated
/// crate's manifest (always `<node>/.peppy/libs/peppygen`).
pub const PEPPY_CONFIG_PATH: &str = concat!(
    env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"), "/../../../peppy.json5"
);
/// One mock peer of a multi pairing slot, as [`Config`] names it:
/// its instance id and the copy it belongs to, `None` outside a copy.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct PeerMemberSpec {
    pub instance_id: String,
    pub copy: Option<String>,
}
/// Optional overrides for [`Harness::start_with`].
pub struct Config {
    /// Typed parameters for the node; `None` uses the schema
    /// defaults (an error at boot if a required parameter has none).
    pub parameters: Option<crate::Parameters>,
    /// Explicit node instance id; `None` generates a unique one.
    pub instance_id: Option<String>,
    /// Which clock the node boots on, the test-side spelling of a
    /// deployment's `framework.clock`.
    ///
    /// `Wall` (the default) reads OS wall time, which the harness
    /// clock ticks like a daemon. `Consumer` binds the node to the
    /// harness's simulated domain, where `peppygen::clock::now_ns`
    /// reads no time until the test advances it with
    /// [`Harness::clock`]'s `tick`. `Publisher` makes the node the
    /// instance supplying that domain: it publishes its own instants
    /// and the test reads them back with
    /// `peppylib::clock::subscribe(harness.node_runner(),
    /// peppylib::messaging::TopicBuffer::KeepLatest)`, which keeps
    /// the newest ticks and never holds up the node's session when
    /// the test reads late.
    pub clock: peppylib::testing::HarnessClock,
}
#[allow(clippy::derivable_impls)]
impl Default for Config {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            parameters: None,
            instance_id: None,
            clock: peppylib::testing::HarnessClock::Wall,
        }
    }
}
/// Every started mock, one field per link. Returned alongside the
/// harness so a test can consume an individual mock (`stop()` is
/// producer-loss) without giving up the harness.
pub struct Mocks {
    pub deps: DepMocks,
    pub pairings: PairingMocks,
    pub observed: ObservedMocks,
}
pub struct DepMocks {
    pub bare_link: crate::mock::deps::bare_link::Mock,
}
pub struct PairingMocks {}
pub struct ObservedMocks {}
/// Typed subscriptions to the node's emitted topics, opened before
/// the node booted (so its very first publish is captured). Each
/// keeps the newest messages the test has not read and never holds
/// up the harness's session.
pub struct Emitted {}
pub struct Harness {
    core: peppylib::testing::HarnessCore,
    /// Observation subscriptions to the node's own emissions.
    pub emitted: Emitted,
    /// The daemon-clock stand-in serving `peppylib`'s
    /// `clock::synchronize` and the `clock` topic, in the shape
    /// `Config::clock` asked for. The service answers wall time in
    /// every mode (skewable via `set_offset_ns`); `tick` drives the
    /// simulated domain and is only accepted under
    /// `HarnessClock::Consumer`.
    pub clock: peppylib::testing::MockClock,
    session: peppylib::MessengerHandle,
    router: Option<peppylib::testing::EphemeralRouter>,
    instance_id: String,
}
impl Harness {
    /// Boots the full fixture under default configuration. `setup`
    /// is the node's real entry point (the exact closure shape
    /// `NodeBuilder::run` takes), spawned only after every readiness
    /// barrier passed.
    pub async fn start<F, Fut>(setup: F) -> crate::Result<(Self, Mocks)>
    where
        F: FnOnce(crate::Parameters, std::sync::Arc<crate::NodeRunner>) -> Fut,
        Fut: std::future::Future<Output = crate::Result<()>> + Send + 'static,
    {
        Self::start_with(Config::default(), setup).await
    }
    /// [`Harness::start`] with parameter / slot overrides.
    #[allow(clippy::vec_init_then_push)]
    pub async fn start_with<F, Fut>(
        config: Config,
        setup: F,
    ) -> crate::Result<(Self, Mocks)>
    where
        F: FnOnce(crate::Parameters, std::sync::Arc<crate::NodeRunner>) -> Fut,
        Fut: std::future::Future<Output = crate::Result<()>> + Send + 'static,
    {
        let router = peppylib::testing::EphemeralRouter::start().await?;
        let instance_id = config
            .instance_id
            .clone()
            .unwrap_or_else(peppylib::testing::unique_test_instance_id);
        let bare_link = crate::mock::deps::bare_link::Mock::start(&router).await?;
        let session = router.connect().await?;
        let clock = peppylib::testing::MockClock::start(
                &session,
                peppylib::testing::STANDALONE_CORE_NODE,
                peppylib::testing::MOCK_CLOCK_INSTANCE_ID,
                config.clock,
            )
            .await?;
        let mut standalone = peppylib::runtime::StandaloneConfig::new()
            .with_messaging(router.host(), router.port())
            .with_instance_id(instance_id.clone())
            .with_clock(clock.binding()?);
        if let Some(parameters) = &config.parameters {
            standalone = standalone.with_parameters(parameters);
        }
        standalone = standalone
            .with_bound_producer(
                "bare_link",
                crate::mock::deps::bare_link::MOCK_CORE_NODE,
                crate::mock::deps::bare_link::MOCK_INSTANCE_ID,
            );
        let publisher_readiness: Vec<peppylib::testing::PublisherReadiness> = Vec::new();
        let mut service_readiness: Vec<peppylib::testing::ServiceReadiness> = Vec::new();
        service_readiness.push(clock.readiness()?);
        service_readiness
            .push(peppylib::testing::ServiceReadiness {
                target: peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("bare_producer", "v1")?,
                name: "ping".to_string(),
                producer: crate::mock::deps::bare_link::producer_ref_for(
                    crate::mock::deps::bare_link::MOCK_INSTANCE_ID,
                ),
                kind: peppylib::testing::ServiceReadinessKind::Service,
            });
        service_readiness
            .push(peppylib::testing::ServiceReadiness {
                target: peppylib::messaging::SenderTarget::node("bare_producer", "v1")?,
                name: "home_all".to_string(),
                producer: crate::mock::deps::bare_link::producer_ref_for(
                    crate::mock::deps::bare_link::MOCK_INSTANCE_ID,
                ),
                kind: peppylib::testing::ServiceReadinessKind::Action,
            });
        let core = peppylib::testing::HarnessCore::start::<
            crate::Parameters,
            _,
            _,
        >(PEPPY_CONFIG_PATH, standalone, &publisher_readiness, &service_readiness, setup)
            .await?;
        let harness = Harness {
            core,
            emitted: Emitted {},
            clock,
            session,
            router: Some(router),
            instance_id,
        };
        let mocks = Mocks {
            deps: DepMocks { bare_link },
            pairings: PairingMocks {},
            observed: ObservedMocks {},
        };
        Ok((harness, mocks))
    }
    /// The running node, for calling its runtime surface directly.
    pub fn node_runner(&self) -> &std::sync::Arc<crate::NodeRunner> {
        self.core.node_runner()
    }
    /// The fixture caller/observer session (not the node's).
    pub fn session(&self) -> &peppylib::MessengerHandle {
        &self.session
    }
    /// The node-under-test's instance id.
    pub fn instance_id(&self) -> &str {
        &self.instance_id
    }
    /// The node-under-test's wire identity.
    pub fn node_producer_ref(&self) -> peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef {
        peppylib::messaging::ProducerRef::new(
            self.core.bound_core_node(),
            &self.instance_id,
        )
    }
    /// Whether the node's `setup` has returned.
    pub fn setup_finished(&self) -> bool {
        self.core.setup_finished()
    }
    /// Tears the fixture down in lifecycle order: node convergence
    /// (cancel → bounded setup await → shutdown hooks, propagating a
    /// setup error), then the clock stand-in, then the fixture
    /// session, then the router.
    pub async fn shutdown(self) -> crate::Result<()> {
        let Harness { core, clock, session, router, .. } = self;
        let result = core.shutdown().await;
        drop(clock);
        drop(session);
        if let Some(router) = router {
            router.shutdown().await?;
        }
        result
    }
}
